# Accepted decisions (implementation log)

Agents append here every question they met during implementation and the decision they took, so the work never stops. Write in English. Do not edit old entries; add a new entry that supersedes them.

Format:

```
## D-NNN: short title
- Date: YYYY-MM-DD
- Question: what was undefined or contradictory
- Decision: what was chosen and why
- References: architecture documents or code paths
- Status: accepted | superseded by D-MMM | needs owner review
```

## D-001: ORM and database layer
- Date: 2026-10-31
- Question: which ORM, given an easy later migration to PostgreSQL, and one SQLite file per module.
- Decision: Drizzle ORM with `better-sqlite3`. Sequelize is rejected (weak TypeScript typing). Prisma is rejected for iteration 1 because it needs a separate schema and generated client per module database. Keep money as exact decimals or integer cents, dates as ISO text, queries behind repositories, so the move to PostgreSQL is a schema rewrite, not a logic rewrite.
- References: `README.md` section 3
- Status: accepted

## D-002: Money representation
- Date: 2026-10-31
- Question: source amounts have up to 12 decimals; payouts need cents.
- Decision: source amounts and accruals are stored as exact decimal strings and calculated with `decimal.js`; payouts and settlement operations are stored as integer cents; rounding happens only when a payout is formed. This follows the open point 4-О1 of the audit (round only at payout).
- References: `аудит/аудит_итог.md`, `07_модуль_взаиморасчётов.md`
- Status: accepted

## D-003: Web pages
- Date: 2026-10-31
- Question: what kind of pages to build.
- Decision: server-rendered HTML pages served by Express next to the JSON API, no SPA framework, minimal styling. API under `/api`.
- References: user request of 2026-10-31
- Status: superseded by D-007

## D-004: Git
- Date: 2026-10-31
- Question: the project is not under git; may agents commit.
- Decision: agents do not run `git commit` or `git push` unless the user asks; `data/` is git-ignored whenever a repository is created.
- References: `README.md` section 6
- Status: accepted

## D-005: Data volume for iteration 1
- Date: 2026-10-31
- Question: how much Believe history to load.
- Decision: develop and test on July 2026 only (about 3.5 million lines); the loader must stream and be able to load the full 34 months later.
- References: `06_модуль_источников.md`
- Status: accepted

## D-006: Reference check against the current ERP
- Date: 2026-10-31
- Question: how to know the calculation is right.
- Decision: compare one month with the current ERP accruals (`data/erp/data/erp.db`). Differences are expected and must be explained and logged here; they are not failures by themselves.
- References: `README.md` section 2
- Status: accepted

## D-007: Frontend is a minimal React app
- Date: 2026-10-31
- Question: the user prefers a minimal React UI over server-rendered pages, for modularity, speed and convenience.
- Decision: `client/` with React, Vite and TypeScript, React Router, a small typed API client reusing the zod schemas from `src/shared/`, plain CSS and no UI kit. Express serves the JSON API under `/api` and the built client files. Supersedes D-003.
- References: `README.md` sections 3, 4, 8
- Status: accepted

---

Entries D-008 to D-071 were written while building iteration 1 (session of 2026-10-31). They are grouped by topic:
- general and infrastructure: D-008 to D-015;
- module 1 (owners): D-016 to D-024;
- splits: D-025 to D-030;
- sources (Believe): D-031 to D-038;
- publishing (B17): D-039 to D-046;
- settlements and payouts: D-047 to D-056;
- facade: D-057 to D-062;
- data import, reference check and reviews: D-063 to D-071.

## D-008: Raw Believe reports and B17 statements are absent: reconstructed files for the acceptance run
- Date: 2026-10-31
- Question: README §7 points to `../../data/data/believe/raw/`, `data/publishing/` and `data/splits/`, but this copy of `../../data/` has no `data/data/` folder. The manifest `МАНИФЕСТ.json` lists the files (the July report is `2152271_1052428_2026-07-01_2026-07-01.csv.zip`), but they are not present. Only `data/erp/data/erp.db` holds the July 2026 revenue (per ISRC, 7,621 rows, net 790,209.904499203132) and the B17 accruals.
- Decision:
  - The loaders implement the real formats: the 21-column `;`-separated Believe CSV, optionally zipped, and the B17 xlsx detail sheet. The formats were learned from the old code (`data/build/believe_stage.js`, `data/build/pub_v3/01_load_b17.js`, the zero-erp parser) and are tested on synthetic files.
  - For the acceptance run, `scripts/import-legacy.ts` reconstructs from erp.db (read-only) two files in the real formats:
    - the July 2026 Believe report: one line per ISRC + UPC, platform `RECONSTRUCTED_FROM_ERP_DB`, sales month = reporting month, gross = net;
    - the B17 2026-Q2 statement (see D-066).
  - Both files are written to `data/demo/import/` (git-ignored) and uploaded through the API like real files.
  - Consequence: platform, country and sales-month breakdowns and the sales-date filter are not meaningful on this demo data. The totals are real. Once the raw files are placed into `../../data/data/`, they load with the same code.
- References: `README.md` §7, §7a; `scripts/import-legacy.ts`
- Status: needs owner review (put the raw reports into `data/data/` and reload)

## D-009: Layout, module boundaries and their enforcement
- Date: 2026-10-31
- Question: how to keep the modules independent.
- Decision: the layout follows README §4.
  - Each module (`owners`, `splits`, `sources`, `settlements`, `publishing`) has `schema.ts` (Drizzle tables + portable DDL), `repository.ts` (all SQL), `service.ts` (rules) and `index.ts` (public interface: `open<Module>Module(file)`).
  - Each module opens its own SQLite file: `owners.sqlite`, `splits.sqlite`, `sources.sqlite`, `settlements.sqlite`, `publishing.sqlite` under the data directory.
  - Only `src/facade/` opens and composes modules. The API calls the facade, or one module service for single-module actions.
  - `tests/boundaries.test.ts` fails if a module imports another module, the facade or the API, if the API opens a database, or if the five files are not created.
  - ESLint `no-restricted-imports` repeats the rule.
  - The structure study of the `typereal` archives (extracted only to the scratchpad, nothing run) was used for conventions only: an error class hierarchy with a JSON envelope, `index.ts` as the module boundary, strict tsconfig.
- References: `README.md` §4; `src/facade/index.ts`; `tests/boundaries.test.ts`
- Status: accepted

## D-010: Money inside the modules: pico-USD integers for source amounts (refines D-002)
- Date: 2026-10-31
- Question: D-002 says source amounts are exact decimal strings. Summing millions of lines exactly in SQL is impossible with text.
- Decision:
  - Source amounts (Believe net and gross, B17 Total Payable) are stored as INTEGER pico-USD (1e-12) and parsed without rounding. A value with more than 12 significant decimals rejects the file.
  - SQL sums are read as BigInt (`safeIntegers`). The SQLite/PostgreSQL bigint limit is about $9.2M per single SUM; per-file and per-unit sums stay far below it.
  - Calculations use decimal.js (precision 40), never floats.
  - Settlement operations and payouts are integer cents.
  - JSON carries money as plain decimal strings: a custom `Decimal.toJSON` emits no exponent and no `-0`.
  - Rounding happens only when a payout is formed: rounded DOWN to cents, so we never overpay. The exact payable is kept in the snapshot.
- References: `src/shared/money.ts`; D-002
- Status: accepted

## D-011: Portability to PostgreSQL
- Date: 2026-10-31
- Question: SQLite now, PostgreSQL later.
- Decision:
  - Each module has a hand-written portable DDL (TEXT/INTEGER, ISO text dates, plain and unique indexes, `ON CONFLICT`) applied on open, next to Drizzle table definitions used for typed queries. drizzle-kit migrations are postponed.
  - Aggregates that need BigInt use raw prepared statements inside the module repository.
  - The "attributes per pair" query uses `MAX()` instead of a bare column.
  - Known gap: the split overlap check is check-then-insert; this is safe in one process, and PostgreSQL will need an exclusion constraint or SERIALIZABLE.
- References: `src/shared/db.ts`, `src/modules/*/schema.ts`
- Status: accepted

## D-012: One consistent read per calculation ("data version", 09 principle 17)
- Date: 2026-10-31
- Question: how the facade fixes the data version for one request.
- Decision:
  - better-sqlite3 is synchronous and runs in one process. `calculate()` reads every module once at its start, with no awaits in between, so no write can interleave.
  - Uploads keep their write transaction open across awaits. They are serialized per module (D-034), and readers only see files with status `loaded`.
  - No caches: every request recalculates from scratch.
- References: `src/facade/calculation.ts`
- Status: accepted

## D-013: No authentication, stubbed payment service, payout threshold
- Date: 2026-10-31
- Question: access rights, payment-service integration and the payout threshold.
- Decision:
  - No auth (README §2).
  - The payment service is a stub that returns a reference. A payout stays deferred until a payment-proof document is attached.
  - The threshold is a configuration value (`PAYOUT_THRESHOLD_CENTS`, default 10000 = $100). It is not part of the calculation; it only disables the button and is refused by the API.
- References: `src/facade/payment-service.ts`; 07 rule 5
- Status: accepted

## D-014: Sensitive sources not used
- Date: 2026-10-31
- Question: PandaDoc, bank and wio statements, personal data.
- Decision:
  - PandaDoc was not called.
  - Bank and wio statements were not read.
  - No personal data is copied into `app/`, tests or logs: fixtures are synthetic.
  - Demo owners are named "Owner of <pseudonym>". Writers without a master pseudonym are "Writer #<party id>". Contract documents get references `legacy-erp:contract/party-<ids>`, not file names.
  - The reconstructed report contains track and release titles but no artist or label names.
- References: `README.md` §6
- Status: accepted

## D-015: Start scripts and data directory
- Date: 2026-10-31
- Question: how to run.
- Decision:
  - `npm run import:legacy` recreates `data/demo` from erp.db, plus the two reconstructed files.
  - `npm run demo` starts the API and the built client on :3000 with `data/demo`.
  - `npm test` runs vitest; `npm run typecheck` runs tsc for the server and the client; `npm run build:client` runs vite build.
  - The data directory can also be passed as the first argument or as `ERP_DATA_DIR`.
  - No `.claude/launch.json` outside `app/` was created.
- References: `package.json`, `src/main.ts`
- Status: accepted

## D-016: Document status
- Date: 2026-10-31
- Question: the statuses "not attached / not approved / approved" and the "requires manager approval" flag.
- Decision:
  - No file reference → `not_attached`.
  - The file is attached, the document requires approval, and nobody has approved it → `not_approved`.
  - Otherwise → `approved`. A document that does not require approval is effective as soon as its file is attached.
  - Approval needs a file. Re-approving keeps the first approval.
  - Attaching a new file resets the approval (review finding).
  - Contracts created in the UI default to "requires approval" (the checkbox is pre-ticked). The flag stays per document, as written in 02.
- References: `src/modules/owners/service.ts` (`documentStatus`)
- Status: accepted

## D-017: When an owner and an artist are confirmed
- Date: 2026-10-31
- Question: what makes an owner "closed".
- Decision:
  - An owner is confirmed when one of its contracts points to an approved document of kind `contract`. This is the payout gate.
  - The contract document must belong to that owner, or have no owner yet; then it is assigned to it.
  - An artist is confirmed when it is listed in an approved contract of its own owner.
  - Artist status: `no_owner` | `unconfirmed` | `confirmed`. It only marks; the money is decided by owner and split confirmation.
- References: 02, «Документ как начало»; `src/modules/owners/service.ts`
- Status: accepted

## D-018: Contract record without personal data
- Date: 2026-10-31
- Question: 02 lists `person_fio` and `person_data` on CONTRACT.
- Decision: in iteration 1 the contract stores owner, document, number and the confirmed pseudonyms. Person data stays in the document file (no PDF parsing; README §2). The fields will be added with access rights.
- References: 02 schema
- Status: needs owner review

## D-019: The owner of an existing artist is fixed
- Date: 2026-10-31
- Question: open question 99/11 (owner change and merge).
- Decision:
  - An artist without an owner gets one when documents arrive; no new artist record is created.
  - Changing the owner of an artist that already has one is refused (`artist_owner_fixed`): create a second artist record instead, as in ADR-001.
  - Merging artists of different owners is refused.
- References: 99 item 11; ADR-001
- Status: accepted (until 99/11 is decided)

## D-020: Merge rules
- Date: 2026-10-31
- Question: what moves on a merge, its direction, chains.
- Decision:
  - Owners: only an owner without approved documents merges, and only into a confirmed live owner. Both must be live records, so there are no chains or cycles.
  - The merge moves artists, aliases, documents and contracts. It sets `merged_into` and logs the merge in the `merges` table.
  - The facade then moves the publishing writer mappings (resolution `merged`) and settlement operations to the survivor.
  - A merge is refused while the merged owner has a payout without payment proof.
  - Artists: both must be live and have the same owner (or `from` has none). Track links and contract links move.
  - References to merged ids are resolved through `merged_into`. Merged records cannot receive new artists, documents or contracts.
  - There is no undo yet (02, open).
- References: 02 «Объединение записей»; M12
- Status: accepted

## D-021: Possible duplicates are shown, never merged
- Date: 2026-10-31
- Question: how to detect duplicates on input.
- Decision:
  - The candidates are owners with the same normalized name or alias, or the same set of words in another order ("LAST, FIRST" vs "First Last").
  - Artists match by normalized pseudonym.
  - The API answers 409 `possible_duplicate` with the candidates; the operator repeats with `force: true` to add a new record.
  - Remembering "these are different people" is not implemented (02, open).
- References: 02 «Похожие записи при вводе»
- Status: accepted

## D-022: Tracks and the artist-track link
- Date: 2026-10-31
- Question: track keys and the link.
- Decision:
  - A track has a generated id. (ISRC, UPC) is unique; both may be empty before delivery.
  - ISRC is normalized and checked; UPC is digits only, and empty means null.
  - The artist-track link is informational only and never used in the calculation; a test asserts this.
  - Releases are not a separate entity in iteration 1: UPC is kept on the track.
- References: ADR-001; 01 principle 10
- Status: accepted

## D-023: Unknown ISRC/UPC pair in Believe
- Date: 2026-10-31
- Question: implementation of 06 decision 10.
- Decision:
  - Nothing is created and the lines are kept.
  - The amount counts as C (`unknown_pair`), and lines without ISRC as C (`no_isrc`).
  - `GET /api/signals` lists the pairs with money, months and report titles.
  - After the pair is entered in module 1, the next request allocates it.
- References: 06 decision 10
- Status: accepted

## D-024: Document upload deadline
- Date: 2026-10-31
- Question: `expiredTime`.
- Decision: the field is `upload_deadline` (an ISO date) and it is only displayed. Nothing happens on expiry; M11 is open.
- References: 02; M11
- Status: accepted

## D-025: Split periods: report date, exclusive end
- Date: 2026-10-31
- Question: the date semantics of splits.
- Decision:
  - `valid_from` is inclusive and `valid_to` is exclusive; null means open-ended. Periods of one ISRC never overlap.
  - The split is chosen by the report date = the first day of the reporting month. So a split or buyout starting mid-month first applies to the next month's report.
  - The sales date is stored for a later switch (04 question 6).
- References: 04 rules 6, 7
- Status: accepted

## D-026: Split change semantics
- Date: 2026-10-31
- Question: changes in the middle of history and on a split's first day.
- Decision:
  - A change needs a document of kind appendix, contract or buyout.
  - The split in force at `from` is closed at `from`, and the new split lasts until that split's old end. If no split is in force, it lasts until the next existing split.
  - A split replaced from its first day remains as an empty period `[from, from)`, so history is kept (review finding).
  - Who closed a split is not recorded separately; the new split carries the document.
- References: 04 rules 6, 8, 10
- Status: accepted

## D-027: Buyout
- Date: 2026-10-31
- Question: a buyout across several periods.
- Decision:
  - The seller's share becomes 0 from the effective date, in the split in force and in every later split where the seller has a share.
  - The document must be of kind `buyout`.
  - The buyout payment is not an operation.
  - Partial buyouts from the old registry (`seller_pct` < 100) are imported as a reduction of the seller's share by that many points, as the old engine did. This is a split change confirmed by the buyout document.
- References: 04 rule 10; 07 question 1
- Status: accepted

## D-028: Split confirmation and the percent format
- Date: 2026-10-31
- Question: confirmation and precision.
- Decision:
  - A split without a document, or with an unapproved one, is calculated but goes to C (`split_unconfirmed`).
  - Its document can be set once (`confirmSplit`); a different document requires a change.
  - Percents are 0..100 with at most 6 decimals, and their sum is at most 100.
  - The facade validates the shares and the overlap before creating new artists, so no orphan artists remain after a refused split.
- References: 04 rules 3a, 8
- Status: accepted

## D-029: ISRC without split
- Date: 2026-10-31
- Question: implementation of 04 rule 15.
- Decision:
  - The income stays in our share.
  - The reconciliation shows it separately ("of it, no split"), and `/api/signals` lists the ISRCs as information for managers.
- References: 04 rule 15, question 4
- Status: accepted

## D-030: Base split templates not implemented
- Date: 2026-10-31
- Question: 04 question 2 and M8.
- Decision: not in iteration 1. Shares are entered explicitly. The UI shows "our share = remainder" while you type.
- References: 04
- Status: accepted

## D-031: Believe file name, account, month, generation date
- Date: 2026-10-31
- Question: where the scope and the generation date come from.
- Decision:
  - The file name pattern is `<reportId>_<account>_<from>_<to>.csv[.zip]`; browser copies `.csv (n).zip` are accepted. The account and the reporting month come from the name.
  - Every line's `Reporting month` must equal that month; otherwise the whole file is rejected.
  - The generation date is not in the file. The order of sources is:
    - the uploader's `generatedAt` field (validated);
    - else the zip entry modification time;
    - else the upload time.
  - `generated_at_source` records which of them was used.
- References: 06; old loaders
- Status: accepted

## D-032: Line key, summing, control check
- Date: 2026-10-31
- Question: implementation of 06 decisions 2 and 11.
- Decision:
  - The key is account, reporting month, sales month, platform, country, ISRC, UPC, currency, subscription type, sales type, release type.
  - Lines with the same key are summed in SQL (`ON CONFLICT DO UPDATE`): quantity, gross, net.
  - The control check is "key sums = stream sums and the line count"; a failure rejects the whole file atomically.
  - Names (artist, label, titles, catalogue number) are stored as deduplicated attribute sets.
  - Unit price, mechanical fee and client share rate are not stored, because they vary per line and are not in the totals; the original file is kept as is.
  - When lines merge, the first attribute set is kept (not the longest title).
- References: 06 decisions 1, 2, 11; 03 item 2
- Status: accepted (stored values: needs owner review)

## D-033: Duplicates and the original file
- Date: 2026-10-31
- Question: a second file for the same period.
- Decision:
  - A second file for an already loaded (account, reporting month) gives HTTP 409 `duplicate_period` with the existing file name and sha256.
  - The same content under another name gives `duplicate_file` (sha256 of the uploaded file; for a zip, the hash of the archive).
  - The original file is copied unchanged to `data/<dir>/files/sources/<sha256>-<name>`.
- References: 06 «Повторный файл на ту же область»
- Status: accepted

## D-034: Upload robustness
- Date: 2026-10-31
- Question: issues from the sources review.
- Decision:
  - Uploads are streamed by busboy to a temporary file. A cut-off upload over the 2 GB limit is an error, and the temporary file is removed on any failure.
  - Loads are serialized per module through a promise queue, so two uploads never share a transaction.
  - A `loading` row left by a crash is removed when the module opens.
  - The insert race on the scope index maps to `duplicate_period`.
  - The CSV parser is strict:
    - invalid UTF-8 is an error;
    - a field longer than 64 KB (an unclosed quote) is an error;
    - header columns are matched after trimming and case-insensitively, and duplicated header columns are an error.
  - An empty currency is taken as USD with a warning; any other currency rejects the file.
- References: sources review
- Status: accepted

## D-035: Lines without ISRC and negative lines
- Date: 2026-10-31
- Question: open question 06/2 and M10.
- Decision:
  - Lines without ISRC are kept with their UPC and shown as C `no_isrc` (unallocated), with a warning on the file.
  - No donor-track rule (the old ERP split them by a donor track on the same UPC).
  - Negative lines are kept and calculated like any other.
- References: 06 question 2; 99 item 13
- Status: needs owner review (M10)

## D-036: Unit of calculation
- Date: 2026-10-31
- Question: what the sources module gives the facade.
- Decision: units grouped by ISRC + UPC + reporting month + sales month, with the net sum. `breakdown(isrc)` gives platform, country, subscription and sales type per month for "why this amount".
- References: 06 decision 3; 09 principle 6
- Status: accepted

## D-037: Performance target
- Date: 2026-10-31
- Question: 1 GB files.
- Decision:
  - The loader never holds a file in memory.
  - A test loads 200,000 lines in about 2 s and checks the heap growth.
  - The 3.5M-line real month was not available to measure (D-008).
- References: `tests/sources.test.ts`
- Status: accepted

## D-038: Other Believe accounts and sources
- Date: 2026-10-31
- Question: more than one account.
- Decision: the scope is per account, so the same month for another account is accepted. Other sources (Vydia and others) are not implemented.
- References: 06 question 6
- Status: accepted

## D-039: B17 format
- Date: 2026-10-31
- Question: which sheet and columns.
- Decision:
  - The detail sheet is the one whose A1 is `Period` and whose header contains `asset title`.
  - Required columns: `asset title`, `rights-holder name`, `Total Payable` (old files: `Payable`).
  - `rights-holder name` is taken as the author (writer).
  - The quarter comes from the file name (`Q22026` or `2Q26`) when exactly one quarter is named, otherwise from the `Period` column. A row whose Period differs from the file quarter is an error.
  - Rows with neither title nor holder (totals) are skipped.
  - Updated files with only a "Songwriter Breakdown" sheet are not supported.
- References: 08; old `01_load_b17.js`
- Status: accepted

## D-040: xlsx reading and number precision
- Date: 2026-10-31
- Question: streaming and float cells.
- Decision:
  - Uploads are streamed to disk, and the statement is read with the exceljs streaming reader.
  - If the archive has `workbook.xml` after the sheets (files written by exceljs), that reader fails; the statement is then read in memory. Statements are a few MB.
  - Numeric and formula cells are doubles. They keep 15 significant digits and are then cut to 12 decimals; text with `$` or `,`, or an Excel error, rejects the file.
- References: publishing review
- Status: accepted

## D-041: A second B17 statement for the same quarter is an error
- Date: 2026-10-31
- Question: the "Updated" statements (Q2 2026 has an original and an Updated file).
- Decision: the same rule as Believe: a second file for a loaded quarter gives `duplicate_period`. Replacing a statement is postponed together with 06 question 5.
- References: 06; 08
- Status: needs owner review

## D-042: Writer → owner resolution
- Date: 2026-10-31
- Question: implementation of 08 decisions 4, 7 and question 10.
- Decision:
  - An exact normalized alias (or IPI) of exactly one person-owner → `matched`.
  - Else a similar person-owner (same words) or several candidates → `ambiguous`. The whole payable stays in C `writer_unmapped` until the operator maps it: `POST /api/publishing/writers/:norm/map` with an owner or `createNew`.
  - Else a new person-owner without documents is created (origin `publishing`) and accrued to; its money is C.
  - Label owners are never candidates, and terms for a label are refused.
  - IPI is not in B17 statements, so it is not stored from them.
- References: 08 decisions 4, 7; questions 9, 10
- Status: accepted

## D-043: Author percentage and its confirmation
- Date: 2026-10-31
- Question: open question 08/12 (author without publishing terms) and the confirmation of terms.
- Decision:
  - The terms are per owner (percent, validity, document) and are chosen by the report date of the statement (D-044).
  - Without terms, the default 50% is calculated but counts as C (`publishing_terms_unconfirmed`), like a split without appendix.
  - Terms with an approved document of that owner count as B.
  - The label share is always Total Payable minus the author amount.
  - Changing open terms requires a document.
- References: 08 decisions 3, 8, 9; question 12
- Status: needs owner review (08/12)

## D-044: Publishing dates
- Date: 2026-10-31
- Question: open question 08/5.
- Decision:
  - A quarterly statement is dated by the last month of its quarter (Q2 → June). Both the period filter and the terms choice use that month.
  - There is no sales date, so the sales-date basis also uses it.
  - A recoupment of publishing "after the date" compares months.
- References: 08 question 5
- Status: accepted

## D-045: Publishing buyouts
- Date: 2026-10-31
- Question: 08 question 6, 99 item 12.
- Decision:
  - A publishing buyout is expressed as terms with 0% from the effective date, confirmed by the buyout document. The import does this for sellers that already had publishing terms.
  - It applies to every work of the owner; per-work exceptions are not modelled.
  - Quarter granularity follows D-044.
- References: 08 question 6
- Status: needs owner review

## D-046: Merge and publishing
- Date: 2026-10-31
- Question: merged owners with terms.
- Decision:
  - Writer mappings move to the surviving owner.
  - Terms stay on their record and are resolved through the merge; the survivor's own terms win.
- References: 02 merge; publishing review
- Status: accepted

## D-047: Operations: direction, sign, meaning
- Date: 2026-10-31
- Question: open question 07/14 (direction of returns and credits) and "no types".
- Decision: an operation stores direction and a signed amount in cents. The meaning is derived, for display only:
  - `out` without scope: payout of earned money (to an owner).
  - `out` with scope: recoupable payout (advance or bonus). It needs an artist, a scope and a basis document.
  - `in` positive: deduction (penalty, recalculation), tied to an artist; its scope defaults to `any`.
  - `in` negative with `operationRef`: return of a payout.
  - `in` negative without `operationRef`: credit (adds to the balance; for example a manual M10 accrual).
- References: 07 «Операции in и out», question 14
- Status: accepted

## D-048: What counts in the balance
- Date: 2026-10-31
- Question: confirmation of operations.
- Decision:
  - A payout counts once its payment-proof document is approved (attached).
  - A recoupable payout applies as a position only when both its payment proof and its basis document are approved (07 question 8).
  - Deductions, credits and returns apply only with an approved document.
  - Operations marked `migration` count without documents (the transfer exception for the superadmin) and never block payouts.
  - Inactive (soft-rolled-back) operations are ignored.
  - Pending operations are listed with the reason.
- References: 07 rules 4, 7; «Перенос выплат»
- Status: accepted

## D-049: Recoupment rules
- Date: 2026-10-31
- Question: open questions 07/6, 07/7 and the scope details.
- Decision:
  - **Order:** positions are recouped largest first by position amount, then by date. Within a position, income is taken oldest report month first.
  - **Master income:** recoups only positions of the same artist.
  - **Publishing income:** the owner's publishing recoups positions of any of the owner's artists whose scope includes publishing.
  - **Scopes:** `any`, `master`, `publishing`, `master_publishing`, `isrcs`, plus `excludeIsrcs` (ISRCs normalized). Releases (UPC) as a scope unit are not supported (07 question 9).
  - **Advances:** recouped from income whose report month is on or after the month of the advance date. The same month counts.
  - **Deductions:** no date restriction (they reduce what is payable). Review finding: the "after the date" rule is written for advances.
  - **Netting:** income is netted per artist + ISRC + report month (publishing: per report month) before recoupment, so negative lines cannot make an out-of-scope position eat other income.
  - **Unrecouped rest:** it stays and never reduces other income.
- References: 07 «Охват погашения»; settlements review
- Status: accepted (07/6 "largest" = position amount: needs owner review)

## D-050: Payable formula and negative balance
- Date: 2026-10-31
- Question: implementation of the formula.
- Decision:
  - payable = earned (B) − recouped − payouts with proof + returns + credits.
  - An advance is not subtracted as such; only its recouped part is.
  - A negative payable is kept with its minus and shown as 0 (`payableShown`).
- References: 07 «Принцип расчёта»
- Status: accepted

## D-051: Payout button
- Date: 2026-10-31
- Question: the payout flow.
- Decision:
  - The button pays one amount per owner: the whole payable, rounded down to cents. Partial payouts are not supported (09 question 2).
  - Checks: owner confirmed; no blocking payout; amount > 0; amount ≥ threshold.
  - The payout is recorded first (deferred, with its snapshot), and only then sent to the payment service. A failed send leaves a blocking record, never an unrecorded transfer (review finding).
  - A plain payout of earned money cannot be created through the operations endpoint (`use_payout_button`), except migrated payouts.
- References: 09 principles 4, 14; 07 rule 4
- Status: accepted

## D-052: Blocking, deferral, returns
- Date: 2026-10-31
- Question: implementation of 07 rule 4.
- Decision:
  - A payout without payment proof, or a deferred one, blocks new payouts of that owner.
  - Attaching the proof clears the deferral; a payout takes only one proof.
  - A return is a separate `in` operation linked by `operationRef`. It is allowed only for an active, counted plain payout. Returns together cannot exceed the payout, and the return goes to the payout's owner.
  - A payout with active returns cannot be deactivated.
- References: 07 rule 4; settlements review
- Status: accepted

## D-053: Payout snapshot
- Date: 2026-10-31
- Question: 09 principles 8, 12 and 06 decision 8.
- Decision: the snapshot stored with each payout contains:
  - the exact payable and the all-time totals;
  - `payoutByArtist`: the payout split pro rata over each artist's master net (earned minus what recoupment consumed from that artist's income) and the owner's publishing net (minus what was recouped from publishing);
  - per-artist all-time figures with up to 200 tracks;
  - the publishing lines and the recoup positions;
  - the active files with sha256 (Believe and B17).
- References: 09 principles 8, 12
- Status: accepted

## D-054: Settlements are kept on the owner of the artist at creation
- Date: 2026-10-31
- Question: positions are evaluated per owner.
- Decision: an operation stores the artist and its owner at creation. Owner changes of an artist are not allowed (D-019); after a merge the operations move to the survivor.
- References: 99 item 11
- Status: accepted

## D-055: Bad debt write-off, rights for adjustments
- Date: 2026-10-31
- Question: 07 questions 4 and 15.
- Decision: not implemented. A write-off can be entered as a credit with a document if needed.
- References: 07
- Status: accepted

## D-056: Soft rollback of operations
- Date: 2026-10-31
- Question: 07 question 3.
- Decision: `deactivate` keeps the record with a reason and the recalculation ignores it. A storno (a reverse operation) is also possible.
- References: 07 question 3
- Status: accepted

## D-057: Completeness check A/B/C and its alarms
- Date: 2026-10-31
- Question: 09 principle 11 and the open items of 3-С2.
- Decision:
  - Per source and month:
    - A = all money of the source;
    - B = confirmed money of artists and owners;
    - C = waiting, with a breakdown: split unconfirmed, artist without owner, owner unconfirmed, publishing terms unconfirmed, unknown pair, no ISRC, writer unmapped;
    - our share = A − B − C.
  - Alarms:
    - `discrepancy` = our share − Σ remainders;
    - `fileDiscrepancy` = A − the totals stored at load (report-date basis);
    - `negativeRemainders` counts lines whose remainder has the opposite sign of the line (shares above 100).
  - Negative report lines are normal and calculated as they are.
- References: 09 principle 11
- Status: accepted

## D-058: Owner view figures
- Date: 2026-10-31
- Question: what "received" means for one owner.
- Decision:
  - For the requested period (report or sales date):
    - received = source money of every unit where the owner has a share, plus the Total Payable of the owner's publishing lines;
    - not ours = the owner's B;
    - waiting = the owner's C;
    - other participants = the shares of other owners on those units;
    - our share = their remainder;
    - also paid, advances paid and recouped in the period.
  - Payable is always all-time "as of today". Per-artist and per-track breakdowns are included.
- References: 09 principles 13, 16
- Status: accepted

## D-059: Owner of a split artist without an owner
- Date: 2026-10-31
- Question: 04 rule 13.
- Decision: such an artist's money is C `artist_without_owner`. It becomes B when an owner is assigned and confirmed.
- References: 04 rule 13; audit 6-Б5
- Status: accepted

## D-060: The API composes modules only through the facade
- Date: 2026-10-31
- Question: facade review finding.
- Decision:
  - Lists, confirmations and publishing terms that need two modules go through facade methods: `listSplits`, `confirmSplit`, `setPublishingTerms`, `mapWriter`.
  - `limit` and filters are validated with zod.
  - Errors have one envelope: `{error: {code, message, statusCode, details}}`.
- References: README §4
- Status: accepted

## D-061: Signals use the chosen date basis
- Date: 2026-10-31
- Question: facade review finding.
- Decision: `/api/signals` filters by the report or sales month, as reconciliation does.
- References: 09 principle 11
- Status: accepted

## D-062: UI scope
- Date: 2026-10-31
- Question: the pages of README §8.
- Decision:
  - All six pages are implemented, one folder per domain in `client/src`.
  - Contracts can be registered on the owner page (document + pseudonyms).
  - The splits page creates splits and changes them with a document; buyouts are API-only.
  - Uploads show the duplicate error from the API.
- References: README §8
- Status: accepted

## D-063: Legacy import: owners and artists
- Date: 2026-10-31
- Question: the old ERP has parties, not owners.
- Decision:
  - Every non-house party in the current split version or in B17 2026-Q2 accruals becomes an artist (if it has splits).
  - Parties are grouped into owners by their payment account (`payee.ext_id`); otherwise one owner per party.
  - A group with a record in `contract` gets an approved migration contract document listing its artists with contracts, plus 50% publishing terms if it has publishing.
  - Result: 1,082 owners (341 confirmed) and 1,107 artists.
- References: `scripts/import-legacy.ts`
- Status: accepted (for the demo)

## D-064: Legacy import: tracks and splits
- Date: 2026-10-31
- Question: which splits are "confirmed by an appendix".
- Decision:
  - Tracks come from `track`: 11,929, with 263 `NOISRC` keys skipped.
  - Splits come from `split_line` of the current version, without house parties (party 1 = our remainder), valid from 2000-01-01. ISRCs in `split_exception` are skipped.
  - A split is confirmed by one migration appendix document (the Backstage register) when every participant with a non-zero share has a contract: 6,778 confirmed, 5,785 not.
  - 112 ISRCs whose split is 100% house have no split in the new system: same money (ours), shown as "no split".
- References: 05 D1–D7
- Status: accepted (for the demo)

## D-065: Legacy import: buyouts
- Date: 2026-10-31
- Question: how the old buyout registry is applied.
- Decision:
  - Each buyout becomes a split change of the seller from `effective` (D-027), confirmed by an approved migration buyout document: 1,047 changes.
  - 43 bought tracks without a seller share are skipped, and buyout #19, which has no tracks, is skipped.
- References: 04 rule 10
- Status: accepted (for the demo)

## D-066: Reconstructed B17 2026-Q2 statement
- Date: 2026-10-31
- Question: how a statement is rebuilt from accruals.
- Decision: from `accrual` (publishing, 2026-Q2, b17), without the publisher-half rows:
  - writer rows → a line for that owner with Total Payable = 2 × writer amount;
  - buyout and override rows → a line for the seller (`from_party`);
  - absorbed, review, name, bought_track and share_error rows → holder "UNRESOLVED RIGHTS-HOLDER" (100% for rows that had no publisher half).
  - The total equals the real statement total, 417,084.933547…. The new system created one owner without documents for the synthetic holder (the unknown-author rule).
- References: D-008
- Status: accepted (for the demo)

## D-067: Reference check, master July 2026: result
- Date: 2026-10-31
- Question: comparison with `erp.db` accruals (`scripts/reconcile-erp.ts`, and an independent reconciliation agent that recomputed from the module databases).
- Decision (differences explained):
  - **Totals:** revenue 790,209.904499 on both sides.
    - New: artists (B + C) 389,153.499, of which B 311,740.815 and C 77,412.684; our share 400,863.724, of which no-split 6,068.315; unallocated (no ISRC) 192.681.
    - Old: artist accruals 385,644.790 without sub-label (390,756.003 with sub-label party 1346); house + sub-label 402,360.459; unallocated 2,204.656.
  - **Pairs:** 12,563 of 12,634 (ISRC, party) pairs are equal to 1e-6 (excluding the sub-label). The differences are:
    1. **Buyout #5** (effective 2026-08-13), 10 pairs, +3,589.99 to the seller. The old ERP moves the seller's share from the report month before the effective date (`firstBuyerMonth`, M−1/M−2); the new one applies it by report date (D-025). Example: DGA072567654 +2,883.05. Buyouts #11, #13 and #15 have no July revenue on their tracks.
    2. **Albums without ISRC**, 56–78 pairs, −81.28 artists (−0.40 sub-label). The old ERP used the donor-track split; the new one keeps the 192.68 in C `no_isrc` (D-035).
    3. **Sub-label NoGhost 25%** (party 1346), 257 pairs, 5,110.81. The old ERP carves it out of the house share (`ref/sublabel_rules.json`); the new system has no sub-label rule, so the money stays in our share. This is a label-side split and needs a decision.
    4. **Classification only:**
       - 2,179.78 on 591 ISRCs that the old ERP leaves unallocated ("нет сплита") are ours in the new system (04 rule 15);
       - 3,888.52 on 112 ISRCs with a 100%-house split are "no split" here (D-064);
       - 76,471.59 + 941.09 of artist money is held as C because the split appendix or the contract is unconfirmed. The amounts are the same; they are only not payable yet.
  - **Bridge:** our share 397,249.246 + 5,110.815 − 3,589.988 + 2,179.780 + 0.018 − 86.146 = 400,863.724 (the independent agent's bridge, house only).
- References: `scripts/reconcile-erp.ts`, `data/demo/recon/`
- Status: accepted; sub-label rule: needs owner review

## D-068: Reference check, B17 2026-Q2: result
- Date: 2026-10-31
- Question: publishing differences.
- Decision (differences explained):
  - **Totals:** statement 417,084.934 on both sides.
    - Old: writers 136,632.54; label 277,799.37 + suspense 2,653.03.
    - New: authors 205,486.52 (B 126,959.45; C owner-unconfirmed 31,720.20 and terms-unconfirmed 46,806.87); label 207,485.47; C writer-unmapped 4,112.94.
  - **Causes:**
    1. **Buyout rows** (+39,968.73): most sellers have no publishing terms in the import, so the default 50% applies as C. Buyouts dated after June (parties 23, 37) would not apply to Q2 anyway (D-044, D-045).
    2. **Override** to the label (party 98, +6,838.14): not modelled; the default 50% is C.
    3. **Absorbed / bought_track / review / name / share_error** rows (+24,105.82): they go to the synthetic "UNRESOLVED RIGHTS-HOLDER" owner as C (D-066).
    4. **An ambiguous writer** (owner of party 40, −2,056.47 author): a similar person-owner exists, so the whole 4,112.94 waits for the operator (D-042).
    5. **0% terms of sellers 14, 79 and 5** apply to all their works (−2.34; the old ERP paid small amounts on works outside the buyout).
  - The remaining owner-unconfirmed money equals the old amounts and is held only for missing contracts.
  - ASCAP 2026-Q2 (184,245.73 in the old ERP) is out of scope.
- References: 08; D-042 to D-045
- Status: accepted; publishing buyout/override modelling: needs owner review

## D-069: Independent module reviews: outcome
- Date: 2026-10-31
- Question: README §9: findings of the six reviewer agents (owners, splits, sources, settlements, publishing, facade + API), each given only file paths.
- Decision: fixed, with regression tests in `tests/review-fixes.test.ts`, `tests/api.test.ts` and `tests/boundaries.test.ts`.
  - **Owners:**
    - merge cycles, chains and direction (D-020);
    - live-owner checks;
    - contract document ownership (D-017);
    - approval reset on a new file (D-016);
    - label owners excluded from publishing (D-042);
    - empty UPC → null.
  - **Splits:**
    - change or buyout on a split's first day, and with later splits (D-026, D-027);
    - orphan artists on a refused split (D-028);
    - confirm only once with an appendix, contract or buyout document;
    - percent precision.
  - **Sources:**
    - concurrent uploads, crash recovery, the duplicate race;
    - truncation and temp cleanup;
    - strict UTF-8, field-length cap, header duplicates;
    - generation date from the zip;
    - portable attribute query;
    - empty-currency warning (D-031, D-034).
  - **Settlements:**
    - returns only of counted plain payouts, cumulative limit (D-052);
    - payout recorded before sending (D-051);
    - migrated payouts never block (D-048);
    - netting of negative income (D-049);
    - advance needs an approved basis (D-048);
    - manual payouts only via the button (D-051);
    - scope ISRC normalization;
    - deactivation with returns.
  - **Publishing:**
    - label exclusion;
    - similar names go to the operator, with a manual mapping endpoint (D-042);
    - terms not moved on merge (D-046);
    - formula and float cells (D-040);
    - totals rows; period mismatch.
  - **Facade/API:**
    - composition only through the facade (D-060);
    - zod for query parameters;
    - JSON money format (D-010);
    - file-total and sign alarms (D-057);
    - snapshot attribution (D-053);
    - signals basis (D-061);
    - ISRC normalized in explain.
- References: review reports (session of 2026-10-31)
- Status: accepted

## D-070: Review findings explained, not changed
- Date: 2026-10-31
- Question: findings left as they are.
- Decision:
  - No RELEASE entity or merge undo (D-022, D-020).
  - No `person_fio` (D-018).
  - Unit price and fees are not stored (D-032).
  - The sha256 of a zip is the archive hash (D-033).
  - No Updated / Songwriter Breakdown support (D-039, D-041).
  - Contracts may be flagged "no approval needed" (D-016).
  - Deduction date rule (D-049).
  - Period "recouped" uses aggregated income items, which is approximate for the sales-date basis.
  - The split overlap check is not enforced by the database (D-011).
  - The B17 ingest and writer resolution span two databases without a shared transaction: unmapped writers are retried on the next ingest, and the operator can map them manually.
  - Ingest + resolve atomicity across modules is not possible without a shared transaction (one SQLite file per module).
- References: review reports
- Status: accepted

## D-072: Second review round: recoupment is capped by the net income in scope (supersedes the netting part of D-049)
- Date: 2026-10-31
- Question: the second independent review of settlements and the facade found two problems with D-049.
  - D-049 netted income only per artist + ISRC + report month. Negative lines of another ISRC or a later month did not reduce what a scoped position could take, so a master advance could still effectively eat publishing income. This breaks README §5: "the unrecouped rest never reduces other income".
  - The order of equal positions was not deterministic.
- Decision:
  - `recoup()` keeps a signed remaining amount per income item.
  - A position takes at most max(0, net of all items in its scope that are still unconsumed), consumed oldest first.
  - Equal positions are ordered by date, then by id.
- References: `src/modules/settlements/recoup.ts`; `tests/review-round2.test.ts`
- Status: accepted

## D-073: Second review round: operations follow merged artists; document kinds and deferral checks
- Date: 2026-10-31
- Question: a position created on an artist that was later merged did not match the survivor's master income. Basis documents of any kind were accepted. Inactive or migrated payouts could be deferred.
- Decision:
  - The facade resolves the operation's artist through the merge before recoupment.
  - The basis of a recoupable payout must be `advance_basis` or `contract`. The basis of an adjustment must be `adjustment_basis`, `contract` or `other`.
  - Inactive and migrated payouts cannot be deferred.
  - An advance whose transfer failed is undone by deactivation, not by a return (returns stay for plain payouts, D-052).
  - The pro-rata `payoutByArtist` of D-053 is an approximation for second and later payouts: it is based on all-time net per artist, not on what earlier payouts covered. Accepted; the exact all-time figures are stored next to it.
- References: `src/facade/balance.ts`, `src/facade/index.ts`, `src/modules/settlements/service.ts`
- Status: accepted

## D-074: Second review round: owners and splits
- Date: 2026-10-31
- Question: issues found in owners and splits.
  - A refused split change still created the new artists, because there was no pre-check.
  - The contract document's owner was assigned outside the transaction.
  - A merged owner could still get aliases.
  - There was no test for the split being chosen by report date versus sales date, nor for money of an artist without owner.
- Decision:
  - `splits.checkChange` runs before the facade creates artists.
  - The document owner is assigned inside the contract transaction.
  - `addOwnerAlias` requires a live owner.
  - Tests were added for:
    - the report-date choice (a line sold in June and reported in July uses the July split);
    - C `artist_without_owner` becoming B;
    - confirm-once;
    - credits, soft rollback, owner merge with operations, period figures;
    - B17 completeness with unmapped writers and unconfirmed terms.
  - Left as documented:
    - splits keep the old artist id after an artist merge; money is correct through resolution, but `listSplits({artistId})` does not show it;
    - a split may be confirmed by any approved appendix: 02 does not require the appendix to belong to an owner of the split;
    - the merge across three databases has no shared transaction (D-070).
- References: `src/modules/splits/service.ts`, `src/modules/owners/service.ts`, `tests/review-round2.test.ts`
- Status: accepted

## D-075: Second review round: sources and publishing
- Date: 2026-10-31
- Question: issues found in sources and publishing.
  - A publishing author without documents could be merged into a label owner, and its money would then go to the label as B. This breaks 08, decision 7.
  - Two simultaneous uploads of the same B17 file could delete the kept original.
  - Months 00 and 13 were accepted.
  - `generatedAt` was weakly validated, and only after the original was copied.
  - The zip path had no test.
- Decision:
  - Owners of different kinds (person / label) cannot be merged. In addition, the calculation treats a writer resolved to a label owner as unmapped (C), never B.
  - B17 loads are serialized like Believe loads. The original is copied with exclusive create and deleted only by the load that created it. A UNIQUE race maps to `duplicate_period`.
  - Months in file names and lines go through `assertMonth`.
  - `generatedAt` must be ISO (date or date-time). It is normalized to ISO UTC and checked before anything is stored, for Believe and B17.
  - A zip test revealed a real bug: yauzl closed the archive after listing the entries (`autoClose`), so `.csv.zip` reports could not be read. Fixed: the archive is closed when the entry stream ends.
  - Tests were added for:
    - zip input and the zip-entry generation date;
    - every key dimension;
    - crash recovery;
    - publishing terms (unapproved, change without document, by date, 0% after buyout, label and owner checks, survivor terms after merge);
    - B17 period mismatch, non-USD currency, the `Payable` column and duplicate files;
    - concurrent B17 uploads.
  - Left as documented: the B17 statement is buffered in memory before the atomic insert (a few MB; D-040).
- References: `src/shared/files.ts`, `src/modules/publishing/service.ts`, `src/facade/calculation.ts`, `tests/review-round3.test.ts`
- Status: accepted

## D-077: Handover package
- Date: 2026-10-31
- Question: how to hand the project to another developer or agent.
- Decision:
  - `HANDOVER.md` (Russian, for people) and `CLAUDE.md` (English, for agents).
  - `.npmrc` with `ignore-scripts=true`: better-sqlite3 and esbuild ship prebuilt binaries, so node-gyp is never needed.
  - `engines.node >= 22.13`.
  - The zip contains `новая_архитектура/` without `node_modules`, build output and test temporary files. It includes the demo databases with their WAL checkpointed.
  - The session export is shipped separately: subagent transcripts may contain fragments of the reference data.
- References: `HANDOVER.md`, `CLAUDE.md`
- Status: accepted

## D-079: Real reports are now available: the real July 2026 Believe report is loaded (updates D-008)
- Date: 2026-10-31
- Question: `../../data/data/` was added. It contains:
  - `believe/raw`: 34 monthly zips of account 1052428;
  - `believe_echo8`: account 1055956;
  - `publishing/raw`: 7 B17 statements;
  - splits and other sources.
- Decision:
  - `scripts/import-legacy.ts <dir> --no-reconstruct` imports only the reference data (owners, artists, tracks, splits, buyouts, terms) into `data/real`.
  - The real July file `2152271_1052428_2026-07-01_2026-07-01.csv.zip` was uploaded through `POST /api/sources/believe`, streaming. Results:
    - 3,487,382 lines summed into 2,345,182 keys in about 90 s. This is exactly the key count in 06 decision 2.
    - Net 790,209.904499203132, equal to erp.db `revenue` to the last digit. Gross 932,755.109990663295.
    - sha256 `11c6c7203312…`, the file the old ERP used (`believe:2026-07#11c6c7203312`).
    - The generation date came from the zip entry: 2026-08-20.
    - 84 lines without ISRC.
  - A second upload of the same period was refused with 409 `duplicate_period`.
  - The demo package keeps the reconstructed files (D-008): the real database holds names from the reports and is not shipped.
  - The other months and the echo8 account were not loaded in this session. The loader accepts them (one scope per account and month). The echo8 July file is named `… 2.csv` (a copy suffix without brackets), which the name pattern rejects; rename it before upload.
- References: `scripts/import-legacy.ts`, D-008, D-031
- Status: accepted

  - **2026-Q1** (`0to8_Q12026_Publishing_Statement.xlsx`, sha256 `3c59ff7dff49…`): 420,436 lines in about 28 s, Total Payable 1,260,625.465113694607. This equals the old ERP accruals 1,260,625.47.

So all author money is C `owner_unconfirmed` (630,312.73 and 116,128.38), our share is the remainder, and payout for these owners is refused (`owner_not_confirmed`).

## D-084: Independent re-verification of the last changes (round 3): outcome
- Date: 2026-10-31
- Question: an independent reviewer, given only file paths, re-checked the changes D-072 to D-083. It found no high problem, two low/medium ones and several low ones.
- Decision (fixed, with tests in `tests/review-round3.test.ts`, 108 tests in total):
  - **Dates:** impossible calendar dates (2024-02-30) are rejected by `assertDate` and `normalizeTimestamp`. A date-time must carry a zone (Z or an offset), so the stored UTC generation date does not depend on the server timezone.
  - **Adjustments:** an `in` operation counts only with an approved basis document. A payment proof can no longer confirm a credit or a deduction, and the API refuses `paymentProofId` on `in` operations.
  - **Contracts:** duplicated or merged artists are refused. Empty owner aliases are refused.
  - **Label guard:** a test now covers it. A writer mapped to a label owner, even through a module-level mapping, stays C `writer_unmapped`, never B.
  - **Import script:** fixed a broken UPC regex in the Zero catalogue import (`/\D/g`).
  - Left as low, documented (see also D-085):
    - "period recouped" on the sales-date basis uses income netted per report month, so it is approximate (totals are exact; D-070);
    - a zip entry stream is not destroyed if the file insert fails before reading, so a descriptor stays until the process ends;
    - the `--no-reconstruct` import path and the `__MACOSX` filter have no automated test; the `--no-reconstruct` path was exercised in this session on real data.
- References: review report (session of 2026-10-31)
- Status: accepted

## D-085: Final independent review, one reviewer per module, on the final code: outcome
- Date: 2026-10-31
- Question: README §9 asks for an independent reviewer per module. After D-084, five reviewers checked owners, splits, sources, settlements and publishing on the final code. Each got only file paths.
- Decision:
  - **Verdicts:**
    - splits, sources and publishing: "no high or medium problems";
    - owners and settlements: no high problems, and the same medium one found independently by both: an advance (out with scope) was recorded without the payout button's checks.
  - **Fixed** (tests in `tests/review-round3.test.ts`, 111 tests in total):
    - An advance is a payout (07 rule 2). `createOperation` now requires a confirmed owner and no blocking payout for any non-migrated `out`.
    - "Blocking" means: no proof, a deferred payout, or a proof that is not approved. The same rule is used by the payout button, advances and the balance view.
    - A split that repeats an existing artist is refused before new artists are created.
    - An empty `documentId` cannot confirm a split.
    - A return cannot be dated before its payout.
    - A B17 statement with several detail sheets loads: line numbers are unique across sheets, and error messages show the sheet row. Before, the line numbers collided and the file was refused with a misleading 409. B17 sha256 and generation date are now tested.
  - **Left as low, with reasons:**
    - The zip DOS time without a UT field is read in the server timezone (generation-date provenance is recorded).
    - Text after a closing quote is accepted by the CSV parser.
    - The field cap does not bound the field count of a record without newlines.
    - Temp-file removal on client abort may hit EBUSY on Windows.
    - Empty UPC lines are not warned about separately (0 such lines in real data, doc 03).
    - Merged artist ids are not followed when creating an operation, track or contract link (display only, or refused with a clear error).
    - Ownerless basis documents can back several operations.
    - The owner page shows only the owner's own publishing terms.
    - Alias queries grow with the number of owners.
    - A buyout on a date with no split in force is refused even if later splits exist.
    - A return keeps counting if its payout's proof is later un-approved.
    - These are recorded here for the next iteration; none affects a README §5 rule.
- References: final review reports (session of 2026-10-31)
- Status: accepted

## D-086: Full history loaded into the local data/real; writer legal names used (user instruction)
- Date: 2026-10-31
- Question: the user asked to load all Believe and B17 reports and to use the authors' legal names (FIO) to link writers to owners.
- Decision:
  - **Where the data lives:** everything is loaded through the API into the local `data/real`, a git-ignored directory. FIO were never printed in the session output; aggregates only.
  - **Believe:** 47 files: account 1052428, 2023-11 to 2026-08 (34 months), and account 1055956 "echo8", 2025-08 to 2026-08 (13 months). About 75M lines.
    - Each file passed the control check. The July echo8 file named with a " 2" copy suffix was uploaded under its proper name.
    - Totals over 34 months: A 16,338,476.40 = B 5,807,594.30 + C 2,285,285.81 + our share 8,245,596.30. No month has an alarm.
  - **B17:** 6 statements covering 7 quarters (2024-Q4 to 2026-Q2). The "Updated" 2026-Q2 file was refused (D-041). Each statement total equals the old ERP's B17 accruals for its quarter (456.99; 43,324.58; 281,160.71; 405,296.99; 273,460.41; 1,260,625.47). 2026-Q2 equals the original file (232,256.76), see D-083.
  - **Writer names:** `scripts/import-legacy.ts --writer-aliases` and `scripts/add-writer-aliases.ts` attach the writer names of the old IMRO registry (`data/data/main_sheet/main.db`, table `pro_imro`, read-only) as owner aliases, by pseudonym. Result: 74 names on 62 owners.
  - **Merges by FIO:** writer owners created earlier "waiting for documents" were merged into confirmed owners when their name was the same as:
    - an IMRO alias of exactly one owner (14 merges);
    - the legal name in exactly one confirmed owner's contract in the old registry (`contract.legal`, 10 merges).
    - These merges are recorded with the reason "user instruction 2026-10-31" (02 «Объединение записей»: the unconfirmed record merges into the confirmed one).
  - **B17 after linking:** B for authors rose to 220,842.39 for 2026-Q1 (67,702.20 before the contract-name merges). 8 rights-holders known neither to IMRO nor to the contracts stay owners "waiting for documents": their author share is C, until the operator maps them (`POST /api/publishing/writers/:norm/map`) or documents arrive.
  - **Differences against the old ERP writer accruals** (Q1 2026: old 289,049.12; new B 220,842.39 + C 409,470.34):
    - the old engine sent bought-out, TEAM, override and unmatched writers' shares to the label or to suspense, and excluded external writers;
    - the new model gives every unknown author 50% as C until confirmed (08 decision 4).
  - **Personal data in the handover package:** this directory is not shipped. On the user's request, the handover package contains the FIO links in the demo data (D-090).
- References: `scripts/import-legacy.ts`, `scripts/legacy-writers.ts`, `scripts/add-writer-aliases.ts`, `scripts/restore-publishing-terms.ts`
- Status: accepted (mapping of the 8 remaining rights-holders: operator)

## D-087: A B17 line belongs to our writers listed in "songwriters" (refines D-039)
- Date: 2026-10-31
- Question: in real statements `rights-holder name` is the payee of the line. The authors are in `songwriters`, a comma-separated list of full names; each part is one writer (checked on 2026-Q2: 96% of the money has an IMRO-known writer in that list).
- Decision:
  - A line's `Total Payable` goes to **our** writers listed in `songwriters`, in equal parts. Our writers are those linked to one of our person-owners. Equal parts follow 08 decision 2 ("almost always equal") until the contract shares are entered.
  - The last part takes the remainder, so the parts add up to the line exactly.
  - External co-writers (not our owners) are not paid and are never created as owners.
  - A listed writer that is ambiguous (several candidate owners) keeps its part unmapped (C) until the operator decides; the part never goes to the other writers.
  - A line without any writer of ours goes to its rights-holder. An unknown rights-holder becomes an owner "waiting for documents" (08 decision 4).
  - A name seen first as a songwriter and later as a rights-holder is upgraded to holder.
  - **Matching:** a songwriter is linked to an owner by an exact alias, or by the same words in another order when that gives exactly one person-owner ("LAST FIRST" vs "First Last"). Automatic matching of songwriters by word order follows the user's explicit instruction to link by FIO (08 question 10 otherwise sends similar names to the operator). Rights-holders with only a similar name still go to the operator.
  - `POST /api/publishing/writers/resolve` re-runs the matching after aliases change.
- References: 08 decisions 2, 4, 7, question 10; `src/modules/publishing/service.ts`, `src/facade/index.ts`; tests "B17 songwriters" in `tests/review-round3.test.ts`
- Status: accepted (equal parts until contract shares exist: needs owner review)

## D-088: Unit aggregate in the sources module (performance)
- Date: 2026-10-31
- Question: on the full history (about 45M keys), `units()` grouped all lines on every request (490 s). A full reconciliation took 468 s.
- Decision:
  - The sources module keeps `source_units`: sums per ISRC + UPC + report month + sales month, per file.
    - It is built inside the load transaction and checked against the file net total before commit.
    - It is backfilled with the same check when the module opens.
    - It is deleted with the file.
  - This is a module-internal aggregate, allowed by 06 «Принцип расчёта» and 09 principle 18. The lines stay the source of truth, and the platform/country breakdown still reads the lines.
  - Publishing memoises owner resolution per request instead of looking it up per row and term (23 s).
  - **Result on the full history:** units in 2.2 s, a full reconciliation in about 10 s, an owner balance in about 6–7 s. The request still recalculates from scratch.
- References: `src/modules/sources/*`, `src/modules/publishing/service.ts`
- Status: accepted

## D-093: Two handover archives: code with development context, and data
- Date: 2026-10-31
- Question: the user asked for (1) a small archive that deploys the ERP and carries the full development context, so another developer can continue with their own Claude; (2) a separate archive with all data used, so the developer gets the same working ERP locally.
- Decision:
  - **Code archive** `royalty-erp_handover_<date>.zip`:
    - `новая_архитектура/` with the code, the demo data and all architecture documents;
    - `context/`: the session transcript, the subagent transcripts (research, module reviews, reconciliation) and the tool results.
    - It replaces the separate session-export archive.
  - **Data archive** `royalty-erp_data_<date>.zip`, unpacked into the same root:
    - `новая_архитектура/app/data/real/`: the five module databases (checkpointed) and the stored originals of every loaded report (`files/`, all Believe zips of both accounts and the B17 statements). A server started on this folder needs no reload.
    - `новая_архитектура/app/data/demo/files/`: the demo originals, left out of the code archive because of Windows path length.
    - `data/erp/data/erp.db` and `data/data/main_sheet/main.db`: the old ERP and the IMRO registry, read by `import-legacy`, `add-writer-aliases` and `reconcile-erp`.
  - Not included: bank and Wio statements, payroll, Tipalti, EasyStaff and the other `data/data` folders. Iteration 1 does not use them.

- References: `HANDOVER.md`, `CLAUDE.md`
- Status: accepted

## D-094: Recoupment: negative lines use up the income they cancel
- Date: 2026-10-31
- Question: the settlements review (final round) found that two positions with overlapping scopes could recoup more than the net income.
  - A position counted the negative lines in its scope to cap itself, but it consumed only positive lines.
  - The negatives stayed, so the next position took the same positive income again.
  - Example: income A +100, A −100 and owner publishing +100. An "any" advance of 100 and a publishing advance of 50 recouped 150 from a net income of 100.
- Decision: before a position recoups, the negative and positive lines in its scope are netted, and both are used up by min(negatives, positives). The position then takes the rest. The total recouped can no longer exceed the net income in the union of scopes.
- Test: `tests/recoup.test.ts`, "positions with overlapping scopes never recoup more than the net income in total".
- References: `src/modules/settlements/recoup.ts`, D-072
- Status: accepted

## D-095: Merged artists in splits and buyouts
- Date: 2026-10-31
- Question: the owners/splits review found two problems with merged artists (D-074).
  - A buyout matched the seller by the raw artist id. A split that still held an artist id merged into the seller was skipped, so the seller kept its share.
  - A split could hold a merged id and its surviving id at once.
- Decision:
  - The facade resolves share artist ids through the merge index before the "artist twice" check and stores the surviving id.
  - A buyout passes every id that stands for the seller (the surviving id plus merged ones) to the splits module, which zeroes all of them. The splits module itself still knows nothing about merges.
- Test: `tests/review-fixes.test.ts`, "a buyout zeroes the seller also in splits that still hold an artist merged into it".
- References: `src/facade/index.ts` (resolveShares, buyout), `src/modules/splits/service.ts` (buyout)
- Status: accepted

## D-096: Final module reviews and section 5 coverage check (second round)
- Date: 2026-10-31
- Question: on the final code, independent reviewers checked owners+splits, sources, settlements and publishing. A separate checker compared README section 5, rule by rule, against the tests. The agents received only file paths.
- Decision:
  - **Fixed:**
    - Settlements, medium: recoupment above net income (D-094).
    - Owners/splits, medium: buyout with merged artists (D-095).
    - Sources, medium: the zip archive was not released when a load failed mid-file, or on an archive error. The entry stream is destroyed in that case, which emits `close`, not `end`.
      - The archive is now closed exactly once on end, error or close.
      - The entry must be a `.csv`, limited to 16 GiB unpacked (`MAX_UNZIPPED_BYTES`). yauzl checks the declared size against the inflated bytes.
      - Test: a non-CSV entry is rejected (`tests/review-round3.test.ts`).
    - Publishing, medium: a test used a name that may belong to a real author (the alias example of 08). It is replaced by a synthetic name.
  - **Coverage:** the checker counted 26 rules: 18 fully covered, 7 partly covered, 1 not covered. New tests in `tests/rules-coverage.test.ts` cover:
    - release type as a key dimension, with gross and quantity summed for identical keys;
    - account as part of the period scope;
    - one split level (a share is always an artist);
    - a buyout payment is not part of the balance;
    - operations have no type field, and optional fields are stored;
    - an outgoing operation without a scope is a plain payout and is refused outside the button;
    - B17 uses `Total Payable`, not `net amount`;
    - the author gets the contract percent of Total Payable;
    - the period filter uses the report date by default.
    - The rule "no floats" stays covered by the money tests and the code rule. The B17 reader converts a number cell through its 15-digit text form (D-040).
  - **Needs owner review:** publishing creates an owner "waiting for documents" for any unknown rights-holder, company names included (D-087).
    - Proposed: keep this. Such an owner gets money only after an approved contract, and the manager does not approve one for a company payee.
    - Automatic detection of company names is left for iteration 2.
  - **Low findings, recorded but not fixed in iteration 1:**
    - Sources:
      - the `source_units` control check covers net only;
      - `breakdown(isrc)` does not split by account;
      - `pairAttrs` picks names by MAX;
      - the ISRC text is not format-checked;
      - amounts above the SQLite integer range give 500, not 400;
      - the BOM check looks only at the first decoded chunk;
      - the UNIQUE error mapping also covers the sha index;
      - the "to" date in the file name is not checked.
    - Owners/splits:
      - an empty documentId can be stored by a direct module call (the API refuses it);
      - (ISRC, null UPC) duplicates are allowed;
      - empty periods take part in the overlap check, which is harmless;
      - `getArtist` and `createTrack` accept a merged id (only affects display after D-095).
    - Settlements:
      - the owner merge uses the weaker blocking check, without proof approval;
      - `setDeferred` has no facade gate;
      - the per-artist shares in the snapshot are rounded without distributing the remainder;
      - a scope on a credit is dropped silently.
    - Publishing:
      - songwriters are split only on commas;
      - multi-quarter file names do not check row periods;
      - a labelled total row would be counted;
      - statement sums above about $9.2M overflow the 64-bit control check (the current maximum is about $1.26M);
      - the SQL lives in service.ts; there is no repository.ts;
      - a mapped writer can be remapped without a document;
      - the UNIQUE error mapping is broad.
  - **Checks:** 126 tests pass; tsc, eslint and vite build are clean.
- References: reviewer reports in the session transcript (`context/session/<id>/subagents`)
- Status: accepted. The company-payee item needs owner review.

## D-097: End-to-end check on the final code (July 2026)
- Date: 2026-10-31
- Question: the main flows had to be shown again on the final code with the July 2026 data.
- Decision: checked through the API and the interface on copies of the data. `data/real` was not changed.
  - **Streaming upload:** the real July 2026 Believe report (110 MB zip, 3,487,382 lines, 2,345,182 keys, net 790,209.904499203132) loads into an empty database. The server's peak memory is 278 MB.
  - **Duplicates:**
    - A second file for 2026-07 gives 409 `duplicate_period`. So does the same file again, because the period is checked before the hash.
    - The original B17 Q2 2026 statement loads (291,069 lines, payable 232,256.76). The "Updated" Q2 2026 statement after it gives 409 `duplicate_period`.
  - **Documents:** an appendix created as not_approved becomes approved with the Approve button on /documents.
  - **Adjustment with a scope** (demo copy, owner "Owner of ASTXR", July 2026 data):
    - A 300.00 adjustment (in, scope master) with an unapproved basis document is pending ("no approved document") and is not deducted.
    - After approval it is recouped: recouped 300, payable 4,112.21 → 3,812.21.
  - **Payout button:**
    - It pays 3,812.20, rounded down.
    - A second payout is refused (`payout_blocked`) until the payment proof is attached.
    - A payment proof needs no approval by default, so it counts once attached.
    - The snapshot carries `payoutByArtist`, the positions and the file hashes.
  - **Balance page with the 2026-07 filter:**
    - For the period: received 11,998.00 = not ours 4,112.19 + waiting 390.37 + other participants 1,492.78 + our share 6,002.66.
    - All time: recouped 300.00, paid 3,812.20, payable 0.01. The Payout button is disabled.
  - **Module independence:**
    - No module imports another. Only `src/facade/{balance,calculation,index}.ts` import modules.
    - The boundaries test passes, and each module has its own `.sqlite` file.
- References: D-033, D-051, D-053, D-092
- Status: accepted

## D-098: README section 5 coverage, second check: all rules covered
- Date: 2026-10-31
- Question: after D-096 the coverage checker was run again independently. It split section 5 into 43 rules and reported 41 covered, 2 partly covered, 0 missing.
  - Partly covered: the line key (account and reporting month were not shown to keep identical lines apart) and "recalculated for today" (no test with a fixed clock).
  - The "no floats" rule had only indirect evidence.
- Decision:
  - **No floats:** `tests/rules-coverage.test.ts`, "the source never parses or rounds money as a float".
    - It scans `src/` for `parseFloat`, `Math.round/floor/ceil/trunc` and `toPrecision`.
    - Every `Number(` call must be on an explicit list of non-money values: counts, row ids, settings, a quarter month.
    - A second test walks the JSON of the balance, the reconciliation and the payout snapshot. No fractional JS number is allowed; money is a decimal string or whole cents. Its exact product (0.1 + 0.2 + 1000.000000000001) × 33.333333% = 333.43332999900033333333 is checked to the last digit.
    - Arithmetic on JS numbers written another way (unary `+`, `*`) is not caught by the scan, only by the output walk. This is accepted.
  - **Account and reporting month in the key:** identical lines in two accounts and in two months stay three separate units and three lines.
  - **"Today":** the meaning is pinned. Nothing is stored or cached, and the all-time view is recalculated from the data present at the moment of the call.
    - The test fixes the clock at 2026-09-15.
    - A later report changes payable at once (50 → 60), while the July period view stays 100.
    - The payout is dated "today" and counts once its proof exists (payable → 0).
    - The only stored record is the operation.
  - The checker's count after these tests: all 43 rules covered.
  - Tests: 130 pass. tsc, eslint and vite build are clean.
- References: `tests/rules-coverage.test.ts`, D-096
- Status: accepted

## D-101: Third review round of owners+splits, settlements and sources; splits and owner card shown
- Date: 2026-10-31
- Question: owners+splits, settlements and sources changed after their earlier reviews (D-094, D-095, zip handling). Each was reviewed again, independently, on the current code. The splits page and the owner card (README section 8) had to be shown on the final code.
- Decision:
  - **Pages on `data/real`** (read only; owners/settlements sha256 unchanged afterwards):
    - `GET /api/splits?isrc=FRX282572355` and /splits: one split from 2000-01-01, open: DJ VGK1 25% + Hwungii 25%, our share 50%, document approved.
    - The full list (13,610 splits) shows buyouts as a new period with the seller at 0% (origin manual).
    - `GET /api/owners/:id` and /owners/:id for "Owner of Hwungii":
      - confirmed;
      - 1 artist (Hwungii) with its tracks: ISRC, UPC, title, split link;
      - 3 documents (contract, adjustment basis, payment proof, all approved);
      - 1 contract;
      - publishing terms 50% from 2000-01-01.
  - **Settlements, high: the D-094 netting was too broad.** A position that recoups nothing still netted the negative lines in its scope, which wiped positive income for other positions.
    - Example: a 150 master advance with net master income 0 cancelled X +100 against Y −100, so a 100 advance scoped to X recouped nothing.
    - Fixed: netting runs only when the position takes something. Test: "a position that takes nothing does not cancel income in the scope of another one".
    - Accepted, not a leak: a negative line outside an advance's scope reduces the artist's earnings, not the advance's recoupment. With X +100 (advance scope X) and Y −100, the advance recoups 100 and payable is −100. This is the negative-balance rule of README section 5 (shown as 0, covered by later income), consistent with D-072.
  - **Settlements, medium: a sent payout could be deactivated and paid twice.**
    - A payout with an `externalRef` (sent by the button) and no proof is now deactivated only with `transferFailed: true` (API body field, refusal code `payout_sent`).
    - A payout with a proof is never deactivated; the money comes back through a return (`payout_paid`).
    - Test updated in review-round2.
  - **Sources, medium-low:**
    - the report stream (and a zip archive) is now destroyed when the file row insert or the transaction fails before parsing;
    - the upload temp file is removed only after the write settles, and a cleanup error never hides the original error.
    - Test: a zipped load that fails mid-file leaves the zip removable at once.
  - **Low, recorded for iteration 2:**
    - Owners/splits:
      - a buyout is refused when the seller is only in a later split, not in the split in force;
      - nothing stops a later split from re-adding the seller after a buyout;
      - the owner of the buyout document is not checked;
      - the merge direction looks only at contracts;
      - extra queries in `artistIndex` and `calculate`.
    - Sources:
      - an orphan stored copy after a crash between copy and insert;
      - some zip errors return 500 instead of 400;
      - exponent amounts with more than 40 significant digits are rounded.
    - Settlements: missing tests for an unapproved proof blocking the button, and for a deduction recouping earlier income.
  - **Checks:** 133 tests pass; tsc, eslint and vite build are clean.
- References: D-072, D-094, D-095, D-096, D-034
- Status: accepted

## D-102: Believe lines move to ClickHouse (storage design)
- Date: 2026-10-31
- Question: `sources.sqlite` is 27 GB for 48M keys (75.7M report lines), growing about 40M keys a year. A GROUP BY over all lines took 490 s (D-088). Where do the Believe lines live and who sums equal keys?
- Decision (design; the loader and the sources module are not ported yet, SQLite still runs the app):
  - PostgreSQL later for the transactional modules and the file registry (account, sha256, status, control totals). ClickHouse for Believe lines only.
  - One table `believe.rows` on plain `MergeTree`: the report as it is, column names as in the report, `file_id` instead of `account`, no `line_no`, no key column, no `attr_sets`. `isrc` and `upc` are normalized (06 decision 6); `release_catalog_number` is kept though it is almost always empty.
