- Status: accepted

## D-130: Stage 3 council fixes: end dates to the month start, sign-safe rounding, 12 decimals in the publishing layers, participation overlap key with the source artist, balance per label of record, the M18 channel rule
- Date: 2026-11-03
- Question: the stage 3 council (`docs/agents/reviews/stage3_{architect,product,qa}.md`) left one blocker (M52 floored only start dates, so a mid-month end date double-paid or blocked the month) and should-fix items: a 1-pico wrong-sign allocation when percents sum to exactly 100, publishing amounts above 12 decimals, the participation overlap key without the source artist, untested signals, module notes, the decision index, the "paid per label" promise without storage, the M18 data rule, committed `.pyc` files.
- Decision:
  - **End dates (refines D-126, M52):** the facade floors every end date to its month start too: `createSplit.validTo`, `createParticipation.validTo`, `closeParticipation`'s date, `createLabelRule.validTo` and the rule close. `POST /labels/rules/:id/close` goes through the new facade method `closeLabelRule` (not the module). A period that becomes empty after flooring (`validTo ≤ validFrom`) is refused: `ValidationError` 400 with code `empty_period` (`ValidationError` takes an optional code). So a predecessor closed on the 15th and a successor from the 15th meet at the 1st: the part-month goes to the new holder and no exclusion constraint fires.
  - **Sign-safe rounding (refines D-127):** `layers/exact.ts` gains `capToBase(base, amounts, keys)`: when the quantised amounts exceed their base (the remainder would have the opposite sign of the base), the excess comes off the largest absolute amount (a tie: the first by key; then the next if needed). `split.ts` caps the rows against the label share, `off-the-top.ts` caps the tops against the net, so the label share and the scaled net are never of the opposite sign of the unit and no row is negative on a positive unit. `carve.ts`: the pool takes its total from the rows with `distributeCapped` (no row gives more than it has); the artist-share and label-share remainders were already sign-safe (`fund` scales to the bucket) and the sublabel takes at most 100 % of what remains.
  - **Publishing 12 decimals (refines D-128 part 2):** `writer-parts.ts` cuts equal parts at 12 decimals (ROUND_DOWN, so no part exceeds its equal share) and the last writer takes the remainder (D-087 kept); `author-share.ts` computes `authorAmount = q(part × percent / 100)` (`pct`); the label amount stays the subtraction in `publishing-categorise.ts`.
  - **Participation overlap key (refines D-126):** migration `0010_participations_overlap_key.sql` drops `participations_no_overlap` and re-adds it over `(isrc, artist_id, funded_from, coalesce(from_artist_id, ''), daterange)`; the service check compares the source artist as well. Two `artist_share` participations of one recipient on one ISRC from two different source artists coexist (G12 style); the same source twice is still `participation_overlap`.
  - **Balance per label of record (replaces the "paid per paying label" promise of 10 §5 and plan 4.4):** one balance per owner. `ownerBalance()` returns `byLabel: Record<labelName, { earned, waiting }>`: all-time master B and C of the owner's allocations per the unit's label of record (`ourLabelName`, `'?'` for an unmapped string); recouped and paid stay one total, because operations carry no label; publishing has no label of record. In `balanceSchema` and as one table on the owner balance page. Stage 5 compares Main per owner.
  - **M18 data rule:** Believe money is the digital channel; a split row's percent is the digital column of the contract; other channel rates are a document note.
  - **Notes and index:** `src/modules/owners/CLAUDE.md` (new), `publishing` and `sources` `CLAUDE.md` open with the three sentences of 10 §1; `facade/CLAUDE.md` says five modules and the residue rule; `03_decision_index.md` header D-001 … D-130, D-067 closed (by D-123/D-127, owner decision 2) and removed from the waiting list; D-125 got its missing `- Status: accepted` line. `10_target_model.md` §2 (end dates), §2 participations key, §5/§6/§7 (balance per label), `11_plan.md` 4.2 (the normalisation moves the predecessor's `valid_to` with the floored `valid_from`) and 4.4; the planned entries are renumbered D-131 … D-135.
  - **Hygiene:** `.gitignore` has `__pycache__/` and `*.pyc`; the three committed `scripts/mvp/__pycache__/*.pyc` are removed from the index.
- Changed expectations (behaviour changed on purpose; no golden expectation changed, G26a–e, `tests/facade.test.ts` and `tests/publishing-terms.test.ts` unchanged):
  - `tests/participations.test.ts` "refuses invalid combinations": `validTo = validFrom` was `/after valid_from/` (module), now `{ code: 'empty_period' }` (facade).
  - `tests/participations.test.ts` "closes a participation": close on `2026-03-01` was `/after valid_from/`, now `empty_period`; close on `2026-05-15` gave `validTo 2026-05-15`, now `2026-05-01`; the in-force checks moved from `2026-05-14` / `2026-05-15` to `2026-04-01` / `2026-05-01`.
  - `tests/participations.test.ts` G25: `part.validTo` was `2026-08-20` ("not floored"), now `2026-08-01`.
  - `tests/review-round3.test.ts` ambiguous songwriter: `writer_unmapped` was `33.33333333333333333334`, now `33.333333333334`; `B_notOurs` was `66.66666666666666666666`, now `66.666666666666`.
  - `tests/layers/publishing.test.ts` "three writers": parts were `33.33333333333333333333` ×2 and `33.33333333333333333334`, now `33.333333333333` ×2 and `33.333333333334`.
- Checked: new tests: `tests/participations.test.ts` (split `validTo` on the 15th ends at the 1st, its successor from the 15th starts at the 1st; P1 closed 2026-06-15 and P2 from 2026-06-15 give June to P2 only; empty period refused; two `artist_share` from two sources coexist, the same source refused; the constraint itself checked by hand on a scratch database), `tests/labels.test.ts` (rule end and close floored, successor rule from the same mid-month date, empty period refused), `tests/api.test.ts` (rule close through the facade 200 with `2026-04-01`, `empty_period` 400 for the close and for a rule), `tests/layers/exactness.test.ts` (10,000 units with row and top percents summing to exactly 100, also as 6-decimal thirds, nets with 12 decimals ending in 1 pico, every carve kind; after every layer: the identity, ≤ 12 decimals, the label share, the scaled net and every allocation never of the opposite sign of the unit, a zero unit only zeros; the same sign check added to the existing random test; the five minimal reproductions of the QA report pinned, rows 50/50 on 10.000000000001 give A 5, B 5.000000000001, label share 0, NoGhost NG 0; the new test fails on the old layers), `tests/layers/publishing.test.ts` (every publishing amount ≤ 12 decimals, author + label = part), `tests/golden.test.ts` (signals: `tracksWithNote` with the note and no amount changed, `participantsWithoutOwner` with amount 5; G08b-style balance per label: Echo8 B 50, 0to8 C 30, `'?'` B 20). Typecheck, lint, `REQUIRE_STACK=1 npm test` (32 files, 354 tests), `npm run build:client` clean.
- Known limits: `byLabel` is all-time and master only (no period view, no publishing); a merged id in an old participation is compared as stored by the overlap check (D-126 limit unchanged); the local dev database gets migration 0010 at the next `npm run migrate:pg`.
- References: `packages/backend/src/facade/{splits,balance}.ts`, `src/facade/layers/{exact,split,off-the-top,carve,writer-parts,author-share}.ts`, `src/api/routes/labels.ts`, `src/shared/errors.ts`, `src/modules/splits/service.ts`, `migrations/postgres/0010_participations_overlap_key.sql`, `src/modules/{owners,publishing,sources}/CLAUDE.md`, `src/facade/CLAUDE.md`, `packages/shared/src/api-responses.ts`, `packages/frontend/src/owners/OwnerBalancePage.tsx`, `tests/{participations,labels,api,golden,review-round3}.test.ts`, `tests/layers/{exactness,publishing}.test.ts`, `docs/agents/{03_decision_index,10_target_model,11_plan}.md`, `.gitignore`
- Status: accepted

## D-131: Reference data of the MVP loaded: labels dictionary, registered splits of Annex 14, buy-outs, participations, exclusions, Echo8 tracks and splits; legacy mid-month dates normalised
- Date: 2026-11-03
- Question: stage 4 steps 4.1 to 4.4 of `docs/agents/11_plan.md`: put the reference data of the MVP model (D-123 to D-130) into the dev database `erp`, so that the layers have labels, splits, participations and documents to work on.
- Decision:
  - **How:** `packages/backend/scripts/mvp/load.ts <step> [--apply]` (dry run by default), steps in this order: `labels`, `tracks`, `normalise-dates`, `splits`, `participations`, `exclusions`, `echo8`. Everything goes through the facade (`createSplit`, `changeSplit`, `createParticipation`, `createLabelRule`; the end-date flooring and the merged-id resolution live there); the labels step was changed from `labels.setRule` to `createLabelRule`. Only `normalise-dates` writes SQL directly (`UPDATE splits.splits`). Every step is idempotent by a marker in the `note` (`mvp:<step>:<key>`) or by title / alias; after each `--apply` the same step in dry run reported nothing left to do. Documents of kinds `contract`, `appendix`, `other` are created with `migration: true` and approved; kind `assumption` is attached and never approved. The loader keeps the artist index in memory for the run (a speed fix: the facade read ~25k rows several times per write; 1,300 splits took 24 s instead of hours); the cache is dropped when the loader creates an artist or an owner.
  - **Counts per step:** labels: 17 labels (0to8, Echo8 and 15 sublabels), 25 aliases, 16 sublabel rules, 15 sublabel owners with one pseudonym each, 15 documents (13 `assumption`, 1 contract, 1 other). tracks: 8,986 created (8,788 Echo8 pairs, 198 Annex ISRC without a track). normalise-dates: 1,043 `valid_from` and 1,010 `valid_to` floored to the month start (the predecessor's end moved with it; no overlap, no conflict). splits (Annex 14): 211 created, 80 changed with the appendix document "Annex 14 Splits by ISRC", 284 different-period records left to the normalisation, 9 handled by the participation rewrites. participations: 9 split rewrites (Sleeping City Funk, Trilalero and MALAME re-entered scaled, each row keeps its document), 3 unchanged, 108 participations created (the Era `artist_share` pair both loaded after the loader's overlap check was given the source artist, as the module's key since D-130), 9 documents. exclusions: 247 Duetti split changes at 100 % from the sale month (P03/P04 instruments), 11 track notes for derivatives outside the authorisation, 12 withdrawn ISRC listed only. echo8: 9,772 Echo8 splits created (387 with an RSA document, the others without a document and so C `split_unconfirmed`), 969 artists created without owner, 7 ISRC skipped because a split already existed. Total in the database afterwards: 26,261 tracks, 24,051 splits, 108 participations; 1,024 artists and 34 owners with origin `mvp:*`.
  - **Assumptions:** an ambiguous pseudonym (several artists with the same name) is resolved to the one candidate whose owner has an approved contract and logged "resolved by contract (assumption)"; none of DJ VETA3, DSHZS, MC FLYXO, !Nxght has such an owner, so every row that needs them is skipped and listed. One balance per owner across both labels (D-130). The 17 pp buy-out ISRC whose ERP period already starts in the effective month keep the ERP rows (instrument-based): an expected stage 5 difference. M18: a split row is the digital column of the contract.
  - **Open items:** XENO XID (Schedule A with the ISRC list and rate is missing; the USD 12,500 advance is in Main only): not loaded. 22 Annex records print no artist rows: skipped. M51: 196 ISRC buy-outs "see the instrument" (seller percent per row unknown): unchanged. 17 pp buy-out ISRC (above). 415 Echo8 ISRC (424 rows) name DJ VETA3 (322), DSHZS (78), !Nxght (20) or MC FLYXO (4): no split, owner or merge decision needed. 126 Echo8 names are an artist plus a sublabel word: kept as printed.
- Checked: dry run after each apply shows only `already_done`; `GET /reconciliation` (see D-132) has discrepancy 0 on all 51 rows; `compare-annex.ts` headline: 107,006 cells, 90,967 matched (receipts 89.38 %), Σ ERP share 7,695,468.68 against Σ Annex NLS 7,241,559.44, differences grouped by cause (split_differs 3,664 cells, annex_no_share 10,547, other 918, sublabel_assumption 278, no_split_erp 442, floor_titles 101) in `data/mvp-load/verify/annex.md` (analysis is stage 5). Typecheck, lint, `REQUIRE_STACK=1 npm test` (32 files, 354 tests).
- Known limits: the logs are in the git-ignored `data/mvp-load/log/` (the log of the first, interrupted echo8 run was not written; the counts above come from the database); the `all` step was not used.
- References: `packages/backend/scripts/mvp/{load,lib,steps-annex,steps-main}.ts`, `data/mvp-load/annex/`, `data/mvp-load/echo8/`
- Status: accepted

## D-132: Payment history and advances loaded from 0to8 Main.xlsx; publishing terms by type
- Date: 2026-11-03
- Question: stage 4 steps 4.5 and 4.6: put the cash history of `0to8 Main.xlsx` into the operations, and the publishing terms of the administration agreement.
- Decision:
  - **Main (`load.ts main`):** each Main nickname resolved to its pseudonym and owner (unmatched nicknames with cash got a person owner with one pseudonym, origin `mvp:main`: 18); through `createOperation` with `migration: true`, marker `mvp:main:<row>` in the comment. Loaded: 2,371 plain payouts (6,700,141.18), 158 recoupable advances (583,833.60; scope `any` 145, `publishing` 14 per registry v7; each with an `advance_basis` and a `payment_proof` document), 26 review-class rows as payouts with the prefix `mvp:main:review` (198,350.72: cash left to the nickname is a fact), 4 deductions (27,244.89) and 100 carve rows as deductions (185,405.22, basis `adjustment_basis`, no participation covers the pair) = 104 operations in. 30 rows are documents only (282,000.00: buy-out prices and one-off fees). The "переплата" rows are loaded as stated (383.67 payout, 9,500 advance); rows routed through another account (route "fl" 7, "echo8 account" 2, "customer" 1) are marked in the comment; the "ноу рекап" Expense rows (4 payouts, 54,205.06; 17 rows in all with the 13 Income ones) are loaded as they are with the tag `[no recap]`, a known stage 5 difference.
  - **Cash reconciliation:** the Expense total is 8,045,246.75 (the sheet adds to 8,045,246.81, per-row cents drift) = payouts 6,700,141.18 + advances 583,833.60 + review 198,350.72 + deductions and carves 212,650.11 + documents only 282,000.00 + Advertising not loaded 2,699.39 (M21 later) + ambiguous nicknames 28,402.51 (25 rows: DJ VETA3, DSHZS, MC FLYXO) + held rows 25,764.11 (3 payouts to MC 7belo, MC JHonny, the third held payout (owner 7129f35a) that need the participant credit, which no participation provides) + carve rows covered by a participation 11,405.19 (3 rows, not loaded because the carve is the participation). Income rows are not loaded (stage 5 compares them).
  - **Terms (`load.ts terms`):** the owner of the Exclusive Music Publishing Administration Agreement (found by the party of the file in `contract_index`, matched in `owners` read-only, one match) gets two terms from 2026-01-01 (effective date 2025-12-23, floored): 90 % for every operation type and 50 % for `Synchronization`, document "Exclusive Music Publishing Administration Agreement (PandaDoc)" (kind contract, migration, approved). Co-publishing owners at 50 % exist; bought-out writers at 0 % stay as D-045. Not written anywhere: the legal name.
- Checked: dry runs after apply report `already_done`; `GET /reconciliation` for all months: 51 rows (believe 1052428: 34, believe 1055956: 13, b17: 4), discrepancy 0 and fileDiscrepancy 0 everywhere. 2024-01..2026-08 totals: account 1052428: A 15,280,793.42 = B(not ours) 5,769,200.74 + C 1,831,010.61 + ours 7,680,582.07 (0to8 7,548,733.79, Echo8 131,848.28); C by reason: split_unconfirmed 1,600,104.02, unknown_pair 150,057.35, owner_unconfirmed 68,802.71, assumption 5,412.65, artist_without_owner 4,360.20, no_isrc 2,273.68. Account 1055956: A 1,050,945.10 = B 52,882.71 + C 330,835.43 + ours 667,226.96; C: split_unconfirmed 320,636.21, assumption 5,922.83, owner_unconfirmed 3,250.95, artist_without_owner 936.43, no_isrc 89.00. B17: A 2,171,639.63 = C 1,365,867.46 (all `owner_unconfirmed`) + ours 805,772.16. `compare-main.ts` headline: 992 owners, 845 differ by more than 1.00; Σ Main income 8,499,624.88; Σ ERP earned B 5,804,985.09 + C 1,799,436.31; Σ ERP payable -1,514,394.18 against Σ Main balance 584,418.47; 21 payout nicknames without an owner (`data/mvp-load/verify/main.md`; analysis is stage 5). Timing: `GET /owners/:id/balance` for the owner with the most operations (51) takes 6.6 s (`calculate` 6.2 s: ClickHouse units 1.2 s for 721,975 units, reference data 1.2 s, layers 3.7 s). Typecheck, lint, `REQUIRE_STACK=1 npm test` (354 tests) pass.
- Known limits: negative payable for many owners is expected before stage 5 (Main income rows are not loaded as earnings, the ERP earns from the reports; the payments of owners whose earnings are in C show as negative); B17 money is all in C `owner_unconfirmed` (writers' owners have no approved contract) and a balance call costs 6.6 s with the real data (no caches by design).
- References: `packages/backend/scripts/mvp/steps-main.ts`, `data/mvp-load/main/`, `data/mvp-load/terms.json`
- Status: accepted

## D-134: The B17 statements 2024-Q4/2025-Q1 and 2025-Q2 loaded
- Date: 2026-11-03
- Question: two B17 publishing statements lay on disk but were never loaded into the dev database `erp`; with 2025-Q3 to 2026-Q2 already loaded, the history of the publishing money had a gap before 2025-Q3.
- Decision:
  - **Files and quarters:** `context_data/ERP_data/publishing/raw/0to8_Q42024_Q12025_Publishing_Breakdown.xlsx` and `0to8_Q22025_Statement.xlsx`, loaded from their original place (nothing renamed or copied). `quartersFromFileName` reads both names correctly: the first gives 2024-Q4 and 2025-Q1 (one file, two quarters), the second 2025-Q2.
  - **How:** `packages/backend/scripts/mvp/load-b17.ts <path>` calls `facade.ingestB17` (the call of `POST /publishing/b17`, which also runs the writer resolution), one file at a time; `--reconcile` prints the b17 rows of the reconciliation read-only. Only counts are printed.
  - **Loaded:** 2025-Q2: 68,703 lines, total payable 281,160.713637746502, no warnings; writers: matched 0, created 0, ambiguous 0, external 14. 2024-Q4 and 2025-Q1: 39,654 lines, total payable 43,781.568599995018 (2024-12 456.99 plus 2025-03 43,324.58), no warnings; writers: matched 0, created 0, ambiguous 0, external 15. No new owner was created; the writers are external co-writers (not our person-owners), so, as for the earlier quarters (D-132), the publishing money of the new quarters is all in C `owner_unconfirmed` for the holders.
  - **Refused, unchanged:** the "Updated" Q2 2026 file is not loaded: a second statement for a loaded quarter stays refused (D-041 ⚠, waits for the product owner).
- Checked: the reconciliation now has 7 b17 rows (2024-12, 2025-03, 2025-06, 2025-09, 2025-12, 2026-03, 2026-06), discrepancy 0 on every row; A / C / ours of the new quarters: 2024-12 456.99 / 228.50 / 228.50, 2025-03 43,324.58 / 21,662.29 / 21,662.29, 2025-06 281,160.71 / 140,580.36 / 140,580.36 (C all `owner_unconfirmed`). The earlier four rows are unchanged. Typecheck, lint, `REQUIRE_STACK=1 npm test` pass.
- Known limits: the first attempt to load the two-quarter file stopped with an unexplained `validation_error` (message not captured) and left no statement; the immediate second attempt loaded it normally and the quarters are present once (A of the two months equals the file total). A balance or reconciliation call is slower with the larger data.
- References: `packages/backend/scripts/mvp/load-b17.ts`, `packages/backend/src/modules/publishing/b17-format.ts`, `packages/backend/src/facade/sources.ts`
- Status: accepted

## D-133: Contract registry loaded from the PandaDoc index; legacy splits equal to the owner's Annex registry confirmed by it
- Date: 2026-11-03
- Question: after stage 4 only 341 of ~1,130 owners had an approved contract document, 1,600,104.02 of 2024-01..2026-08 net sat in C `split_unconfirmed` (legacy splits without a document), 68,802.71 in `owner_unconfirmed` (master) and all B17 money (1,365,867.46) in `owner_unconfirmed`. The signed agreements exist in the PandaDoc index (`context_data/contract_index/documents.json`, 1,146 records).
- Decision (orchestrator, step 4.7, `load.ts contracts`, dry run by default, `--apply`):
  - **(a) Contract registry:** index kinds recording, co_pub, co_ownership, remix and `other` with a participation / exclusive / administration / short form / publishing file name become a `contract` document (`migration: true`, fileRef `pandadoc:<sha256>`, approved) plus an owners `contracts` row with `contract_artists`; kinds schedule and advance become approved `appendix` documents of the owner (no contracts row). Not loaded: imro, nda, w8, indemnification, consulting, work_for_hire, sample_license, asset_purchase, assignment, caa, beat_lease, beat_purchase, sublabel (already handled by the labels step) and the other `other` files. Matching: `party.pseudonyms` and `filename_pseudonym` (and the parts split at `/`; role words such as Composer, Owner are ignored) against `owners.artists` by the normalised pseudonym, exact; one document per owner of the matched artists (one contract per owner, all that owner's matched artists linked); no match is logged, an ambiguous name (several artists) is skipped and logged, an artist without owner is skipped and logged. Titles never use file names (they carry legal names): `<kind label> <number> <pseudonym> [<first 8 of sha256>]`, so (kind, title, owner) is stable and the load idempotent.
  - **(b) Annex confirmation:** the shared appendix "Annex 14 Splits by ISRC 2026-11-02" (created by `splits`) is approved once; every split overlapping the Annex period of a record of class `same/rows` whose `document_id` is null and whose rows equal the Annex rows (artist and percent) gets it through `facade.confirmSplit`. `same/label_share_only`, `same/annex_share_not_established`, different rows and unresolved rows stay without a document (C).
  - **(c) Co-publishing and administration:** the 26 co-pub agreements match through (a) (440 contract rows: recording 411, co_pub 26, other 2, remix 1). The B17 money does not move: the B17 payees are owners created from writers' legal names (31 owners hold the C money, none has a pseudonym or a contract), while the index is matched by stage name.
- Counts: contract documents 440 (contracts rows 440), appendix documents 61 (60 schedules, 1 advance addendum), owners with a new contract row 350, confirmed owners 341 -> 408 (most matched owners were confirmed already), splits confirmed 3,504 (4,246 already had a document; 11 differ from the Annex; 64 with unresolved rows). Not loaded from the index: 123 without a match, 18 ambiguous, 21 with an artist without owner (documents by kind: recording 103, schedule 34, advance 7, co_ownership 6, other 6, remix 5, co_pub 1).
- Checked: dry run after apply shows only `already_done`; `GET /reconciliation` discrepancy 0. 2024-01..2026-08, account 1052428: C 1,831,010.61 -> 889,957.87 (split_unconfirmed 1,600,104.02 -> 133,870.04, owner_unconfirmed 68,802.71 -> 593,983.94, unknown_pair 150,057.35, assumption 5,412.65, artist_without_owner 4,360.20, no_isrc 2,273.68 unchanged); account 1055956: C 330,835.43 -> 330,354.64 (split_unconfirmed 320,636.21 unchanged, owner_unconfirmed 3,250.95 -> 2,770.16); B17 C 1,365,867.46 unchanged. `compare-main.ts`: Σ ERP payable -1,514,394.18 -> -633,959.30; owners within 1.00 of Main 147 -> 193 of 992. Typecheck, lint, `REQUIRE_STACK=1 npm test` (354 tests) pass.
- Known limits: the money released from split_unconfirmed now waits for the owner (`owner_unconfirmed` grew by about 525,000): owners whose agreements are not in the index under their stage name still have no contract. B17 payees need a match by the legal name of the party (not in this decision). Asset purchase, assignment and CAA documents are not owner contracts and are not loaded. The 320,636.21 of account 1055956 has no split document in the Annex perimeter.
- References: `packages/backend/scripts/mvp/steps-main.ts` (`stepContracts`), `context_data/contract_index/documents.json`, `data/mvp-load/annex/splits_diff.json`
- Status: accepted

## D-136: Signals for a sublabel without a rule in force and for a rule recipient that is also a split row; catalogue cases M53–M58 and the out-of-scope list completed
- Date: 2026-11-03
- Question: the stage 7 findings (zero-erp `imprint_without_deal`, the double-pay shape of a sublabel rule whose recipient also has a split row, two pseudonyms of one owner on one ISRC) had no signal, and six cases and several consciously omitted items were missing from the catalogue.
- Decision:
  - **Signals (refines D-127, no amount changes):** `signals()` gains `sublabelWithoutRule` (units whose label string maps to a sublabel with no rule in force at the report month, aggregated `{ label, month, net }`), `ruleRecipientIsRow` (a unit with a sublabel allocation to pseudonym R and also a split row to R: `{ isrc, artistId, pseudonym, month }`) and `twoRowsOneOwner` (two split rows of one ISRC-month on pseudonyms of the same owner: `{ isrc, ownerId, month, pseudonyms }`, information only). They follow the period filter and date basis of the other lists and are read from the unit results, not from storage.
  - **State:** `UnitState` and `UnitResult` carry `sublabelId` / `sublabelName` (set by the label-of-record layer) and `UnitResult.ruleId` (the rule in force); no layer reads them, so no amount changes.
  - **Surface:** `signalsSchema` has the three lists; the sources page shows them under the other signal lists.
  - **Catalogue:** `09` §3 gains M53 (non-economic documents: out), M54 (known ISRC under a second UPC: data rule, D-135), M55 (Echo8 artist agreements: data gap), M56 (SoundOn statements, fixed shares: later), M57 (accrual-basis royalty: later), M58 (sublabel per-artist exceptions: open); §4 gains the +2 months payout lag row. `10` §6 lists the signals; `10` §9 adds SoundOn / fixed shares, per-ISRC imprint exclusions, legal interest (owner answer 7), release dates (owner answer 1), the 779 coverage list, approvals and assets (owner answer 8) as consciously out.
- Checked: `tests/golden.test.ts` G21b (sublabel rule starting 2026-06: March shows 140 without a rule, June shows the recipient also a row on ISRC 1, the period filter removes March) and G21c (two pseudonyms of one owner); on the dev database: `sublabelWithoutRule` 10 items, Σ net 230.84 (label "lost"), `ruleRecipientIsRow` 0, `twoRowsOneOwner` 785 ISRC-months on 45 ISRCs.
- Known limits: `ruleRecipientIsRow` compares the recipient pseudonym (merged ids resolved), not other pseudonyms of the same owner; `twoRowsOneOwner` is information, not a defect (one owner may hold several pseudonyms legitimately).
- References: `packages/backend/src/facade/reports.ts`, `layers/label-of-record.ts`, `docs/agents/09_settlement_cases.md`, `docs/agents/10_target_model.md`
- Status: accepted

## D-135: Stage 6 data fixes: Believe pairs of known ISRC registered, `0to8/lost` an alias of 0to8, the LUNAR MEDIA licence as an assumption, B17 holders confirmed by their own contracts, Main carve pairs as participations
- Date: 2026-11-03
- Question: the stage 7 reading of the loaded data found five data gaps: 44 (ISRC, UPC) pairs of known ISRC in C `unknown_pair` (E9 against D-023), the string `0to8/lost` treated as a sublabel (M7: Lost Label is a representative), the LUNAR MEDIA 10 % vocal licence not loaded, the 32 B17 holders without a contract (all B17 money in `owner_unconfirmed`), and the Main carve rows loaded as deductions although they are participations (M43) with the three participant payouts held.
- Decision (`load.ts <step> [--apply]`, five new steps appended to `all`, all through the facade or the module services, idempotent by markers / titles, dry run then apply then dry run proving `already_done`):
  - **pairs:** every (ISRC, UPC) pair of the loaded Believe files whose ISRC is a track but the pair is not gets `createTrack` with the printed title (the rule of a split by ISRC covers every UPC of it): 44 pairs, net 150,364.38.
  - **label-fixes:** the labels service cannot re-point an alias, so the rule of sublabel `lost` (50 %, open from 2025-10-01) is closed at 2025-11-01 (`closeLabelRule`): October 2025 stays a sublabel assumption, later months of `0to8/lost` have no sublabel rule and the whole label share stays with 0to8 (the same money result as an alias of 0to8).
  - **lunar:** owner `LUNAR MEDIA` (kind label, one pseudonym); participation `(net, artist_pool)` 10 % on the 34 ISRC of the Annex schedule whose title is the "Tem que tirar o chapeu" family, from the first report month of each to 2026-05-01 (Duetti sale), document kind `assumption` "Assumption Q1: LUNAR MEDIA 10 % vocal licence (cash base read as net; pool funding per owner decision 4)". 20 more tracks of the same title family that are not in the Annex schedule (Echo8 releases, fan-made versions) are listed in the log and left out. Of the 34, 34 had a report month.
  - **b17-holders:** 32 holders (owners with origin `publishing`) matched in memory against `party.legal_name` of co-publishing, administration and recording agreements (exact normalised compare of the owner name and its aliases; the administration agreement prints all parties in one string, so there the whole owner name must occur in it). Matched 13 (12 by a recording agreement, 1 by the administration agreement), confirmed 13 (22 contract documents with contracts rows, migration, approved, titles without names), unmatched 19; no co-publishing agreement matched an owner; terms set 0 (the administration holder already had its two terms; recording agreements set none, the default 50 stays `publishing_terms_unconfirmed`). 2 unmatched holders would match with the words of the name in another order (not applied: exact compare). No merge (D-083 stays open).
  - **carves:** of the 50 carve groups of `carve_pairs.json`, 24 state no percent or no UPC and stay deductions; the others become participations `(bucket, artist_share from the source artist)` on every ISRC of the UPC, one per participant and source artist, with the percent of the comment ("16.66 % (8.33 %/8.33 %)" and "12.5 % (6.25 % each)" read as 8.33 and 6.25 per participant; a list of percents is matched to the participants in order), from the first month of the rows, open-ended, document kind `assumption` "Assumption M43: participation of <pseudonym> on UPC <upc> from 0to8 Main.xlsx carve rows" per participant and UPC (29). 336 participations, 2 participants created (owner kind person, origin `mvp:carve`), 55 carve deduction operations deactivated with the reason `replaced by participation <id>`, Σ moved 101,552.83. Two rows of the ambiguous nickname DJ VETA3 are not resolved and stay. The three held payouts (MC 7belo 19,660.71, MC JHonny 4,010.83, the third held payout (owner 7129f35a) 2,092.57 = 25,764.11) are loaded as migration payouts with the marker of the main step.
- Checked: dry run of every step after the apply shows only `already_done`; `GET /reconciliation` discrepancy 0 on all 54 rows. 2024-01..2026-08, account 1052428: C 889,957.87 -> 748,978.81 (unknown_pair 150,057.35 -> 0, assumption 5,412.65 -> 15,885.63, owner_unconfirmed 593,983.94 -> 592,602.73, split_unconfirmed 133,870.04 -> 133,856.56), ours 7,680,582.07 -> 7,788,578.04, B 6,710,253.48 -> 6,743,236.57; account 1055956 unchanged; B17 (all months): B 0 -> 682,348.36, C 1,528,338.61 -> 845,990.25 (owner_unconfirmed 520,584.25, publishing_terms_unconfirmed 325,405.99). Typecheck, lint, `REQUIRE_STACK=1 npm test` (32 files, 356 tests) pass.
- Known limits: the three held payouts are loaded although only the carve groups with a stated percent create the participant credit (MC 7belo and the third held payout (owner 7129f35a) have none, so their payable is negative until the percents are known); the 24 groups without percent (Σ 83,852.39 of the rows of those groups; part of it was never loaded as an operation) need the owner; the sublabel rule of `lost` is closed, not re-pointed; B17 holders unmatched by name stay in C `owner_unconfirmed`.
- References: `packages/backend/scripts/mvp/{load,lib,steps-main}.ts` (`stepPairs`, `stepLabelFixes`, `stepLunar`, `stepB17Holders`, `stepCarves`), `data/mvp-load/main/`, `data/mvp-load/annex/`
- Status: accepted

## D-137: Stage 6 rework: settlement share before its start to the paid artist, Main carve history as pool participations of net from the first month, Q1/Q5 assumption rows attached, empty-period splits removed; golden cases G45 and G46
- Date: 2026-11-03
- Question: the stage 5 verification (`docs/agents/14_verification.md` §6) found four data or loader errors: E1 the settlement participant earned 137,982.02 B before 2026-02 through its legacy split row (owner decision 6: no money before the settlement; Main paid that 15 % to the artist LDRR); E2 the D-135 carve participations took the percent of the source row and started in 2026-07, so 4,967.80 replaced 101,552.83 of Main carve history; E3 the Q1 and Q5 rows (D-123) carried no `assumption` document (19,169.49 + 270.31 in B); E4 38 splits with an empty period.
- Decision (`load.ts <step> [--apply]`, through the facade, dry run, apply, idempotent dry run; logs `data/mvp-load/log/`; steps appended to `all`):
  - **Golden cases** G45 (a split change before the participation start: legacy rows A 35, S 15 rewritten to A 50 from the first report month, the window from 2026-02 unchanged) and G46 (Main carve "16.66 % (8.33 %/8.33 %)": A 16.67, B 16.67, M1 8.33, M2 8.33, asserted for the per-source entry of 12 and for pool participations of net, the D-137 loader form, in two months) appended to `docs/agents/12_golden_cases.md` with the hand answers of 14 §6, in `tests/golden.test.ts`. Data-shaped: no layer change.
  - **settlement-prestart (E1):** on the 30 settlement ISRC (`annex/participations.json`, group `settlement_15`), every split before 2026-02-01 that holds the participant's row is changed (`changeSplit`) from max(its `valid_from`, the ISRC's first report month): the participant's row removed, its percent added to the LDRR row (Main's payee; 35 → 50, 17.5 → 32.5, 14 → 29), the LDRR row and the split under the approved migration document of kind `other` "Owner decision 6 of 2026-11-02: settlement share before 2026-02-27", other rows keep their documents; the participation from 2026-02-01 is unchanged. 38 splits changed, marker `mvp:settlement-prestart:<isrc>:<from>`. Participant B before 2026-02 137,982.02 → 0; payable 139,847.50 → 1,865.47; LDRR payable −326,645.18 → −188,663.15.
  - **carves (E2), reworked:** a comment's percent is a percent of net per participant (8.33 each for "16.66 % (8.33 %/8.33 %)", 6.25 each for "12.5 % (6.25 % each)"), entered as one participation `(net, artist_pool)` per participant and ISRC of the UPC, from the first report month of the ISRC, open-ended, under the same assumption documents (29), marker `mvp:carve2:<participant>:<isrc>`. The 336 D-135 participations (origin `mvp:carve`, note `mvp:carve:<participant>:<upc>:<source>:<isrc>`, funded from `artist_share`) are deleted by id through the splits module in the same transaction as the creation: loader-owned rows found by their marker, a migration-only delete (`splits.deleteParticipations`, repository SQL), stated in the log. 141 created; MC Denny's 13 % on the three Sleeping City Funk ISRC is the existing pool participation of the Annex step (D-131) and is kept. The 55 deactivated deductions (101,552.83) stay deactivated, the three held payouts (25,764.11) stay. Carved 4,967.80 → 107,914.94 (C assumption); against Main per group up to the Main month: 103,263.51 vs 101,642.19 (group 18 excluded, paid in B by the D-131 participation), 20 of 26 groups within 1.00; outliers group 7 (+570.07: Main carves one ISRC of the UPC), 17 (−1,053.31: pool short, 469 `bucket_short` signals 1,065.94), 41 (+2,114.54: "12.5 % shared" reading), 18 (−11,405.19 as said).
  - **assumption-rows (E3):** same percents, the row's own document of kind `assumption` (`changeSplit` with per-row `documentId`; the splits service has no row-document update), each split from max(its `valid_from`, the ISRC's first report month) so a legacy record keeps the months without reports: the MC ROBA CENA 20 % licensor row on FRX762590589…592 (8 splits) "Assumption Q1: licence base less other participants; envelope not closed"; the Lost Label rows on the 60 ISRC whose split also has DJ DYLANFK or DJ Raulipues rows (60 splits) "Assumption Q5: conflicting schedules (Lost Label vs direct artist schedules)". B 19,169.49 and 270.31 → C `assumption`.
  - **hygiene (E4):** splits with `valid_to = valid_from` are deleted with their shares (`splits.deleteEmptySplits`, repository SQL guarded by the empty-period condition, migration only, logged per split): 50 = the 38 of §6 + 8 left by E1 and 4 by E3. Their source is the splits service, which keeps a split replaced from its first day as `[d, d)` (history), not `normalise-dates`; `normalise-dates` now refuses an ISRC whose flooring would leave an empty period (`refused_empty_period`). No amount changes.
- Checked: `GET /reconciliation?from=2024-01&to=2026-08` (port 3012, stopped): 52 rows, discrepancy 0, `fileDiscrepancy` 0 on 45 Believe rows. Account 1052428: B 6,743,236.57 → 6,622,334.45, C 748,978.81 → 869,880.94 (assumption 15,885.63 → 138,272.57, owner_unconfirmed 592,602.73 → 591,391.69, split_unconfirmed 133,856.56 → 133,582.80), ours 7,788,578.04 unchanged; 1055956 and B17 unchanged. Annex compare unchanged (91,364 of 107,006 cells, 92.37 % of receipts, Σ ERP − Annex 411,847.85). Main compare: 802 owners differ (190 within 1.00), Σ|diff| 2,322,605.04 → 2,124,352.00, Σ payable −506,569.03 → −627,471.16; explained within max(1.00, 2 %) 633 → 640, not explained 169 → 162 (Σ|residual| 430,254.18 → 346,014.05; decomposition with E1 = 0 and the carve take as its own component, 14 §9.3). Typecheck, lint, `REQUIRE_STACK=1 npm test` (32 files, 360 tests) pass.
- Known limits: the carve groups 7, 17 and 41 and the 24 groups without a stated percent need the owner; a carve participation is by ISRC, so it also applies to other UPCs of the ISRC (M54); the carve, Q1 and Q5 money stays C until the owner approves the assumption documents; deleting empty-period splits removes history records of split replacements (the replacing split keeps the rows and the document).
- References: `packages/backend/scripts/mvp/{load,steps-stage6,steps-main,steps-annex}.ts` (`stepSettlementPrestart`, `stepCarves`, `stepAssumptionRows`, `stepHygiene`, `stepNormalise`), `src/modules/splits/{service,repository}.ts` (`deleteEmptySplits`, `deleteParticipations`), `tests/golden.test.ts` (G45, G46), `tests/participations.test.ts`, `docs/agents/12_golden_cases.md`, `docs/agents/14_verification.md` §9, `docs/agents/13_completeness_check.md` §1
- Status: accepted

## D-138: Stage 7 closing: the settlement share before its start spread pro rata over the legacy rows (Main payment fact), golden case G47; the owner decomposition in the repo; report figures refreshed
- Date: 2026-11-03
- Question: the stage 7 council (`docs/agents/reviews/stage7_{architect,product,qa}.md`) found one blocker and several should-fixes. Product finding 1: D-137 `settlement-prestart` gave the participant's legacy 15 % before 2026-02 entirely to LDRR, while Main's payment fact (the source D-137 cites) split it pro rata between the co-artists (DJ Ikeraus and LDRR +7.5 each where both held 17.5); about 8.3 k of B on the wrong owners. QA: the owner decomposition lived only in the session scratchpad (absolute paths, a hard-coded E1 constant); three figures of `15` §2 came from `13` §1, which left out report month 2024-01. Architect: the root `CLAUDE.md` test count and "through the facade" claim, `01` §5 stage labels, `05` and `03` ranges, the undocumented Python extractors, a misleading dry-run counter, a held-payout nickname that reads like a legal name.
- Decision:
  - **settlement-prestart (E1), pro rata.** On the 30 settlement ISRC the participant's percent in the ISRC's first split that holds it (15, the legacy appendix) is spread over that split's other rows pro rata to their percents (6 decimals, remainder to the last row): 35 → 50 where LDRR is the only other row; 17.5 / 17.5 → 25 / 25 (DJ Ikeraus, LDRR; 8 ISRC); 14 / 10.5 / 10.5 → 20 / 15 / 15 (LDRR, RD12, DJ MRCL; DGA0L2423523…526). A row that is 0 in a later split before the window (the DJ Ikeraus buy-out of 2025-12) takes nothing: its part stays with the label, since the buy-out moved his whole row to the label (LDRR 25, ours 75 in 2025-12 … 2026-01). Every split before 2026-02-01 is changed from max(its `valid_from`, the first report month), under the same approved document "Owner decision 6 of 2026-11-02: settlement share before 2026-02-27", note `mvp:settlement-prorata:<isrc>:<from>`. Re-entry of the D-137 form: a split with the old marker `mvp:settlement-prestart:` is read back as the legacy rows (the payee row less the base percent) and changed from the same date (the splits service keeps the replaced split as an empty period, which `hygiene` deletes, logged); a D-137 split whose only other row is LDRR already has the pro rata rows and is left (`already_pro_rata`). Applied: 20 splits re-entered (12 before, 8 in the buy-out period), 18 left, `hygiene` deleted 20 empty periods; the next dry run reports `already_done=12 already_pro_rata=18`. This is a decision with a source (Main's payment fact), not an assumption; its money is B except where the owner is unconfirmed.
  - **Golden case G47** (`12_golden_cases.md`, `tests/golden.test.ts`): legacy rows A 17.5, B 17.5, S 15 under one appendix to 2026-02-01; from 2026-02-01 rows A 17.5, B 17.5 and the settlement S 15 `(net, label_share)`; the loader form from 2026-01: A 25, B 25. Unit 2026-01: A 25, B 25, ours 50; unit 2026-02: A 17.5, B 17.5, S 15, ours 50. Data-shaped; no layer change.
  - **`scripts/mvp/decompose-main.ts`**: the stage 5/6 decomposition (`owner_dump.mts` + `carve_take.mts` + `decomp2.py`) as one read-only script: reads `data/mvp-load/verify/main_owners.csv` (warns when an owner's diff no longer matches it), the Main JSON and the database; prints the explained / not explained counts at max(1.00, 2 %) and at 1.00, by size, and the Σ per cause; no absolute paths, no E1 constant. On the same state it gives the counts of `decomp2.py … noE1`. Recipe 9.
  - **Docs**: `13` §1 re-measured on the final state with report month 2024-01 included (the caveat removed; `no_split` 622,047.62 on 2,500 ISRC, 81 above 1,000 = 519,882.96; the old 596,724.70 left out 2024-01's 25,322.92); `15` §1, §2, §3 (all sublabel money in C with its reasons; C11, C22, C25, C26 with amounts; Zachz Winner's 21,225.40 as money owed; the post-buy-out 165.80), §4 (E1 as a decision on the payment fact; Q12 11,234 without Q11's 63.30); `14` §0, §4.3 (Zachz Winner) and new §10 (before / after); root `CLAUDE.md` (test count, the loader's three bypasses, the Python extractors); `01` §5 (stage 5 = verification, stage 6 = rework, D-123 … D-138, `15` exists); `05` (D-131…D-138); `03` (D-135 refined by D-137, D-137 refined by D-138, this line); recipe 9 (the extractors, their inputs and outputs, that `annex/*.json` and `echo8/*.json` were built from session dumps and cannot be rebuilt from the repository alone; Python 3 stdlib only, run with `-I`).
  - **Dry-run counter**: `steps-main.ts` `owner_created_for_nickname` renamed `holder_owner_for_nickname` (it counts every holder nickname resolved, found or created; creations are `owners_created`).
  - **Privacy**: the nickname of the third held payout, which reads like a legal name, is replaced in the tracked files by "the third held payout (owner 7129f35a)": D-132 and D-135 in this log (an exception to append-only, for personal data only, nothing else in those entries changed), `13` §2.6 and C10, and two explanation strings in `scripts/mvp/build_main.py` / `report_main.py`. The stage 7 verdicts do not contain it. The other two held payouts are stage names and stay.
- Checked: the five totals of the product verdict, report months 2024-06 … 2026-01 on the 30 settlement ISRC: DJ Ikeraus B 18,402.84 → 26,289.77 (+7,886.93), RD12 B 318.66 → 455.22 (+136.56), DJ MRCL C `owner_unconfirmed` 318.66 → 455.22 (+136.56), ours 458,112.59 → 458,278.39 (+165.80), LDRR B 438,298.73 → 429,972.87 (−8,325.86), the participant 0; the changes sum to 0 (the verdict's ours +178.18 was LDRR's whole reduction in 2025-12 … 2026-01, which includes 12.38 to RD12 and DJ MRCL). Reconciliation 2024-01 … 2026-08: 52 rows, discrepancy 0, `fileDiscrepancy` 0 on 45 Believe rows; 1052428 B 6,622,334.45 → 6,622,032.09, C 869,880.94 → 870,017.51, ours 7,788,578.04 → 7,788,743.83; 1055956 and B17 unchanged. Annex: 91,364 → 91,348 cells within 0.01 (the 8 buy-out ISRC in 2025-12 and 2026-01: Annex keeps the label share at 67.5 %), 92.37 % → 92.35 % of receipts, Σ ERP − Annex 411,847.85 → 412,013.64. Main: 802 owners differ, Σ payable −627,471.16 → −627,773.52; explained within max(1.00, 2 %) 640 → 641, not explained 162 → 161 (Σ 346,014.05 → 337,982.01); within 1.00 513 / 289 (373,414.03 → 363,535.47). Typecheck, lint, `REQUIRE_STACK=1 npm test` (32 files, 361 tests), `npm run build:client` pass; `load.ts all` dry run reports nothing to do.
- Known limits: after the buy-out the 165.80 is ours by Main's LDRR credit; Annex keeps it off the label (owner check, `15` §3 item 14). The pro rata weights are the rows of the first legacy split; a later legacy change of a co-artist's percent (other than a buy-out to 0) would not re-weight (none exists). `annex/*.json` and `echo8/*.json` remain not rebuildable from the repository alone.
- References: `packages/backend/scripts/mvp/{steps-stage6,decompose-main,steps-main,build_main,report_main}.*`, `tests/golden.test.ts` (G47), `docs/agents/{12_golden_cases,13_completeness_check,14_verification,15_mvp_report,04_recipes,01_big_picture,05_doc_drift,03_decision_index}.md`, root `CLAUDE.md`, `docs/agents/reviews/stage7_*.md`
