## D-139: Frontend goal: the operator's workplace design (rules, stack, operator trace, e2e harness)
- Date: 2026-11-03
- Question: `notes/goal_prompt_frontend.md` asks for a frontend that is the operator's workplace at the level of zero-erp: every question of the label's operator answered on a screen and every unblocking action taken there, without SQL or scripts; the current pages (D-007, D-062, D-129) are lists without money, drill-down, confirmations or tests. Stage 1 (`docs/agents/16_frontend_jobs.md`) described the jobs; stage 2 (`17_frontend_model.md`, `18_frontend_plan.md`, `19_golden_screens.md`) is the design, accepted by the council (`reviews/frontend_stage2_*.md`, revision 2).
- Decision:
  - **Rules R1–R8 of `17` §1, each with a machine check**: the frontend computes nothing (every amount, total and gate is an API field; eslint bans `Number(`, `parseFloat`, `parseInt`, `Math.*`, unary `+`, decimal.js imports and arithmetic on money-named identifiers in `packages/frontend/src`; `formatMoney` is the one formatter); one money call per screen and no server-side cache (D-012 stands; the e2e counts money requests); keys are ISRC and UPC, a route never carries a name; the backend decides (gates are response fields, e.g. `balance.payout`); every write carries `X-Operator` and is recorded by the `audit` module; types and schemas only from `@erp/shared`, every response parsed (`schema.parse`, a failure is the error state `contract_mismatch`: a change from D-119, where the client did not parse); one question per page; no personal data outside the operator's screen.
  - **Stack (each library pinned exactly)**: `@heroui/react` and `@heroui/styles` 3.2.7, `tailwindcss` and `@tailwindcss/vite` 4.3.4, `@tanstack/react-query` 5.105.3, `ag-grid-community` and `ag-grid-react` 36.2.0, `@heroicons/react` 2.2.0, `react-router-dom` 7.18.5, `@playwright/test` 1.64.1; not taken: Refine, three.js, charts, i18next, framer-motion, dnd-kit, xlsx, axios, ag-grid-enterprise. React, Vite, TypeScript and the typed client of D-007 stay; **D-062 (minimal UI without a kit) is superseded**; D-007 is refined (HeroUI + Tailwind instead of plain CSS). Build rules: react never split into its own chunk; AG Grid modules registered once.
  - **The shell and the pages** of `17` §2–§4 (21 pages, one sentence each, Russian interface), the components of `17` §5 with their test hooks, the actions of `17` §6 with one confirmation dialog for every irreversible action and the backend's answer to a second request stated per action.
  - **Endpoints only for the screens** (`17` §7, N1–N20): aggregates of one `calculate()` (overview with the queue summary, the C queue per reason, owners' payables, track summary and money by month, releases, document uses), lists and search (search with `pg_trgm`, duplicate pseudonyms, filters), the payout gate as a field computed by the same helper as `createPayout`, the `audit` module and `GET /audit`, the ClickHouse database name honoured by `connectSources` (N20, corrects the D-121 claim). No money rule (D-123 … D-138) changes; every aggregate is tested equal to the figure it restates.
  - **The event loop (допущение)**: the synchronous 4 s layer fold is accepted (one operator; 7–10 s per recalculation is the norm of the goal); `calculatedAt` on every money response and the "идёт пересчёт" state make it visible; a `setImmediate` yield is measured in 3.3a and kept only under 5 %.
  - **Money text (допущение)**: `Intl.NumberFormat('ru-RU', { minimumFractionDigits: 2, maximumFractionDigits: 2, roundingMode: 'halfExpand' })` applied to the decimal string, the exact string in `title`.
  - **The e2e harness**: Playwright specs for the golden screens of `19` on private PostgreSQL and ClickHouse stores created and seeded through the facade by `scripts/e2e/seed.ts` (`createPrivateStores`, shared with the tests), one database per run, every golden case with its own synthetic Believe account and ISRC range, the API with `PAYOUT_THRESHOLD_CENTS=0`, `retries: 0`, `workers: 1`, no sleeps; `npm run e2e` from the root. The dev database `erp` is never touched.
  - **Plan**: `18` §2 steps 3.1 … 3.10 with D-140 … D-149; stages 4–6 in `18` §3–§5; the assumptions A1–A9 of `18` §6 are recorded in their D-entries marked "допущение".
- Checked: the stack installs under npm 11 with React 19.3.1, Vite 8.3.5, `@vitejs/plugin-react` 6.2.1, TypeScript 6 and builds a page with a HeroUI button, tabs and an AG Grid table (sandbox, 2026-11-03); `pg_trgm` 1.6 is available in the compose PostgreSQL; the council accepted stage 2 (architect, product, QA: `reviews/frontend_stage2_*.md`).
- Known limits: authentication and roles stay out (the operator field is a trace, not a login); the payout side (threshold rules, fees, taxes, payment tools), ads, artist statements and analytics beyond the source lines facets stay out (`17` §10); a second identical split change is accepted by the backend today (A9, decided in 3.6).
- References: `notes/goal_prompt_frontend.md`, `docs/agents/{16_frontend_jobs,17_frontend_model,18_frontend_plan,19_golden_screens}.md`, `docs/agents/reviews/frontend_stage{1,2}_*.md`
- Status: accepted

## D-140: Operator trace (`X-Operator`, the `audit` module, `GET /audit`), private stores shared by tests and e2e, the ClickHouse database name honoured
- Date: 2026-11-03
- Question: the frontend goal requires every action to leave a trace (who, when) without authentication (D-139 R5), the e2e tests need a private database pair created the way the tests create theirs (D-139 §harness), and `17` N20 found that `connectSources()` ignored `CLICKHOUSE_DATABASE` (the columnar fell back to `believe`), so an API started on private stores would have read the dev data. Step 3.1 of `18_frontend_plan.md`.
- Decision:
  - **`audit` module** (`modules/audit/`, schema `audit`, table `audit.actions`: id, at, operator, method, route template, entity_ids text[], summary jsonb; migration `0011_audit_search.sql`, which also installs `pg_trgm` and the trigram GIN indexes for the search of `17` N10). The module stores and lists; it decides nothing. `boundaries.test.ts` lists it.
  - **The hook** `api/audit.ts`, mounted before the v1 router: every POST / PATCH / PUT / DELETE under `/api/v1` must carry `X-Operator` (URI-encoded, 1–200 characters after decoding), else 400 `operator_required`; the operator is `res.locals.operator`; after a 2xx the hook awaits `facade.recordAction()` before the response is sent (a following `GET /audit` sees the row; a failed record is 500 `audit_failed`). Recorded: method, the route template (never the concrete path: a writer's name can be a path segment), the ids of the params, of the known body fields (ownerId, artistId, documentId, isrc, upc, isrcs, fromId, intoId, sellerArtistId, recipientArtistId, labelId, paymentProofId) and of the response body (`id` of a create), a summary of params and whitelisted body keys (amount, percent, dates, kind, status, direction, reason): never names, titles, comments or aliases. `approveDocument` takes the operator as `by` (the header wins over the body). `GET /audit?entityId&limit` (`auditListSchema`); `operations.ts` declares the header on every write and the OpenAPI document emits it. **Допущение** (A1 of `18` §6): one operator role, the trace is a typed name; roles and login stay out.
  - **Private stores**: `src/shared/private-stores.ts` `createPrivateStores(name, { dropFirst })` creates `erp_<name>` (PostgreSQL) and `believe_<name>` + `believe_<name>_stage` (ClickHouse) with every migration (`splitStatements` now in `src/shared/clickhouse-sql.ts`), `reset`, `resetModules`, `drop`; `tests/helpers/sources-stack.ts` sits on it; `scripts/e2e/seed.ts <name>` (skeleton: creates the stores, opens a facade, runs `scripts/e2e/scenarios/`) is the e2e seed; the `Case` builder of `golden.test.ts` moved to `tests/helpers/golden-case.ts` so the seed scenarios reuse it.
  - **N20**: `connectSources()` reads `CLICKHOUSE_DATABASE` (default `believe`) and passes `{ database, stageDatabase: <name>_stage }` to the columnar (`clickHouseDatabaseNames(env)`); this corrects the D-121 statement that `CLICKHOUSE_DATABASE` switched the data set (until now only the client default changed).
  - Every test that writes through supertest uses `tests/helpers/writer.ts` (the header by default); the contract scenario asserts one audit row per write with the route template and the ids, and the refusal without the header.
- Checked: `REQUIRE_STACK=1 npm test` 369 tests (361 + 8: `audit.test.ts`, `connect-sources.test.ts`, the contract additions); `npm run migrate:pg` applied 0011 to the dev database (`applied 1, total 11`); `npm run migrate:ch` up to date; the seed skeleton created and dropped a scratch store pair.
- Known limits: a write to an unknown `/api/v1` route without the header gets 400 instead of 404 (the hook runs before routing); the audit row of a create carries the new id only when the response body has one; `docker-compose.yml` mounts `src/shared/clickhouse-sql.ts` into the ClickHouse migration container (the container path was not exercised; the host `npm run migrate:ch` was); `allowImportingTsExtensions` set in the backend tsconfig for the `.ts` import of the migration script.
- References: `packages/backend/src/modules/audit/`, `src/api/audit.ts`, `src/api/routes/audit.ts`, `src/connect-sources.ts`, `src/shared/{private-stores,clickhouse-sql}.ts`, `scripts/e2e/`, `tests/{audit,connect-sources,api-contract,boundaries}.test.ts`, `tests/helpers/{writer,golden-case,sources-stack}.ts`, `packages/shared/src/{api-schemas,api-responses}.ts`, `docs/agents/17_frontend_model.md` §7 N16, N20
- Status: accepted

## D-144: The frontend foundation: shell, components, the client contract, the money text rule, the e2e harness as one command
- Date: 2026-11-03
- Question: step 3.4 of `18_frontend_plan.md`: the shell and the components of `17` §2 and §5, one API client that follows R6, the guard tests of R1, R3, R8, and `npm run e2e` on private stores, with the old pages kept alive under `/legacy/*` until step 3.9.
- Decision:
  - **Stack installed** with the exact versions of D-139 (plus `react-aria-components` 1.22.1 as a direct dependency for the `HiddenRacDialogAnchor` of the controlled dialog); `index.css` = `@import "tailwindcss"; @import "@heroui/styles";` + tokens; `html.dark` theme toggle; one vendor chunk (react is never split; `chunkSizeWarningLimit` 1700).
  - **Shell**: `nav.ts` (sections and routes of `17` §2–§3, the breadcrumbs derived from it with an entity label slot), `AppShell`, `Sidebar` (plain `Link`: `NavLink` broke the payouts-view highlight), `GlobalSearch` (Cmd/Ctrl+K), `OperatorField` (localStorage `erp.operator`; empty = every write disabled), `RecalcIndicator` (`useIsFetching` on the money keys), `ThemeToggle`; per-domain route files; every route of `17` §3 exists (a `PlaceholderPage` until its step); a dev route `/dev/states` renders the page states, a 26,000-row grid and a 2,100-item picker for the e2e `S-states` checks.
  - **Components** of `17` §5 with their test hooks: `DataGrid` (AG Grid community, `themeQuartz.withParams` on HeroUI variables, own Russian `localeText` written from the v36 community keys, pagination 50/100/500, `getRowId` required, `[row-id]` rows, sort and filter state in the URL), `Money` / `formatMoney` (`Intl.NumberFormat('ru-RU', …, roundingMode 'halfExpand')` on the decimal string; **допущение** A7 of `18` §6), `StatusChip` (one enum → label table, Russian labels for every C reason and signal, guarded by `tests/reasons-labels.test.ts`), `EntityLink`, `EntityCard` + `CardTabs` (`?tab=`), fields (`TextField`, `SelectField`, `DateField` as a native date input in HeroUI styles, `EntityPicker` over `/search`), `ConfirmDialog`, `PageState` + `StaleBadge` + `QueryView`, `toast` (the hooks sit on a span inside the HeroUI toast title: its root takes no attributes), `ActionButton`; `useAction` with a synchronous in-flight guard (a double click sends one request).
  - **Client contract (R6)**: `src/api/client.ts` has one `call()` that sends `X-Operator` URI-encoded on writes (a client-side `operator_required` when empty), parses every response with its `@erp/shared` schema (`contract_mismatch` on failure), and one function per operation of `operations.ts`; `src/api/queries/<domain>.ts` key factories, `MONEY_KEYS`, `invalidateMoney`; `tests/no-dto.test.ts` forbids hand-written body types. The search response schema is local (`src/api/search-schema.ts`) until step 3.3b puts it in `@erp/shared`.
  - **Guard tests and lint (R1, R3, R8)**: an eslint block for `packages/frontend/src/**` (not `src/legacy/**`) bans `Number(` / `parseFloat` / `parseInt`, unary `+`, `Math.*` and the imports of `decimal.js`, `big.js`, `bignumber.js`; vitest `tests/{money,no-dto,routes-no-names,privacy,reasons-labels,nav}.test.ts` (26 tests).
  - **E2E as one command**: `npm run e2e` → Playwright `webServer` runs `scripts/e2e/start.sh` (Docker check, `scripts/e2e/seed.ts e2e` on the private stores, client build, the API on `:3100` with `DB_DATABASE=erp_e2e`, `CLICKHOUSE_DATABASE=believe_e2e`, `PAYOUT_THRESHOLD_CENTS=0` serving the built client), `workers: 1`, `retries: 0`, test timeout 120 s, `globalTeardown` drops the stores (`scripts/e2e/drop.ts`) unless `E2E_KEEP=1`; `e2e/smoke.spec.ts` and `e2e/states.spec.ts` (22 tests). **Допущения** A4 (every list client-side in AG Grid) and A8 (one account per golden case, threshold 0) of `18` §6.
  - **Legacy**: the old pages, client and CSS moved to `src/legacy/`, mounted lazily under `/legacy/*`; the legacy client sends the header too; removed in step 3.9.
- Checked: `npm run typecheck`, `npm run lint`, `REQUIRE_STACK=1 npm test` (369 backend + 26 frontend), `npm run build:client` (index 403 kB, vendor 1,520 kB, CSS 474 kB), `npm run e2e` 22 passed in 24 s with the stores created, seeded (empty scenarios) and dropped.
- Known limits: `GET /audit` has no client function yet (added with the History tab); the seed writes its originals under `data/test-tmp/e2e-e2e` while the API runs on `data/test-tmp/e2e-data` (aligned when a spec needs the originals); closing the search modal with text typed takes two Escapes (the SearchField clears first).
- References: `packages/frontend/{CLAUDE.md,package.json,vite.config.ts,playwright.config.ts,vitest.config.ts}`, `packages/frontend/src/{nav.ts,App.tsx,main.tsx,index.css,components/**,api/**,i18n/**,lib/**,routes/**,pages/**,legacy/**}`, `packages/frontend/{tests,e2e}/**`, `scripts/e2e/start.sh`, `packages/backend/scripts/e2e/drop.ts`, root `eslint.config.js`, `.gitignore`
- Status: accepted

## D-141: The C queue as an aggregation of the C items, the overview in one calculation, signals with counts and limits
- Date: 2026-11-03
- Question: step 3.2 of `18_frontend_plan.md`: the operator's queue (`17` P2) needs every category-C dollar grouped by the entity that unblocks it, and the overview (`17` P1) needs reconciliation, the queue summary and the signal counts from one recalculation instead of two.
- Decision:
  - **`facade/queue.ts`**: `cItems(calc, f)` yields every C item exactly as `reconcile()` counts it (the C allocations of the units in the period, the label share when it is not ours, the publishing C and the whole payable of a line without an owner as `writer_unmapped`); `reconcile()` now sums its C side from `cItems`, so the queue and the reconciliation cannot drift (the golden tests pin `reconcile()`). `cQueue(calc, f, opts, labels)` groups the items per reason by the key of `16` §2.6: owner (`owner_unconfirmed`), split (`split_unconfirmed`, `assumption` on rows), participation or label rule (`rule_unconfirmed`, `assumption` on carves and the sublabel, with `assumptionBasis`), artist (`artist_without_owner`), label string (`label_unmapped`), pair (`unknown_pair`, `no_isrc`), writer (`writer_unmapped`), owner terms (`publishing_terms_unconfirmed`); a group carries owner, pseudonym, document and its status, rule id, amount, ISRC count, months and the top 20 ISRCs; zero items are skipped; `tests/queue.test.ts` asserts Σ per reason = `C_breakdown[reason]` for every account and month and Σ summary = `C_waiting`.
  - **`GET /queue/:reason?account&month&ownerId&q&limit&offset`** (`queuePageSchema`: `calculatedAt`, `total`, `amount`, paged `items`); an unknown reason is 400.
  - **`GET /overview?from&to&basis`** (`overviewSchema`): one `calculate(modules, opDocIds())`, then `reconciliationFrom` (the same rows as `GET /reconciliation`), the queue summary, the signal counts and the loaded file counts; `calculatedAt` on the response.
  - **Signals**: `signalsFrom(ctx, calc, f)` as a function of a calculation; the response gains `counts` (the full length of every list) and `limit` cuts every list (default 100); the list shapes are unchanged (additive).
  - `/sources/files` no longer returns `storedPath` (a server path, `17` R8).
- Checked: typecheck, lint, `REQUIRE_STACK=1 npm test` 372 backend + 26 frontend, `npm run build:client`; the contract scenario calls `/overview` and `/queue/:reason`.
- Known limits: `q` filters the groups, not the summary; a group whose allocations disagree on owner, artist or document carries null there; `/overview` was not timed against `/reconciliation` (one calculation instead of two is by construction).
- References: `packages/backend/src/facade/{queue,calculation,reports}.ts`, `src/api/routes/{reports,sources}.ts`, `src/api/operations.ts`, `packages/shared/src/{api-responses,api-schemas}.ts`, `tests/{queue,api-contract}.test.ts`, `docs/agents/17_frontend_model.md` §7 N1, N3, N18
- Status: accepted

## D-142: Money aggregates for the screens: the payout gate field, payables, track and release money, document uses; G48, G49; the event-loop yield
- Date: 2026-11-03
- Question: step 3.3a of `18_frontend_plan.md`: the pages need payable and the payout gate per owner, the money of every track and release, what waits for a document, and the stored payout snapshot as a typed response, without the client computing anything.
- Decision:
  - **`facade/payout-gate.ts` `payoutGate({ confirmed, blocking, payableCents, thresholdCents })`** → `{ allowed, reason, amountCents }`; the order is the one `createPayout` always had: `owner_not_confirmed`, `payout_blocked`, `nothing_to_pay` (payable cents <= 0), `below_threshold`. `createPayout` now takes its decision from `balance.payout` (one code path; the error codes and `details.operationIds` are unchanged). `amountCents` is the floored payable, 0 when negative. `balance.payout` (`payoutGateSchema`) is filled by `balanceWithFiles`.
  - **`GET /owners/payables?from&to&basis`**: one `calculate`, `ownerIndex(calc)` (per owner the units where it has an allocation and its publishing lines, calculation order kept), the operations grouped by owner, then the same `ownerBalance(calc, id, ops, f, index)` per owner (the index only replaces the arrays the loops walk). The totals are all-time like the balance; the period shapes `waitingBreakdown` only. `lastPayoutDate` = newest active operation of meaning payout.
  - **`GET /tracks/summary?account&month`**: units grouped by ISRC (units without an ISRC are signals, not tracks), plus every track of module 1 with zeros; B, C by reason (the `cItems` of `queue.ts`), ours, net; `hasSplit` / `hasParticipations` from the split and participation lists; Σ over the tracks equals the believe rows of `reconciliation` for the same filter. **`GET /tracks/:isrc/money`**: the units of one ISRC with allocations labelled (pseudonym, owner name, document status), the label share, the state after every layer; no breakdown. **`GET /tracks/:isrc/breakdown/:month`**: `columnar.breakdown(fileIds, isrc, month)` over the loaded files (`reporting_month = {month:Date}`), rows as `explain` returns them (net text, quantity a number; no gross: the existing rows are pinned by a test).
  - **`GET /releases?q`**: tracks grouped by UPC, names of the first pair (by key) of the UPC, `columnar.netByUpc(fileIds)` (one `GROUP BY upc` on the `units` view over loaded files), `q` filters UPC / title / artist in JS; no calculation. **`GET /releases/:upc`**: one calculation, tracks and report months with net, B, C, ours; 404 when neither a track nor a unit has the UPC.
  - **`GET /documents/:id/uses`**: the records citing the document (splits, rows with their own document, participations, label rules, contracts, operations as basis or proof) and `waiting`: the C allocations that cite it plus the `owner_unconfirmed` C (allocations and publishing) of the owners of its contracts (a contract document unblocks its owner).
  - **`payoutSnapshotSchema`** for `GET /settlements/operations/:id/snapshot` (what `createPayout` stores: totals, per-artist, publishing, positions, the files of both sources); no free-form part.
  - **Event loop**: `calculate()` yields with `setImmediate` every `YIELD_EVERY_UNITS = 50_000` units. Measured on the dev database `erp` (721,975 units, `GET /reconciliation` x3, API on :3022): with the yield `allocate units` 3777 / 3840 / 3605 ms and 3810 / 3667 / 3720 ms, requests 7.38 / 7.44 / 7.11 s and 7.41 / 7.14 / 7.16 s; without it 4237 / 4084 / 3736 ms, requests 8.06 / 7.72 / 7.29 s. The difference is inside the noise between runs (the 14 yields cost less than a millisecond each), far under 5 %: kept.
  - **G48** (deduction and credit as the balance rule: deduction 10 → payable 40, credit −5 → 45, deduction above the income recouped 50 remaining 20) and **G49** (deactivate a migration payout 30: 20 → 50; a button payout deactivated with `transferFailed` unblocks; S31/k4 chain 40, 45, 55, payout 55 → 0, return → 55) appended to `12`; no new rule.
- Checked: typecheck, lint, `REQUIRE_STACK=1 npm test` (395 backend), `npm run build:client`; `tests/aggregates.test.ts` (17 tests) and the contract scenario call every new route.
- Known limits: `GET /owners/payables` and the track summary are as slow as one calculation (7 s on the dev data); the summary leaves out units without an ISRC; `quantity` of the breakdown stays a number.
- References: `packages/backend/src/facade/{payout-gate,aggregates,balance,context,payouts,calculation}.ts`, `src/modules/sources/{columnar,service}.ts`, `src/api/routes/aggregates.ts`, `src/api/operations.ts`, `packages/shared/src/{api-responses,api-schemas}.ts`, `tests/{aggregates,golden,api-contract}.test.ts`, `docs/agents/12_golden_cases.md`
- Status: accepted

## D-143: Search, duplicate pseudonyms, list filters and paging (step 3.3b)
- Date: 2026-11-03
- Question: step 3.3b of `18_frontend_plan.md` (17 N10, N11, N12, N14, N18): the pages need a global search, the groups of equal pseudonyms with a hint where a split is missing, filters on documents and operations (with the owner name and the pseudonym), and paging of splits and participations, all without the client filtering whole tables.
- Decision:
  - **`GET /search?q&kinds`** (`facade/search.ts` `search(q, kinds?)`; two modules, so the facade): `{ owners, artists, tracks, documents, labels }`, at most 10 per kind, `q` trimmed with at least 1 character, `kinds` a comma list of `owners|artists|tracks|documents|labels` (an unknown kind is a 400). The SQL is in the repositories (`searchOwners/Artists/Tracks/Documents` in owners, `searchLabels` in labels): `lower(col) LIKE '%q%' ESCAPE '\'` with `% _ \` of `q` escaped, so it uses the `pg_trgm` indexes of `0011`. Owners match the name or any alias (live owners only), artists the pseudonym (merged ones are hidden), tracks the ISRC, UPC or title with an exact ISRC / UPC match first, documents the title, labels the name or an alias. Owner and artist `status` is the one of the lists (confirmed sets computed once in the same snapshot).
  - **`GET /artists/duplicates`** (`artistDuplicates()`): live artists grouped by `normalizeName(pseudonym)` (NFKC, lower case, punctuation and spaces collapsed; the module's own key), groups of 2 or more, sorted by the key. Each artist has `ownerId`, `ownerName`, `status`, `splitRows` (split shares naming it, merged ids resolved) and `participations`. `candidateIsrcs` per group: pairs of `pairAttributes` whose normalised Believe artist name equals the key and whose ISRC has no split of any period, with `upc`, `trackTitle` and `net` from the new `columnar.netByIsrc(fileIds, isrcs)` (the `units` view, loaded files only). No `calculate()`. The response is `{ groups }`.
  - **`GET /documents?status&kind&q&ownerId&limit`** (`documentsQuery`): the status is a SQL condition equal to `documentStatus()` (no file, file waiting for approval, otherwise approved); `q` is a title substring; the response stays the array.
  - **`GET /settlements/operations?ownerId&artistId&meaning&direction&needsProof&active&from&to`** (`operationsQuery`): all filters are SQL in the settlements repository; `meaning` is rebuilt from direction, sign, scope and `operation_ref` exactly as `meaningOf` does, `needsProof` = direction out AND no payment proof AND active, `from` / `to` inclusive dates, booleans are the strings `true` / `false`. The route goes through the facade (`listOperations`), which adds `ownerName` and `pseudonym` (module 1 in the same snapshot). `operationSchema` gains the two fields as `nullable().optional()` because the other operation responses (create, proof, deferred, return) do not carry them. The response stays the array.
  - **N18**: `listQuery` gains `offset` and `status` (`unconfirmed`). `/participations` honours `limit` / `offset` (no limit = all, as before; `total` is the count before paging, the document statuses are read for the page only). `/splits` honours `offset` and `status=unconfirmed` (document missing, not attached or not approved, from the `documentStatus` the list already computes); `total` is the count after the status filter, the default limit stays 200.
  - **Assumptions of `18`**: A3, the open-question list is static data in `@erp/shared` (`open-questions.ts`, written by step 3.5's agent), not an endpoint; A6, documents and operations are arrays with filters and no paging (only participations and splits are paged).
  - `tests/search.test.ts` (4: exact ISRC first, substring on a pseudonym, owner alias, label, document, wildcards as text, kinds, the 10 limit, refusals; duplicates with an orphan ISRC listed and a split ISRC not) and `tests/lists.test.ts` (3: documents, operations, paging); the contract scenario calls the changed operations with the new parameters.
- Checked: typecheck, lint, `REQUIRE_STACK=1 npm test` in the backend (402 tests), `npm run build:client`.
- Known limits: the search is a substring match, no ranking beyond the exact ISRC / UPC; `candidateIsrcs` reads all pair names of the loaded files (one ClickHouse query, as the releases list does); a participation list has no `status` filter; `ownerName` / `pseudonym` are absent (not null) on the write responses.
- References: `packages/backend/src/facade/{search,owner-view,splits}.ts`, `src/modules/{owners,labels,settlements,sources}/{repository,service}.ts`, `src/modules/sources/columnar.ts`, `src/api/routes/{owners,settlements,splits,participations}.ts`, `src/api/operations.ts`, `packages/shared/src/{api-schemas,api-responses}.ts`, `migrations/postgres/0011_audit_search.sql`, `tests/{search,lists,api-contract}.test.ts`, `docs/agents/17_frontend_model.md` §7, `docs/agents/18_frontend_plan.md`.
- Status: accepted

## D-145: The work pages: overview, the C queue with its actions, the signals page with its actions, the open-question list (step 3.5)
- Date: 2026-11-03
- Question: step 3.5 of `18_frontend_plan.md`: the pages P1 «Обзор», P2 «Очередь ожидания (C)», P21 «Сигналы», P3 «Решения руководителя» of `17` §3 with every action of `16` §2.6–2.7, and their Playwright specs.
- Decision:
  - **P1** `/` (`pages/work/OverviewPage.tsx`): one `GET /overview` (period `from` / `to` / `basis` in the URL); a plain table (not AG Grid: every row is in the DOM, the C cell has its per-reason breakdown) per source, account and month with A, B, C (+ per reason, each → `/queue/:reason?account=&month=`), ours (+ per own label), no-split ours, discrepancy and file discrepancy (red when not `"0"`); B and ours → `/tracks?account=&month=`; the account → `/sources/lines?account=&month=` (the overview has no file id: the lines page resolves account and month, step 3.8); the queue summary and the signal counts as link strips. **No totals row**: the API gives none and the client does not sum (R1). Empty state when no file is loaded (`files.believe` and `files.b17` are 0).
  - **P2** `/queue` reuses the overview query (one money call shared with P1) with the reason label, amount, groups, ISRCs and one sentence of what to do (`ru.reasonActions`, typed against `C_REASONS`); `/queue/:reason` = `GET /queue/:reason` with `account`, `month`, `ownerId`, `q`, `limit` (50/100/500), `offset` in the URL; the offsets of the server pages are multiples of the page size up to `total` (paging arithmetic, no money). One action per reason, each a dialog (`pages/work/actions/dialogs.tsx`): contract (new contract document of the owner → approve with confirmation → `POST /contracts` with the owner's pseudonyms from `GET /owners/:id`, confirmation "владелец становится подтверждённым"), merge (picker + reason + confirmation), document and confirm split, replace an assumption (split: `POST /splits/change` from a month with the assumption document of the row swapped for the new one and the split's own document kept; participation / label rule: close at the month and re-create with the new document), Q11 / R1 groups read-only with the decision, assign owner (or create one), alias or sublabel with rule, approve the rule's document (or close and re-create), register the track of a pair, `no_isrc` read-only (⚠ D-035), map writer then resolve, publishing terms.
  - **The document step** (`actions/DocumentStep.tsx`): pick an approved document (picker restricted to approved documents of the accepted kinds) or create one with its file reference in one `POST /documents` and approve it with the confirmation; the approve dialog states «ждёт этого документа: X» and what the document confirms from `GET /documents/:id/uses` (N13), and the group amount until that answers.
  - **P21** `/signals` lists the 11 lists with `counts` (one `GET /signals?limit=500`); `/signals/:name` shows one list with its columns and actions: register the track (pair with ISRC; without ISRC read-only), create a split, map a writer, close and re-create an unfunded participation (prefilled from `GET /participations?isrc=`), alias / sublabel with rule, set the rule of a sublabel (found by name or alias in `GET /labels`), close the rule whose recipient is a row (the open rule of that pseudonym), set / clear a track note (`PATCH /tracks/:id`), assign the owner of a participant, assumptions link to the document and to `/queue/assumption`.
  - **P3** `/questions` lists `OPEN_QUESTIONS` of `@erp/shared/open-questions` (new export): the 14 items `15.3.1` … `15.3.14` with a Russian title, the decision ids as ⚠ chips, the place and a route that holds their money; no names, no amounts; `tests/open-questions.test.ts` checks the ids, that every link is a route and that no amount is in the text.
  - **Components**: `FormDialog` (HeroUI Modal: title, fields, footer of action buttons; irreversible steps open `ConfirmDialog` on top); `DataGrid` gains `rowTestId` (a `data-testid` on the row element via `processRowPostCreate`) and `autoHeight` (every row of the page in the DOM); `EntityPicker` gains `accept` (keep some matches) and searches the reference list of its kind by the typed text while `GET /search` answers 404; `documentStatus` gains `missing`.
  - **Row keys**: P1 `overview-row-<source>-<account>-<month>`; P2 `queue-group-<key.kind>-<key.id>`; P21 `signal-row-<name>-<key>` with the key `isrc|upc` (unallocated), `isrc` (isrcsWithoutSplit), `writerNorm`, `isrc|month|ruleId` (unfunded), the label string, `label|month`, `isrc|month`, `trackId` / ISRC (notes), `participationId`, `documentId|basis` (assumptions).
  - The writer group of `writer_unmapped` is keyed by the writer's display name, not its normalised key; the map dialog finds the normalised key in `GET /publishing/writers` (display name or key).
  - **Specs** `e2e/golden/{overview,queue,signals,questions}.spec.ts` (28 tests incl. the P1 states; the whole run 50 tests in about 70 s): S01–S11 P1 rows with exact strings; S12, S13, S14, S15, S16, S17, S18, S19, S32 through the queue dialogs with the P1 row after and the second press of `19` §5 (verified: map writer twice 200, an identical split change 201, a second track with the same ISRC + UPC 409 `duplicate_track`); S20 and S21 through P21 (the S20 track from the `unallocated` list, the queue groups checked before); S35/1–4; S33's merge is only opened (it belongs to P10, step 3.7). `e2e/helpers.ts` reads `.seed-keys.json` (or `E2E_KEYS`) and re-exports the naming of `scripts/e2e/keys.ts`. The smoke spec expects the built pages' root hooks instead of the placeholder.
- Checked: typecheck, lint, `npm test -w @erp/frontend`, `npm run build:client`, `npm run e2e`.
- References: `packages/frontend/src/pages/work/**`, `src/components/{FormDialog.tsx,grid/DataGrid.tsx,fields/EntityPicker.tsx}`, `src/api/{client.ts,queries/reports.ts,queries/documents.ts}`, `src/i18n/ru/work.ts`, `packages/shared/src/open-questions.ts`, `packages/frontend/e2e/golden/*.spec.ts`, `docs/agents/17_frontend_model.md` §3–§6, `19_golden_screens.md` §4–§5.
- Status: accepted

## D-146: The catalogue pages: tracks, the track card ("whose is every dollar"), releases (step 3.6)
- Date: 2026-11-03
- Question: step 3.6 of `18_frontend_plan.md`: the pages P5–P8 of `17` §3 on the endpoints of D-142, with the rules actions of `17` §6 and the golden screens of `19` for the track card.
- Decision:
  - **P5 Треки** (`/tracks?account&month&hasSplit&q`): one `GET /tracks/summary`, every row on the client; with an account or month in the URL only the tracks with units in that scope are shown (the endpoint lists every registered track with zeros outside the scope); create track.
  - **P6 Трек** (`/tracks/:isrc?tab=`): one `GET /tracks/:isrc/money`; a month row expands into its allocations (pseudonym, owner, layer, kind, percent, amount, category, reason, document, assumption basis), the layer-by-layer table (the "added" column lists the allocations new or changed since the previous layer, compared as text: the API's `layers[].allocations` are cumulative) and, on request, the platform × country breakdown of that report month (`GET /tracks/:isrc/breakdown/:month`, loaded once per month and shared by its UPC rows); tabs rules (create split without a document, change from a date with a required document prefilled from the newest split, buy-out, confirm split (disabled while the split has a document), create and close a participation), documents (the documents referred to by the rules and the allocations, from one `GET /documents`), history (`GET /audit?entityId=<isrc>`), lines (a link to `/sources/lines?isrc=`); the note is edited on the first registered track of the ISRC. Dates are sent as typed; the toast shows the stored date and "начало округлено до …" when the backend floored it (M52); every dialog states the rule in words.
  - **P7 Релизы** (`/releases?q`, `GET /releases`, no calculation) and **P8 Релиз** (`/releases/:upc`, `GET /releases/:upc`: tracks and months).
  - Facts of the API recorded for the specs: a 0 % row is not allocated (G41 shows only NG 50; after a full buy-out the seller has no row); G44's shortfall 29 comes back as three carve shortfalls (9.666666666666 and twice 9.666666666667); second requests: an identical split change is accepted (201, A9 of `18` §6 stands), a repeated buy-out returns an empty list, the same participation again is 409 `participation_overlap`, closing at the same date is 200.
  - Specs `e2e/golden/{track,rules,release}.spec.ts` (27 tests): P6 numbers of S01–S11 incl. the layer rows of G18 and the exact strings of G34 / G44; S13, S25, S26, S27, S28, S29 through the dialogs; the release of S10 (G24). Where a scenario is acted on by an earlier spec file (S13, S16 by `queue.spec.ts`, S21 by `signals.spec.ts`) the spec asserts the state the API reports or the numbers that hold before and after.
- Checked: typecheck, lint, 30 vitest, `npm run build:client`, `npm run e2e` 91 passed, 1 skipped (the merge-dialog test of `queue.spec.ts` skips itself once `merge.spec.ts` has merged S33), 2.2 min.
- Known limits: the S29 refusal test takes about 19 s (not investigated); the HTTP status of `top_above_100` is accepted as 409 or 422 by the spec (to pin in 3.9 from the backend's `RuleViolationError`).
- References: `packages/frontend/src/pages/catalog/**`, `src/api/catalog.ts`, `src/api/queries/{tracks,releases}.ts`, `src/i18n/ru/catalog.ts`, `src/routes/catalog.tsx`, `e2e/golden/{track,rules,release}.spec.ts`
- Status: accepted

## D-147: The money pages: owners with payable and the gate, the owner card, the payout, operations, merge, the duplicate dialog (step 3.7)
- Date: 2026-11-03
- Question: step 3.7 of `18_frontend_plan.md`: the pages P9, P10, P20 of `17` §3 on the endpoints of D-142 and D-143, the payout button behind `balance.payout` (R4), the operations with their row actions, the merge and the duplicate dialog, and the golden screens S22–S24, S31, S33, S36 of `19`.
- Decision:
  - **P9 Владельцы** (`/owners`, `/owners?view=payouts`): one `GET /owners/payables`; the payouts view is the same rows filtered on the client to `payout.allowed` or `payout_blocked` and ordered by `payout.amountCents` (an API integer compared with `>`; no arithmetic on strings); «Создать владельца» opens the duplicate dialog on 409 `possible_duplicate` («тот же» opens the record, «новый» resends with `force`).
  - **P10 Владелец** (`/owners/:id?tab=`): `GET /owners/:id` and one `GET /owners/:id/balance` shared by the tabs; the balance as fields (earned, recouped, paid, returns, credits, payable, payableShown, waiting by reason with links to the queue, per label, per pseudonym and ISRC, positions, pending and blocking operations); the red "negative payable" warning is shown when `payableShown !== payable` (a field comparison, the exact payable in `title`); «Выплатить» is enabled only by `payout.allowed` with the reason next to it; the confirmation lists the formula fields and the pseudonyms; the toast shows the response's `amountCents`. Tabs Документы и договоры (create, attach, approve with the `uses` lines, create contract), Паблишинг (terms), Операции (the shared grid and row actions), История (`GET /audit?entityId`); «Слить в другого владельца» with the "необратимо" confirmation; a merged owner's page shows the banner (the detail resolves to the survivor: `detail.owner.id !== route id`).
  - **P20 Операции** (`/operations?ownerId&meaning&needsProof&active&from&to`): `GET /settlements/operations` with the URL filters; row actions proof, deferred, return, deactivate, snapshot, shown by the row's own fields (an active out without proof, an active out, an active payout, any active, a non-migration payout); the «Аванс / вычет / кредит» dialog (pseudonym from the owner's artists, scope, basis document by direction, a warning when an equal operation exists); the grid lays the actions right after the amount (AG Grid's column virtualisation at 1280 px).
  - **Cents as text**: `centsToText(cents)` (`pages/owners/components/cents.ts`) turns the integer `amountCents` into a decimal string by padding and slicing only (500 → `5.00`, −5500 → `-55.00`, 7 → `0.07`); no division, no float (R1). Operation, payout and merge actions invalidate every query (the operations and audit lists are not money keys).
  - Specs `e2e/golden/{owner,payout,operations,merge,duplicate-dialog}.spec.ts` (25 tests): S22 (pay, deferred without proof, `payout_blocked`, proof, deferred toggle, second presses), S23, S24 (negative payable shown 0 with the warning; G39 after a retroactive top), S31 (the G48/G49 chain: advance scopes, deduction, credit, deactivate, pay with proof, return), S33 merge, S36 duplicate dialog.
- Checked: typecheck, lint, 30 vitest, `npm run build:client`, `npm run e2e` 101 passed, 1 skipped (the merge-dialog test of `queue.spec.ts`), 2.4 min.
- Known limits: `centsToText` has no unit test yet (added in 3.9); AG Grid `autoHeight` does not grow a row with wrapped buttons, so the state cell holds proof, deferred and active in one cell.
- References: `packages/frontend/src/pages/owners/**`, `src/pages/operations/**`, `src/api/money.ts`, `src/api/queries/{owners,settlements}.ts`, `src/i18n/ru/money.ts`, `src/routes/{owners,money}.tsx`, `e2e/golden/{owner,payout,operations,merge,duplicate-dialog}.spec.ts`
- Status: accepted

## D-148: The reference pages with input: pseudonyms and duplicates, documents, splits, participations, labels, publishing, reports (step 3.8)
- Date: 2026-11-03
- Question: step 3.8 of `18_frontend_plan.md`: the pages P11–P19 of `17` §3 on the endpoints of D-143 and the existing lists, reusing the action dialogs of steps 3.5 and 3.6.
- Decision:
  - **P11 Псевдонимы** (`/artists?q&status`, filtered on the client: `GET /artists` has no server filters; `/artists/duplicates` on `GET /artists/duplicates`: the groups with their artists, owners, split rows and participations, the candidate ISRCs without a split; assign owner, create a split for a candidate with the artist preselected, create artist with the duplicate dialog), **P12 Псевдоним** (`/artists/:id`: owner, splits, participations).
  - **P13 Документы** (`/documents?status&kind&q&ownerId`: the N12 filters; create, attach, approve; for kind `assumption` the approve button is replaced by «допущение заменяется документом»; without a file it is disabled with «сначала приложите файл»), **P14 Документ** (`/documents/:id` on `GET /documents/:id/uses`: «ждёт этого документа», the splits, rows, participations, rules, contracts and operations it confirms; confirm a split with it, create a contract with it, a link to the assumption wizard).
  - **P15 Сплиты** and **P16 Участия** (`limit` 50/100/500 and `offset` in the URL, the next button disabled from `total`; creating asks for an ISRC first and then opens the catalogue dialogs; change and buy-out load the ISRC's splits first).
  - **P17 Лейблы** (`/labels`, `/labels/:id`: aliases, rules; create label, add alias, set rule (the work dialog, no `validTo`), close rule with the confirmation), **P18 Паблишинг** (writers with resolution, map to an owner, resolve; the terms of a picked owner and the terms form), **P19 Отчёты** (`/sources?q` with the upload forms, `q` over account, month and file name because the grid pages at 50; `/sources/lines` ported from the legacy page: facets, server paging and sorting, the API totals row, the "slow" hint over 4 files, `account&month` from the overview resolved into the file ids; the lines paging counts with `BigInt` on `total.keys`, a count, not money).
  - Specs `e2e/golden/{duplicates,documents,splits,labels,publishing,sources}.spec.ts` (36 tests): S30, S32 and S19 as the state the API reports (their actions belong to `queue.spec.ts`), S37 (close the label rule: month 04 → ours 50), S34 (upload the fixture CSV built in memory with the seeded name; a second upload → 409 `duplicate_period`), the writers and terms (a typed term; the same `validFrom` → 409 `terms_overlap`).
- Checked: typecheck, lint, 30 vitest, `npm run build:client`, `npm run e2e` 130 passed, 1 skipped, 2.7 min.
- Known limits: `/artists` filters on the client (2,134 rows today); the label card's set-rule dialog has no `validTo` (close the rule instead).
- References: `packages/frontend/src/pages/reference/**`, `src/api/reference.ts`, `src/api/queries/{artists,documents,splits,sources}.ts`, `src/i18n/ru/reference.ts`, `src/routes/{catalog,rules,money}.tsx`, `e2e/golden/{duplicates,documents,splits,labels,publishing,sources}.spec.ts`
- Status: accepted

## D-149: Operation coverage guard, the reason and signal action table, the legacy pages removed, the guides refreshed (step 3.9)
- Date: 2026-11-03
- Question: step 3.9 of `18_frontend_plan.md`: stage 6 requires every operation of `operations.ts` to have a screen or a conscious refusal and every C reason and signal an action; the old pages of D-007 / D-062 were still mounted under `/legacy/*`.
- Decision:
  - **`src/api/coverage.ts`**: `OPERATION_COVERAGE` maps every operation key of `operations.ts` (63 today) to a page (P1–P21 or `shell` for `GET /search`) and its route, or to a refusal with its reason; `PAGES_WITHOUT_OPERATIONS` names P3 (the static list) and P4 (the payouts view of P9). `src/routes/paths.ts` is the one list of route patterns the route files build from; `tests/coverage.test.ts` asserts the key sets are identical, every page route exists, every page is covered. Refusals: `GET /health` (a liveness probe), `GET /reconciliation` (superseded by `/overview`, the same rows), `GET /tracks/:isrc/explain` (superseded by `/money` and `/breakdown/:month`, kept for API users). `GET /owners` and `GET /tracks` are still used as lookups (owner names on the reference pages; the track id of an ISRC in the note dialog) and are mapped, not refused.
  - **`src/pages/work/actions/action-table.ts`**: `REASON_ACTIONS` (10 reasons) and `SIGNAL_ACTIONS` (11 lists) name the dialog of each (typed as a key of the dialogs module, so a wrong name does not compile) or mark it information-only (`no_isrc`, `twoRowsOneOwner`); the queue and signals pages read the flag; `tests/actions-table.test.ts`.
  - **Legacy removed**: `src/legacy/**`, the `/legacy/*` route and sidebar link, the eslint exemption, the placeholder page; the search schema comes from `@erp/shared` (`searchResultSchema`, `SEARCH_KINDS`), the 404 fallbacks are gone; `tests/cents.test.ts`; the `top_above_100` status pinned to 422.
  - **Guides**: `packages/frontend/CLAUDE.md` (pages P1–P21, guards, how to run e2e), `docs/agents/01_big_picture.md` §5–§6 (the frontend goal and the pointers to `16`–`19`), `04_recipes.md` (recipe "Add a page"; recipe 2 step 6), `05_doc_drift.md` (README and root `CLAUDE.md` statements about the minimal frontend), root `CLAUDE.md` (layout, `npm run e2e`, the definition of done).
- Checked: typecheck, lint, `REQUIRE_STACK=1 npm test` 402 backend + 43 frontend, `npm run build:client`, `npm run e2e` 129 passed, 1 skipped.
- Known limits: the dialog switches of the queue and signals pages stay inline (the table decides only information-only); `GET /owners` and `GET /tracks` are pulled whole for lookups (a later clean-up could turn them into refusals).
- References: `packages/frontend/src/api/coverage.ts`, `src/routes/paths.ts`, `src/pages/work/actions/action-table.ts`, `tests/{coverage,actions-table,cents}.test.ts`, `packages/frontend/CLAUDE.md`, `docs/agents/{01_big_picture,04_recipes,05_doc_drift}.md`, `CLAUDE.md`
- Status: accepted

## D-150: Stage 5 backend rework: audit summary without free text and no 500 after a committed write (X-Audit: failed), schemas for every operation, duplicate details schema, unfunded signals with the recipient, /releases performance, payableNegative
- Date: 2026-11-03
- Question: the stage 3 reviews (architect findings 2 and 3, product finding 4) and the stage 4 walk (F2, F6) found: free text in the audit summary, a 500 after a committed write, three responses without schemas, unfunded rows that cannot be told apart, a 14.8 s `/releases`, and a circular import.
- Decision:
  - **Audit summary**: `reason` is no longer copied (`api/audit.ts`); only ids, amounts, dates, kinds, statuses and directions stay. **Audit failure**: a failed `recordAction` after the handler has committed is logged (`console.error`) and the original 2xx response is sent with the header `X-Audit: failed` (the operator sees the write happened; no 500, so a retry cannot duplicate an advance). Changes D-140 on this point.
  - **Schemas**: `free(...)` is removed from `operations.ts`. `POST /sources/believe` → `sourceFileSchema` (the route no longer sends `storedPath`, the server path); `POST /publishing/writers/:norm/map` → `ownerSchema`; `POST /contracts` → `createdContractSchema` (contract + `ownerId`). `possibleDuplicateDetailsSchema` (`{ similar: [{ id, name, matchedBy[] }] }`, the shape of both `findSimilarOwners` and `findSimilarArtists`) is exported from `api-responses.ts` for `error.details` of 409 `possible_duplicate`.
  - **Unfunded signals** gain `artistId`, `pseudonym` (the participation recipient; the rule id is a participation id, resolved from the participations list `signalsFrom` already reads) and `fundedFrom`; additive.
  - **Signals cap**: `listQuery.limit` already allows 5000 and `counts` stay full; a test pins 5000 accepted, 5001 refused.
  - **`/releases`**: the 13-15 s were not ClickHouse (`netByUpc` about 1.1 s) but `firstPair` in `releases()`, which scanned and sorted all report pairs for each of the 11 k UPCs. It is now one pass building the smallest `isrc|upc` key per UPC; the response is byte-identical (md5 compared before and after).
  - **Import cycle** `calculation.ts` <-> `queue.ts` broken: `PeriodFilter`, `DateBasis`, `unitInPeriod`, `pubInPeriod` moved to the leaf module `facade/period.ts` (re-exported from `calculation.ts`).
  - **`payableNegative: boolean`** (true when payable < 0) in `balance.total` and in the payables items, computed in `balance.ts`.
- Checked: backend typecheck, `REQUIRE_STACK=1 npm test -w @erp/backend` 406 passed (361+ earlier; new: merge reason out of the summary, `X-Audit: failed` with a stubbed `recordAction`, duplicate details parsed by the shared schema for owners and artists, signals limit 5000/5001, unfunded recipient in G22, payableNegative in three tests), `npm run build:client`. `/releases` on the dev database (`data/real`, API on :3061, LOG_TIMINGS=1): 15.0 s and 13.2 s before, 1.29 / 1.21 / 1.15 s after. Root lint and the frontend typecheck were red only on frontend e2e files being edited in parallel.
- Known limits: `/releases` still spends about 1.1 s in the ClickHouse `GROUP BY` over `units` (acceptable; a projection would need a migration); `listTracks()` still ships 26 k rows.

## D-151: Stage 5 rework of the frontend: the month row key, money sorting as decimal text, one dialog per action, Russian labels and errors, readable grids, the e2e order and the widened guards
- Date: 2026-11-03
- Question: the stage 3 council (`reviews/frontend_stage3_*.md`) and the stage 4 walk on the real data (`reviews/frontend_stage4_walk.md`: 0 mismatches screen = API, 0 dead ends) listed the should-fixes of stage 5 (`18` §4): one real bug (the P6 month row key ignored the sales month, so one ISRC had 205 rows under 29 keys and expanding a row opened its twin), money columns sorted as text, raw ids and English values on Russian screens, truncated grids, a toast that covered the dialog footer, dialogs duplicated across pages, spec files with order dependencies, a lint narrower than D-139 promised.
- Decision:
  - **Frontend**: the month row id is account | UPC | report month | sales month | label string (the hooks `month-<account>-<upc>-<month>` stay); `compareDecimal` in `lib/money.ts` (sign, integer length, digits; no number) sorts every money column, and the P1 drill-down sorts the tracks by B or ours; one `CloseRuleDialog` (the operator picks the rule of the label; preselected when one is in force), one `CreateContractDialog`, one `MergeOwnerDialog` (states the merged owner's earned, waiting and payable from one balance call, OK disabled until it arrives), one `DuplicateDialog` on the shared `possibleDuplicateDetailsSchema` (also when an owner is created from the assign dialog), one `AuditTable` (date `DD.MM.YYYY HH:mm`, a Russian action label per route template, summary as name: value); the approve dialog keeps OK disabled until `GET /documents/:id/uses` answers and shows only the API's «ждёт этого документа»; shortfalls, pending and blocking operations, resolutions, «совпало по» in Russian with pseudonyms, dates and amounts instead of ids; `i18n/ru/errors.ts` maps every API code to Russian (the code stays in `data-code`, the English message in small print); the toast region moved to the top right (it covered the dialog footer); grids: wrapped headers and cells, no column virtualisation (`suppressColumnVirtualisation`), one pager per page, wider money columns, «К выплате» / «К выплате (показано)»; signals fetch `limit=5000` and page on the client; unfunded rows show the participant, the funding and the reason; `payableNegative` from the API; a P14 of an assumption document offers only the wizard; B17 statuses as chips, a Russian file picker; the unused client functions removed; `NoteDialog` keeps `GET /tracks` (the search result has no track id; noted in `coverage.ts`).
  - **E2E**: spec files carry an order prefix (`10-overview` … `31-questions`, then `smoke`, `states`); an acting spec owns the after state and the order is documented in `19` §6 and `packages/frontend/CLAUDE.md`; the dual-branch assertions are gone; the merge-dialog test of the queue is removed (0 skipped); the missing assertions of `19` added (before rows, owner earned, P21 rows, S18 on the owner page, S30 create split, G36, the credit second press); `expectMoneyCalls(page, 1)` on every money page (R2 by machine); the allowed shared mutations are listed in `19` §1; `verify-seed.ts` runs in `start.sh`; `E2E_BASE_URL` runs the specs against a hand-started stand (`E2E_KEYS` names its keys file).
  - **Guards**: eslint bans `.toFixed(`, `BigInt(`, `parseFloat`, `Number.*`, `parseInt`, `Intl.NumberFormat`, unary plus, `Math.*` and the operators `-` `*` `/` on money-named identifiers in `packages/frontend/src`, with overrides for `lib/money.ts`, the paging helper of `pages/reference/shared.tsx` and the line count of `SourceLinesPage.tsx`; `no-floating-promises` for the frontend; the privacy test scans the e2e seed too; react, react-dom, vite, vitest, `@vitejs/plugin-react` and the React types pinned exactly, `zod` declared, testing-library and jsdom removed.
- Checked: typecheck, lint (0 violations with the widened rules), `REQUIRE_STACK=1 npm test` 406 backend + 43 frontend, `npm run build:client`, `npm run e2e` 132 passed, 0 skipped (2.7 min); on the dev data: FRX282403913 shows 205 unique month rows and one expands; the queue rows and the owners and operations grids are not truncated at 1440 px.
- Known limits: the `unfunded` signal has 507 rows under 122 distinct `isrc|month|ruleId` keys on the real data (the rows differ by sales month, which the response does not carry: stage 6 adds `salesMonth`); the two map-writer dialogs and the two attach dialogs stay separate (their fields and toasts differ); `/dev/states` ships in the bundle; a P10 page title above the card is still missing; `compareDecimal`, `monthRowId` and the route labels have no unit test yet (stage 6).
- References: `packages/frontend/src/{lib/money.ts,lib/datetime.ts,components/{AuditTable,DuplicateDialog,DataGrid,ConfirmDialog,toast}.tsx,pages/work/actions/{owner-dialogs,rule-dialogs,dialogs}.tsx,i18n/ru/{errors,audit}.ts}`, `packages/frontend/e2e/**`, `packages/frontend/tests/**`, `eslint.config.js`, `docs/agents/19_golden_screens.md` §1 §6, `docs/agents/reviews/frontend_stage{3_architect,3_product,3_qa,4_walk}.md`
- Status: accepted

## D-155: Advertising expenses: the owner's model (design only, not implemented)
- Date: 2026-11-03
- Question: how are advertising expenses (case M21, MVP item 33 "later") entered and recovered from artists? 804 k of ads were charged in `0to8 Main` and are not in the ERP; the old ERP never charged any because the recoup rule was never stated.
- Decision (the product owner, 2026-11-03, in chat):
  - An ad expense is bound to an **ISRC** (the ISRC gives the artists and their splits) and behaves like an advance.
  - It has a **recovery percent 0–100** (replaces a "recoupable" tick): the share of the expense returned from the artists. The rest (100 − percent) is borne by us and shows in "ours" with a minus sign; at 0 % all of it. Where the track belongs to a sublabel, the unreturned part is **borne by the sublabel**.
  - The base is the **artists' shares**, not the track income before the split: income 100, split 50/50 artist/label, ad 40 at 100 % → artist 10, label 50. Between the artists of one ISRC the expense is divided **in proportion to their split percents; MC pseudonyms do not bear it**, only artists do (the brand and MC share is redistributed to the others).
  - The recovery **scope is the one of an advance**: the whole balance, publishing, or the income of this ISRC. An unrecovered rest carries over to later income.
  - The percent can be changed at any time, also back in time: the ERP recalculates from scratch, the payable changes and may go negative (D-094 keeps a negative payable). Nothing is protected from this, also what is already paid.
  - **No document** is needed for an ad expense. This departs from the rule that a deduction counts only with an approved basis document (D-048, D-072); the guard is the audit of who changed the percent and when (`X-Operator`, D-140).
  - An ISRC without a split: the expense waits in a signal and is ours until a split appears; the recalculation then moves it to the artists. An unconfirmed artist: the debt is counted, the payout stays blocked as usual.
  - **Input:** every arrival of data is a new record (immutable); aggregation per ISRC and month happens at calculation time.
  - Open for later: how posts get their ISRC and the upload interface (the owner will send dashboard examples), and the move of the old Main data (decided when the dashboard exists).
- Checked: nothing implemented; the facts and the questions are in `docs/рекламные_расходы_вопросы_владельцу.md`.
- References: `docs/рекламные_расходы_вопросы_владельцу.md`, `docs/agents/09_settlement_cases.md` (M21, M44)
- Status: accepted (design only)

## D-156: Advertising expenses implemented: the ads module, the allocation in calculate(), the debt in the balance, the Main history
- Date: 2026-11-03
- Question: implement the owner's model of D-155 and load the advertising history of `0to8 Main` (ads there are bound to a nickname only; the column "ОБНУЛИТЬ ADS расход" says recoupable or not).
- Decision:
  - **Module `ads`** (PostgreSQL schema `ads`, `migrations/postgres/0012_ads.sql`): one immutable record per arrival of data (ISRC, month = first day, amount in cents > 0, recovery percent 0–100 with 2 decimals, scope `any` | `publishing` | `isrc`, optional pseudonym, external ref unique: a repeated one is skipped, source, migration flag, comment). Only the percent, the scope and the deactivation change. No document is needed (D-155); the audit records the operator, and the audit summary now keeps `recoveryPercent` and `scope`, a batch records the created ids.
  - **Allocation** (`src/facade/ads.ts`, pure, run inside `calculate()` for every active record, nothing stored): the split of the ISRC on the first day of the month; rows of MC pseudonyms (`MC␠` / `MC_` / `MC-` at the start, the rule of the report-processor pipeline) and rows whose owner is of kind `label` bear nothing, their weight goes to the other artists; parts pro rata to the split percents (`distribute`, 12 decimals), recovered = `pct(part, percent)`, unrecovered = amount − Σ recovered (by subtraction). A record with a pseudonym is `pre_allocated`: the whole amount on it. The unrecovered part goes to the recipient pseudonym of the sublabel rule in force (the ISRC's label string of that month, else the nearest earlier, else the earliest later Believe line) or to us. Status `no_split` (no split that month): the whole expense is ours; `no_artist`: only MC and label rows.
  - **Balance:** every recovered part (and a sublabel's unrecovered part) is a debt position of the owner (`calc.adPositions`), recouped by `recoup()` together with the advances and deductions (largest first, capped by the net income in scope, no date limit, so a percent changed back in time recalculates and an already paid income makes payable negative). Scope: `any`, `publishing`, `isrc` = master income of that ISRC; a sublabel's part always `any`. The balance shows `ads[]` and `total.adsRecouped` (part of `recouped`); the payout snapshot keeps `ads`.
  - **API:** `GET /ads`, `POST /ads` (batch `{ items }`), `GET /ads/summary` (per month and label of record: total, returned from artists, borne by sublabels, borne by us, without split), `PATCH /ads/:id`, `POST /ads/:id/deactivate`. Page P22 `/ads` and the section «Реклама» of the owner balance.
  - **Main history** (`scripts/mvp/extract-main-ads.py` read-only → `data/mvp-load/ads/main_ads_mapping.json`; loader step `ads`): one record per INCOME/EXPENSE row with ads (2,708 rows, gross 1,023,759.71 = charged 803,890.72 + flagged 219,868.99; the flag has no exception: 1 → charged 0 → recovery 0 %, otherwise charged = gross → 100 %); month = `fix_date_reports` (the statement that charged it); the row's pseudonym as the Main loader resolved it; scope `any` (Main took it off the statement total). The ISRC: the PROMO post title of the spend month or the one before (until 2025-07, when PROMO still had titles; 335 rows, 103,960.23), else the top Believe net ISRC of the pseudonym in the spend month or the report month (2,308 rows, 906,892.41), else the top all-time (10), else none (33 rows, 6,972.44). The one Echo8-sheet ad row duplicates an INCOME/EXPENSE row and is left out. Not loaded: 22 rows of unmatched or ambiguous nicknames (4,246.63). Loaded 2,686 records, 1,019,513.08; a second run created nothing.
  - `calculate()` requires the ads module (scripts that build their own `Modules` pass it), and test databases empty the `ads` schema.
- Checked: `tests/ads.test.ts` (13: the D-155 example 100 / 50 / 40 → 10, MC and label rows, 50 %, sublabel, no split, no artist, pre-allocated with scope isrc, exactness over three parts, summary; on the stack: percent change back in time, cap by income, scope isrc, deactivation, sublabel debt, skipped external ref, refusals); the API contract scenario calls the five operations. Real data (`scripts/mvp/ads-effect.ts`): payables before 293 positive 1,396,191.82 / 153 negative −1,413,899.70; after 138 / 1,131,871.01 and 176 / −1,603,912.45; ads returned from artists 799,644.09, borne by sublabels 596.78, by us 219,272.21. `compare-main.ts` now subtracts the charged Main ads from the Main balance: ERP recouped 458,374.80 of the 791,174.51 charged to matched owners; 332,799.71 waits for income (Main took it at once). Counted as if taken at once, 803 of 992 owners differ by more than 1.00 (Σ|diff| 2,143,338.06), as before the ads (802, 2,140,428.23): the history adds no new difference.
- Open for the owner: whether an ad debt above the income may make payable negative at once (Main) or waits for income like an advance (now); the ISRC of the 2025-08 … 2026 Main rows is the top earner of the month, not the promoted track (the promoted track is the top earner in about half of the checked cases); the duplicate pseudonyms of 8 nicknames without an ISRC.
- References: `docs/реклама_тз.md`, `docs/рекламные_расходы_вопросы_владельцу.md`, `packages/backend/src/modules/ads/`, `packages/backend/src/facade/{ads,ads-api,balance,calculation}.ts`, `packages/backend/scripts/mvp/{extract-main-ads.py,steps-ads.ts,ads-effect.ts,compare-main.ts}`, `packages/frontend/src/pages/ads/`
- Status: accepted

## D-157: Advertising is taken off payable at once and in full, like a payout (replaces the recoup part of D-156)
- Date: 2026-11-04
- Question: D-156 recouped an ad debt like an advance (from income in scope, capped by it), so 332,799.71 of the Main history waited for income while Main had taken it off the balance at once. Which one?
- Decision (the product owner, 2026-11-04, in chat: «да мы вычитаем у артиста сразу»): every charged amount of an expense (an artist's recovered part, a sublabel's unrecovered part) is subtracted from payable at once and in full, whatever the income: payable = earned − recouped − paid − ads charged + returns + credits; a negative payable is kept and shown as 0 (D-050). Ads no longer take part in `recoup()` and no longer compete with advances. The balance shows `total.adsCharged` and `ads[]` (ISRC, month, pseudonym, role, amount). The scope stays on the record but no longer decides anything about money (to be removed or given a meaning by the owner). The allocation (who bears what) is unchanged.
- Checked: `tests/ads.test.ts` (ad above the income: payable 60 − 140 = −80, shown 0; an ISRC with less income than the ad is charged in full; sublabel 7 and artist 3 charged). Real data: ads charged to matched owners 791,174.51 in ERP = 791,174.51 in Main; `compare-main.ts`: 802 of 992 owners differ by more than 1.00, Σ|diff| 2,140,428.23, exactly as before the ads were loaded. Payables: 138 positive 1,131,871.01 (unchanged), 662 negative −1,946,447.92 (651 below −10, 188 below −1,000 holding −1,798,583.26). These minuses are the starting-balance difference of the owner file item 1.1 (ERP earned B + C is 858,652 below Main income for the matched owners), no longer hidden by unrecouped ads.
- References: `packages/backend/src/facade/balance.ts`, `packages/shared/src/api-responses.ts`, `packages/frontend/src/pages/owners/BalanceTab.tsx`, `packages/backend/scripts/mvp/{compare-main,ads-effect}.ts`
- Status: accepted

- Decision (the product owner, 2026-11-04, in chat: «Оставить как есть»): the field stays in the record, the API and the page as it is; it is stored and shown and does not change payable. No code change.
