## D-160: The KPI cards of the overview: four sums of the period, from the backend, by source and account
- Date: 2026-11-04
- Question: the operator wanted the four numbers of the period at the top of P1 (received, confirmed to others, waiting, ours) in the style of the finance panel of the old project (four tiles: tinted icon, label with a tooltip, large amount, a click opens more). R1 says the frontend computes nothing and `GET /overview` sent only rows.
- Decision:
  - **`GET /overview` gains `totals`** (`overviewTotals()` in `facade/reports.ts`, pure over the reconciliation rows of the period): `all`, `bySource.believe` / `bySource.b17` and `byAccount[]` (source, account), each the six sums `received` (Σ A), `notOurs` (Σ B), `waiting` (Σ C), `ourShare`, `ourShareNoSplit`, `discrepancy`. The existing fields are unchanged. The sums are the backend's: the page never adds (R1 holds, the eslint block stays as it is).
  - **Four cards over the table** (`pages/work/OverviewKpis.tsx`, `components/KpiCard.tsx`): «Получено» (A, green), «Подтверждено артистам» (B, blue), «Ждёт решения» (C, amber: not confirmed by a document, by the reasons of the queue), «Остаётся лейблу» (ours, green, red when negative; its tooltip says it is the remainder of the label's share before our own expenses, not a profit, and the card shows «из них с треков без сплита» = Σ `ourShareNoSplit`). Under every amount the identity check «A = B + C + наша: расхождение» with Σ `discrepancy`, red when it is not 0. Skeletons while the query is pending, muted zeros for an empty period, both themes through the HeroUI tokens.
  - **The account filter of the table selects the cards** with the same substring rule: no filter shows `totals.all`, one matching account its `byAccount` entry; several matching accounts cannot be summed on the client, so the cards show «—» with a hint and the breakdown lists each. The cards filter nothing (there are no categories).
  - **A click (or the chevron) expands one card** into the same metric by source (Believe (мастер) with its accounts indented, then B17 (паблишинг)); under a filter only the matching accounts. No new request: everything is the one money call (R2).
  - Hooks: `overview-kpis`, `kpi-received` / `kpi-confirmed` / `kpi-waiting` / `kpi-ours` (`data-active`), `<id>-toggle`, `<id>-check` (`data-ok`), `<id>-hint`, `<id>-none`, `<id>-skeleton`, `kpi-ours-nosplit`, `kpi-breakdown` (`data-metric`), `kpi-breakdown-believe` / `-b17` / `-account-<account>`.
- Checked: `tests/overview-totals.test.ts` (backend, pure: exact sums over all, per source, per account; a non-zero discrepancy carried; zeros for no rows), the contract scenario asserts `totals` against the one row of its period, `packages/frontend/tests/overview-kpi.test.ts` (the pick: all, one account, ambiguous, none; the breakdown lines), `e2e/golden/10-overview.spec.ts` (the headline equals the API total, the check is 0, S10/2's second account 57 / 25 / 7 / 25 by the filter, the breakdown with and without the filter, one money call, skeleton and muted zeros in the mocked states), the page in the browser in both themes.
- In passing: the SPA fallback of `api/app.ts` sends `index.html` with `root: dist` instead of an absolute path, because `send` refuses an absolute path that has a dot segment (a checkout under `.claude/worktrees/` answered 500 «Not Found» on every deep route and the e2e could not run there).
- References: `packages/backend/src/facade/reports.ts`, `packages/shared/src/api-responses.ts` (`kpiSumsSchema`, `Overview`), `packages/frontend/src/pages/work/{OverviewKpis.tsx,overview-kpi.ts}`, `packages/frontend/src/components/KpiCard.tsx`, `packages/frontend/src/i18n/ru/work.ts` (`overview.kpi`), `packages/backend/src/api/app.ts`
- Status: accepted

## D-161: The unit scope of the facade: a method reads only the Believe units its answer can depend on
- Date: 2026-11-05
- Question: every money endpoint ran `calculate()` over all 721 975 units of the real data (7–11 s per call on the owner's machine), including the pages that show one track, one release, one account and month, one period of the C queue or the advertising records. The product owner asked to limit it at the facade level, `calculate()` untouched, no cache, and to freeze the current answers first and compare them line by line.
- Decision:
  - **`calculate()` is unchanged.** The facade narrows what it reads: `scoped(modules, scope)` (`facade/scope.ts`) is the same modules with a sources service whose `snapshot()` is narrowed by a `SnapshotFilter` (`isrcs`, `upcs`, `accounts`, `from` / `to` / `basis`; an empty list = no unit). The sources module pushes the filter into the ClickHouse read of the `units` view (`isrc IN`, `upc IN`, the month range) and takes the files of the accounts; the list of files in force stays complete.
  - **Why it is exact:** the allocation of a unit depends on the unit and the reference data alone (the layer context is built from PostgreSQL: splits, participations, labels, documents, owners), so the answer of a method that uses a subset of units is byte for byte the full answer restricted to that subset. The scope is used only where every number of the answer is such a restriction: `trackMoney` and `explainTrack` (one ISRC), `release` (one UPC), `trackSummary` with an account and/or a month, `queue(reason, period)` (the groups hold only the items of the period), `listAds` / `getAd` / `adsSummary` (the ISRCs of the records, all months, because the label of an earlier month may decide the bearer; a record on a pseudonym alone needs no unit).
  - **Not scoped, on purpose:** owner balances and payables (D-012: the payout snapshot is the full calculation; the recoup needs every income of the owner), the overview and the reconciliation (`negativeRemainders` is a count over all units, and the signal counts of the overview are over everything), the signals, the document uses, the queue without a period, the track summary without filters.
  - `FACADE_SCOPE=off` turns every scope off (the A/B switch of the comparison below and of the guard test).
  - In passing: the `breakdown` query of ClickHouse orders equal nets of a month in no fixed order, so two identical runs could differ in the row order of `/tracks/:isrc/breakdown/:month` and of `explain`; the `ORDER BY` now ends with the key columns.
- Checked: `tests/scope.test.ts` (on the stack: every scoped method gives the same JSON with the scope on and off, reads exactly one snapshot, and the snapshot carries the expected filter and unit count; the full methods read every unit; a pseudonym-only ad reads none). Real data (`data/test-tmp/api-dump.sh`, 26 answers of the money endpoints frozen before the change and dumped again after it, keys sorted, `calculatedAt` removed, then `api-compare.sh` line by line): every answer equal except the row order of the breakdown (the tie above, fixed afterwards). Times on the owner's machine, before → after: track money 8.5 → 3.0 s, explain 7.9 → 1.9 s, track summary of a month 8.8 → 3.8 s, of an account and month 9.3 → 3.4 s, the C queue with a period 8.5 → 4.9 s (writer_unmapped by sales months 7.6 → 4.5 s), the ads list 6.9 → 2.1 s, an ad card 6.9 → 1.6 s, the ads summary 6.8 → 2.1 s; the unscoped calls unchanged within noise (overview 10.8 → 12.4 s, balances 7.2 → 7.0 s). What remains of a scoped call is the reference snapshot of PostgreSQL and the publishing allocation over all B17 lines: the next candidate.
- References: `packages/backend/src/facade/scope.ts`, `facade/{aggregates,reports,ads-api}.ts`, `modules/sources/{service,columnar}.ts` (`SnapshotFilter`, `UnitsFilter`), `tests/scope.test.ts`, `docs/agents/03_decision_index.md`
- Status: accepted

## D-162: The reference reads follow the unit scope; P5 «Треки» asks the money of the rows on the screen
- Date: 2026-11-05
- Question: after D-161 a scoped call still took about 3 s, of which 2.5 s were reads the scope did not narrow: the B17 statement lines (1.1 s, 8 660 lines, read for answers that never use them), the pair names of ClickHouse (1.2 s over every row) and the split index (0.2 s, every split). The product owner asked to filter the reference reads at the point of reading too, to think through every screen (a page that lists tracks should ask the money of the rows it shows), and above all to prove on the frozen full summary that nothing is lost.
- Decision:
  - **The scope narrows the reference reads** (`facade/scope.ts`): with `isrcs` the split index (`splits.index({ isrcs })`: splits, shares, participations of those ISRCs) and the known pairs (`owners.knownPairs({ isrcs })`) are read for those ISRCs only; `publishingLines: false` makes `publishing.snapshotForCalculation({ lines: false })` skip the statement lines (writers and terms are still read), for the answers that use the master side only: track money, explain, release, track summary, the ads list, card and summary. The queue, the balances, payables, overview, reconciliation and signals keep reading everything. The services replaced are prototypes of the originals (`Object.create`), so the test proxy and every other method stay as they are. The list reads beside a calculation take the same filters: `owners.listTracks({ isrcs })`, `splits.listSplits({ isrcs })`, `splits.listParticipations({ isrcs })`, `sources.pairAttributes({ isrcs | upcs })` (`isrc IN` / `upc IN` in ClickHouse).
  - **`GET /tracks/summary?isrcs=A,B,…`** (at most 500, the largest grid page): the summary of those ISRCs from a calculation of their units, splits and names alone; `isrcs` is also a filter of the answer (the same rows with the scope off). **`GET /tracks/catalog`** (new): every ISRC that earns in the loaded files or is registered, with UPCs, names, `hasSplit`, `hasParticipations`, `registered`; no money, no calculation. Its ISRC set is the one of `trackSummary()`.
  - **P5 in two phases** (`pages/catalog/TracksPage.tsx`): without an account or month the rows come from the catalog, and the money of the rows on the screen (page, sort, filter; `DataGrid` `onVisibleRowsChanged`, settled 150 ms) from one `GET /tracks/summary?isrcs=` per visible page, cached by its ISRC list; a cell shows «…» (`money-pending`) until its money arrives; the money columns do not sort by pages; «Посчитать все» (`all=1`) or an account / month (the P1 drill-down) is one `GET /tracks/summary` of every row as before, sortable. R2 is read as «one money call per visible page» here; a load still makes one.
  - **Every screen, reviewed:** P1 overview, P2 queue summary, P21 signals, P9 owners / payouts, P10 owner (balance, pseudonyms and tracks), P13 / P14 document uses: full calculations (D-012; counts and the recoup over everything; the tracks of an owner's pseudonyms come from its balance, a scoped balance would need the owner's whole reach: the open question of D-122). P2 `/queue/:reason` with a period, P5 with an account / month or by pages, P6 track, P8 release, P22 advertising: scoped. P7 releases, P11 / P12 pseudonyms, P15 – P20: no calculation.
- Checked: `tests/scope.test.ts` (the summary of an ISRC list equals the full summary restricted to it, the catalog has every ISRC of the summary with the same reference fields, the master-only methods skip the B17 lines and narrow the split index, the full ones read everything, 501 ISRCs refused); the contract scenario calls `/tracks/catalog` and the summary by ISRCs; `e2e/golden/12-track.spec.ts` (P5: the first call carries `isrcs=` of the visible row, a second visible row gets its money, «Посчитать все» and back). **Real data, line by line against the frozen baseline of D-161** (`data/test-tmp/api-check-isrcs.sh`): the catalog has exactly the 25 854 ISRCs of the full summary with equal reference fields; the summary of the first, a middle and the last page of 50 and of 500 ISRCs at once equals the frozen full summary restricted to them (0 differing lines each); the 26 frozen answers are all equal (the breakdown differs in row order only: D-161). Times: track money 8.5 → 0.23 s, explain 7.9 → 0.49 s, a page of 50 tracks 0.2–0.3 s, 500 at once 0.33 s, the ads list 6.9 → 0.7 s, an ad card 0.14 s, the summary of an account and month 9.3 → 2.3 s (its reference reads are not by ISRC), the catalog 1.5–2 s on an idle machine (pair names over every row: a projection is the next candidate); the full calls unchanged.
- References: `packages/backend/src/facade/{scope,aggregates,reports,ads-api}.ts`, `modules/{splits,owners,publishing,sources}/`, `packages/shared/src/{api-schemas,api-responses}.ts`, `packages/backend/src/api/{routes/aggregates,operations}.ts`, `packages/frontend/src/pages/catalog/TracksPage.tsx`, `packages/frontend/src/components/grid/DataGrid.tsx`, `packages/frontend/src/api/queries/tracks.ts`, `tests/scope.test.ts`
- Status: accepted

## D-163: The reach of an owner: the owner balance and the owners list from the units that can carry the owner's money
- Date: 2026-11-05
- Question: the owner screens (P9 Владельцы, P10 Владелец) were left full calculations by D-161 and D-162 because the balance recoups over every income of the owner and the payout snapshot is the full calculation (D-012, the open question of D-122). The product owner asked to think through every screen («получить список треков артиста из овнера»), then to treat P9 like P5, and above all to prove on the frozen data that nothing is lost.
- Decision:
  - **The reach** (`facade/owner-scope.ts`, `ownerReach` / `ownersReach`): every ISRC whose units can carry an allocation to one of the owner's pseudonyms or an advertising position of the owner, built from the reference data without a calculation: the ISRCs of every split row and participation of the owner's pseudonyms (any period, merged pseudonyms by every id they had), the ISRCs whose label string normalises to an alias of a sublabel whose rule (any period) names one of them as recipient (`sources.isrcsOfLabelAliases`: the distinct label strings of the loaded files matched in JS by `normalizeLabelString`, then `isrc IN` over the `units` view), and the ISRCs of the active advertising records on one of them. The publishing side stays whole (the B17 lines are few and the balance needs them).
  - **Why it is exact:** `ownerBalance()` reads only the units where the owner has an allocation (the same loop the payables index uses), so a calculation over any superset of those units gives the very same balance; the reach is such a superset, and `tests/scope.test.ts` pins that it is (a pseudonym with a split, one with an unconfirmed split, one that is only the recipient of a sublabel rule).
  - **`GET /owners/:id/balance` stays the full calculation** (D-012 unchanged): the facade gains `ownerBalanceByReach` and `ownerReach`, used by the check below; whether the owner page should read by reach is still the product owner's call (D-122), now with the evidence.
  - **`GET /owners/payables?ids=a,b,…`** (at most 200: UUIDs are long and a request line must stay under Node's 16 KiB header limit, a 431 otherwise): the payables of those owners from one calculation of the units of their union reach, every other field as before; `ids` is also a filter of the answer.
  - **P9 in two phases** (`pages/owners/OwnersPage.tsx`): the rows from `GET /owners` (no money), the money of the rows on the screen from `GET /owners/payables?ids=` (one call per visible page, in chunks of 200 for a page of 500), «…» until it arrives, the money and gate columns not sortable by pages; «Посчитать все» (`all=1`) or the payouts view (it filters and orders every owner by the gate and the amount) is one `GET /owners/payables` of every owner, as before. A period change forgets the money seen under the old one.
- Checked: `tests/scope.test.ts` (the reach of each golden pseudonym, the balance by reach equals the full balance byte for byte and reads the reach's units only, the payables of a list equal the full list restricted to it, 201 ids refused); the contract scenario calls the payables by ids; `e2e/golden/24-owner.spec.ts` waits for the visible-rows call. **Real data** (`packages/backend/scripts/check-owner-scope.ts`: one full calculation, then for every owner the reach, a calculation over it and the balance, compared as JSON with the balance from the full calculation; the facade path checked for the first owners): all 1 151 owners equal, 0 differing; the reach is 0 to 695 ISRCs (median 10); a balance by reach took 1.6 s per owner (1.1 s of it the B17 lines, read whole) against 7–8 s for the full one. `data/test-tmp/api-check-owners.sh` against the frozen payables of D-161: the owners list has exactly the 1 151 owners of the payables; the payables of the first, a middle and the last page of 50 and of a page of 200 equal the frozen payables restricted to them (0 differing lines each). Times on an idle machine: a page of 50 owners 2.5 s and of 200 owners 3.6 s against 9.5 s for the full payables (one page of 50 in the middle of the list 8.8 s: an owner with a wide reach), the owners list 0.03 s. In passing: `ownersReach` reads every split and participation once and filters in memory, because `listSplits({ artistId })` loads all splits per call (a page of 50 owners took 8 s that way).
- References: `packages/backend/src/facade/{owner-scope,context,reports,aggregates}.ts`, `modules/sources/{service,columnar}.ts` (`labelStrings`, `isrcsOfLabelStrings`), `packages/shared/src/api-schemas.ts` (`payablesQuery.ids`), `packages/frontend/src/pages/owners/OwnersPage.tsx`, `packages/frontend/src/api/queries/owners.ts`, `packages/backend/scripts/check-owner-scope.ts`, `tests/scope.test.ts`
- Status: accepted

## D-164: The owner balance and the payout snapshot go by reach; the facade has one path; the B17 lines are pre-grouped
- Date: 2026-11-05
- Question: D-163 proved on every real owner that a calculation over the owner's reach gives the full balance, and left the balance route and the payout on the full calculation for the product owner to decide. His decision (relayed by the architecture session, 2026-11-05): the owner page balance and the payout snapshot go by reach («снимок выплаты = полный расчёт — это понятно, но мы же работаем в рамках одного владельца, другие вообще никак не влияют на его выплату»), and the `FACADE_SCOPE` switch goes («что за FACADE_SCOPE, если это переход между легаси и новым, то не нужен»). Then the next cost: of the 1.7 s of a reach balance, 1.3 s was the read of the B17 lines.
- Decision:
  - **`GET /owners/:id/balance` and `createPayout` calculate over the owner's reach** (`balanceWithFiles` always calls `ownerReach` and `scoped(modules, { isrcs })`; `ownerBalanceByReach` is folded into `ownerBalance`). D-012 is refined, not reversed: the payout snapshot is still the very calculation the payout used, the file list it records still comes from the registry and is complete (every loaded Believe file), and the balance is the same number because `ownerBalance()` reads only the units where the owner has an allocation and the reach is a superset of them (1 151 real owners equal, D-163). The contract and the OpenAPI document do not change shape.
  - **One path:** `scoped()` always narrows; there is no environment switch in the facade. The guard tests obtain the full answer by patching the test's own reads to ignore every filter (`withFullReads` in `tests/scope.test.ts`); `scripts/check-owner-scope.ts` compares the facade path with a full calculation made through the internal path.
  - **The B17 lines are pre-grouped** by the calculation key (period, payee, songwriters, work, operation type) in the materialized view `publishing.pub_lines_by_key` (`migrations/postgres/0013_pub_lines_by_key.sql`), refreshed inside the load transaction of every statement (`refreshLinesByKey` after `insertLines`); `aggregatedLines()` reads it. Derived data, like the `units` projection of ClickHouse (D-122): the raw lines are untouched and the view is rebuilt from them. Measured: the grouping of 1 162 586 raw lines into 8 660 groups took 1.31 s of PostgreSQL time on every calculation; the read of the view takes milliseconds.
  - **Things that will bite you:** a new way for money to reach a pseudonym (a new layer, rule kind or record kind) must extend `ownerReach`, with `tests/scope.test.ts` pinning balance-by-reach = full and `scripts/check-owner-scope.ts` run on the real data (root `CLAUDE.md`, `facade/CLAUDE.md`).
- Checked: `tests/scope.test.ts` (the balance route reads the reach's units only and equals the full balance; a payout made over the reach records `amountCents`, `total`, `publishing`, `positions` and the per-artist rows of the full balance, and every loaded file; the scoped methods equal the full ones without any switch), the contract scenario, `REQUIRE_STACK=1 npm test`, the real-data checks of D-163 rerun after the change (the 26 frozen answers equal (the breakdown differs in row order only, D-161), the three frozen balances among them now from the reach route; the owners list and the payables pages of 50 and 200 owners equal the frozen payables; the catalog and the track pages equal the frozen summary; `check-owner-scope.ts` on the first 100 owners: 100 equal). Times on an idle machine after the change: the balance of the measured owner (1 pseudonym, 0 ISRCs in reach) 0.46–0.51 s from 1.7–2.1 s, of another owner 0.55 s from 8.1 s; a page of 50 owners 1.5 s from 2.5 s; a track 0.21 s; the full calls (overview 9.9 s, a queue with a period 4.0 s) unchanged, since their B17 read is now the grouped view too but their units are everything.
- References: `packages/backend/src/facade/{context,reports,scope,owner-scope}.ts`, `modules/publishing/{repository,service}.ts`, `migrations/postgres/0013_pub_lines_by_key.sql`, `tests/scope.test.ts`, `scripts/check-owner-scope.ts`, `CLAUDE.md`, `packages/backend/src/facade/CLAUDE.md`
- Status: accepted (the product owner's decision, 2026-11-05)

## D-165: The page of a pseudonym (P12): the card and the money of one pseudonym from its reach
- Date: 2026-11-05
- Question: The owner page (P10) shows the money of an owner with a row per pseudonym, but there was no place that shows everything about one pseudonym: its owner, the ids merged into it, its tracks, its split rows and participations with their periods and documents, the sublabel rules naming it as recipient, its advances and the advertising charged to it, and its money (earned, waiting and why, recouped, charged, paid). P12 `/artists/:id` was a thin reference card (pseudonym, owner, split rows). The frontend computes nothing (R1), so the money had to come from the backend, and as fast as the owner balance (D-164: by reach, under 1 s on the real data).
- Decision:
  - **`GET /artists/:id`** answers the card of the survivor for any id the pseudonym had (`artistIndex`, D-131; `merged: true` and `previousIds` when the asked id was merged): the artist, its owner (nullable), the merges into it, its tracks (the registry links and the tracks of the ISRCs of its rows and participations), the contracts of the owner naming it, its split rows and participations by every id (sorted by ISRC, period, id), the sublabel rules where it is the recipient (with the label name), and the documents all of these cite. One `db.snapshot`, no money (`artistDetailSchema`; the old answer was a subset of it, so the other consumers are untouched).
  - **`GET /artists/:id/balance`** (`artistBalanceSchema`, the period filters of the owner balance) is the money of one pseudonym: `artistReach` (the reach of D-163 generalised to a list of artist ids: the splits and participations of every id of the pseudonym, the label strings of the sublabels whose rule names it, its ads), `calculate(scoped(modules, { isrcs }))`, then `artistBalance()` (`balance.ts`): with an owner it runs `ownerBalance()` of the owner over that calculation and takes **the artist row verbatim** (earned, waiting, recouped, unrecouped, recoupedFromMaster, the tracks), the owner's positions, ads and pending operations filtered to the pseudonym, plus per period received, earned, waiting with its C reasons and by label, and `paid` = the counted payouts carrying this pseudonym. Without an owner the row is built from the pseudonym's allocations alone (everything waits `artist_without_owner`). The row of the reach calculation is the row of the full one because the row reads only the units where the pseudonym has an allocation, master income recoups only the positions of the same pseudonym (`layers/recoup.ts`), and the owner's publishing (which can recoup any of its pseudonyms) is read whole with all the owner's operations. **The payout stays the owner's**: no payout button, no snapshot on P12 (D-051, D-164); the header links to P10.
  - **P12** (`pages/artists/ArtistPage.tsx`, D-139 page list): one card call and one money call shared by the tabs Деньги (`balance-<field>`, `waiting-<reason>`, `by-label-<label>`, `balance-track-<isrc>`, `position-<opId>`, `ad-position-<adId>-<role>`, `pending-<opId>`), Сплиты и участия (tracks, split rows with every pseudonym linked, participations, sublabel rules, merges), Документы (read-only), Реклама (`AdsOfEntity`, its own call on its tab), Операции (`GET /settlements/operations?artistId=`), История (`GET /audit?entityId=`). Pseudonyms are links to P12 wherever they are shown as a name: the artist rows of P10, the shares column of P15 and of the split rows of P14.
  - **Things that will bite you:** a new way for money to reach a pseudonym must extend `reachOf` in `owner-scope.ts` (it serves `ownerReach`, `ownersReach` and `artistReach` alike) and `tests/scope.test.ts` must pin artist-by-reach = the owner's full row for it; `scripts/check-artist-scope.ts` proves it on the real data.
- Checked: `tests/scope.test.ts` (per pseudonym: the reach read has the expected units only, `artistBalance` equals the same method over full reads and its row equals the row of the owner's full `ownerBalance`; the golden cases: a split with a participation off the top (A: 135 of 300, the advance of 30 recouped, the ad of 40 charged, the April period 90 of 200), an unconfirmed split (B: 15 waiting `split_unconfirmed`), a sublabel-rule recipient without a split (R: 10 waiting `assumption`, R1), a participant (P: 30), a second pseudonym of the same owner apart (A2: 16) and merged into A (the old id answers for A with 151 = 135 + 16, its card lists both ids, every split, the contract), a pseudonym without owner (2 waiting `artist_without_owner`), unknown ids refused), the contract scenario (`GET /artists/:id` and `/balance`), `REQUIRE_STACK=1 npm test` (429 backend, 93 frontend), `npm run build:client`, `npm run e2e` (`33-artist.spec.ts`: S24/1 and S23 read, S24/2 acts with an expense of 10; 22-duplicates keeps its `artist-header` hook). Times on an idle machine against the real data (`LOG_TIMINGS=1`): a median pseudonym (8 ISRCs in reach) balance 0.49–0.53 s (reach 0.18 s, calculation 0.25 s), the two widest reaches (695 and 576 ISRCs, 24 667 units) 0.85–0.96 s, the card 0.20–0.25 s; `check-artist-scope.ts` on the first 200 of the 2 134 real pseudonyms (reaches of 3 to 695 ISRCs, median 58): 200 equal from the reach and from the full calculation, every row equal to the owner's full row, 0 differing, 0.52 s per pseudonym by reach (the full calculation 5.8 s, 721 975 units).
- References: `packages/backend/src/facade/{artist-view,balance,context,owner-scope}.ts`, `api/routes/owners.ts`, `api/operations.ts`, `packages/shared/src/api-responses.ts`, `tests/scope.test.ts`, `tests/api-contract.test.ts`, `scripts/check-artist-scope.ts`, `packages/frontend/src/pages/artists/`, `api/queries/artists.ts`, `api/coverage.ts`, `e2e/golden/33-artist.spec.ts`, `docs/agents/19_golden_screens.md` §6, `packages/frontend/CLAUDE.md`, `packages/backend/src/facade/CLAUDE.md`
- Status: accepted

## D-166: The pseudonyms of the catalog: every track, release and list names its registry pseudonyms, each a link to P12
- Date: 2026-11-05
- Question: P5 «Треки», P7 «Релизы», P8 «Релиз» and the header of P6 «Трек» showed an «Артист» column that was the artist string of the Believe pair (`artistName`): a text of the report that may name no pseudonym of the registry, spelled differently from it, or several of them, so it could not link anywhere. The product owner asked to reach the page of a pseudonym (P12, D-165) from the list of tracks, and to have «Артисты» as a column or line on the tracks, the releases and the other catalog lists. Every recipient of money is a pseudonym of the registry (D-025 ff.), so a link is possible only to a registry record, and the frontend matches nothing (R1).
- Decision:
  - **The backend names the pseudonyms of an ISRC** (`facade/track-artists.ts`, pure): the pseudonyms of its split rows and participations (any period) and the registry links of its tracks (`owners.artist_tracks`, read by the new `owners.artistLinks({ trackIds })`), merged ids resolved to the survivor through the artist index (D-131), each once, sorted by pseudonym. The artist string of the report stays as it is (`artistName`, text) and is not matched against the registry: a pseudonym is a record or nothing. A new field `artists: [{ id, pseudonym }]` (`artistRefSchema`) is added to every item of `GET /tracks/catalog` and `GET /tracks/summary`, to `GET /tracks/:isrc/money`, to every item of `GET /releases` and to the header and the track rows of `GET /releases/:upc` (the union over the tracks of the UPC). Additive: no field changed or removed. The reads follow the unit scope (D-162): the summary of an ISRC list reads the links of those tracks only, no calculation is added; `q` of `/releases` matches pseudonyms too.
  - **`ArtistLinks`** (`components/ArtistLinks.tsx`) is the one place that renders them: links to `/artists/:id` separated by commas (`artist-links`, `artist-link-<id>`), and when the registry names none the report string as muted text with the hint «Нет в реестре …» (`artist-unregistered`); no link to a queue or signal, since no signal lists an unmatched artist string. Used by the «Артисты» column of P5 (the grid's column filter and the quick search `q` match the pseudonyms and the report string), of P7, the header and the track rows of P8, the header of P6 (the report string in brackets when it differs). The pseudonyms shown as text until now become links too: the positions and the advertising of P10 Баланс, the contracts of P10 Документы, the parts of the advertising tab of P6 / P12. `twoRowsOneOwner` of P21 carries names only and stays text.
- Checked: `tests/track-artists.test.ts` (rows, participations and links once each, merged ids resolved, a track without ISRC ignored, unknown ids kept with «?», the union), `tests/aggregates.test.ts` (the summary, the catalog, the summary of an ISRC list and the track money name the same pseudonyms; a registry link without a row counts and a merged pseudonym answers as the survivor once; the releases list and the release carry the union and `q` finds a pseudonym), `tests/scope.test.ts` (the catalog equals the summary with the artists), the contract scenario (`/tracks/catalog` names the pseudonym of the split row), `REQUIRE_STACK=1 npm test`, `npm run build:client`, `npm run e2e` (`12-track.spec.ts`: the P5 row of S11 links A-111 and P-111, the link opens P12 and back keeps the row, an ISRC without a pseudonym shows the report string with the hint and no link, the search finds a pseudonym; `11-release.spec.ts`: P7 rows, the P8 header and track rows, the P6 header, the search of P7 by a pseudonym). **Real data** (`main.ts data/real`, the local `erp` store, while the backend tests ran): the catalog 2.0 s (1.5–2 s in D-162), the releases list 1.6 s, a summary page of 50 ISRCs 0.33 s (0.2–0.3 s), a release of 3 tracks 0.47 s; 21 932 of the 25 854 ISRCs name at least one pseudonym (at most 7), none with an unknown id; the release header equals the union of its track rows.
- References: `packages/backend/src/facade/{track-artists,aggregates}.ts`, `modules/owners/{repository,service}.ts`, `packages/shared/src/api-responses.ts`, `packages/frontend/src/components/ArtistLinks.tsx`, `pages/catalog/{TracksPage,ReleasesPage,ReleasePage}.tsx`, `pages/catalog/track/TrackPage.tsx`, `pages/owners/{BalanceTab,DocumentsTab}.tsx`, `pages/ads/AdsOfEntity.tsx`, `i18n/ru/{common,catalog}.ts`, `e2e/golden/{11-release,12-track}.spec.ts`, `packages/frontend/CLAUDE.md`
- Status: accepted

## D-167: P11 «Псевдонимы» as a table: the rows from `GET /artists`, the money of the visible rows from `GET /artists/balances?ids=`
- Date: 2026-11-05
- Question: P11 `/artists` was a thin reference list (pseudonym, owner, status) filtered on the client, while every recipient of money in the system is a pseudonym of the registry (D-025 ff.) and P12 (D-165) shows the money of one of them. The product owner asked for the list of pseudonyms as a full table like P9 «Владельцы» and P5 «Треки»: every pseudonym, in the row a link to its page and to its owner, useful reference columns, and the money, with the speed of D-162 and D-163 on the real data (2 134 pseudonyms).
- Decision:
  - **`GET /artists` is a row per live pseudonym without money** (`facade/artist-view.ts` `artistList`, one `db.snapshot`): the former fields (id, pseudonym, ownerId, status) plus the owner (id, name, status), `previousIds` (how many ids were merged into it), `tracks` (distinct tracks it is on: the registry links `owners.artist_tracks`, the ISRCs of its split rows and participations, by every id it had), `splitRows` and `participations` not closed by today (`valid_to` null or after today), `contracts` (contracts of its owner naming it, from the new `owners.contractLinks()`), `undocumented` (open rows and participations without a document, their money waits). Sorted by pseudonym. Additive over the old item (`artistListItemSchema` extended): `useArtistNames` and P14 are untouched.
  - **`GET /artists/balances?ids=a,b,…`** (`artistBalances`, `artistBalancesQuery` = the period query plus `ids`, at most 200 like `/owners/payables?ids=`) is the money of a list of pseudonyms from **one calculation over the union of their reaches** (`artistsReach` in `owner-scope.ts`: `reachOf` over every id of every asked pseudonym; a merged id stands for its survivor, an unknown id is left out), then the very `artistBalance()` of each (D-165): `earned`, `waiting`, `recouped`, `unrecouped`, `adsCharged`, `paid` of its total, and `remaining` = earned − recouped − adsCharged − paid, what this pseudonym's income left for its owner's payable (the payout stays the owner's, D-051: no gate, no button). Without `ids` every live pseudonym from the full calculation («Посчитать все»). `artistBalance()` takes prebuilt indexes (`artistUnitIndex`: the units where the pseudonym has an allocation; `ownerIndex` for the owner's row), the same loops over fewer units, so 200 rows cost 0.25 s of JS instead of 2.5 s.
  - **P11 is a `DataGrid`** (`pages/reference/artists/ArtistsPage.tsx`) in two phases as P9 (D-163): the rows from `GET /artists`, then the money of the rows on the screen from `GET /artists/balances?ids=` (`onVisibleRowsChanged`, 150 ms, chunks of 200, a page seen before keeps its numbers, `money-pending` «…» until it arrives, the money columns not sortable); `action-compute-all` (`all=1`) is one call of every pseudonym and the money columns sort. Columns: Псевдоним (a link to P12, «слит: N» on a merged pseudonym), Владелец (a link to P10 or «—»), Статус, Треки, Сплиты / участия, Контракты, Без документа (warning colour when > 0), Заработано, Ждёт (C), Удержано, Реклама, Выплачено, К выплате (`remaining`, with the hint that the payout goes to the owner). The quick search `q` matches the pseudonym and the owner's name; the status filter stays; column filters and sorting on the reference columns; «Создать псевдоним» and the link «Дубликаты» as before. The frontend sums nothing (R1): `remaining` comes from the API.
- Checked: `tests/scope.test.ts` (D-165 describe: the balances of a list equal the single `artistBalance` of each row field by field and read only the units of the union reach (I(1) and I(3): 3 units), `remaining` = earned − recouped − adsCharged − paid (A: 135 − 30 − 40 − 0 = 65), without `ids` the full calculation gives the same rows, the list rows count owner, tracks, open rows, participations, contracts and rows without a document for A, A2, B, P, R, a merged id answers for the survivor once with the units of both ids, an unknown id is left out, 201 ids refused), the contract scenario calls `/artists/balances?ids=`, `REQUIRE_STACK=1 npm test`, `npm run typecheck`, `npm run lint`, `npm run build:client`, `npm run e2e` (`34-artists.spec.ts`: S24/1 A-241 row with the owner link, tracks 1, rules 1 / 0, contracts 1, the money of the visible rows in one call (earned 50, remaining 50), S23 NG-231 earned 25 recouped 25 remaining 0, the search by the owner's name, the links open P12 and P10, «Посчитать все» is one call). **Real data** (`main.ts data/real`, the local `erp` store, `LOG_TIMINGS=1`, an idle machine): the list of 2 134 pseudonyms 0.28–0.33 s (0.6 MB); the first page of 50 pseudonyms 0.7–1.0 s (reach 840 ISRCs, 21 916 units), a middle and the last page 0.68–0.69 s, a page of 200 1.7 s (reach 3 542 ISRCs, 80 038 units: `artistsReach` 0.35 s, ClickHouse 0.2 s, the layers 0.46 s, the rows 0.25 s); 12 rows of a middle page compared with `GET /artists/:id/balance`: 0 differing. The targets (list ≤ 0.5 s, a page of 50 ≤ 2 s) are met.
- Consequences: a pseudonym's money on P11 is the row of P12 and of its owner's P10 (one `artistBalance`), so the three screens cannot drift; a new way for money to reach a pseudonym extends `ownerReach` as before (D-163) and `artistsReach` follows. The list is a reference read (no calculation), so `GET /artists` stays cheap whatever the catalog size; the cost of a page is the union reach of its 50 pseudonyms. «К выплате» of a pseudonym is not a payable: the owner's payable also carries returns, credits and the other pseudonyms.
- References: `packages/backend/src/facade/{artist-view,owner-scope,balance}.ts`, `modules/owners/service.ts` (`contractLinks`), `api/routes/owners.ts`, `api/operations.ts`, `packages/shared/src/{api-schemas,api-responses}.ts`, `packages/frontend/src/pages/reference/artists/ArtistsPage.tsx`, `api/queries/artists.ts`, `api/coverage.ts`, `e2e/golden/34-artists.spec.ts`, `tests/scope.test.ts`, `tests/api-contract.test.ts`.
- Status: accepted

## D-168: The label as a book: every sum of the ERP also by own label; operations and advertising carry a label; the balance and the payout per owner × label; a label switch in the admin
- Date: 2026-11-05
- Question: the label has two own labels (0to8, Echo8) and 15 sublabels. The analysis `docs/идеи/разделение_по_лейблу/README.md` (four roles, 2026-11-05) established the facts: the label boundary exists in the data at exactly one place, the label string of a Believe unit (string → alias → own label; a sublabel belongs to an own label through `own_label_id`); no ISRC of the real data is under two own labels; 357 owners and 358 pseudonyms have releases under both labels in the same months; 2,400 payouts and 159 advances carry no label (1,152 payouts of 4.07 M and 68 advances of 293,924 belong to mixed owners); 2,686 advertising records, 33 without an ISRC; the Believe account 1052428 (0to8) carries an «Echo8» line of 222,130 net for 2024-12…2025-07. The product owner's decisions: an operator of a label sees only its artists; settlements are tied to the label; under 0to8 only its income and expenses are visible; the advances of 0to8 never cross with Echo8; a contract that differs is a new owner under the label; reports are loaded under a concrete label; and the formula «take all the data as before, just tie it to the label and get the sums».
- Decision:
  - **The label is the own label; the label of money is the unit's label string** (`label-of-record.ts`, as today): no transfer by ISRC or by account. `calculate()` and the layers are unchanged.
  - **Operations carry a label** (`settlements.operations.label_id`, migration `0014_label_books.sql`; no foreign key across modules, D-121: the facade checks that the id is an own label). A new operation (advance, deduction, credit) needs `labelId` in the body: 400 `label_required` without it; a migrated operation (`migration: true`) may still come without one. A return inherits the label of its payout. `PATCH /settlements/operations/:id` `{ labelId }` lets the operator set the label of an existing operation (audited). The existing operations are marked by `scripts/assign-operation-labels.ts` (dry run by default, `--apply`): when the owner (a payout) or the pseudonym (an advance, deduction, credit) has units under one own label only, that label is set; a return takes the label of its payout. **The undecidable history is 0to8** by the product owner's decision of 2026-11-05 («по выплатам, давай пока укажем что это 0to8 был», relayed by the architecture session): the operations of the mixed owners and pseudonyms (units under both labels) and of those without units go to 0to8, counted apart so he can revisit them; only an owner or pseudonym whose units are exclusively Echo8 gets Echo8. After `--apply` no operation stays without a label; the NULL handling and the signal `operationsWithoutLabel` (P21) stay in the code for an operation a future import could leave without one, and every label is editable per operation through `PATCH /settlements/operations/:id` (audited).
  - **Advertising**: `ads.expenses.label_id` (the same migration) is required for a record without an ISRC (400 `label_required`); a record with an ISRC takes the label of the ISRC's line at the month of the expense as before (`facade/ads.ts`), the stored label being the fallback when no line of the ISRC is mapped. What an expense charges carries that label (`AdChargeView.labelId`).
  - **The balance is per owner × label** (`balance.ts`). `ownerBalance()` partitions every field by the label key (the label name, `'?'` = without a label): `earned` and `waiting` by the unit's label of record (publishing has none: `'?'`); `recouped` by the label of the **income consumed** (a position with a label L is recouped from the master income of L only, `layers/recoup.ts`: `Position.labelId`, `IncomeItem.labelId`; publishing income stays eligible by the scope kind as before; a position without a label is recouped from any income as today and its consumption is attributed to the label of the income it took, so the payable of a label never counts income already recouped); `adsCharged` by the charge's label; `paid`, `returns`, `credits` by the operation's label; `payable_L` = earned_L − recouped_L − adsCharged_L − paid_L + returns_L + credits_L. `byLabel` becomes the book of every label (`labelId`, the eight money fields, `payableShown`, `payableNegative`); **Σ over the books, «without a label» included, equals the total field by field** (the main test; `Case.verify()` pins it on every golden case, `tests/scope.test.ts` on the label world, `scripts/check-label-books.ts` on every real owner). The artist rows, the positions, the charges and the period fields are partitioned the same way. A view under a label (`view: { labelId }`) is the same computation projected to one book: `total` = the book, `byLabel` = that book alone, the artist rows restricted to the label's units, the positions, charges and pending operations of that label, the publishing lines empty. The recoup always runs over everything, so a label view is never calculated apart: the same reach calculation (D-164), one projection. `artistBalance()` has the same books (`byLabel` with `earned`, `waiting`, `recouped`, `adsCharged`, `paid`) and the same view. The full balance of every real owner is unchanged by the migration: no operation has a label before the script, and the script labels only operations whose pseudonym's or owner's income is under one label.
  - **The payout under a label**: `createPayout(ownerId, { labelId })` (400 `label_required` without) pays `payable_L` floored to cents, records `label_id`, and its snapshot is the balance of (owner, L) plus `labelId` and `labelName`; the gate (`payoutGate`) keeps its owner checks: the contract, the blocking payouts of the owner (any label), the threshold on the label's amount; a payout without proof blocks every new payout of the owner, whatever its label, as before (D-051).
  - **Visibility under a label is derived** (`facade/label-scope.ts`): the label book of an own label L = its id, name, the raw label strings of the loaded files that normalise to an alias of L or of its sublabels, the ISRCs of those strings. Under L a pseudonym is visible when it has a split row or a participation on an ISRC of L, is the recipient of a sublabel rule of L, carries an operation or an advertising record under L; an owner is visible when one of its pseudonyms is, or it carries an operation under L. The query parameter `label=<own label id>` on `GET /owners`, `/owners/payables`, `/owners/:id/balance`, `/artists`, `/artists/balances`, `/artists/:id/balance`, `/tracks/catalog`, `/tracks/summary`, `/releases`, `/releases/:upc`, `/queue/:reason`, `/signals`, `/ads`, `/ads/summary`, `/settlements/operations` (404 for an unknown id, 400 `label_not_own` for a sublabel). The unit reads take the new `SnapshotFilter.labelStrings` (`label_name IN` in ClickHouse, over the unit scope of D-161); the reference lists are restricted to the ISRCs of the label; the B17 lines are not read under a label (publishing has no label: «Все» only); the advertising lists are filtered by the label of the result (the read stays by the ISRCs of the records, since the label of an earlier month may decide). `tests/scope.test.ts` pins «under a label = the full answer restricted to the label» for every one of them. The overview (P1) and the reconciliation stay «Все» (a Believe file is reconciled whole) and gain the split by label: `byLabel` on every reconciliation row (A, B, C, ours per label of record, `'?'` for an unmapped string and for publishing) and `totals.byLabel` of the overview.
  - **Believe accounts of a label**: `labels.label_accounts (account PK, label_id)`, `POST /labels/:id/accounts` on an own label (409 `account_taken`), `accounts` on the label item. A loaded file is never refused: the units whose label string leads to another own label than the account's are the signal `labelOutsideAccount` (account, month, label string, own label, net), the known history (Echo8 inside 1052428, 222,130 net).
  - **Transport**: no `X-Label` header. The switch «0to8 / Echo8 / Все» sits in the header beside the operator, is stored like the operator (`localStorage` `erp.label`) and in the URL `?label=`, the URL winning; every money query key includes the label; the client adds `label=` to the reads above and `labelId` to the bodies of operations, payouts and advertising records. The owner and pseudonym pages under L show the money and the operations of L only, the card (documents, pseudonyms) whole; under «Все» the full balance with the table of books. The payout button under «Все» is unavailable with the hint «выберите лейбл».
  - **Not in this step**: the transfer of Echo8 to another person from a date T (its artists are not paid by us after T, the units of Echo8 go to the transferee) is the next step; the label document keeps the optional fields `transferred_at` and `transferee_artist_id` in reserve, without a migration. Authorisation and roles are not done.
  - D-123 («one balance per owner») and D-130 (`byLabel` with B and C only) are refined: one balance per owner × label, the books partition every field. D-051 stands: a plain payout is only possible through the button, now under a label.
- Checked: `tests/scope.test.ts` (the label world: Σ books = total field by field, the view under a label = the book, the reads under a label = the full answers restricted to the label, the payout under a label, the refusals), `tests/golden.test.ts` (G50: one owner under both labels with an advance without a label, an advance of Echo8 and a payout under 0to8; `verify()` pins Σ books = total on every case), the contract scenario, `REQUIRE_STACK=1 npm test`, `npm run build:client`, `npm run e2e`; the real data: `scripts/check-label-books.ts` (every owner: Σ books = total, the view through the facade = the book, the balance with the labels of the operations stripped = the same balance) and `scripts/assign-operation-labels.ts`. **Real data** (`data/real`, the local `erp` store, 2026-11-05): `check-label-books.ts` over all 1,151 owners before the marking: 54 reconciliation rows with Σ books = row, 0 owners differing (484 owners with several books), 7 ms per owner, the view through the facade 0.7–1.0 s; `assign-operation-labels.ts` on the 2,608 active operations without a label: 1,293 by their one label (0to8 1,286, Echo8 7: 1,200 payouts, 79 advances, 14 deductions), then, by the owner's decision, 1,315 to 0to8 as the undecidable history (mixed owners and pseudonyms: 1,152 payouts, 68 advances, 35 deductions; without units: 48 payouts, 12 advances); after `--apply` every operation carries a label (0to8 2,601, Echo8 7) and the check again: 0 differing; the labels change the totals of exactly one owner (an advance of a mixed pseudonym, now 0to8, no longer takes its Echo8 income: payable 3,175.93 → 3,878.35), every other owner is unchanged. The accounts 1052428 → 0to8 and 1055956 → Echo8 registered locally (the signal `labelOutsideAccount` then shows the Echo8 line of 1052428).
- Open (for the product owner): publishing (B17) has no label and sits in the «without a label» book, which no label payout pays; until Q8 of the analysis is answered (B17 = 0to8, or a payout of the «without a label» book) an owner's publishing income cannot be paid by the button.
- References: `packages/backend/migrations/postgres/0014_label_books.sql`, `src/modules/{settlements,ads,labels}/`, `src/modules/sources/{service,columnar}.ts`, `src/facade/{label-scope,balance,payouts,ads,ads-api,reports,aggregates,artist-view,calculation,context,scope}.ts`, `src/facade/layers/recoup.ts`, `src/api/{operations,routes/*}.ts`, `packages/shared/src/{api-schemas,api-responses}.ts`, `scripts/{assign-operation-labels,check-label-books}.ts`, `tests/{scope,golden,api-contract}.test.ts`, `packages/frontend/src/{lib/label.ts,components/LabelSwitch.tsx,api/queries/core.ts,pages/*}`, `docs/идеи/разделение_по_лейблу/README.md`
- Status: accepted (the product owner's decisions, 2026-11-05)

## D-169: The book of a unit is the label of the account of its file; uploads under a label; advertising by payer; documents and the audit carry a label
- Date: 2026-11-06
- Question: D-168 made the label a book but found the boundary of money in the label string of a Believe unit, so the «Echo8» line inside the 0to8 account 1052428 sat in the Echo8 book, publishing (B17) sat in a «without a label» book no payout could pay, and an advertising record charged the book of its ISRC's string. Stage 1 of «label from zero» (`docs/идеи/лейбл_с_нуля/бриф_для_агента.md` section «D-169», the API/UI project `docs/идеи/лейбл_с_нуля/api/этап1_D-169.md`) sets the goal: every Believe unit sits in the book of the label its file's account is registered under; a file and a statement are loaded under a label and remember it; an advertising expense charges the book of its payer; a document and an audit row may carry a label; `calculate()`, the layers `facade/layers/*`, `recoup.ts` and the reach (`owner-scope.ts`) do not change mathematically. The product owner's answers of 2026-11-05: (1) all six B17 statements are 0to8, publishing is to be paid as a separate payroll later (D-170); (2) the advertising history was paid by 0to8 (Main 0to8), the disputable records go to him for review; (3) the seven Echo8 operations of D-168 were paid by Main 0to8, so they are 0to8; (4) the 1,400 documents stay «any label» (unmarked) and are marked by hand later (Н1); 0to8 and Echo8 are two labels of one organisation whose money and files never cross, even when one ISRC was reported by both; a Believe account belongs to a label without a period; the advertising payer is always an own label; «label decision» (a document kind to pay without a contract) comes later (D-171); the contract of a label = a contract whose document carries that label.
- Decision:
  - **The rules** (the brief, in English):
    - **R6.** The book of a unit = the own label in `source_files.label_id` of its file. The label string of the unit still decides the sublabel rule and «ours / `label_unmapped`» (`layers/label-of-record.ts` unchanged). A unit has two fields: `bookLabelId` (the account, the book) and `ourLabelId` (the string). A file without `label_id` (the history before marking) is the book `'?'`.
    - **R7.** A Believe file is loaded under an own label: `POST /sources/believe?label=<id>`. The account of the file name must be registered under that label in `labels.label_accounts`; otherwise the upload is refused before the file is read, with no registry row, no ClickHouse rows and no audit row. `source_files.label_id` is written at load and never changes (no PATCH). The lines of another own label **inside the label's own account** still load (the signal `labelOutsideAccount` stays).
    - **R8.** A B17 statement is loaded under an own label: `POST /publishing/b17?label=<id>`; `pub_statements.label_id`; the lines inherit it through the materialized view; publishing income sits in the book of the statement's label. Under `?label=L` the B17 lines of L are read (before, no B17 line was read under a label). One quarter per source (the PK of `pub_statement_periods` does not change; a second label with the same quarter is a limit, see Open).
    - **R9.** Advertising: `ads.expenses.label_id` is required on every record and means the payer; the book charged = the payer. The ISRC's label string at the month of the expense decides only the sublabel rule (who bears the unrecovered part). New signal `adOutsideBook`: the ISRC's income at the month of the expense goes to another book (information).
    - **R10.** `owners.documents.label_id` is optional; empty = «any label». The payout and advance gate under L needs an approved contract with `label_id ∈ {L, NULL}`. The C categorisation (`owner_unconfirmed`) does not look at the contract's label. The history (1,400 documents) is **not marked** ⚠.
    - **R11.** `audit.actions.label_id` = `?label=` of the request, else `labelId` of the body, else empty; the history is not back-filled. Refined by the project (Open В4 of the plan): after the body, the one label of `items[]` (`POST /ads`), then the `labelId` of the response for an explicit route list, then empty (below).
    - Under the switch «Все» every write button (upload, operation, advertising record, document, payout) is closed with the hint «выберите лейбл», like the payout button of D-168.
  - **D-168 refined.** Its decision 1 («the label of money is the unit's label string») is replaced for the book: the book is the label of the file (R6); the string keeps the label of record, the sublabel rule, «ours / `label_unmapped`», `ourShareByLabel` of the reconciliation and the signal `labelOutsideAccount`. The visibility by label strings and «B17 only in Все» are replaced: a label book is its loaded files, a read under a label filters `SnapshotFilter.labelIds` (the field `labelStrings`, `list('labelStrings')`, `service.labelStringsOfAliases` and `service.isrcsOfLabelStrings` are removed; `columnar.labelStrings()` / `columnar.isrcsOfLabelStrings()` stay for `isrcsOfLabelAliases`, the reach of sublabel-rule recipients), and the B17 lines of the label are read under it. Advertising is charged to its payer, not by the ISRC's string. **D-123 / D-130 refined** again: one balance per owner × book, the book now being the file's label (master), the statement's label (publishing), the payer (advertising) and the operation's label (D-168).
  - **Data model**: one migration `packages/backend/migrations/postgres/0015_label_books_2.sql` for the whole stage, idempotent, no `NOT NULL`, no foreign key (D-121): `public.source_files.label_id` (index on `label_id` where `status = 'loaded'`) and `uploaded_by` now filled with the operator; `publishing.pub_statements.label_id`; `publishing.pub_lines_by_key` recreated with `s.label_id` in the SELECT and GROUP BY only (index and row order as in 0013: a period determines its statement through `pub_statement_periods`, hence its label, so the label never splits a group; this rests on the refusal of a second statement per quarter, D-041 ⚠); `owners.documents.label_id`; `audit.actions.label_id` (index). `ads.expenses.label_id` exists since 0014. ClickHouse: no migration, no label column in `believe.rows`; the label of a unit comes from the registry map file → label in `sources.snapshot()`.
  - **Modules**: sources: `SourceUnit.labelId`, `SnapshotFilter.labelIds` (only the loaded files of those labels, beside `accounts`; the file list of the answer stays complete, D-161), `filesOfLabel`, `isrcsOfFiles`, `LinesQuery.labelId` / `listFiles({ labelId })`, `assignFileLabel` (scripts only, `WHERE label_id IS NULL`). publishing: `ingestB17({ labelId })`, `StatementView.labelId`, `AggregatedLine.labelId`, `assignStatementLabel` (script, refreshes the view). labels: `removeAccount`. ads: `update({ labelId })`, `list({ labelId })`, `assignLabel` (script, NULL only). owners: `createDocument({ labelId })`, `setDocumentLabel`, `listDocuments({ labelId })` (= the label's documents and those without one), `confirmedContracts()`, `isOwnerConfirmedUnder(id, L)`, both through `computeConfirmedOwners` over the documents with `label_id ∈ {L, NULL}`. audit: `record({ labelId })`, `listRecent` / `listByEntity` with a label (strictly `label_id = L`). No module imports another; `tests/boundaries.test.ts` is unchanged.
  - **Facade** (the calculation area changes by fields and keys only): `UnitResult.bookLabelId / bookLabelName` set in `calculate()` after `allocateUnit` from `SourceUnit.labelId` (`LayerContext` unchanged); `PublishingResult.labelId` from the statement's line; `Calculation.confirmedOwnersUnder(L)` from `owners.confirmedContracts()` inside the same `db.snapshot` (`confirmedOwners` for the layers as before). `balance.ts` keys the books by `bookLabelId` (owner and pseudonym), the publishing lines by `p.labelId`; the master income item carries the book as `labelId` and as a suffix of its id (`m|artist|isrc|month|<book>`: one ISRC-month from two accounts is two items, so an advance of one book is never recouped from the other; the suffix keeps the order of `recoup()`); `confirmed` under a view comes from `confirmedOwnersUnder`, `confirmedAny` is an internal field (not in `balanceSchema`, stripped from the payout snapshot); the formula of `payable_L` is unchanged. `reconcile()` books a Believe row (account × month) whole in the book of its file (one named key per row), b17 rows by the statement's label, C items by book; `ourShareByLabel` stays by the string. `label-scope.ts`: `LabelBook { id, name, fileIds }` (the loaded files with `label_id = L`), `labelIsrcs` = `sources.isrcsOfFiles(fileIds)`; every read under a label filters `labelIds`; `labelVisibility` extended: a pseudonym is visible under L with a split row or participation on an ISRC of L's files, as the recipient of a sublabel rule of L, with an operation under L, as the pseudonym of an advertising record paid by L or a split row of its ISRC (a superset of those it charges), and the owners of the publishing lines of L (commit 5). B17 under a label: `scoped(modules, { labelIds: [L] })` without `publishingLines: false`, then `underLabel(calc, L)` keeps the publishing results of L (`scope.ts` unchanged). `facade/ads.ts` `allocateAd`: `ourLabelId = ad.labelId` always, `labelStringOf` only for the sublabel rule; new `incomeBookLabelId / Name` (the book of the ISRC's income at the nearest month; the larger net of several, a tie by name) and the pure `adsOutsideBook(results)`; no new multiplication. The payout under L (`createPayout`) pays the master **and** the publishing of book L (after commit 5), its snapshot takes `activeStatements()` of L and the Believe files of L; the area «master / publishing» of the button comes with D-170. Signals: `filesWithoutLabel` (`fileId, account, reportingMonth, fileName`), `statementsWithoutLabel` (`statementId, periods, fileName`), `adsWithoutLabel` (`adId, isrc, month, amount`), `adOutsideBook` (`adId, isrc, month, amount, payerLabelName, bookLabelName`; under a label a second calculation scoped to the ISRCs of the label's ads gives the records where L is the payer or the income book); under a label the three «WithoutLabel» lists are empty. Gate: `owner_not_confirmed` (no approved contract at all) → **`no_contract_for_label`** (none of L or without a label) → the other checks in their old order (`payout-gate.ts`); the same reason refuses an advance under L in `createOperation`.
  - **API**: `POST /sources/believe?label=` and `POST /publishing/b17?label=` (the label is checked by `facade.assertUploadLabel` before the multipart body is received; on a refusal the body is drained unread so the client finishes writing and never sees EPIPE); `GET /sources/files?label=`, `/sources/lines?label=`, `/sources/lines/facets?label=` (the routes no longer open the modules: `sourceFiles`, `sourceLines`, `sourceFacets`); `POST /labels/:id/accounts/:account/remove` (new, only while the account has no loading or loaded file); `POST /ads` (`labelId` on every item), `PATCH /ads/:id { labelId }`, `GET /ads?label=` and `/ads/summary?label=` by the payer; `POST /documents { labelId? }`, `PATCH /documents/:id { labelId | null }` (new; `null` = any label), `GET /documents?label=`; `GET /audit?label=`; `labelId` in `sourceFileSchema`, `statementSchema`, `documentSchema`, `auditActionSchema`; `months[].bookLabelId / bookLabelName` on `/tracks/:isrc/money`; the four signal lists on `/signals`. Error codes: `label_required` 400 (no label on an upload; «Item N» on `POST /ads`, except a migrated record with an ISRC, which only the stage 4 loader creates); `not_found` 404 (unknown label); `label_not_own` 400 `{ labelId }` (a sublabel); `account_unregistered` 422 `{ account }`; `account_of_other_label` 422 `{ account, labelId, labelName }` (the label the account belongs to); `account_has_files` 409 `{ account, files }`; `no_contract_for_label` 422 `{ labelId }` on `POST /owners/:id/payouts` and an advance in `POST /settlements/operations`. Upload order: `operator_required` → `label_required` → `not_found` / `label_not_own` (before the body) → multipart → `validation_error` (file name) → `account_unregistered` → `account_of_other_label` → `duplicate_period` → sha256 → `duplicate_file`, all before the module's serial load. B17 the same without the account checks.
  - **The audit label** (`api/audit.ts` `labelOfWrite`): `?label=` → `labelId` of the body → the one `labelId` of `body.items[]` → the `labelId` of the response only for `RESPONSE_LABEL_ROUTES` (`PATCH /settlements/operations/:id`, `POST /settlements/operations/:id/proof`, `/deferred`, `/deactivate`, `/return`, `PATCH /ads/:id`, `POST /ads/:id/deactivate`, `PATCH /documents/:id`, `POST /documents/:id/attach`, `/approve`) → empty. The `labelId` of a rule response (`/labels/:id/rules`, `/labels/rules/:id/close`) is a sublabel and is never recorded; account registration and removal stay without a label (the label is in `entityIds`). The summary carries `labelId`, `null` included, so a cleared document label and a changed payer are visible.
  - **Script `scripts/assign-label-books.ts [dataDir] [--apply] [--step <name>]`** (through the modules, no audit, dry run by default, idempotent: a second dry run prints only `already_done`): `files` (every loaded Believe file without a label → the label of its account; an unregistered account stops the step), `statements` (every statement → 0to8, refreshing the view; numbers: see Checked), `ads` (every active record with an ISRC and without a label → 0to8; the dry run lists the disputable records, those whose income book is another label; a record without ISRC and label stops the step), `operations-echo8` (the active migrated operations under Echo8 → 0to8 through `setOperationLabel`; another count than 7 stops the step unless `--force`). Documents are not marked by a script. `scripts/check-label-books.ts` gains (а) file label = account label, (б) one named book per Believe reconciliation row, (в) the books of an owner / pseudonym given as arguments, (д) owners with a `'?'` book. Also (е) `--dump <file>` / `--compare <file>`: the owners whose `payable` changed between two runs (ids and Δ only), and (ж) the count and Σ of the В1 leak (recoupments of a labelled position from publishing of another book) through the diagnostics callback `ownerBalance(…, onConsumed)` of `balance.ts` (no money change). The brief's item (г) (Σ `labelOutsideAccount` of 1052428 = 222 130, in the 0to8 book) is not a check of the script: the signal is 222 107,15 in 9 groups and the shift of the books 222 129,81 (the 22,67 are `unknown_pair` / `no_isrc` units, which leave the reconcile loop before the account check); both are pinned by `compare-reference` (the signal groups and the movement of the books). `scripts/compare-reference.ts <dir> [--api] [--strict]` compares the reference JSON with the running API by stable keys (owners by name|kind, labels by name, reconciliation by source|account|month); `--strict` compares the books too, the default prints their movement. Recipe 11 of `docs/agents/04_recipes.md` is the rebuild order.
  - **Tests flipped consciously by R6** (the instruction forbade editing golden expectations; R6 inverts them): G08b (the Echo8 unit now sits in ACC2; the `'?'` book of an unmapped string in ACC1 is gone: it is in the 0to8 book), G50 and its screen S38 (two files in two accounts, the same numbers), the describe «the label as a book (D-168)» of `tests/scope.test.ts` (Echo8 units in ACC2, the spy on `labelIds`, the signal under 0to8), the D-164 pin «a payout records the snapshot» (the 0to8 payout records the 0to8 view, now tied field by field to the 0to8 book of the full balance: 10, not 110); its snapshot file list holds the files and the statements of the paid label only, 1 of the 2 files: D-164 refined), `tests/review-fixes.test.ts` «payout snapshot attribution» (D-168 pinned publishing in the «?» book no payout pays; R8 reverses it: the 0to8 book 300 / 150 / 150, the payout 150.00 = master 100 + publishing 50, no «?» book). New golden cases (`docs/agents/12_golden_cases.md`): **G51** another label's string in the own account (book 0to8, label of record Echo8, signal 1, nothing under Echo8); **G52** an ISRC moved from ACC1 to ACC2 with a 0to8 advance: 0to8 earned 60, recouped 60, payable 0; Echo8 earned 40, recouped 0, payable 40; **total payable 40** (the brief's «0» contradicted its own Echo8 40), and G52 «same month» (both files in one month, the same numbers, by the book suffix of the income id); **G54** an ad paid by 0to8 on an ISRC reporting in ACC2 (adsCharged in the 0to8 book, `adOutsideBook` 1, `label_required` without a payer); **G55** a statement under Echo8 for an owner with a 0to8 contract only (book Echo8, `no_contract_for_label` under Echo8); **G56** a document without a label opens both labels, a 0to8 contract does not open Echo8. `Case.verify()`: at most one named book per Believe row. `tests/layers/*` and `tests/recoup.test.ts` are unchanged: the math did not move.
  - **Loader fixes found by the clean rebuild (commit 0)**: `modules/publishing/b17-format.ts` reads a statement whose archive stores `sharedStrings.xml` after the sheets in memory when the streaming pass finds no detail sheet (the two-quarter 2024-Q4/2025-Q1 file: this is the «unexplained `validation_error`» of D-134, closed); `migrate-sqlite-to-pg.ts` tolerates a column added by a later migration (nullable or with a default); `steps-ads.ts` gives the 33 records without an ISRC the payer 0to8; `extract-main-ads.py` must be re-run before the `ads` step on every rebuild (the mapping carries pseudonym ids, which the loader generates anew).
- Checked: `REQUIRE_STACK=1 npm test` per commit (42 files, 445 tests after commit 1, 469 after commit 4; on the loaded machine serially), frontend tests (94 after commit 4), typecheck, lint, `npm run build:client`, the e2e specs of each commit; the contract test calls every new operation and refusal. **Real data** (`data/real`, the local `erp` store): commit 0 rebuilt the reference data on a clean stack (47 files, 75,744,752 rows; owners 1,151, artists 2,134, tracks 26,305, documents 1,400, contracts 803, splits 24,107 / shares 42,070, participations 283, operations 2,663, statements 6 with 1,162,586 lines, labels 17, ads 2,686) and `compare-reference --strict` matched the API snapshot of 2026-11-05 field by field (0 differing, with the 33 ISRC-less ads temporarily without a label); commit 1: the strict compare differs by exactly those 33 ads now in the 0to8 book, every invariant 0 differing; commit 2: step `files` 34 files → 0to8 (1052428), 13 → Echo8 (1055956), 0 unregistered; the 54 reconciliation rows, the owners' totals, tracks, queue and the 13 signal counters unchanged; the books move exactly as decided: the «Echo8» string of account 1052428, 9 rows (2024-12…2025-07 and 2025-12, −0.06), received 222,129.81 (not ours 23,603.80, waiting 66,677.73, ours 131,848.28) from the Echo8 book to 0to8; Σ Δ recouped = Σ Δ payable = 0; `check-owner-scope` 1,151 / 1,151, 0 differing; `check-label-books` (а) 0, (б) 0, (д) 26 owners with a `'?'` book (the B17 book until commit 5); the signal `labelOutsideAccount` stays 9 groups, Σ 222,107.15 (the 22.67 against 222,129.81 is the `unknown_pair` / `no_isrc` part the signal skips); commit 3: step `ads` 2,653 records → 0to8 (Σ 1,012,540.64), the disputable list = income book Echo8: 117 records, Σ 30,012.71 (not the 44,605.93 of D-168 by the string), = `adOutsideBook` 117 after `--apply`; step `operations-echo8` 7 operations (2 payouts, 5 recoupable payouts), Σ 12,505.29 → 0to8; invariants 0 differing, Σ Δ recouped = Σ Δ payable = 0; the Echo8 ads book by the string (44,605.93) and the `'?'` ads row (6,972.44) are gone; commit 4: no data change (documents stay unmarked). commit 5: the step `statements` marked the 6 statements (7 quarters) 0to8 and refreshed the view; the compare without `--strict` gives 0 differing (every invariant: `totals.all`, `byAccount`, the 54 reconciliation rows with A / B / C / ours / discrepancy / `C_breakdown` / `ourShareByLabel`, the owners' `total.*`, 25,854 tracks, the queue, 17 labels, the 13 signal counters, 47 files) and prints the two decided movements only: the «Echo8» string of account 1052428 from the Echo8 book to 0to8 (9 rows, 222,129.81 received) and the publishing «?» book to 0to8 (7 b17 rows, 2,496,581.91 received), Σ Δ recouped = Σ Δ payable = 0; `check-label-books.ts` over all 1,151 owners: (а) 0, (б) 0, (д) 0 owners with a «?» book, (ж) В1 0 recoupments, (е) 0 owners whose payable changed against the dump taken before the step, Σ books = row on the 54 rows, 0 differing (one owner's totals still depend on the labels of the operations, the D-168 case 3,175.93 → 3,878.35, unchanged against the reference); `check-owner-scope.ts` 1,151 / 1,151 equal, 0 differing (after commit 2 and after commit 5); the full backend suite 42 files after commit 5 (see the commit message for the count). The books that moved against the reference per-label payables (owners keyed by name and kind): the Echo8 / 0to8 book `waiting` of 225 owners, `payable` of 94, `earned` of 10 (B of the «Echo8» string in 1052428 is only 23 603,80; most of the 222 129,81 is C and ours); the brief's «≈266 owners» counted owners with any unit under that string. The pseudonym of the owner's review (id in the local data): the 0to8 book earned 28 660,33, advance 500 recouped, advertising 7 122,40, paid 20 698,54 → payable +339,39 (shown as is); the Echo8 book earned 43 167,77, nothing paid. The brief expected the 0to8 book at earned ≈ 27 228 → payable ≈ −1 091,77; the 1 431,16 difference is in `earned` and goes to the owner (Open).
- Open:
  - В1: an advance of label L with scope `any` can still be recouped from publishing income of another book (`layers/recoup.ts` `eligible` returns for `stream === 'publishing'` before the label check); Σ of the books holds, but `payable_0to8` would pay for Echo8. `recoup.ts` is forbidden until stage 5; the case is counted, not fixed.
  - В8: the removal of an account and the upload of a file are not atomic (the registry has its own pool, outside `db.transaction()`; the account check precedes the serial load): accepted, one operator; `check-label-books` (а) catches a file without a registration.
  - В9: `setOperationLabel` (`PATCH /settlements/operations/:id`) does not check the gate: an advance can be moved under a label without a contract of that label; kept so the history can be marked; for the product owner.
  - Н1: the documents history stays unmarked («any label») by the owner's decision; marked by hand through `PATCH /documents/:id`; until then `GET /documents?label=` shows all 1,400 under either label.
  - A second label with the same B17 quarter is refused (the PK of `pub_statement_periods`, D-041 ⚠); the income id `p|<month>` and the view's grouping rely on it.
  - The visibility of advertising: an ad paid by L on an ISRC whose string is a sublabel of another own label does not make that sublabel rule's recipient visible under L.
  - The `'?'` books: none expected after the four steps; `filesWithoutLabel`, `statementsWithoutLabel`, `adsWithoutLabel` catch a new one.
  - The 117 disputable advertising records (Σ 30,012.71) wait for the owner's review; the payer is changed by `PATCH /ads/:id`.
  - The pseudonym of the owner's review: our 0to8 book has earned 28 660,33 where the brief's manual figure is ≈ 27 228 (Δ 1 431,16 → payable +339,39, not −1 091,77); by R6 the «Echo8» rows of this pseudonym inside account 1052428 are in the 0to8 book, which the manual figure may exclude; for the owner's check with the row-by-row payroll (D-170).
  - A period of an account under a label and of a sublabel's own label: not done (owner: no such case).

## D-170: The organization is the isolation boundary (variant A): org_id on every table, the modules opened under an organization, a minimal login with a JWT cookie, /orgs for the system administrator, a constant 500
- Date: 2026-11-06
- Note on numbering: the brief (`docs/идеи/лейбл_с_нуля/бриф_для_агента.md`) and D-169 use «D-170» (ведомость, the payroll operations), «D-171» (onboarding, the label's decision) and «D-172» (users and roles) as working names of stages, not numbers of this log; the numbers follow the sequence of this log, so this stage («Граница арендатора», stage 2) is D-170 and the later stages take the next free numbers.
- Question: the owner clarified on 2026-11-05 that the boundary of isolation is not the label but the tenant (the owner of one or two labels): reports, owners and pseudonyms of two tenants never meet. The consilium of 2026-11-05 (`docs/идеи/лейбл_с_нуля/консилиум_изоляция/итог.md`) proposed a pair of databases (PostgreSQL + ClickHouse) per tenant plus a control database. The owner chose **variant A** instead (README §4а of the idea folder): one database, a table of organizations with real ids, `org_id` on every row of data, `label_id` on money as in D-169; an organization has N labels (companies) with their sublabels (0to8 and Echo8 are two labels of one organization). Kept from the consilium (`итог.md` §6 and the brief): the constant 500 with a `requestId`, ids generated by the server (never by a client), `createPrivateStores` writing `schema_migrations`, database names never from a client. The owner's answers used here (the end of the brief): 2 (users are created by the administrator, who records logins and passwords: `scripts/create-user.ts` and a simple screen of the system administrator, no invitations, no mail), 4 (one Believe account in two organizations does not collide: allowed, unique per organization), 5 (USD only; the organization card holds only a name), 10 (concurrent edits are not handled: the commit «expected values on the three money PATCHes» is dropped, «the last write wins, both in the audit»), 11 (rights by an abilities layer: stage 3), 12 (an organization switch at login: stage 3, memberships), 13 (the system administrator's rights are decided later: minimum = the list of organizations and users). The approved plan with its review: `docs/идеи/лейбл_с_нуля/api/этап2_организация.md`.
- Decision:
  - **The rules** (the brief, in English, as implemented):
    - **O1.** `orgs.organizations (id, name, created_at, created_by)`; every table of data of the modules carries `org_id NOT NULL` (owners, aliases, artists, tracks, merges, documents, contracts, splits and shares, participations, labels, aliases, rules, Believe accounts, operations and payout snapshots, advertising, statements and their periods, lines, writers, publishing terms, the file registry, the audit). No foreign key across modules (D-121): `org_id` is opaque text the modules filter by.
    - **O2.** The book of money is the label of the file (R6, D-169), not the label string. The string decides only the sublabel rule and «ours / `label_unmapped`», inside the files of its own label; an alias is unique per `(org_id, alias_norm)`.
    - **O3.** The filter by organization is applied **once, centrally**: every module is opened under an organization and its repository adds `org_id = <org>` through one helper, never by hand in a query; a static test checks every table and every repository statement. The facade is kept per organization (a map `orgId → facade`); the routes do not change and never see the organization.
    - **O4.** ClickHouse has no organization or label column: every read starts from the `loaded` files of the organization's registry and asks ClickHouse with `file_id IN (...)`.
    - **O5.** Uniqueness per organization: the file registry `(org_id, source, account, reporting_month)` and `(org_id, sha256)`, B17 quarters `(org_id, source, period)`, labels `(org_id, name)`, aliases `(org_id, alias_norm)`, Believe accounts `(org_id, account)` (one account in two organizations is allowed, answer 4). One advisory write lock for the whole database stays (another organization's transaction delays mine for its duration; a key `hashtext(org_id)` if the load grows).
    - **O6.** **The organization comes only from the session and is decided by the backend**; a client has no organization header or parameter, except `X-Org` of the system administrator. The minimal login is part of this stage (`auth.users`, `POST /auth/login` → a JWT in an httpOnly cookie, authentication on every request of `/api/v1` except the public paths). Roles, memberships in several organizations, labels of a user and sessions with revocation stay in stage 3. `AUTH_MODE=off` for tests, scripts and the local stand = the organization `ERP_DEFAULT_ORG`. The label of a request (`?label=`, `labelId` in a body) must be a label of the organization (a foreign label is an unknown one: `404 not_found`, not the brief's `label_not_found`, plan К5); an empty label = «Все» = every book of the organization. A foreign id of any entity under one's own organization is a 404 (or the same answer as a random unknown id), never data. An error 500 answers a constant text and the `requestId`.
    - **O7.** Optimistic control on the three money PATCHes: **dropped** by the owner's answer 10; the last write wins, both writes are in the audit, the payout stays protected by its transaction.
