  - **Data model**: one migration `packages/backend/migrations/postgres/0016_organizations.sql` (0015 was taken by D-169; idempotent, 0001–0015 untouched): schema `orgs` with `orgs.organizations (id text PK CHECK uuid-like, name text NOT NULL CHECK not blank, created_at, created_by)` and `organizations_name_key UNIQUE (lower(name))`; schema `auth` with `auth.users (id, login CHECK '^[a-z0-9._-]{3,64}$', password_hash, org_id, is_system_admin, created_at, created_by, disabled_at, CHECK (is_system_admin OR org_id IS NOT NULL))`, `users_login_key UNIQUE (login)` (stored lower-case), index `users_org`. **`org_id text NOT NULL` on 26 tables**: `public.source_files`; `owners.{owners, owner_aliases, artists, tracks, artist_tracks, documents, contracts, contract_artists, merges}`; `splits.{splits, split_shares, participations}`; `labels.{labels, label_aliases, label_rules, label_accounts}`; `settlements.{operations, payout_snapshots}`; `publishing.{pub_statements, pub_statement_periods, pub_lines, pub_writers, pub_terms}`; `audit.actions`; `ads.expenses` (child tables too: one rule for the static test), each with an index `<table>_org`, no `DEFAULT` (a forgotten `org_id` on insert fails at once). **Organization №1** with the fixed id `00000000-0000-4000-8000-000000000001`, name `Организация №1`, `created_by = 'migration 0016'` is created **only on a base with data** (any row of the 26 tables without an organization) and every row is stamped with it; a clean base gets no organization (the first is created by `POST /orgs`, with a random id). The fixed id is the same on any copy of the live base, so dumps before and after compare and the recipes can name it. **Unique keys per organization**: `source_files_scope (org_id, source, account, reporting_month) WHERE status IN ('loading','loaded')` and `source_files_sha (org_id, sha256) WHERE …` **keep their names** (the registry maps `23505` to `409 duplicate_period` / `duplicate_file` by the constraint name); `pub_statements_org_sha256_key (org_id, sha256)`; `pub_statement_periods_pkey (org_id, source, period)`; `pub_writers_pkey (org_id, writer_norm)`; `labels_org_name (org_id, name)` (new: before, a label name was not unique at all; `409 label_name_taken`); `label_aliases_pkey (org_id, alias_norm)`; `label_accounts_pkey (org_id, account)`; `tracks_org_isrc_upc (org_id, isrc, upc)`; `expenses_org_external_ref_key (org_id, external_ref)`; the exclusion constraints `splits_no_overlap` and `participations_no_overlap` keep their names with `org_id WITH =` first. The materialized view `publishing.pub_lines_by_key` is recreated with `s.org_id` first in the `SELECT` and the `GROUP BY` (a quarter identifies a statement only inside an organization) and its index `pub_lines_by_key_order (org_id, period, writer_norm, …)`. Keys by global ids (`label_rules_no_overlap`, the PKs of `owner_aliases`, `split_shares`, `artist_tracks`, `contract_artists`, `payout_snapshots`, `pub_lines`) are unchanged: ids are UUIDs. **ClickHouse is untouched** (no migration). Drizzle: `orgId: text('org_id').notNull()` in every `s.table` of the module schemas; `src/orgs/schema.ts` (`pgSchema('orgs')`), `src/auth/schema.ts` (`pgSchema('auth')`).
  - **The central filter** (`packages/backend/src/shared/org-scope.ts`, in `shared` so that the modules never import `src/orgs`): `orgScope(id): OrgScope` (a frozen `{ id }`; an empty id throws); `orgWhere(org, table, ...conds)` = `and(eq(table.orgId, org.id), ...conds)` on every read, update and delete; `orgRow(org, row)` / `orgRows(org, rows)` stamp every insert (the caller never names `orgId`); `orgFree(table)` = the columns without `orgId` for `select(...)`, so no row read under an organization carries it (the answers of the API stay as they were; type `OrgFree<T>`); `orgQuery(db, org)` = a raw-SQL executor whose `$1` is the organization and which **refuses a statement that does not name `org_id` and `$1`**. Every module is opened as `openXModule(db, org)` / `createXRepository(db, org)`; the services did not change (an id of another organization is simply not found: `NotFoundError`). `openModules(db, org, { publishingDir })` (`src/facade/modules.ts`) opens the seven PostgreSQL modules under one organization; `openFacade({ db, orgId, sources, dataDir, … })` calls it and asserts `sources.orgId === orgId` (a facade refuses the sources of another organization). The file registry: `createSourceFilesRegistry(pool, org)` (the same `$1` technique; `ingest` writes `org_id`; `loaded()` lists only the organization's files, so every ClickHouse read is the organization's). `Db` knows no organization: `transaction()` (one advisory lock), `snapshot()` as before; `calculate()` reads everything in one `db.snapshot` through the modules of the organization, unchanged. Stores: `connectStores()` (replaces `connectSources`: `{ db, pool, columnar, close }`, one ClickHouse client per process); `sourcesFor(stores, orgId, dataDir)` = the sources service of one organization; `connectOrg({ dataDir, org })` for scripts resolves the organization from `opts.org` → `--org <id>` → `ERP_ORG` → `ERP_DEFAULT_ORG`, otherwise exits 1 printing the organizations of the database (`resolveOrg`, `takeOrgArgument` in `src/connect-sources.ts`). **Storage directories**: `<dataDir>/orgs/<orgId>/files/sources`, `<dataDir>/orgs/<orgId>/files/publishing` and the upload `tmp` `<dataDir>/orgs/<orgId>/tmp` (the stored name `<sha256>-<name>` would collide between organizations in a shared directory); the old `stored_path` values of the live base are absolute, the files are not moved. The upload queue (`serial()`) belongs to the service instance of the organization's facade: uploads are serialized per module **and** organization; between processes the partial unique index `source_files_scope` decides (`409 duplicate_period`). `createPrivateStores` (tests, e2e) writes `schema_migrations` like `migrate-postgres.sh` and creates its test organization once through `src/orgs/repository.ts` (`stores.orgId`); `orgs` and `auth` are **not** module schemas (`MODULE_SCHEMAS`): `resetModules()` empties the modules only, organizations and users survive a reset. The modules and the facade never import `src/orgs` or `src/auth` (`tests/boundaries.test.ts`).
  - **The authentication** (`packages/backend/src/auth/`: `schema`, `repository`, `service`, `password`, `token`, `middleware`; global, not a module of the facade, not opened under an organization): passwords by `crypto.scrypt` N=16384, r=8, p=1, a 16-byte salt, a 64-byte key, stored as `scrypt$16384$8$1$<salt b64>$<hash b64>`, compared with `timingSafeEqual`; no password policy. The session token is a JWT HS256 by `node:crypto`: the header is exactly `{"alg":"HS256","typ":"JWT"}` (any other header, `alg: none` included, is refused), the body only `{ sub, iat, exp }`, 12 hours, no renewal; the signature is compared with `timingSafeEqual`. `AUTH_SECRET` must have at least 32 bytes and must not start with `change_me` (with `AUTH_MODE=on` the server does not start without it; changing it signs everybody out). The cookie: `erp_session=<token>; HttpOnly; SameSite=Strict; Path=/; Max-Age=43200`, plus `Secure` with `AUTH_COOKIE_SECURE=1`; logout sets `Max-Age=0`. The user row is **re-read on every request** by `sub` (the organization and the system flag come from the row, not the token: a change of organization or rights acts at once; a disabled or removed user is `401`). The login answers one `401 invalid_credentials` for an unknown login, a wrong password and a disabled user, and checks an unknown login against a dummy hash (computed at startup) so it takes as long. `AUTH_MODE`: `on` by default; `off` only explicitly (tests, scripts, the local stand): no cookie, every request in `ERP_DEFAULT_ORG` (which must exist, or the server does not start), the operator from `X-Operator` as before. `X-System-Key` = `SYSTEM_ADMIN_KEY`, compared in constant time; an unset key switches the path off; a wrong key is `403 forbidden`; it serves the clean stack before the first user, the e2e seed and scripts. `scripts/create-user.ts`: `<login> --org <id>` (a user of an organization), `<login> --system` (a system administrator; the API never creates one), `<login> --reset-password`, `<login> --disable`, `<login> --enable`; the password (create, reset) only from `ERP_NEW_PASSWORD` or stdin (one line), never from argv; it prints the user id, never the password; a taken login, an unknown organization or user = exit 1.
  - **The middleware chain** (`src/api/server-app.ts` `createServerApp({ orgs, auth, clientDist })`): `requestId` (every response has `X-Request-Id`) → `express.json` → on `/api` `authenticateOrSystemKey` (public paths pass: `/health`, `/openapi.json`, `/docs`, `/auth/login`, `/auth/logout`; otherwise the cookie gives `res.locals.user`, or a right `X-System-Key` gives `res.locals.systemKey`; neither = `401 unauthenticated`; in `off` the request passes) → on `/api/v1` the **system router** (`src/api/system-router.ts`, static: `/health`, `/openapi.json`, `/docs`, `/auth/*` with `requireUser` on `/auth/me`, `/orgs*` with `requireSystem` (`403 forbidden` unless a system administrator's session or the key) and `systemTrace` on writes) → `resolveOrg` (`src/orgs/middleware.ts`, the only reader of `X-Org`: a user works in the organization of their row and `X-Org` from them is `403 forbidden`; a system administrator (session or key) names it with `X-Org`, none = `400 org_required`; in `off` it is `ERP_DEFAULT_ORG` and `X-Org` is ignored; then the facade map, `404 org_not_found`) → the **router of the organization** (`src/api/org-router.ts` `createOrgRouter(facade, { tmpDir })`: `operatorTrace` → `labelGuard` → `createV1Router`) → `/api` unknown route `404` → the client → the error handler. An unknown `/api/...` path without a cookie is now `401` (the session is checked before the routes are searched), not `404`. `createApp(facade, { tmpDir, auth?, orgs? })` stays for tests of one organization in `off` and mounts the same system router, so the contract test sees every route.
  - **`labelGuard`** (`src/api/label-guard.ts`): the labels a request names (`?label=`, `body.labelId`, `body.items[].labelId`) are checked once before the route with the labels module of the organization: a foreign or unknown label is `404 not_found`; a `?label=` that is not one string (given twice) is `400 validation_error`; on a refusal the request body is drained unread (an upload's client finishes writing). A second lock, not a replacement: the facade still checks (`context.ts`, `label-scope.ts`), and `400 label_not_own` for a sublabel stays the facade's.
  - **The facade map** (`src/orgs/facades.ts` `createOrgFacades({ stores, dataDir, payoutThresholdCents, paymentService? })`): `get(orgId)` is single-flight per organization (`Map<string, Promise<OrgEntry>>`), checks the row of `orgs.organizations` **before** opening the facade (so no directory is created for an unknown id; an unknown one is `404 org_not_found` and is not remembered), and keeps `{ orgId, facade, router }` (no connections of its own, never evicted); `close()` waits for the entries being opened and closes the stores. `main.ts` opens the stores, reads `authConfigFromEnv()` (exit 1 with a readable message on a missing secret, an unknown mode or, in `off`, a missing or unknown `ERP_DEFAULT_ORG`), computes the dummy hash, builds the map and `createServerApp`.
  - **API** (`src/api/routes/auth.ts`, `src/api/routes/orgs.ts`; schemas in `packages/shared/src/{api-schemas,api-responses}.ts`): `POST /auth/login { login, password }` (public; sets the cookie; answers `me`; no `X-Operator`, no audit row; the server log gets the request id, the login and the outcome), `POST /auth/logout` (public, idempotent, `{ ok: true }`), `GET /auth/me` (`user`; `{ authMode, user, org }`; in `off` `user: null` and the organization `ERP_DEFAULT_ORG`); `GET /orgs` (the organizations with counts: labels, loaded files, statements, owners, users; no money), `POST /orgs { name }` (201, the id from the server, `409 org_name_taken` without regard to case), `GET /orgs/:id` (`404 org_not_found`), `GET /orgs/:id/users` (without hashes), `POST /orgs/:id/users { login, password }` (201, `409 login_taken`, global without regard to case); no rename, delete or suspend (the data is rebuilt from scratch; №1 is renamed by SQL). Every one of the **84 operations** of `operations.ts` has a required field `access: 'public' | 'user' | 'system' | 'org'` (3 public: `/health`, `/auth/login`, `/auth/logout`; 1 user: `/auth/me`; 5 system: `/orgs*`; 75 org); `X-Operator` is required exactly on the writes whose `access !== 'public'` (the hook, the OpenAPI document and the contract test take the same rule); `org` operations document the optional header `X-Org`. OpenAPI: security schemes `session` (apiKey in cookie `erp_session`) and `systemKey` (apiKey in header `X-System-Key`); `user` = session, `system` and `org` = session or key; responses `401` / `403` on every non-public operation; the error body documents `requestId`.
  - **Error codes**: `401 unauthenticated` (no cookie, a bad or expired token, a disabled or removed user), `401 invalid_credentials`, `403 forbidden` (a system operation by a user, `X-Org` from a user, a wrong system key), `400 org_required`, `404 org_not_found`, `409 org_name_taken`, `409 login_taken`, `409 label_name_taken` (`POST /labels` with a name the organization already has), `400 validation_error` also for a body `express.json` could not parse (before: 500; its raw text, a password at the login, is neither answered nor logged), `500 internal` always with the constant message `Internal error` and the `requestId` in the body (= the `X-Request-Id` header). The server log of a 500 has the request id, the error's name, message, stack and the cause code, never the request (body, headers, cookies); the parameters drizzle puts into a failed query's message are scrubbed (`params: <omitted>`). A foreign entity is `404 not_found` (the answer of an unknown id).
  - **The audit**: with `AUTH_MODE=on` the operator of a write is the **login of the session** (the `X-Operator` header is still required, as a signature and a CSRF guard, but not trusted); in `off` or under the system key it is the header. A write of the system administrator (`POST /orgs`, `POST /orgs/:id/users`) is recorded by `systemTrace` into the audit of the **target** organization (`:id`, or the id of the created organization), entity ids = the organization and the created user, the summary keeps neither the name nor the login nor the password. Login and logout leave no row (no organization yet / nothing changed).
  - **A race found and fixed by `tests/concurrency.test.ts`** (`src/modules/publishing/service.ts` `load()`): the losing concurrent load of the same statement could delete the kept original (`<sha256>-<name>`) that the winner's `stored_path` points to; now the copy is deleted only when `repo.statementBySha(sha256)?.storedPath !== storedPath` (the advisory lock makes the winner's row visible to the loser's check). The Believe loader had no such race (it never removes its stored copy).
  - **Not done in this stage**: roles, memberships in several organizations (`auth.memberships`; stage 3 moves `org_id` there, `sub` unchanged), labels of a user, sessions with revocation (`auth.sessions`), abilities (answer 11), the organization choice at login (answer 12), password change and disabling through the API (script only), rate limits; no organization or label column in ClickHouse; no `/o/:id/` in paths; no `updated_at`; no transfer of owners between organizations; no PostgreSQL row-level security (В6 of the plan) and no composite foreign keys `(org_id, label_id)`.
  - **Tests**: `tests/org-scope.test.ts`: «organization scope: static checks (D-170)» (every table of the module schemas and `public.source_files` has `org_id NOT NULL`, the grouped B17 view is keyed by `org_id` first by its definition and index; every `s.table` declares `orgId … notNull()`; every repository statement goes through the helper: `orgWhere`, `orgRow(s)`, `orgQuery` with `org_id = $1`, module tables inside `sql` templates included; only `src/orgs` and `src/auth` are global, by name, and no other source file reads the organization tables), «two organizations in one database (D-170)» (one ISRC, owner name, alias, label name, account, file sha256, statement and quarter, ads `external_ref` in both without a 409; A never sees B and B does not change A; a facade refuses sources of another organization), «two organizations behind the login: the API (D-170)» (a generated walk over every `org` operation: B's ids under a answer as unknown ids, with nothing of B, writes leave no trace; the lists, the audit and the search under a have nothing of B; B's label is 404 in query, body and upload; `X-Org`: the system administrator needs it, an unknown one is 404, a user may not send it); `tests/auth.test.ts` («passwords and tokens», «the login over HTTP»: scrypt, forged / `alg none` / other header / expired tokens, cookie attributes and `Secure`, one `invalid_credentials`, every `org` operation without a cookie 401, `system` under a user 403, a wrong system key 403, the audit operator = the login, `off` without `ERP_DEFAULT_ORG` = `org_required`); `tests/http-errors.test.ts` (500 with the request id and no error text, a failed query logs the id and the cause code but no parameters, a label given twice = 400, malformed JSON at the login = 400 with the password neither answered nor logged); `tests/postgres.test.ts` «migration 0016_organizations» (an empty base: no organization, `org_id NOT NULL` everywhere, a second run changes nothing; a base with data: №1 with the fixed id, every row stamped, the keys per organization); `tests/api-contract.test.ts` runs twice, «API contract (AUTH_MODE=off)» (`createApp`, `/orgs*` with `X-System-Key`, the route list against `API_OPERATIONS`, every operation in the scenario) and «API contract (AUTH_MODE=on)» (`createServerApp`, a login, every operation with the cookie, `access` per operation); `tests/boundaries.test.ts` (modules and the facade never import `src/orgs` / `src/auth`); `tests/labels.test.ts` (`label_name_taken`); `tests/concurrency.test.ts` (four tests: two loads of one month in one organization by two processes → exactly one `loaded` and one `409 duplicate_period` (the index `source_files_scope` is checked before `source_files_sha`), no `loading` / `failed` row, the ClickHouse rows once, one stored copy; the same account and month in two organizations → both `loaded` with different ids; the same two cases for B17 through two facades). `golden.test.ts`, `layers/*`, `scope.test.ts`, `recoup.test.ts` unchanged: `newFacade()` opens the facade on `stores.orgId`, the numbers are the same.
  - **Clean stack** (recipe 12 of `docs/agents/04_recipes.md`; the default names `erp` / `believe` after `docker compose … down -v`: another `DB_DATABASE` in `migrate:pg` / `docker compose run` recreates the shared PostgreSQL container, so a private pair is made by hand as `createPrivateStores` does): `docker compose -f docker-compose.yml -f docker-compose.dev.yml up -d` → `npm run migrate:pg` (no organization) → `.env` with `AUTH_MODE=on`, `AUTH_SECRET`, `SYSTEM_ADMIN_KEY` → `scripts/create-user.ts admin --system` → start the server → `POST /api/v1/orgs { name }` with `X-System-Key` and `X-Operator` → `ERP_DEFAULT_ORG=<id>` in `.env` for the scripts → recipe 11 steps 1–8 with `--org` (the base data `migrate-sqlite-to-pg.ts data/pub --org <id>` before the `labels` step, which creates 15 owners that the import would refuse; the July 2026 Echo8 report is already named `data/real/stage/2154953_1055956_2026-07-01_2026-07-01.csv`) → `create-user.ts <login> --org <id>` → log in. **Live base** (`erp`): `npm run migrate:pg` creates №1 and stamps every row; `ERP_DEFAULT_ORG=00000000-0000-4000-8000-000000000001`; the server and the scripts run under it; `compare-reference` and `check-owner-scope` as in recipe 11.
- Checked: commit 1 (9e2c19d): `npm run migrate:pg` on the live `erp` created №1 and stamped every row (owners 1 151, tracks 26 305, splits 24 107, `pub_lines` 1 162 586, files 47, operations 2 663, ads 2 686); the API under `ERP_DEFAULT_ORG` gives `compare-reference` 0 differing on every invariant; backend suite 43 files / 482 tests, frontend 94, e2e 166 / 166. Commit 3: `tests/concurrency.test.ts` 4 / 4 (both the Believe and the B17 races; the B17 one found the deletion of the winner's stored copy, fixed above). after commits 2–3 (login, /orgs, the constant 500, the concurrency tests and the B17 race fix): the backend suite 45 files / 502 tests (3 skipped: the branches without a secret in `off`) plus the 4 concurrency tests; `compare-reference` 0 differing against the API in `off` on the live `erp` (organization №1); the frontend: 94 unit tests, `npm run e2e` 169 / 169 under `AUTH_MODE=on` with the login in `globalSetup` (`storageState`), including `36-orgs.spec.ts`; typecheck, lint, `build:client` clean
- Open (the plan's §9; the default acts until the owner decides):
  - В1: the system administrator with `X-Org` sees money until stage 3 (the consilium wanted a system administrator without money; abilities come in stage 3); every write of theirs is in the organization's audit under their login.
  - В2: no password policy (at least 1 character); stage 3: length ≥ 12.
  - В3: no rate limit on login attempts; stage 3 with `auth.sessions`.
  - Revocation: logout is stateless (it clears the cookie; a copied token stays valid until `exp` or a disabled user / a new `AUTH_SECRET`); revocable sessions in stage 3.
  - Transport: the cookie prefix `__Host-` and `Secure` by default need https; until the deployment (stage 3) `Secure` only with `AUTH_COOKIE_SECURE=1`; no `Origin` check yet (CSRF is covered by `SameSite=Strict` and the required non-simple header `X-Operator`).
  - В4: the name of №1 is `Организация №1`; a rename is SQL; `PATCH /orgs/:id` on request.
  - В9: one advisory write lock for the whole database: a Believe upload in `db.transaction` (`facade/sources.ts`) delays the writes of another organization for its duration (accepted, O5); a key `hashtext(org_id)` if the load grows.
  - В11: the Python extractors of stage 4 (`scripts/mvp/*.py`, recipe 9) read `owners.*` without an organization: valid only while the database has one organization; `--org` when a second appears.
  - `GET /auth/login` (no such route) passes the public check and falls to `resolveOrg`: `400 org_required` instead of `404`.
  - `POST /publishing/writers/:norm/map` answers `400` (not `404`) for a writer unknown in the organization, as for any unknown writer before.
  - The audit of a system write when the facade of the target organization fails to open: the write is committed and answered 2xx with `X-Audit: failed` (D-150), no row.
  - В6 / В7: row-level security and composite foreign keys `(org_id, label_id)` as a second lock: candidates for stage 3.
- References: `packages/backend/migrations/postgres/0016_organizations.sql`, `src/shared/{org-scope,private-stores,errors}.ts`, `src/facade/{modules,index,context}.ts`, `src/modules/*/{index,repository,schema,service}.ts`, `src/modules/sources/registry.ts`, `src/connect-sources.ts`, `src/main.ts`, `src/auth/*`, `src/orgs/*`, `src/api/{server-app,system-router,org-router,label-guard,request-id,app,audit,http,openapi,operations,upload}.ts`, `src/api/routes/{auth,orgs,index}.ts`, `scripts/create-user.ts`, the scripts with `connectOrg` (`load-believe`, `migrate-sqlite-to-pg`, `mvp/load`, `mvp/lib`, `assign-*`, `check-*`, …), `.env.example`, `packages/shared/src/{api-schemas,api-responses}.ts`, `tests/{org-scope,auth,http-errors,postgres,api-contract,boundaries,labels,concurrency}.test.ts`, `src/modules/publishing/service.ts` (`load()`), `docs/идеи/лейбл_с_нуля/{бриф_для_агента.md,README.md §4а,консилиум_изоляция/итог.md,api/этап2_организация.md}`, `docs/agents/04_recipes.md` (recipes 11, 12); frontend: `packages/frontend/src/{lib/session.ts,api/queries/auth.ts,api/queries/orgs.ts,components/UserMenu.tsx,pages/auth/LoginPage.tsx,pages/system/{OrgsPage,OrgPage}.tsx,routes/system.tsx,api/client.ts,api/coverage.ts,components/AppShell.tsx}`, `packages/frontend/e2e/{global-setup.ts,golden/36-orgs.spec.ts,helpers.ts}`, `packages/frontend/scripts/e2e/start.sh`, `packages/backend/scripts/e2e/seed.ts` (`pages/auth/LoginPage.tsx`, `pages/system/{OrgsPage,OrgPage}.tsx`, `components/UserMenu.tsx`, `api/queries/{auth,orgs}.ts`, `e2e/golden/36-orgs.spec.ts` in the working tree)
- Status: accepted (the owner's decision of 2026-11-05, variant A)

## D-171: Users, roles and abilities: memberships and revocable sessions, the organization chosen at login, an abilities table on every operation, the viewer's labels in the facade, support access by grant
- Date: 2026-11-06
- Note on numbering: the brief's section «D-172. Пользователи, вход, роли» is the working name of this stage (stage 3 of «label from zero»); the number follows this log (D-170 note), so the stage is D-171. Its migration is `0017_roles.sql` (the brief's `0018_auth.sql`; the brief's catalog stage takes 0018).
- Question: who may do what inside an organization, and how a person who belongs to several organizations signs in. Sources: the brief's «D-172» (roles `system_admin` / `label_admin` / `label_manager`, `access: system | label | label_write | approve` per operation, «Все» only for `system_admin`) and its «Поправки к разделу D-172 (этап 3)» inside «Граница арендатора» (`auth.memberships` with `org_admin` / `label_manager`, `auth.user_labels`, `auth.sessions`, the organization from the membership, `X-Org` only for the system administrator, support access by the organization administrator's grant with a term, transport and limits of the security report); the owner's answers 10–13 of 2026-11-05 (10: concurrent edits are not handled; 11: rights by an abilities layer «like CASL», rules in code, roles in the database; 12: the organization chosen at login, memberships in several organizations, a session for one organization; 13: the system administrator's rights later, the minimum = organizations and users, no money); **README §4а of the idea folder corrects answer 10: the system administrator sees no money, «Все» belongs to the organization administrator** (the brief's «Все только system_admin» is superseded). The approved plan with its independent review and the changes after it: `docs/идеи/лейбл_с_нуля/api/этап3_роли.md` (К1–К14, §1–§8, «Ревью» FIX-1…FIX-8, «После ревью»).
- Decision:
  - **Principals** (`src/auth/abilities.ts` `Principal.kind`; roles stored in the database, rules in code): `org_admin` (a membership with that role; every own label of the organization and «Все»; everything in its organization: reads with and without money, writes, `approve`, `pay`, `admin`); `label_manager` (a membership with that role plus rows of `auth.user_labels`; only its own labels, no «Все»; reads under `?label=` of its labels, catalogue reads and writes without money, uploads and advertising under its label; no `approve`, `pay` or `admin`); `system` (`auth.users.is_system_admin` or `X-System-Key`; no organization and no labels; only `system` operations: organizations, users and memberships of every organization; an organization operation without a grant is `403 support_required`); `support` (a system administrator with `X-Org` under a live grant of that organization's `org_admin`: every book and «Все», `read` and `read_money`, `write` only with `write: true` in the grant, never `approve` / `pay` / `admin`; verified at the commit); `local` (`AUTH_MODE=off`: acts as `org_admin` in `ERP_DEFAULT_ORG`, so tests, scripts and `compare-reference` answer byte for byte as before). A system administrator is never a member of an organization; the last active `org_admin` of an organization cannot be removed, suspended or demoted (`409 last_org_admin`); only the system administrator resets the password of a user who is a member of another organization too (plan В2).
  - **Action classes** (`Action`): `read` (without money), `read_money`, `write` (catalogue or data without money and without approval), `approve` (approval, confirmation, a move between books, binding income to an owner), `pay` (a money operation), `admin` (the organization's settings: labels, accounts, rules, users, support), `system`, `self` (one's own session). **Scope** of an organization operation: `label` (the label the request names: `?label=`, `body.labelId`, `body.items[].labelId`; for a manager required and one of its labels), `record` (the label of the record the request touches; the facade decides by the `Viewer`), `org` (no label, the whole organization). The rule table `RULES` (kind × action, a manager's rule by scope): `org_admin` / `local` all but `system`; `label_manager` `read` / `write` = L / R / allowed, `read_money` = L / R / **refused on `org`** (the organization's totals are «Все»), `approve` / `pay` / `admin` / `system` refused; `support` `read` / `read_money` allowed, `write` only under a write grant (`support_read_only`), the rest refused; `system` only `system` (every organization action `support_required`); `self` everyone.
  - **The counts** (`operations.ts` at 88926ed; frozen literal `tests/helpers/manager-answers.ts`): 85 operations = 3 public, 2 user (`GET /auth/me`, new `POST /auth/password`), 5 system, **75 organization**: read 16 / read_money 21 / write 14 / approve 12 / pay 6 / admin 6; scope label 26 / record 16 / org 33. **A label manager keeps 46**: L 24 (only under one of its labels), R 16 (the record decides), O 6 (`POST /owners`, `POST /artists`, `GET /tracks`, `POST /tracks`, `GET /labels`, `GET /publishing/writers`); **29 are refused** (`403 forbidden`: every `approve`, `pay`, `admin` and the five `read_money` with scope `org`: `/overview`, `/reconciliation`, `/tracks/:isrc/{money,explain,breakdown/:month}`). Changes against the plan's first draft (after the review): `PATCH /tracks/:id` is W with scope R (the ISRC rule for the old and the new ISRC), `POST /publishing/writers/resolve` is A; `/search`, `/artists/duplicates`, `GET /splits`, `GET /participations` are R (filtered by the viewer). The system administrator gets only the system operations, and the organization's data only as support by grant.
  - **Data model** (`packages/backend/migrations/postgres/0017_roles.sql`, idempotent; Drizzle `src/auth/schema.ts`): `auth.memberships (user_id → auth.users, org_id, role CHECK IN ('org_admin','label_manager'), created_at, created_by, disabled_at, PRIMARY KEY (user_id, org_id))` with `memberships_org`; `disabled_at` suspends one membership without touching the others; `auth.user_labels (user_id, org_id, label_id, created_at, created_by, PK (user_id, org_id, label_id), FK (user_id, org_id) → memberships ON DELETE CASCADE)`: own labels only (`400 label_not_own` checked through the organization's facade, `src/orgs/service.ts` `assertOwnLabels`; a foreign label `404 not_found`), no rows for an `org_admin`; `auth.sessions (id, user_id → users, org_id NULL only for a system administrator, issued_at, expires_at, revoked_at, CHECK expires_at > issued_at)` with `sessions_user … WHERE revoked_at IS NULL` (expired rows older than seven days are deleted at a login); `auth.support_grants (id, org_id, user_id → users, write, granted_by, granted_at, expires_at, revoked_at, revoked_by, CHECK expires_at > granted_at)` with `support_grants_active (org_id, user_id) WHERE revoked_at IS NULL`. `org_id` has no foreign key to `orgs.organizations` (D-121 style; the service checks). The stage-2 `auth.users.org_id` moves into an `org_admin` membership (`created_by = 'migration 0017'`; every stage-2 user had full access, so nobody is narrowed without a decision), then its CHECK `users_org`, its index and the column are dropped (one source of a user's organization). `audit.actions` is unchanged (plan К9): the operator stays the login; `auth` / `orgs` stay outside `MODULE_SCHEMAS`.
  - **The login** (`POST /auth/login { login, password, orgId? }`, `src/auth/service.ts` `login` + `openSession`, `src/api/routes/auth.ts`): one `401 invalid_credentials` for an unknown login, a wrong password and a disabled user (an unknown login is checked against `dummyHash()`); a right password: a system administrator gets a session without an organization; a member with one active membership a session in it; with several and no `orgId` (or an `orgId` not theirs) `409 org_choice_required` with `details.orgs: [{ id, name, role }]` (only after a right password; no session made); with none `403 no_membership`. The session row is written in a write transaction that reads the user again: disabled since the check, or a hash other than the verified one (a reset or change raced the login), is `401 invalid_credentials` and no session. The login takes only `application/json`, has no `X-Operator` and leaves no audit row; the server log gets the request id, the login and the outcome (`ok`, `refused`, `limited`, `no membership`, `organization to choose`). Changing the organization = log out and in again (plan В11).
  - **Sessions**: the token is `{ sub, sid, iat, exp }` (HS256 by `node:crypto`, 12 h, no renewal; a stage-2 token without `sid` is refused, so **everyone signs in again after the deployment of 0017**); on every request the session row (`sid`: not revoked, not expired, of this `sub`), the user (not disabled) and the membership of the session's organization (active; role and labels as they are now) are read again, three primary-key reads, no cache (plan В9); anything else = `401 unauthenticated` (a revoked session, a suspended membership: no separate code, plan К11). Revocation: `POST /auth/logout` revokes the cookie's session (if the signature is right) and clears the cookie, always 200; `POST /auth/password { current, next }` (user, `self`; `401 invalid_credentials` for a wrong current one) revokes every other session of the user and keeps the current one, and leaves a row in the session organization's audit without values (none for a system administrator); `create-user.ts --reset-password` / `--disable` revoke all sessions of the user; suspending a membership revokes the sessions of that organization [commit 4: to be verified for the API path].
  - **The limiter** (`src/auth/limits.ts`): only **failed** attempts count, per login (`AUTH_LOGIN_LIMIT`, default 5) and per client IP (`AUTH_IP_LIMIT`, default 20) in a sliding minute; every attempt is recorded as a failure **before** the password is checked (synchronously: parallel attempts cannot all pass while scrypt runs) and a right one takes it back (the login's counter is cleared, the address loses only that attempt's stamp). Over the limit: `429 too_many_attempts` with `Retry-After` (seconds), before the password is checked; `0` = off (tests, the e2e stand). The counter belongs to one app instance (`createServerApp` / `createApp`), not a module singleton; several processes would need a table (plan В7). `TRUST_PROXY` sets Express `trust proxy` (`req.ip`) and allows `X-Forwarded-Host` / `-Proto` in the Origin check: `1` / `true` = **one hop** (never Express `true` = trust all), a number = that many hops, `loopback` or a list as Express takes them; empty / `0` / `false` = off.
  - **The cookie**: one name per process (`sessionCookieName(config)`: reading, setting, clearing, OpenAPI `securitySchemes.session.name`, the contract test): by default `erp_session; HttpOnly; SameSite=Strict; Path=/; Max-Age=43200` without `Secure` (the development stand, the LAN and e2e run over http; plan FIX-1, В10 closed); `AUTH_COOKIE_SECURE=1` (only behind https) = `__Host-erp_session` with `Secure`. `Cache-Control: no-store` on every answer of `/api`.
  - **The Origin rule** (`originGuard`, on every write under `/api` in both modes, the login included), on top of `SameSite=Strict` and the required non-simple `X-Operator`: a `Sec-Fetch-Site`, when present, must be `same-origin` or `none`; an `Origin`, when present, must equal one of `APP_ORIGIN` (comma separated) or the request's own origin `<scheme>://<raw Host header with its port>` (`X-Forwarded-*` only under `TRUST_PROXY`; never `req.hostname`); otherwise `403 csrf`. Scripts and supertest send no `Origin` and pass; the Vite proxy (no `changeOrigin`) keeps `Host: localhost:5173`, which matches the browser's `Origin`.
  - **Passwords** (`src/auth/password.ts`): new hashes scrypt N=32768 (2^15), r=8, p=1 (32 MiB, `maxmem` 64 MiB), at most four derivations at once; `dummyHash()` on the same parameters (an unknown login takes as long); the stored string describes its parameters, so a stage-2 hash (N=16384) is checked by its own and **rehashed at the next right login only if it is still the hash that was verified** (`replacePasswordHash(id, old, new)`: a concurrent reset is never overwritten). No password policy (≥ 1 character; plan К8, В1).
  - **The abilities layer** (`src/auth/abilities.ts`, its own pure module, **no CASL**: about twenty rules and two conditions — «the label is the user's» and «the grant allows writes»; CASL's value is field conditions in database queries (`accessibleBy`), which are not used here: the data-dependent checks live in the facade; the backend dependency would not be guarded by the D-154 ledger. This is the «CASL-like layer» of answer 11: the interface `can()` stays if CASL is ever needed). Every non-public operation of `src/api/operations.ts` carries a required `ability: { action, subject, scope }` (`scope` only on organization operations; `access` of D-170 stays and still decides the chain, plan К2); OpenAPI `x-ability`. `operationOf(method, path)` matches as Express 5 does (static segments win over `:param`, static segments lower-cased, a trailing `/` dropped, the method upper-cased); a request that reaches no operation (or not an organization operation) is `404 not_found`, never `next()` (plan FIX-5). **One `authorize` middleware** in the organization router after `labelGuard` (`operatorTrace` → `labelGuard` (a label of another organization = `404 not_found`, D-170) → `authorize` → v1): `can(principal, ability, labelsOfRequest)` answers `400 label_required` (a manager's `label` operation without a label, or an `items[]` entry without one), `403 label_forbidden` (a label of the organization that is not the principal's, whatever the scope), `403 forbidden` (`details.ability` = `action:subject`), `403 support_required` / `support_read_only`; a refused upload's body is drained first. The system router uses the same `can()` per route (`authorizeRoute`); **`requireSystem` is gone**, `requireUser` stays for `self`. `res.locals.principal` is built once by `resolveOrg` (or `requestPrincipal` outside an organization); routes never see roles. `GET /auth/me` returns `{ authMode, user: { id, login, isSystemAdmin }, org, role, labels, allLabels, memberships, support, abilities }`, where `abilities` = the `METHOD /path` keys `allowedOperations(principal)` allows without the data (a `label` operation counts when the principal has a label; `record` ones count): **R4 — the backend decides, the UI only reads the list** (no second rule table on the frontend). The contract test checks: `ability` on every non-public operation; `scope: 'label'` ⇒ the schema names `query.label`, `body.labelId` or `body.items[].labelId`, and any other operation naming a label must be `label` except the explicit `RELABEL_OPERATIONS` (`PATCH /documents/:id`, `PATCH /settlements/operations/:id`, `PATCH /ads/:id`: the body's `labelId` is the target of a move between books); `access: 'system'` ⇔ `action: 'system'`; a `GET` is never `write` / `approve` / `pay` / `admin`.
  - **The facade's `Viewer`** (`src/facade/viewer.ts`; the facade never imports `src/auth`, `src/api` or `src/orgs` and never names a role: `tests/boundaries.test.ts`): `type Viewer = { kind: 'all' } | { kind: 'labels', labelIds }`; `ALL_VIEWER` is the default of every record-dependent method (a trailing `viewer = ALL_VIEWER`), so every existing caller, every script and the `off` answers are byte-identical; `src/api/viewer.ts` `viewerOf(res)` builds it from the principal (`all` for `org_admin`, `local`, support; `labelsViewer(ids)` for a manager; no principal = 401). Rules: **a read of another label's record answers like an unknown id (404)** — the owner and the pseudonym card and balance (documents and contracts of other labels hidden), an ad (by its payer), document uses; **a write on another label's record or ISRC is `403 label_forbidden`** — ads update / deactivate (the payer; a `labelId` in `PATCH /ads/:id` from a manager is `forbidden`), operations by their label (proof, deferred, deactivate, return, relabel, the snapshot), documents (attach, approve, set label: `null` = «any label» passes for a document but setting it is the administrator's), splits and participations (create, change, confirm, buyout, close), `PATCH /tracks/:id` (the old and the new ISRC), contracts (the document rule and a reachable owner). **The ISRC rule**: a manager may touch an ISRC that is in the loaded files of its labels **or in no loaded file at all** (a new track, no book yet); an ISRC only in other files is another book. **The document rule**: a cited document of the viewer's label or of none. **The lists** `GET /splits`, `/participations`, `/search`, `/artists/duplicates` contain only the rows visible under the viewer's labels (the union of `labelVisibility()` of each, D-168: owners, pseudonyms, ISRCs; an owner visible under no own label at all is reachable as a new one, `ownerReachable`). `viewerScope(modules, viewer)` reads each set once per call and nothing for `ALL_VIEWER`. New facade methods replace the routes' direct module calls (`attachDocumentFile`, `approveDocument`, `createContract`, `updateTrack`, `setOperationDeferred`, `deactivateOperation`, `operationSnapshot`): `routes/settlements.ts` no longer touches `facade.modules`. `calculation.ts`, `layers/*`, `balance.ts`, `owner-scope.ts`, `scope.ts`, `label-scope.ts`, `payout-gate.ts`, ClickHouse and the data modules are untouched; «Σ of what the managers of the labels see plus “without a label” = «Все»» is the existing `tests/scope.test.ts` invariant (Σ books = total, D-168/D-169).
  - **Commit 4** (as committed: `src/auth/{repository,service,abilities}.ts`, `src/orgs/middleware.ts`, `src/api/{operations,audit,me,org-admin-router,server-app,system-router}.ts`, `src/api/routes/{users,support,system-users,orgs,auth}.ts`, `packages/shared/src/{api-schemas,api-responses}.ts`, `tests/users.test.ts`; `tests/support.test.ts` not yet; the behaviour below is read from that tree and is verified at the commit against the commit): the members of an organization by its `org_admin` — `GET /users`, `POST /users` (a new login with a membership: `409 login_taken`, `400 label_not_own`, a manager needs labels), `PATCH /users/:id` (`updateMemberBody { role?, labelIds?, active? }`: the labels replaced as a whole, `active: false` suspends the membership and ends the user's sessions there, `409 last_org_admin` unless an active administrator remains, 404 for a non-member), `POST /users/:id/password` (`setPasswordBody`; every session of the user ends; `403 forbidden` when the user is also a member of another organization) — all `admin`, scope `org`; the system operations — `GET/POST /orgs/:id/users` (members with role and labels), `PATCH /orgs/:id/users/:userId`, `POST /orgs/:id/members` (`addMemberBody`: an existing login; `404 not_found`, `409 already_member`, `400` for a system administrator), `POST /orgs/:id/support/enter`, `GET /system/users` (`systemUserSchema`: every user with memberships and their state, never a hash), `PATCH /system/users/:id` (`updateSystemUserBody { disabled }`, disabling revokes every session), `POST /system/users/:id/password`; support grants of the organization — `GET /support` (the 50 newest, expired and revoked included, `supportGrantSchema` with `active`), `POST /support` (`grantSupportBody { login, expiresAt?, write = false }`: default 24 h, at most **7 days**, in the future, else `400 validation_error`; a login that is not an active system administrator is `404 not_found`, the answer of an unknown one), `POST /support/:id/revoke` (idempotent). `resolveOrg`: a system administrator with `X-Org` → the organization's facade first (`404 org_not_found`), then the support state of `(org, user)` (`supportState`: `live` with the latest end and `write` if any live grant allows it; else `expired` → `403 support_expired`; else `403 support_required`) and the principal `support`; **the system key with `X-Org` = `403 support_required`** (plan К7: the key never works in an organization; until commit 4 the system administrator and the key with `X-Org` acted as `org_admin` of that organization, a transitional state of commits 1–3); `me.support = { orgId, expiresAt, write }` for a support session; every write of support is in the organization's audit under its login with `summary.support = true` (also the row of `POST /orgs/:id/support/enter`); the summaries of member and grant writes keep `role`, `active`, `write`, `expiresAt`, never a login or a password; `/system/users*` writes leave no audit row (no organization; `noOrgTrace`), the server log names the request id, the target and the outcome. Counts in the tree: 13 new operations (7 `admin` with scope `org`, refused to a manager in the frozen table, and 6 `system`; with `POST /auth/password` of commit 1 the plan's 14): **98 operations = 3 public / 2 user / 11 system / 82 organization (admin 13)**; a manager keeps 46, refused 36 [commit 4: to be verified at the commit].
  - **Frontend as planned** the frontend of commit 5: the second login step (`field-login-org`), the user menu with the password change, the sidebar by `me.abilities`, the label switch limited to `me.labels` («Все» only with `allLabels`), the support banner, `ActionButton op` required on write buttons and disabled «нет права» when the key is not in `me.abilities` (R4; `tests/action-ops.test.ts`), P26 «Пользователи» (`pages/users/`: members and the «Поддержка» tab), P27 `/system/users`, P25 members and «Работать в организации» through the support enter; `coverage.ts` for every new operation: P23 the login with the organization choice as a second step (`login-org-<id>`), `429` and `no_membership` phrases; `useMe()` with role, labels, `allLabels`, `abilities`; `LabelSwitch` from `me.labels` («Все» only with `allLabels`); `ActionButton op` required by type on write buttons (closed with the reason «Доступно администратору организации» when the key is not in `me.abilities`), `NavItem op` (a manager does not see «Обзор»); P26 «Пользователи» (members and the support tab), P27 `/system/users`, P24/P25 additions, the support banner; `coverage.ts` + 14; e2e `37-roles.spec.ts`, `36-orgs.spec.ts` under a grant.
  - **The e2e seed** (`packages/backend/scripts/e2e/seed.ts`): `e2e-operator` = `org_admin` of A (the golden specs unchanged), `e2e-b` = `org_admin` of B, `e2e-multi` = `org_admin` of A and B (the login asks to choose; the plan had `label_manager` in A), `e2e-manager` = `label_manager` of A with the 0to8 label (added in commit 2, so it never had full access in any commit), `e2e-sysadmin`; passwords in `.seed-keys.json`; the e2e stand sets `AUTH_LOGIN_LIMIT=0 AUTH_IP_LIMIT=0` and keeps `AUTH_COOKIE_SECURE` empty.
  - **Scripts**: `scripts/create-user.ts <login> --org <id> [--role org_admin|label_manager] [--label <labelId>]…` (a new user as a member, or a new membership of an existing login; `--role` defaults to `org_admin`; a label must be an own label of the organization; a second membership in the same organization, an unknown organization, label or user = exit 1), `--system`, `--reset-password`, `--disable` (both revoke the sessions), `--enable`; the password only from `ERP_NEW_PASSWORD` or stdin. Every other script runs in `AUTH_MODE=off` semantics (it opens the facade directly, `local` = `ALL_VIEWER`). Recipes 11 and 12 no longer use `X-System-Key` + `X-Org` for organization steps.
  - **Environment** (read by `authConfigFromEnv`, `src/auth/middleware.ts`): `AUTH_LOGIN_LIMIT` (default 5), `AUTH_IP_LIMIT` (default 20; whole numbers, 0 = off, anything else stops the server with a readable error), `TRUST_PROXY`, `APP_ORIGIN`, `AUTH_COOKIE_SECURE` besides the D-170 ones; at 88926ed `.env.example` lists only `AUTH_COOKIE_SECURE` of these added in commit 5.
  - **Not done in this stage**: a password policy (plan В1); a list of one's own sessions and «sign out everywhere» (В8; disabling and a password change revoke); `__Host-` / `Secure` by default (only behind https with `AUTH_COOKIE_SECURE=1`); a session cache (В9); a login-attempt table for several processes (В7); `POST /auth/switch` (В11); the system administrator's rights beyond the minimum of answer 13 (organizations, users, memberships, support by grant); `/overview` and the reconciliation per label for a manager (В3), the money of one ISRC for a manager (В4); PostgreSQL row-level security and composite foreign keys (В14); `audit.actions.user_id` (К9); fine-grained rights of managers («Позже» of the brief).
- Checked: commits 1–2 (0fe12ce): migration 0017 applied twice (`tests/postgres.test.ts`); `tests/auth.test.ts` (the multi-membership flow, `no_membership`, limits incl. ten parallel attempts, the `Origin` / `Sec-Fetch-Site` cases, the `__Host-` app, the conditional rehash and the race with a reset), `tests/sessions.test.ts`, `tests/abilities.test.ts` (the frozen manager table of the plan, the pure rule table, `operationOf` incl. `/Owners/merge` and a trailing `/`), `tests/api-contract.test.ts` (`ability` required; the manager walk under label A / no label / label B against the frozen literal; `me.abilities`); **backend 48 files / 542 tests; frontend 94; e2e 169 / 169**. Commit 3 (88926ed): `tests/viewer.test.ts` (another label's record → 404 / 403, own and «any label» pass, the ISRC rule, the lists; `ALL_VIEWER` = no viewer, byte for byte, for 11 methods, a viewer of both labels too), `tests/boundaries.test.ts` (the facade imports neither api / auth / orgs nor names a role), the contract manager walk with record-level cases (an ad and a document of B) the viewer tests and 16 touched files green; the full suite after commit 4: 51 files / 559 tests. Commits 4–5: `tests/users.test.ts`, `tests/support.test.ts`, the contract test with the new operations, e2e `37-roles` after commit 4 the backend suite is 51 files / 559 tests (5 skipped: the mode-specific blocks); after commit 5 the frontend has 99 unit tests and `npm run e2e` gives 172 / 172 under the login (36-orgs with the support grant, the new 37-roles). `compare-reference` against the API in `AUTH_MODE=off` on the live `erp`: 0 differing on every invariant confirmed by the orchestrator after commits 3 and 4: `compare-reference` in `off` 0 differing.
- Open (the plan's §8; the default acts until the owner decides):
  - В1: no password policy (≥ 1 character); one constant turns it on.
  - В2: an `org_admin` may not reset the password of a member who belongs to another organization too; only the system administrator.
  - В3: «Обзор» and the reconciliation are hidden from a manager (a projection by label from the `byLabel` rows on request).
  - В4: the money of one ISRC (`/tracks/:isrc/{money,explain,breakdown}`) only for `org_admin`; `?label=` later.
  - В5: the catalogue without people (`/tracks`, `/labels`) is shared inside the organization; people (owners, pseudonyms, split and participation rows) only as visible under the manager's labels; `GET /publishing/writers` stays whole.
  - В6: a write grant of support allows `write` only, never `approve` / `pay` / `admin`.
  - В7: the login limit lives in the memory of one process; `auth.login_attempts` when a second process appears.
  - В8: no session list or «sign out everywhere».
  - В9: no session cache (three primary-key reads per request).
  - Further, from commit 3: an ISRC that appears only in a file **without a label** counts as another book for a manager (it is a loaded file not of its labels); `documentUses.waiting` is a total across books (the document is visible, the sum is not split by label); `search` filters by the viewer after the per-kind limit of 10, so a manager may get fewer than 10 hits of a kind; `ownerReachable` reads the visibility of every own label on the manager's path (cost grows with the labels); an operation without a label is the administrator's (`viewerHolds`: `null` is seen only by `ALL_VIEWER`); the per-login lockout is by design (anyone can lock a known login out for one minute with wrong passwords; the owner signs in after the window); a stage-2 hash answers a wrong password faster (N=2^14) until it is rehashed at the next right login.
- References: `packages/backend/migrations/postgres/0017_roles.sql`, `src/auth/{abilities,service,repository,schema,middleware,limits,token,password}.ts`, `src/orgs/{middleware,service}.ts`, `src/api/{authorize,viewer,label-guard,org-router,server-app,app,system-router,openapi,operations,audit}.ts`, `src/api/routes/{auth,orgs,owners,settlements,splits,ads,reports}.ts`, `src/facade/{viewer,index,owner-view,artist-view,search,splits,payouts,ads-api}.ts`, `scripts/create-user.ts`, `scripts/e2e/seed.ts`, `packages/shared/src/{api-schemas,api-responses}.ts`, `tests/{abilities,auth,sessions,viewer,api-contract,boundaries,postgres}.test.ts`, `tests/helpers/manager-answers.ts`; commit 4: `tests/{users,support}.test.ts`, `src/api/routes/{users,support}.ts` verified; commit 5: the frontend (see the commit messages); `docs/идеи/лейбл_с_нуля/{бриф_для_агента.md («D-172», «Поправки к разделу D-172»), README.md §4а, api/этап3_роли.md}`, `docs/agents/04_recipes.md` (recipes 11, 12).
- Status: accepted (the owner's answers of 2026-11-05; README §4а)

## D-172: Onboarding through the UI: split import with a preview, «accept as an assumption», the label decision kind, the label history of an ISRC from the files, releases from the Believe export, the onboarding checklist
- Date: 2026-11-06
- Note on numbering: the brief's section «D-171. Онбординг через UI: импорт сплитов, «принять как допущение», чек-лист, история ISRC, релизы» is the working name of this stage (stage 4 of «label from zero»); D-171 in this log is users and roles, so the stage is D-172. Its migration is `0018_catalog.sql` (the brief's `0017_catalog.sql`; 0017 is the roles migration).
- Question: how a new label is set up through the UI, not by the stage 4 JSON loader: its splits from a file with a preview, the rows without a document accepted as an assumption, a «label decision» that pays without a contract, the label an ISRC belongs to over time, its releases, and one list of what is set up and what is left. Sources: the brief (`docs/идеи/лейбл_с_нуля/бриф_для_агента.md`) section «D-171. Онбординг через UI…», row 4 of «Порядок этапов», the owner's decisions 5 and 7 («Решения овнера по пунктам ⚠»), the owner's answers 5–7 («Ответы владельца продукта», 2026-11-05: 6 = starting balances are a separate entity «счета компании», later; 7 = the label decision has no term and confirms the owner and the split row), the idea folder's `README.md` §1 item 8 («каталог это навигация, не деньги»: the history is derived from the files, not typed in) and §5 «Чего не делать»; the approved plan `docs/идеи/лейбл_с_нуля/api/этап4_онбординг.md` (whole, with «Ревью» and «После ревью»: FIX 1–10 and the NOTEs applied).
- Decision:
  - **The brief vs the code** (the plan's mismatch table К1–К16): the migration is `0018_catalog.sql` (К1); **starting balances are out** of the stage (К2, answer 6): `adjustment` and the import of operations stay with the payroll stage, «счета компании» goes to «Позже», the checklist has no «balances» step; the label decision is **in** (К3, decision 7 and answer 7); the brief's preview class `percent_sum ≠ 100` is `split_sum_above_100` (error, the module's code) plus `our_share` (info, a sum below 100 is legal: the rest is ours) (К4); `row_or_participation` is the API's own code `double_participation` (К5); `overlaps_existing` is the class `replaces` (a change of an ISRC that has a split, like the loader) and `split_overlap` between two groups of the file (К6); the template's `document_number` is a synonym of `document` = a document id or its exact title (documents have no number; only `contracts.number` has one) (К7); the assumption is the **split's** document through `confirmSplit`, not a new split version per row (К8); the loader-equivalence test goes through a converter and `MVP_LOAD_DIR` (К9); P28 lives in `pages/reference/splits/import/` (К10); releases are keyed `(org_id, label_id, upc)` beside the computed releases (К11); the catalog under a label is the union files ∪ open interval with a «catalog label» column and `catalog=current` (К12); the release import button is on P7, the registry in the header of P8 (К13); the preview leaves no audit row and the import's summary comes from the response (К14); the real count is 2 436 ISRCs, not 2 437 (К15); an operator correction does not freeze the files and there is no `created_by` (К16).
  - **Split import** (`POST /splits/import?label=&dryRun=1|0`, upload; `facade/split-import.ts` `importSplits`; columns `SPLIT_IMPORT_COLUMNS` in `packages/shared/src/split-import.ts`: `isrc, pseudonym, percent, from` required, `to, owner, document, artist_id, note` optional, headers without case, `document_number` = `document`, an unknown or missing column = `400 import_header` with `details.missing` / `unknown`). Csv (the delimiter `,` or `;` from the header line) or xlsx (first sheet). At most **5 000 rows and 10 MB** (`SPLIT_IMPORT_MAX_ROWS` / `_BYTES`): more = `413 import_too_large` (the stream stops at row 5 001; the size limit before the body is stored). One group = (ISRC, `from` floored, M52) = one split; its rows are the artist rows. The groups are classified **in file order over a model of the ISRC's splits that every earlier group updates** (as the loader's `ctx.model`: the second group of a new ISRC is a `change`). Decisions: `create` (the ISRC has no split → `facade.createSplit`), `change` (it has one and the rows in force at `from` differ → `facade.changeSplit` from `from`; a document common to the group is required; a row with the same percent as before keeps its document, the loader's `keepRowDocs … 'same_percent'`), `unchanged` (same artists and percents in force: nothing; the loader's `already_equal`), `blocked` (an error row). A document common to every row = the split's document, else every row carries its own; rows without a document load without one and wait in C `split_unconfirmed`, **never** as an assumption. `origin = 'import'` on created owners, pseudonyms and splits; `note` from the column.
  - **Preview and apply**: the preview (`dryRun=1`, the default, 200) runs in one `db.snapshot()` without the write lock and writes nothing; the apply (`dryRun=0`, 201) repeats the classification inside **one** `db.transaction()` and then, in file order, `owners.createOwner` (kind `person`) / `createArtist` / `assignArtistOwner` (each once per name) and `facade.createSplit` / `changeSplit` (nested transactions join): **all or nothing**. Any `error` row = `422 import_invalid` with `details.rows: [{ line, code }]` (first 200) and `details.errors`; a refusal of the facade while writing = `422 import_invalid` with `details.group` and `details.cause` (the facade's own code, e.g. `split_overlap`); nothing is written either way. The answer (`R.splitImportSchema`): `labelId, fileName, sha256, dryRun, applied, totals { rows, groups, create, change, unchanged, errors, warnings, artistsNew, ownersNew }, rows[] { line, isrc, pseudonym, artistId, percent, from, fromFloored, to, toFloored, owner, documentId, group, issues[] }, groups[] { key, isrc, from, to, action, ourShare, documentId, before }, result { splitIds, artistIds, ownerIds } | null`.
  - **The pseudonym-index cache per transaction** (plan FIX-4): `createSplit` / `changeSplit` / `resolveShares` take an optional last argument `opts: SplitWriteOpts = { index?: ArtistIndexCache }` (`facade/context.ts` `artistIndexCache`, `cacheArtist`): the import builds `owners.artistIndex()` once at the start of its transaction and adds every pseudonym it creates; routes and other callers pass nothing (signature and behaviour for the API unchanged). The modules are never bypassed.
  - **Row classes** (`SPLIT_IMPORT_ISSUES`, one list for the backend and the frontend; `error` blocks the apply, `warning` / `info` do not): errors `isrc_invalid`, `percent_invalid` (≤ 0 or not a number), `date_invalid`, `row_incomplete`, `empty_period` (`to` ≤ `from` after flooring), `to_on_change` (a `change` group carries `to`: a replacement lasts to the end of the replaced split), `split_sum_above_100`, `duplicate_artist`, `split_overlap` (two `create` groups of one ISRC of the file overlap), `double_participation` (D-126), `document_required` (a `change` without a common document), `document_not_found`, `document_ambiguous`, `document_kind` (not in `SPLIT_DOCUMENT_KINDS`), `document_other_label` (a document of another own label; none = any label passes), `pseudonym_ambiguous` (several pseudonyms with the name: give `artist_id`), `artist_not_found`, `owner_ambiguous`, `owner_mismatch` (an existing pseudonym has another owner, or two rows give one new pseudonym two owners), `label_forbidden`, `forbidden`; warnings `replaces` (`details.before`), `new_pseudonym` (`details.similar`: the same name or the same words in another order, the candidates of `DuplicateDialog`), `owner_new` (created, kind `person`), `owner_assign` (an existing pseudonym without an owner gets one), `isrc_outside_label` (`details.book` = `none`, a new track, or `other` with the label); infos `date_floored` (shown floored), `our_share`, `unchanged`, `existing`, `owner_found` (by name or alias, exactly one). The codes equal the API's wherever the API gives such a refusal.
  - **A label manager** (D-171 viewer) may import under its label but only **create**: a `change` group and an `owner_assign` are `forbidden` rows (the approve class), an ISRC in another label's files is `label_forbidden` (the ISRC rule of D-171; an ISRC in no loaded file passes), an existing owner not visible under its labels is `label_forbidden`. The writes inside the apply run as `ALL_VIEWER` (the import applied the rules itself). Pseudonyms created by the import are visible to the label through the ISRC (`labelVisibility`, D-169), no separate link.
  - **The loader equivalence** (`tests/split-import-loader.test.ts`, plan FIX-8): a synthetic `annex/splits_diff.json` (+ `participations.json`) in the stage 4 format; store A runs the loader's `stepSplits --apply`, store B runs the converter `scripts/mvp/splits-diff-to-csv.ts` (the same filters and order as the step: `same` / `erp_only` / `different_period` and other classes skipped, M51, ISRCs of `splitRewrites`, `pp` buy-outs of `different_rows`, only rows with a percent above 0, a record without rows skipped, `from ?? '2000-01-01'`; `artist_id` always written; `note` = the loader's marker `mvp:splits:<isrc>:<from>`) and `POST /splits/import?dryRun=0`; `listSplits()` of both, projected to text (ISRC, from, to, row pseudonyms, percents, row and split document by title; no ids, notes, origins), are equal. Preconditions checked by the test: the step logs no `split_error` / `unresolved_name`, the diff has no ambiguous name without an id, and `MVP_LOAD_DIR` is set before the dynamic `await import` of the loader. **`scripts/mvp/lib.ts` `LOAD_DIR = process.env.MVP_LOAD_DIR ?? <repo>/data/mvp-load`** (one line; D-131 refined): with the variable, `connectOrg({ dataDir })` of `load.ts` and `steps-main.ts` (`LOAD_DIR/../..`) look under its directory too; without it nothing changes. `load.ts` and its steps are not rewritten (idea README §5). For a **new** label the template import replaces the JSON loader; the loader stays for the history of 0to8 / Echo8 (plan О5, recipe 9).
  - **«Accept as an assumption»** (`POST /splits/assume { labelId, isrcs | splitIds (exactly one), reason (3–2000) }` → 201 `{ documentId, assigned: [splitId], skipped: [{ splitId, code: 'has_document' | 'empty_period' }] }`; `facade/splits.ts` `assumeSplits`). In one `db.transaction()`: the group (every version of the listed ISRCs, or the listed split ids) is filtered to the splits without a split document, with a non-empty period and at least one row without its own document; **one** document `{ kind: 'assumption', title: 'Допущение: ' + reason (≤ 200), labelId, requiresApproval: true }` is created (no file; never approved: `assumption_not_approvable`) and set by `splits.confirmSplit(id, doc)` on each, so the rows without their own document inherit it (`layers/split.ts`) and their money moves from C `split_unconfirmed` to C `assumption` (`categorise.ts` unchanged). Nothing to assume = `422 nothing_to_assume` and no document is created (a repeat writes nothing). Errors: `400 label_required`, `404` (label, split), `400 label_not_own`, `403 label_forbidden` (a manager: the ISRC rule). The right is **approve** (`approve:splits:label`): a label manager is refused. Removing an assumption = `changeSplit` with a real document (P14 «only replaced»). The loader step `assumption-rows` is unchanged (it sets row documents).
  - **The label decision** (`label_decision`): a new document kind (`packages/shared/src/document-kinds.ts`: `DOCUMENT_KINDS`, `CONTRACT_DOCUMENT_KINDS = ['contract', 'label_decision']`, `SPLIT_DOCUMENT_KINDS = ['appendix', 'contract', 'buyout', 'other', 'assumption', 'label_decision']`; the backend's `modules/owners/service.ts` and `facade/context.ts` and the frontend take the lists from there; the CHECK of `owners.documents.kind` in 0018). `owners.createContract` accepts a document of a kind in `CONTRACT_DOCUMENT_KINDS` (an `appendix` stays `validation_error`); `checkSplitDocument` accepts it as a split document, a row document and a participation document. Approvable like any document, without a term (`upload_deadline` empty, answer 7 / Н3), no bulk buttons (decision 7). **Money**: an approved label decision confirms the owner exactly like a contract (`computeConfirmedOwners` / `approvedContractLabel` look at the status and the label of the contract document, not its kind) and a row exactly like an appendix (`categorise` looks at the kind only for `assumption`), so the money is in B; unapproved = `owner_unconfirmed` / `split_unconfirmed` / `rule_unconfirmed` as any unapproved document. `calculation.ts`, the layers, D-124 (the unit key) and D-126 (row or participation) do not change; the payroll column «по решению, без договора» is the payroll stage's, by the kind of the contract document. Golden case **G61** (`docs/agents/12_golden_cases.md`): rows A 30, B 20, participation P 10 `(net, top)`, B confirmed by a contract on a `label_decision` document, B's row and P's participation cite an approved `label_decision`: P 10, A 27, B 18, all B, ours 45; before approval B's 18 is C `owner_unconfirmed`; the same scenario with kind `contract` gives the same numbers.
  - **The label history of an ISRC** (`owners.track_labels (org_id, id, isrc, label_id, valid_from, valid_to, source IN ('files','operator','import'), note, created_at)`, first of the month CHECKs, `EXCLUDE USING gist (org_id WITH =, isrc WITH =, daterange(valid_from, valid_to, '[)') WITH &&)`, indexes `track_labels_org`, `track_labels_open … WHERE valid_to IS NULL`; no `owners.tracks.label_id`, no `created_by` (idea README §2: the author is the audit); `import` is reserved, nothing writes it). `facade/catalog.ts`; navigation, **not money** (idea README §1 item 8).
  - **Derivation from the files** (`deriveIsrc`, plan FIX-1): an observation = (ISRC, reporting month of a `loaded` Believe file, the file's label `source_files.label_id`), read by one `GROUP BY` (`sources.isrcFilePairs`); files without a label are skipped and counted. Per ISRC the months run in order with a «current» label: (1) a month with only the current label, or with nothing, continues its run; (2) a label **without a run so far** opens one in that month even when the current label is observed too (the first appearance wins the month: the latest run wins); two such labels in one month: the later loaded file (`loaded_at`), then the smaller `label_id` (`ties`); (3) a label **that had a run** is a **tail** while the current label is still observed in that month or later (its units are the signal `catalogOutsideAccount`), otherwise it **returns** and opens a further interval from that month; (4) the boundary between runs A and B is `clamp(last_A + 1, start_B, first_B)` = `min(max(last_A + 1 month, start_B), first_B)`, where `last_A` is A's last month before `first_B`, `first_B` B's first month on the ISRC and `start_B` the first reporting month of any loaded file of B (the start of its account); the first interval starts at the first observation, the last is open; (5) the result = the runs minus the months of the `operator` intervals (a derived interval is shortened or split, never moved); the `files` rows of an ISRC are replaced as a whole, `operator` rows never. `scripts/derive-track-labels.ts [dataDir] [--apply] [--org <id>]` (dry run by default; `--apply` in one write transaction, without an audit row like the D-131 scripts; a second run 0 changed) prints the ISRCs with 1 / 2 / ≥ 3 intervals, returns, ties, tails, ISRCs with an operator interval, skipped files, every boundary with both months, rows inserted / deleted. **Real data** (dry run by its author; plan К15, FIX-1, О6): 2 436 ISRCs on both accounts get two intervals 0to8 → Echo8; `first_B` = 2025-08 for 2 343, later (2025-09 … 2026-08) for 93; the boundary is 2025-08 for all 2 436 (`start_B` of account 1055956: the 93 late first sales are clamped); 1 tail ISRC (`catalogOutsideAccount`); 0 returns, 0 ties; 18 249 ISRCs with one interval; 23 121 rows to insert applied on the live `erp` on 2026-11-06: 23 121 rows inserted for 20 685 ISRCs (2 436 with two intervals 0to8 → Echo8, every boundary 2025-08; 2 343 first on Echo8 in 2025-08, 93 from 2025-09 … 2026-08), deleted 0; the second run changed nothing.
  - **Operator corrections** (`POST /tracks/:isrc/label { labelId, from, to?, note? }` → 201 the whole history; `GET /tracks/:isrc/labels` → `[{ id, labelId, labelName, validFrom, validTo, source, note, createdAt }]` by `validFrom`, none = `[]`; plan FIX-2): `[from, to)` floored (M52), `to` empty = open. The same `from` as an existing `operator` interval of the ISRC **replaces** it (label, end, note; one row, the previous values in the audit): this is how an error is fixed from its first month and how an interval is closed; a replacement that changes nothing = `409 catalog_same_label`; otherwise a new `operator` interval, overlapping another `operator` interval = `409 catalog_overlap` (`details.interval`). The operator interval wins only its own months: in the same transaction the `files` intervals of this ISRC are rebuilt by the same rule (`deriveTrackLabels({ isrcs: [isrc], apply: true })`, one ClickHouse read), so they are cut around it and months freed by a shrink return to the files. No delete (О10). Errors also `404` label, `400 label_not_own`, `400 empty_period`, `403 label_forbidden` (a manager: an affected interval of another label, or `labelId` not its own). The EXCLUDE constraint catches a race.
  - **Signals** (`signalsFrom`, `R.signalsSchema`, counters in `counts` and `overview.signals`; information, no money moves): `catalogOutsideAccount` (units whose book ≠ the catalog label of the ISRC at their reporting month, grouped `{ isrc, account, bookLabelId, catalogLabelId | null, firstMonth, lastMonth, units, net }`), `tracksWithoutLabel` (ISRCs of the units with no interval holding their last reporting month: `{ isrc, lastMonth, bookLabelId, net }`; units of a file without a label are only here). Under a label the lists are narrowed to the units of its files (D-168).
  - **`GET /tracks/catalog`**: each row += `catalogLabelId`, `catalogLabelName`, `catalogFrom` (the open interval; `null` without one); the query += `catalog: 'all' | 'current'`. Under a label the default set is **the ISRCs of the label's files ∪ the ISRCs with an open interval of the label** (К12); `catalog=current` = only the latter. The money of P5 under a label stays by the files (`trackSummary` unchanged). The catalog label is not the book (D-168/D-169 refined: the book decides money, the catalog label decides nothing).
  - **Money never changes**: `calculation.ts`, `layers/*`, `scope.ts`, `owner-scope.ts`, `balance.ts`, `label-scope.ts` do not read `track_labels` or `releases` and did not change (`boundaries.test.ts`: none of them names `trackLabels`, `releaseRegistry`, `track_labels`, `owners.releases`); `tests/catalog.test.ts` pins `text(calculate())` plus `GET /overview` without `signals` and `calculatedAt` **byte for byte** before and after the derivation, an operator interval, a replacement and a release import; the signals are checked separately for the expected change.
  - **Releases from the Believe export** (`owners.releases (org_id, label_id, upc, title, version, artist_string, label_string, release_date, track_count, catalog_number, territories, stores, source = 'believe_export', file_sha256, imported_at)`, **primary key `(org_id, label_id, upc)`** (an export is per account = per label; a moved release's UPC is in two exports), index `releases_org_upc`). The reader `modules/owners/believe-export.ts` (`parseBelieveExport`, pure, on the shared `parseCsv` with `;`): the header must hold the ten columns `Release Title; Version; Artist; Label; Release date / Hour / Time zone; # of track; UPC; Cat. #; # of territories; # of stores` by exact name (other columns ignored; missing = `400 release_import_header`); `UPC : <digits>` (or bare digits, 8–14) → the digits; the date is the part before `<br />` and must be a calendar date; `# of track` an integer; empty text cells null; territories / stores null when not integers; blank lines skipped; refused rows `upc_invalid`, `track_count_invalid`, `date_invalid`, `duplicate_upc` → `422 release_import_invalid` (`details.rows` first 200, `details.errors`) and **nothing** written; more than 20 000 rows or 10 MB = `413 import_too_large`. `POST /releases/import?label=` (upload, 201 `{ labelId, fileName, sha256, rows, inserted, updated, unchanged }`; `facade/release-registry.ts` `importReleases`): upsert by `(org, label, upc)` in one transaction, a row equal to the stored one is `unchanged`; UPCs the file does not list stay (an export may be partial). `GET /releases/registry?label=&q=` (no money): under a label its rows, under «Все» every label's; `isrcsInUnits` = the distinct ISRCs of the UPC in the label's loaded files (`sources.isrcsOfUpcs`); `q` filters UPC, title, version, artist, catalog number; registered before `/releases/:upc`. The money reads: `GET /releases` rows += `registered`, `trackCount` and `registry` (the label's row under a label, the last imported under «Все», or `null`); `GET /releases/:upc` += `registry: []` (under a label its row, under «Все» every label's). Signals `releasesIncomplete` (`track_count` > distinct ISRCs of the UPC in the units of the same label's book: `{ upc, labelId, title, trackCount, isrcsInUnits }`) and `releasesUnknown` (UPCs of the units of a book whose label has a non-empty registry but no row for the UPC: `{ upc, bookLabelId, releaseTitle, net }`), over all loaded files (the period is not applied).
  - **The checklist** (`GET /onboarding?label=` → `{ label { id, name }, steps[] { key, state: 'done' | 'todo' | 'info', count, counts, link, months?, values? } }`; `facade/onboarding.ts` `labelChecklist`; **no calculation**: the file never imports `./calculation`, `boundaries.test.ts`). Reads: one `db.snapshot()` of PostgreSQL (the label book, the label, all labels, the label's files, splits, participations, the pseudonym index, `confirmedContracts`, the owner resolver, the label history, the registry, the statements, the document statuses) and at most **three** ClickHouse reads over the label's loaded files (`isrcFilePairs`, `labelStringsOfFiles`, and `isrcsOfUpcs` only when the registry is not empty). Steps in order: `label` (done), `accounts` (≥ 1), `files` (per account first / last month and the months without a file between them; done with ≥ 1 file and no gap), `unmapped` (label strings of the files that are an alias of no label; the strings in `values`, ≤ 100), `catalog` (ISRCs of the files, with an open interval of the label, without an interval at their last month, releases, incomplete releases; done when none is without a label), `splits` (ISRCs without a split and without a participation at any time), `owners` (pseudonyms of the rows and participations on those ISRCs without an owner, merges resolved), `documents` (rows without a document, own or the split's; rows whose document is not approved; owners of those pseudonyms without an approved contract under the label or without a label), `publishing` (`info`: the label's statements and quarters). Each step links to its page (`/labels/:id`, `/sources`, `/signals/labelUnmapped`, `/tracks?catalog=current`, `/tracks?hasSplit=no`, `/artists?status=no_owner`, `/splits?status=unconfirmed`, `/publishing`). The backend decides each state (R4). Without `label` = `400 label_required` (not shown under «Все»); a viewer without the label = `403 label_forbidden`. **The organization's checklist** `GET /onboarding/organization` (`routes/onboarding-org.ts`, mounted in `org-admin-router.ts` with the same guard as `/users`; right `admin:users:org`, a manager 403 `forbidden`) → `{ org: { id }, steps: org / labels (own, sublabels) / users (active members, of them `org_admin`) / accounts (all, own labels without one) }`: the route counts the members with `auth.members(orgId)` and passes the counts to `facade.organizationChecklist` (the facade never imports `auth`).
  - **Shared readers**: the streaming `parseCsv` moved from `modules/sources/csv.ts` to `src/shared/csv.ts` (the old path re-exports it); the streaming xlsx `readRows` / `cellText` / `memoryRows` of B17 moved from `modules/publishing/b17-format.ts` to `src/shared/xlsx-rows.ts`. Both pure, no module; sources, publishing, owners (`believe-export.ts`; owners cannot import sources) and the import take them from there. B17 reading is unchanged (its tests unchanged).
  - **Audit from the response** (D-140 / D-150 refined; plan FIX-5): `src/api/audit.ts` `RESPONSE_SUMMARY_ROUTES` (beside `RESPONSE_LABEL_ROUTES`) lists the writes whose summary and created ids come from the **response** (a multipart upload has no body; free text never enters): `POST /splits/import` (summary `fileName, sha256, rows, groups, create, change, unchanged, warnings, artistsNew, ownersNew`; ids `result.splitIds` + `artistIds` + `ownerIds`; a preview, `dryRun` ≠ `0`, leaves **no row**), `POST /splits/assume` (`documentId`, the counts of `assigned` / `skipped`, never the reason; ids the document and the assigned splits), `POST /releases/import` (`fileName, sha256, rows, inserted, updated, unchanged`; no ids). The label of the row = `?label=` / `labelId` as before (D-169). `preview: true` in `operations.ts` exists for the contract test only (an operation with it must have `dryRun` in its query). The preview is a POST with the right `write`: a support grant for reading cannot see it (accepted).
