  - **Abilities** (`operations.ts`, D-171 table; frozen literal `tests/helpers/manager-answers.ts` + 8 lines): `POST /splits/import` write:splits:label (manager L), `POST /splits/assume` approve:splits:label (✗), `GET /tracks/:isrc/labels` read:tracks:org (O), `POST /tracks/:isrc/label` write:tracks:label (L), `POST /releases/import` write:releases:label (L), `GET /releases/registry` read:releases:label (L), `GET /onboarding` read:reports:label (L), `GET /onboarding/organization` admin:users:org (✗; a GET with `admin` is allowed by the contract test only as a read under `admin:users`, like `GET /users`). Counts (`tests/abilities.test.ts`): **90 organization operations**: read 19 / read_money 21 / write 17 / approve 13 / pay 6 / admin 14; **a label manager keeps 52**: L 29 / R 16 / O 7, refused 38. Changed operations: `POST /documents`, `/documents/:id/approve`, `/contracts`, `/splits*`, `/participations*` (the kind accepted), `GET /tracks/catalog`, `GET /releases`, `GET /releases/:upc`, `GET /signals`.
  - **Data model** (`packages/backend/migrations/postgres/0018_catalog.sql`, one idempotent file): the CHECK of `owners.documents.kind` dropped and re-added with `label_decision`; `owners.track_labels`; `owners.releases`; Drizzle `trackLabels`, `releases` in `modules/owners/schema.ts`, repository groups `trackLabels` / `releases` through `OrgScope`; `label_id` is text without a foreign key (D-121), the own label is checked by the facade (`assertOwnLabel`). `tests/org-scope.test.ts` counts 28 org tables (was 26).
  - **Sources reads** (no ClickHouse schema change): `sources.isrcFilePairs(fileIds, isrcs?)` (the (ISRC, file, reporting month) pairs, one `GROUP BY`), `isrcsOfUpcs(fileIds, upcs?)` (the distinct ISRCs per UPC), `labelStringsOfFiles(fileIds)` (the raw label strings of the files, over `columnar.labelStrings`). The caller passes `loaded` files of the registry.
  - **Frontend** the frontend: P28 `pages/reference/splits/import/` (the preview grid, issue chips, the template Blob from `SPLIT_IMPORT_COLUMNS`, no sidebar item), the P15 «Загрузить из файла» button and `AssumeDialog` (P15 / P6), `TrackLabelsDialog` (P6, P21 `tracksWithoutLabel`), the «Лейбл каталога» column and the `catalog=current` toggle on P5, the registry view and `ReleaseImportDialog` on P7, the registry rows on P8, the four lists on P21, the P1 checklist (`pages/work/onboarding/`: under a label the label steps, under «Все» the organization steps for an administrator), `components/fields/UploadForm.tsx` shared by P19 / P28 / P7, `lib/document-kinds.ts` deriving every kind list from `@erp/shared/document-kinds` (`tests/document-kinds.test.ts`), `coverage.ts` for the 8 operations; the six backend parts landed in one commit because they share `operations.ts`, the schemas and the manager table (planned: P28 «Импорт сплитов» `/splits/import` from the P15 button only, no `nav.ts` item (plan О11: the page is a step of the split work, an exception to §8 of the agent instruction, recorded in `coverage.ts`); `AssumeDialog` on P6 / P15; the kind «Решение лейбла» in the six hard-coded kind lists taken from `@erp/shared`; the checklists on P1 (`OnboardingChecklist` under a label, `OrgChecklist` under «Все» for the administrator); the «catalog label» column on P5 / P7, `TrackLabelsDialog` on P6, `ReleaseImportDialog` on P7, the registry on P8, four lists on P21; the shared `components/fields/UploadForm.tsx`; e2e 38–40; numbers in `docs/agents/19_golden_screens.md`).
- Checked: backend commits 1–6 in the working tree: `tests/split-import.test.ts` (9 cases: csv `;` / `,`, BOM, quotes and xlsx = the same rows; every class; floored dates; all or nothing with `details.cause`; a repeat = all `unchanged`; rows without a document → C `split_unconfirmed`; the manager's `forbidden` / `label_forbidden`; two groups of a new ISRC = `create` + `change`; the audit: preview 0 rows, apply 1 with the summary from the response; 5 001 rows → 413; **5 000 rows (2 000 groups) applied in ≤ 30 s with one `artistIndex` read**), `tests/split-import-loader.test.ts` (1: the loader equivalence with its preconditions), `tests/assume.test.ts` (5), `tests/owners.test.ts` (`createContract` takes `label_decision`, refuses `appendix`), `tests/golden.test.ts` (G61), `tests/catalog.test.ts` (12: the derivation cases — a move with a tail, a late first sale clamped to `start_B`, empty months, ties, A → B → A, operator cuts — and on the stack the **money pin**, idempotence, operator replace / close / shrink, `catalog_same_label`, `catalog_overlap`, `empty_period`, the viewer, `catalog=current`), `tests/releases.test.ts` (9: the export format, refusals write nothing, a repeat = `unchanged`, the two signals, the registry and the money reads), `tests/onboarding.test.ts` (5: an empty label all `todo`, a gap, `unmapped` = the strings of `labelUnmapped`, 400 without a label, the organization checklist for `org_admin` and 403 for a manager), `tests/sources.test.ts` (`parseCsv` from `src/shared/csv.ts`), `tests/boundaries.test.ts`, `tests/abilities.test.ts` / `api-contract.test.ts` (the counts above, every new operation called), `tests/org-scope.test.ts` (28), `tests/postgres.test.ts` (0018 twice). the full backend suite after the stage: 57 files / 613 tests (5 skipped: the mode-specific blocks); the frontend 102 unit tests in 16 files; `npm run e2e` 181 / 181 (new specs 38-split-import, 39-onboarding, 40-catalog on synthetic data inside the specs; 21-signals expects 21 lists); `compare-reference` in `off` 0 differing after the backend and again after `derive-track-labels --apply` (the new signals only: catalogOutsideAccount 1, tracksWithoutLabel 0, releasesIncomplete 0, releasesUnknown 0) (expected `0 differing` in the default mode: the new signals enter only `overview.signals`, no total moves); the derive dry run on the live `erp` as above applied on the live `erp` on 2026-11-06: 23 121 rows inserted for 20 685 ISRCs (2 436 with two intervals 0to8 → Echo8, every boundary 2025-08; 2 343 first on Echo8 in 2025-08, 93 from 2025-09 … 2026-08), deleted 0; the second run changed nothing.
- Open (the plan's §8; the default acts until the owner decides):
  - О1: no import of participations (P16) from a file; by hand or the loader step `participations`.
  - О2: no own C reason for an unapproved `label_decision` (`split_unconfirmed` / `owner_unconfirmed` / `rule_unconfirmed`); an approved one confirms participations too (one `SPLIT_DOCUMENT_KINDS`), wider than the letter of answer 7 («the owner and the split row»); the payroll column is the payroll stage's.

## D-173: Operations for the payroll: explicit operation kinds, the forgiveness of an advance, write-off and adjustment in the books, asOf, the payroll export, the import of operations from a file, the replacement of a B17 statement
- Date: 2026-11-06
- Note on numbering: the brief's section «D-170. Операции под ведомость: явный вид, прощение, списание, корректировка, `asOf`, выгрузка, замена B17» is the working name of this stage (stage 5 of «label from zero»); D-170 in this log is the organization boundary, D-171 the roles, D-172 the onboarding, so the stage is D-173. Its migration is `0019_operation_kinds.sql` (the brief's `0016_operation_kinds.sql`; 0016 is the organization, 0017 the roles, 0018 the catalog).
- Question: how the books of a label carry everything the payroll of a month needs: an explicit kind for every operation (today the meaning is derived from direction, sign, scope and reference, D-047), the payment for a buyout outside the royalty balance, the forgiveness of an advance, a write-off of publishing, an adjustment (a starting balance or a correction to the owner's cabinet), the books as of a month (`asOf`), a payroll export per label, a bulk entry of operations from a file (the cash after 30.10 of the payment channels), and the replacement of a B17 statement by its «Updated» version (D-041 ⚠). Sources: the brief (`docs/идеи/лейбл_с_нуля/бриф_для_агента.md`) section «D-170. Операции под ведомость…», row 5 of «Порядок этапов», the owner's decisions 1, 5, 6, 7 («Решения овнера по пунктам ⚠», 2026-11-05) and the owner's answers (the idea `README.md` «Ответы владельца продукта (2026-11-05, вечер)» 1 = November is paid from the owner's own ERP, our payroll is a line-by-line control, not urgent; 2 with the brief's answer 6 = starting balances become a separate entity later («счета компании», «Позже»), `adjustment` stays as an operation kind of this stage; 3 = the cash file of the payment channels comes by **our** template, the holds and channels stay in the owner's ERP; the brief's answer 5 = currency USD, no legal name of a label now (`legal_name` / `currency` not introduced); decision 6 = the mechanism of the Q2 «Updated» replacement now, its run only after the owner's confirmation; decision 7 and the brief's answer 7 = the label decision `label_decision` gets its own payroll column «по решению, без договора»); the idea folder's `README.md` §1 items 6–7; the approved plan `docs/идеи/лейбл_с_нуля/api/этап5_ведомость.md` (whole, with «Ревью» and «После ревью»: FIX-1…FIX-11 and the NOTEs applied; the plan's mismatch table К1–К13).
- Decision:
  - **The brief vs the code** (the plan's К1–К13): the history has **no** buyout operations (К1: the 25 buyout prices of the Main workbook, Σ 275 000, were loaded as documents of kind `other`, the 19 documents of kind `buyout` are registry deals of `import-legacy.ts`, D-027; no operation carries «выкуп»), so the derived kind of the history is in {payout, advance, deduction} and `paid` / `payable` of every owner do not move; the forgiveness capacity is clamped at 0 and the forgiveness taken is the **effective** one (К2); at the moment of a forgiveness `payable` does not change, later income of the months ≥ its date goes to `payable` (К3); the brief's test file `tests/layers/recoup.test.ts` is `tests/recoup.test.ts` (К4); `adjustment` is `in` only with a signed amount (К5); a write-off never touches `earned` (a calculation), it is its own minus in `payable` (К6); the export is its own operation `GET /owners/payroll`, not `format=csv` on `/owners/payables` (К7); the replacement is its own operation `POST /publishing/statements/:id/replace` (one ability per operation, D-171) (К8); the import needs `pay`, not `admin` (К9); `settlements.operations.area` is a column, not only a snapshot field (К10); no `legal_name` / `currency` (К11, answer 5); the import writes `migration: true` (К12); D-169 Open В1 is not fixed here (К13).
  - **Nine operation kinds** (`packages/shared/src/operation-kinds.ts`, one list for the backend and the frontend: `OPERATION_KINDS = ['payout','advance','deduction','credit','return','buyout','forgiveness','writeoff','adjustment']`, `DERIVED_KINDS` (the five of the history), `KIND_DIRECTION`, `KIND_SIGN` (`+`, `-`, `±`), `KIND_DOCUMENT_KINDS`, `KIND_WITH_SCOPE` (advance, deduction), `BLOCKING_KINDS` (payout, advance), `IMPORTABLE_KINDS` (all but return and forgiveness), `LABEL_SOURCES`, and `kindOf` (the derivation)). `settlements.operations.kind` is stored (D-047 refined: the kind is no longer derived for display); `S.createOperationBody` takes `kind` optionally: without it the kind is derived by the old rule (`kindOf` = the former `meaningOf`), so old callers and tests work; `buyout`, `writeoff`, `adjustment` only explicitly. The rules (the CHECK `operations_kind_rules` and the facade):
    - `payout`: out, > 0, owner; no scope, no ref; counts with an approved payment proof or `migration`; in `paid`; only the button, `POST` with `migration`, the import.
    - `advance`: out, > 0, owner, pseudonym, scope (the scope of recoupment), document `advance_basis` | `contract`; no ref; a position (→ `recouped`, `unrecouped`); `POST` (the label contract gate, blocking).
    - `deduction`: in, > 0, pseudonym, scope (default `any`), document `adjustment_basis` | `contract` | `other`; a position. `credit`: in, < 0, pseudonym; in `credits`. `return`: in, < 0, `operation_ref` to a counted **`payout`** (by kind: a `buyout` is refused), document `return_proof`; in `returns` (`/return` unchanged).
    - **`buyout`**: out, > 0, owner, document of kind `buyout`, no scope, no ref; counts with an approved payment proof or `migration`; in the book field `buyouts`, **not in `paid` and not in `payable`** (README §5: the buyout payment is not part of the balance; D-027 refined: the payment for a buyout is now recorded as an operation, outside the balance); it **does not block** payouts (D-052 refined: blocking = `kind IN ('payout','advance')`, in the three places `settlements/service.ts` `blockingPayouts`, `balance.ts` `blockingPayouts`, `context.ts` `blockingPayoutsOf`); `setDeferred` and `attachPaymentProof` are allowed on it.
    - **`forgiveness`**: in, > 0, `operation_ref` to an active `advance`, document `adjustment_basis` | `contract` | `other` | `label_decision`, a date ≥ the advance's date, the amount ≤ the unrecouped (`remaining` of the position); owner, pseudonym, label and area inherited from the advance (`label_source = 'payout'`); only through `POST /settlements/operations/:id/forgive`.
    - **`writeoff`**: in, > 0, owner, document `adjustment_basis` | `other`, `area = 'publishing'` (set by itself; another area = `400 writeoff_area`), no scope, no ref; the amount ≤ the available publishing of the book (publishing B − recouped by publishing − earlier write-offs; `422 writeoff_exceeds { available }`, the import too); in `writtenOff`, a minus in `payable`. D-055 refined: the write-off is implemented, for publishing only.
    - **`adjustment`**: in, signed ≠ 0, owner, document `adjustment_basis`, no scope, no ref; in `adjustments = −Σ amount` (> 0 lowers `payable`, < 0 raises it, the sign convention of deduction / credit); a starting balance or a correction to the cabinet.
    - The order of refusals of `createOperation`: `label_required` → `kind_direction` → `kind_sign` → `use_payout_button` / `use_return_action` / `use_forgive_action` → `owner_required` / `artist_required` → `scope_required` / `scope_forbidden` / `writeoff_area` → `document_required` → `document_kind` → `document_owner_mismatch` → the gate (`owner_not_confirmed`, `no_contract_for_label`, `payout_blocked`) → `writeoff_exceeds`. The label contract gate stays for `advance` only; `buyout`, `writeoff`, `adjustment` need just their document (R10 of D-169 applies to it).
    - The settlements service branches by `kind`, not by direction (plan FIX-4): deactivating an advance with an active forgiveness = `409 has_forgiveness`, a payout with an active return = `has_returns`; `payout_sent` / `payout_paid` only for `kind = 'payout'`. D-048 refined: a `buyout` counts like a payout (proof or `migration`), `forgiveness` / `writeoff` / `adjustment` with an approved document; `migration` counts without documents and never blocks, as before.
  - **`meaning` stays an alias** of `kind` for this stage, as landed in commit 1 with its **old five values** (the plan had `recoupable_payout` → `advance`): `advance` answers `meaning = 'recoupable_payout'`, the other history kinds their own name, a new kind the meaning of its fields (`meaningOfKind`); a `meaning` filter value is read as its kind (`kindOfMeaning`: `recoupable_payout` → `advance`); the balance reads `meaning` until commit 2 branches by `kind`; its removal is «Позже». [commit 1: as in the working tree on 2026-11-06] `R.operationSchema` += `kind`, `labelSource` (`'one' | 'default' | 'operator' | 'payout' | null`), `channel`, `area`; `GET /settlements/operations` += the filters `kind`, `labelSource`.
  - **Migration `0019_operation_kinds.sql`** (one, idempotent): the function `settlements.kind_of(direction, amount_cents, scope, operation_ref)` = `meaningOf` (`out` + scope → `advance`, `out` → `payout`, `in` > 0 → `deduction`, `in` < 0 with a ref → `return`, else `credit`); `operations.kind` filled from it for the history, then `NOT NULL` and the CHECK of the nine kinds; the index `operations_kind (org_id, kind)`; `operations_out_rules`, `operations_deduction_rules`, `operations_credit_rules` and a previous `operations_kind_rules` dropped and `operations_kind_rules` added per kind (FIX-2: repeatable); `label_source` (CHECK of the four values, `NULL` until marked), `channel` (text 1–100), `area text NOT NULL DEFAULT 'master' CHECK (area IN ('master','publishing'))`, the index `operations_external_ref (org_id, external_ref) WHERE external_ref IS NOT NULL` (the duplicate search of the import, not unique); `publishing.pub_statements.replaced_by text` (no foreign key, D-121). The live history (2 663 operations, read-only SQL of the plan's review): payout 2 400 (all `migration`), advance 159 (158 `migration`), deduction 104 (49 active, 55 inactive without a label), credit 0, return 0, **buyout 0** — so `paid` and `payable` are unchanged and every history row passes the new CHECKs (the old CHECKs are a subset). `tests/org-scope.test.ts` still counts 28 org tables.
  - **Where the label came from** (`label_source`, D-168 refined): `scripts/assign-operation-labels.ts --mark-source [--apply]` repeats `decide()` over the same units for **every** active operation: equal to the current label → `one` / `default` (the mixed and no-unit cases) / `payout` (a return); otherwise `operator`; the 7 Echo8 → 0to8 operations of the step `operations-echo8` (`assign-label-books.ts`, the owner's decision without an audit row) get `operator` and are printed on their own line («по решению владельца 05.11»). Expected on the real data: `default` = **1 315** (D-168: mixed 1 152 + 68 + 35, without units 48 + 12), otherwise the step stops without `--force`. New operations of `POST`, the button and the import get `operator`; `return` and `forgiveness` get `payout` (inherited). `GET /settlements/operations?labelSource=default` is the list for the owner.
  - **The change of a label with a reason**: `PATCH /settlements/operations/:id { labelId, reason }` (approve; `reason` required: `400 reason_required`), `label_source = 'operator'`, the reason and `fromLabelId` in the audit summary (`RESPONSE_SUMMARY_ROUTES` of D-172).
  - **Signals** (`signalsFrom`, counters in `counts`): `operationsDefaultLabel` (the rows with `label_source = 'default'`, action `action-set-label`), `operationsRelabelled` (the number of operations with ≥ 1 audit row of `PATCH /settlements/operations/:id`; under L those whose current label is L or whose `fromLabelId` is L; `audit.countRelabelled(labelId?)`; the history before the stage has no `fromLabelId`), `forgivenessReduced` (a forgiveness whose effective amount is below the recorded one: the income before its date arrived later).
  - **The one layer change: the forgiveness in `facade/layers/recoup.ts`** (the brief and the agent instruction §2 allow exactly this one; `calculation.ts`, every other layer, `scope.ts`, `owner-scope.ts`, `label-scope.ts` untouched). `Position.forgiven?: { month, amount }[]` (the counted forgivenesses by `operation_ref`, the month of their date, ascending; several in one month in the order (date, id)); `RecoupResult.forgiven` (the effective sum); `remaining = amount − recouped − forgiven ≥ 0`, so `amount = recouped + forgiven + remaining` always. In the loop over the income items of a position (oldest report month first), before an item of month m every forgiveness with month ≤ m is applied as `effective = min(forgiven, max(0, amount − recouped_before_date − forgiven_so_far))` and the capacity of the position is capped `left = min(left, max(0, amount − consumed − forgivenEff))`; the forgivenesses after the last item (or the break) by the same rule. That is the brief's «capacity from the month ≥ the date = `amount − recouped_before_date − forgiven`» clamped at 0 (К2): income before the date that arrives later (a file for an earlier month) is still recouped and shrinks the effective forgiveness (signal `forgivenessReduced`). D-101 is kept (plan FIX-1): the D-094 netting runs only if the position **takes something with the forgivenesses applied** (a trial pass of the taking loop on a copy of the capacities replaces the test `left > 0`); without forgivenesses the trial gives the same answer, so the result is byte for byte the old one. The order of the positions (largest, date, id) and `eligible` do not change; no new multiplication (only `min` / `max` / subtraction of cent amounts and already quantised 12-decimal income), remainders by subtraction (D-127 / D-130 exactness: `tests/layers/exactness.test.ts` unchanged). D-049 refined (the capacity after a forgiveness). as landed in `recoup.ts` (reviewed line by line: the per-position body in `settle()`, the forgivenesses applied in month order before each item of that month or later and once after the loop, `forgiven += min(f.amount, max(0, amount − recouped − forgiven))`, `left = min(left, max(0, amount − recouped − forgiven))`, the trial copy kept only when the position takes something (D-101), the `eligible` filter and the position order untouched; without forgivenesses the result equals the old algorithm by proof and by a 32-bit seeded test over 200 rounds)
  - **The books** (`facade/balance.ts`; `LabelBook`, `total`, `byLabel`, `ArtistLabelBook`, `ArtistBalance`, `PositionView` += `forgiven`): new fields `buyouts` (Σ counted `buyout`), `forgiven` (Σ effective forgivenesses of the positions of the book), `writtenOff` (Σ counted `writeoff`), `adjustments` (−Σ counted `adjustment`); **D-050 refined**: `payable_L = earned − recouped − paid − adsCharged + returns + credits + adjustments − writtenOff`; `buyouts` and `forgiven` are outside `payable` (a forgiveness only lowers `unrecouped`). The operation branches go by `o.kind`. A deliberate deviation recorded here: the new terms of `payable` are formally in the «calculation zone» of the agent instruction §2 but ordered by the brief; on the history every new field is 0 and the reference does not move.
  - **The area of a book** (`settlements.operations.area`, a deliberate deviation from the brief, which kept the area in the snapshot only: `payable_{L,area}` needs `paid` per area and the balance never reads snapshots): every book has `byArea: { master, publishing }` with `earned, recouped, adsCharged, paid, returns, credits, buyouts, writtenOff, adjustments, payable` (earned / recouped by the stream of the income, advertising master, the rest by `operations.area`, a write-off publishing; the history `master`); Σ areas = the book field by field. **D-051 refined**: the button `POST /owners/:id/payouts { area }` (default **`master`**, plan О6) pays `floor(min(payable_{L,area}, payable_L))`, so the history of payouts without an area (= `master`) can never pay publishing twice; the snapshot stores the area (`payoutByArtist` at `master` without the publishing row). **Reads without `area` are `all`** (FIX-7): `GET /owners/payables` and `GET /owners/:id/balance` gate on `payable_L` as before; `area` on `/owners/payables` changes only its `payout` object, never the money fields. Tests where the button pays publishing (`scope.test.ts` «B17 of a label… paid by the label payout», `review-fixes` «payout snapshot attribution») pass `area: 'all'`, their numbers unchanged. `payoutSnapshotSchema`: the new fields (`buyouts`, `forgiven`, `writtenOff`, `adjustments`, `positions[].forgiven`, `byArea`, `area`) are **optional**, so a snapshot made before the stage still passes the response (FIX-6).
  - **`asOf`** (`YYYY-MM`, a cut of the **inputs** of `ownerBalance`, nothing stored; `calculate()` gets no `asOf`): from one full calculation, the units with `reportingMonth ≤ asOf`, the publishing lines with `reportMonth ≤ asOf` (through the filtered `ownerIndex`), operations and positions (advances, deductions, forgivenesses) with `date ≤` the last day of `asOf`, advertising with `adMonth ≤ asOf` (a new input `index.ads?: AdPosition[]` instead of `calc.adPositions.get(owner)`); `PeriodFilter.to = min(to, asOf)`; the reconciliation `reconcile(calc, { to: asOf })`. Document and contract statuses are those of **now** (Open О4). On `GET /owners/payables`, `GET /owners/:id/balance` and the payroll. The pin in `tests/scope.test.ts`: the balance at `asOf` = the full balance of a world without the later operations and files, field by field.
  - **The payroll export** `GET /owners/payroll?label=&asOf=&area=master|publishing|all&format=json|csv&sheet=payroll|waiting|buyouts|control` (`facade/payroll.ts`, read_money owners label; registered before `/owners/:id`; `area` default `master`; without `label` = `400 label_required`: the payroll is a label's book, no «Все»): **one** full calculation (`calcWithOps()`, as `ownerPayables`), the `asOf` cut, the view `{ labelId }`. Csv: `text/csv; charset=utf-8` with a BOM, `;`, a dot in decimals, the name `payroll_<label>_<asOf|all>_<area>_<sheet>.csv`; `operations.ts` gets `OperationBase.csv?: true` (a second OpenAPI content `text/csv`; the contract test calls both). Sheets:
    - `payroll` (`rows[]`): `rowKind` `pseudonym` | `owner` | `total`; owner, pseudonym, label name; `contract` = the approved contract with `label_id ∈ {L, NULL}` (own label before «any», newest) `{ number, documentKind: 'contract' | 'label_decision', approvedAt, approvedBy } | null`; **`byDecision`** = the column «по решению, без договора» (the confirming document is a `label_decision`, owner's decision 7); money `earned, recouped, unrecouped, adsCharged, paid, returns, credits, buyouts, forgiven, writtenOff, adjustments`, on `total` also `payable, payableShown`, `gate` (`reason`, `amountCents`; `payout_blocked` with `blockedByOtherLabel: true` and **without** the amount of that payout: the gate «другим лейблом») and `belowThreshold` («ниже порога»). A `pseudonym` row carries the master earned, **`recouped` = `recoupedFromMaster`** (taken from this pseudonym's income by any position, by the artist of the income, FIX-10), the `unrecouped` and `forgiven` of the positions it holds, its advertising; the `owner` row the publishing earned, **`recouped` = `publishingRecouped`**, paid, returns, credits, buyouts, writtenOff, adjustments; Σ of an owner's rows = `total` = `byArea` of the chosen area. At `area=publishing` there are no `pseudonym` rows.
    - `waiting`: owner × C reason (`C_REASONS`) in the cut, with `paidAlready` (the owner's `paid` in the book). `buyouts`: operation, date, amount, document, counted or not. `control`: the rows of `reconcile(calc, { to: asOf })` of the book of L (`source, account, month, A, B, C, ours, discrepancy`). `totals`: `payable`, `payableShown`, `bookB` (Σ earned of the `total` rows) and `reconciliationB` (Σ `B_notOurs` of the book in the cut; `bookBMatches` false when they differ), `relabelledOperations`, `defaultLabelOperations`.
  - **The import of operations** `POST /settlements/operations/import?label=&dryRun=1|0&documentId=` (upload, `facade/operation-import.ts` `importOperations`, pay settlements label: a manager and support 403): csv through `src/shared/csv.ts` (`;` / `,`, BOM), ≤ 5 000 rows / 10 MB (`413 import_too_large`), the columns `OPERATION_IMPORT_COLUMNS` (`owner?, pseudonym?, date, amount, kind, channel?, confirmation?, document?, external_id?, scope?, comment?`; owner / pseudonym = an id or the exact name, at least one; `document` = an id or the exact title, else `?documentId=`; `confirmation` = the reference of a payment document: a `payment_proof` is created with `requiresApproval: false`); the template is a frontend `Blob` from the same list. Every row is written with **`migration: true`** (paid outside the ERP, counted without a payment document, never blocks, D-048), `labelId = ?label=`, `label_source = 'operator'`, `channel` in its column. **D-051 extended**: besides migrated payouts of `POST`, the import may create payouts — the payment service is never called and the right `pay` is `org_admin`'s only. The preview (`dryRun=1`, `preview: true` of D-172: no audit row) in one `db.snapshot()`: `rows[] { line, kind, owner, pseudonym, amount, date, action: create | unchanged | error, issues[] }`, `totals { rows, create, unchanged, errors, warnings, byKind }`; classes (error) `owner_not_found`, `owner_ambiguous`, `pseudonym_not_found`, `pseudonym_other_owner`, `kind_unknown`, `kind_not_importable` (`return`, `forgiveness`), `amount_invalid`, `date_invalid`, `document_not_found`, `document_kind`, `scope_required`, **`external_ref_required`** (a `payout` or `buyout` row without `external_id`: a `migration` payout counts at once, so a repeated cash file would double `paid`; FIX-9), `duplicate_in_file`, `duplicate_external_ref` (an active operation with the same `external_ref` and another content), the `kind_*` / `*_required` codes and `writeoff_exceeds`; (warning) `no_external_ref` (other kinds), `no_contract_for_label`. A row whose **content key** (`kind`, owner, pseudonym, date, amount, channel, `external_ref`) matches an active operation is `unchanged` (skipped), so a repeat of the file writes nothing. The apply (`dryRun=0`, no error row) in **one** `db.transaction()`: the classification again, then `facade.createOperation` in file order; any refusal rolls everything back (`422 import_invalid { line, cause }`); the answer `R.operationImportSchema` + `created: id[]`. Without `?label=` = `400 label_required` before the body. Starting balances by this import (decision 5) wait for the «company accounts» entity (answer 6); `adjustment` is importable.
  - **The replacement of a B17 statement** `POST /publishing/statements/:id/replace?label=` (upload, approve publishing label, 201 `R.b17ReplaceSchema { superseded, statement, writers }`: the old statement now `superseded`, the new one `loaded`, the writers resolved as on an upload; the label is checked before the body, `assertUploadLabel`; the old route `POST /publishing/b17` unchanged; facade `sources.ts` `replaceB17(oldId, input, labelId, viewer)`): `publishing.replaceStatement(oldId, input, labelId)` runs through the **same** `load()` (a `replace` parameter: the writers registered and the answer `writers` as on a load, no second path) inside `serial()` and **one** module transaction: the old statement must be `loaded` (`409 replace_not_loaded`) and of the label `?label=` (`409 replace_label_differs`); the quarters of the new file (by its lines) must equal those of the old as sets (`409 periods_differ { old, new }`); `duplicate_file` and `duplicate_period` (a quarter of the file name held by another statement) as today; the old one becomes **`superseded`** with `replaced_by = <new id>`, its lines, file and sha stay (payout snapshots cite the sha: `getSnapshot` of an earlier payout is byte for byte the same); its `pub_statement_periods` rows are deleted and the new statement, its periods and lines inserted; `refreshLinesByKey()` (the view takes only `loaded`); the line control. The pre-check by file name skips the quarters of the replaced statement. The statement schema (`R.statementSchema`) gets `status: 'loaded' | 'superseded' | 'failed'` and `replacedBy`, so `GET /sources/files` shows the link (P19 reads statements there; there is no `GET /publishing/statements`). **D-041 is closed by the mechanism**; the run on Q2 2026 «Updated» (417 085 instead of 232 257) waits for the owner's confirmation (decision 6) and is not done in this stage.
  - **Abilities** (`operations.ts`, D-171 table; the frozen `tests/helpers/manager-answers.ts` + 4 lines): `POST /settlements/operations/:id/forgive` pay:settlements:org + record (✗), `POST /settlements/operations/import` pay:settlements:label (✗), `GET /owners/payroll` read_money:owners:label (manager L), `POST /publishing/statements/:id/replace` approve:publishing:label (✗); changed: `POST /settlements/operations` (+`kind`, `area`), `GET /settlements/operations` (+`kind`, `labelSource`), `PATCH /settlements/operations/:id` (+`reason`; approve, ✗), `POST /settlements/operations/:id/deactivate` (+`has_forgiveness`), `GET /owners/payables` and `GET /owners/:id/balance` (+`asOf`, `area`), `POST /owners/:id/payouts` (+`area`), `GET /settlements/operations/:id/snapshot` (+`area`), `GET /signals` (three signals), `GET /sources/files` (+`replacedBy`). Expected counts: 94 organization operations (pay 8, read_money 22, approve 14), a label manager keeps 53. 94 organization operations after the stage (read 19 / read_money 22 / write 17 / approve 14 / pay 8 / admin 14), a manager keeps 53

Planned before the commit: P20 «Операции» the kind chip and filter (`StatusChip kind="operationKind"`, nine phrases), the filter «откуда лейбл» with the chip «по умолчанию», `CreateOperationDialog` with the kind select (without payout / return / forgiveness) and the fields by kind, `ForgiveDialog` on an advance row (`action-forgive`, the hint «непогашено»), `SetOperationLabelDialog` with a required reason, the button to P29; **P29 «Импорт операций»** `/operations/import` (entry from P20 only; the shared `UploadForm` / `IssueChips` of D-172, the template Blob); P9 / P4 `asOf` and area, the export button (`action-export-payroll`, closed under «Все»); **P10** the button with the area (`field-payout-area`, default `master`, the gate amount of that area) and the new balance rows and the area table; P19 `ReplaceStatementDialog` on a `loaded` statement and the `superseded` row with its link; P21 the three signals; `coverage.ts` for the four new operations; e2e 41–44.

## D-174: Opening balances (the start of 15.10.2026): the cabinet position of every pseudonym replaces the recalculated history of the book up to the cut
- Date: 2026-11-06
- Question: the owner's team fixed the position of every participant as the cabinet showed it on 15.10.2026 15:09:49 UTC (reports through 2026-08, every payout, advance, sample and advertising of Main at that moment; 1 180 nicks, Σ −9 031,23; 5 nicks the cabinet answered an error for; 10 approved corrections of the start, Σ +17 300; two publishing advances reopened). The pack of 2026-11-06 asks that this position stands **instead of** the recalculated history («стартовую позицию поставить вместо пересчёта истории до 2026-08 включительно»): a recalculation of 2023–2026 differs from the cabinet for 237 participants by $1,9M in absolute value, and the artists have been paid by the cabinet's numbers. `docs/07` had foreseen the start as a settlements row (2026-10-30); D-173 О1 left it for later. The analysis of the pack is `docs/разбор_пакета_2026-11-06.md` (§3.1–3.2: the two constructions, the pseudonym / owner question).
- Decision:
  - **A table of its own, not an adjustment operation** (`settlements.opening_balances`, migration `0020_opening_balances.sql`, Drizzle `openingBalances`; `org_id`, a book `label_id`, `artist_id`, `kind` `start` | `adjustment`, `cutoff_month` (the first day of the last report month inside the start), `synced_at` (the boundary of the movements), `master_cents`, `publishing_cents`, `missing`, `basis` (required, ≤ 2000), `document_id`, `operation_ref` (an advance kept alive), `comment`, `created_at`, a soft rollback `deactivated_at` / `deactivated_reason`; one active `start` per (org, label, pseudonym, cut); a `missing` row is a start with both amounts 0; an adjustment has a non-zero amount). An adjustment operation (construction А of the analysis) would make the start a function of our recalculation: every later change of an input ≤ the cut (a document approved, a split, a book) would move it, and `asOf` cuts operations by month while the cabinet's boundary is a timestamp. A row of the start is never edited: a correction is a new `adjustment` row with its basis, a wrong import is deactivated with a reason (the owner's principles 4 and 5).
  - **On the pseudonym, the payable on the owner.** The cabinet's position is per nick; the snapshot carries no owner id. The rows are stored per pseudonym (lossless, as the cabinet), the balance sums them into the owner's book (the owner's rule «ники не сальдируются» is not adopted: on the real snapshot it touches 3 owners and $5 699; see the analysis §3.2). The area split of a start row comes from the snapshot's `terms`: `publishing = income.publishing + expense.publishing`, `master = amount − publishing`; the button pays `min(payable of the area, payable of the book)` (D-173), so a negative half cannot be overpaid.
  - **The cut is per owner and book** (`facade/balance.ts` `startCuts`, `ownerBalance(…, index.starts)`): an owner with an active row in a book loses, in that book, the units of report months ≤ `cutoff_month` (B and C alike), the publishing lines of report months ≤ the cut, the advertising of months ≤ the cut and the operations dated ≤ the UTC day of `synced_at` (payouts, advances, deductions, credits, returns, every kind; a cut advance is no position and recoups nothing; a cut operation never blocks), except an operation named by `operation_ref` of an active row (the reopened publishing advances, with their `scope: publishing`). A pseudonym of the owner **without** a row in a book where the owner has rows had no position in the cabinet: its start is 0 and its history up to the cut is inside the start too (on the real data one owner of three nicks would otherwise keep $10 605 the cabinet never recognised). Other books (Echo8) are untouched. Then `payable_L = start + earned − recouped − paid − adsCharged + returns + credits + adjustments − writtenOff` (the start added last: a book without one keeps its exact value); `LabelBook`, `total`, `AreaBook` (`start` per area), `ArtistBalance` / `ArtistLabelBook` / `ArtistMoney` (`start`) carry the new field; `startMissing` on the book and the total; `OwnerBalance.start = { cuts, rows, insideStart }` (what the cut removed, reference only). `calculate()`, the layers, `scope.ts`, `owner-scope.ts` are untouched: the start is read from the settlements module beside the operations (`balanceWithFiles`, `payroll`, `ownerPayables`, `artistBalanceWithReach`), never from the units, so the reach pins hold.
  - **`asOf`** (D-173) runs first; a cut at or before `cutoff_month` leaves the start alone («внутри старта»: the answer is the start).
  - **The gate**: `start_missing` (a start row without an amount: the position is not known, not zero) after `no_contract_for_label` and before `payout_blocked`; `createPayout` throws it. The book of the label sees every pseudonym with a row (`labelVisibility`), split or no split.
  - **The import** `POST /settlements/start/import?label=&cutoff=YYYY-MM&syncedAt=<ISO>&dryRun=1|0&basis=` (upload, `facade/start.ts` `importStart`, pay settlements label; a manager and support are refused): the csv `START_IMPORT_COLUMNS` = `pseudonym, kind, master, publishing, missing, basis, document, operation_ref, comment` (`@erp/shared/start-import`, ≤ 5 000 rows, 10 MB); the preview in one snapshot classifies every row (`START_IMPORT_ISSUES`: `pseudonym_not_found` / `_ambiguous`, `artist_without_owner`, `kind_unknown`, `amount_invalid`, `missing_invalid`, `missing_with_amount`, `amount_required`, `basis_required`, `document_not_found` / `_ambiguous`, `operation_not_found` / `_not_advance` / `_other_owner` / `_after_cut` / `_forbidden`, `duplicate_in_file`, `start_exists` (an active start of the pseudonym at this cut with other amounts; the same amounts = `unchanged`), the warning `no_contract_for_label`, the info `unchanged`); the apply repeats it inside ONE transaction through `settlements.createOpeningBalance` (all or nothing, `422 import_invalid`). `GET /settlements/start?label=&active=` (read_money settlements label: the rows with pseudonym and owner, the sums), `POST /settlements/start/:id/deactivate { reason }` (pay settlements org; `409 already_inactive`). The audit records the apply from the response summary (no row on a preview); the manager table + 3 lines.
  - **The scripts** `scripts/start/load-cabinet.ts <snapshot.json> <transition.json> [--create-owners] [--skip-errors] [--apply]` (the owner's files → `data/mvp-load/start/start_<cutoff>.csv` → the facade import; `--create-owners` gives a nick without an owner a person owner named after it and creates the unknown nicks (`origin: 'start'`), `--skip-errors` applies the rows without an error from `start_<cutoff>_clean.csv`; the issue report `start_issues.csv`) and `scripts/start/check-start.ts` (one `ownerPayables` under 0to8 at `asOf = cutoff`: payable = Σ rows of every owner with rows, the missing ones `start_missing`).
  - **The real data (the live `erp`, 2026-11-06):** 1 195 rows built (1 180 starts, 5 missing, 10 adjustments); 1 070 nicks found as they are, 177 owners created (141 pseudonyms without an owner, 36 nicks unknown to the registry), 16 ambiguous nicks (two live pseudonyms of one name, P11 duplicates) skipped for the operator; 1 179 rows applied; the check: 1 144 owners equal to the cabinet to the cent, 5 `start_missing`, 0 differ. The two reopened advances were found by pseudonym, date and amount.
  - **Frontend**: P30 «Старт 15.10» `/operations/start` (`pages/operations/start/`, a sidebar item under «Деньги»): the list of the book (`GET /settlements/start`, `start-totals`, rows `start-row-<id>` with `data-kind` / `data-active` / `data-artist-id`, `field-start-show-inactive`, `action-start-deactivate-<id>` → `field-start-deactivate-reason`, `action-start-deactivate-submit`) and the import as P29 (`field-start-cutoff`, `field-start-synced-at`, `field-start-basis`, `field-start-import-file`, `action-start-import-preview`, `start-import-totals`, rows `import-row-<line>`, chips `issue-<code>`, `action-start-import-apply`, `start-import-result`, `action-start-import-template`); P10 `balance-start` in the formula, `start-missing`, `balance-start-section` (`start-cuts`, `start-cut-<label>`, `start-rows`, `start-inside`), the `start` column of `by-label-<name>` and `balance-area-<area>`; the gate chip `start_missing`; the payroll column `start` (on the pseudonym rows: the area's part of their rows). e2e `45-start.spec.ts` (S55).

## D-175: Stage 2 of the pack of 2026-11-06: the September report, an ISRC without a split waits in C, the «100 % label» split rows, the buyouts of the registry, the write-offs, the threshold of $50, the start as one number of the master area
- Date: 2026-11-06
- Question: the second stage of `docs/разбор_пакета_2026-11-06.md` (A3–A8, A12) on the owner's answers of 2026-11-06: (4) an ISRC without a split is «нераспределено», not the label's; (5) the default payout threshold is $50; (2) a buyout takes the whole seller share from the signing date; the 112 «100 % label» versions of folder 2, the buyout registry of folder 7, the write-offs of folder 6 and the September report of folder 1 go into the live store.
- Decision:
  - **D-029 refined: the label share of a unit without a split waits in C `no_split`** (`layers/split.ts`: `labelShareCategory: 'no_split'` when it was ours; an earlier C reason of the label share, an unmapped label string, stays; `LabelShareCategory` and `C_REASONS` += `no_split`, the label in `reasons.ts`, the frontend reason and the queue hint; the queue groups it by the ISRC / UPC pair like `unknown_pair`, the action `CreateSplitDialog`). The sublabel carve does not run on such a unit (as with `label_unmapped`): R1 of the categorise layer («a sublabel allocation on a no_split unit is an assumption») is unreachable and stays as dead guard. `Reconciliation.ourShareNoSplit` keeps its name and now measures the no-split part of C (P1's column «Без сплита (ждёт в C)»); `A = B + C + ours` holds. G21 changes (C 100 `no_split`, ours 0, no NG allocation); the e2e S08/4 (P1 `100 / 0 / 100 / 0`, the queue group `pair` under `/queue/no_split`, no r1 assumption row), S30 (`9 / 0 / 9 / 0`); the scope-test worlds give I(3) a split without rows so the sublabel carve they pin still runs.
  - **The «100 % label» row of the split import** (`@erp/shared/split-import`: a row with an empty `pseudonym`, no `artist_id` and `percent` 100 → the class `label_only` (info); its group is a split without rows, `ourShare` 100; a label row with other rows in the group = `label_row_with_rows` (error); a repeat = `unchanged`). This is how the 112 versions of `split_changes.json` enter: `scripts/stage2/load-label-splits.ts` creates one approved document per basis group (A1–A5 kind `buyout`: the assignment of the work to the company; A6 kind `label_decision`: the owner's decision in the chat of 2026-10-23; the file reference points to the pack, the DD-pack documents named in `basis` stay there) and imports the rows from 2026-09-01 under 0to8 (112 created on the live store).
  - **The buyouts** (`scripts/stage2/load-buyouts.ts`): one approved `buyout` document per contract of `buyouts_pandadoc_2026-10-16.csv` and `buyouts_extra.json` (idempotent by title), `facade.buyout` on the ISRCs whose current split still carries the seller's row from the signing date (M52: the month; `buyout_overrides.json` names Habazane's 2026-03), the registry's `seller_pct` compared with the row (a difference is reported, not applied), the titles of the extras matched to the seller's tracks by base title. On the live store 15 of 20 contracts were already in the splits from stage 4 (D-131 / D-065: 525 ISRCs `already_zero`), Habazane's 4 ISRCs were bought out from 2026-03, 17 ISRCs of DJ ZENITE differ in share (the owner decides), 32 ISRCs of DJ FRIZER / DJ FZ DA ZN / prodbydxm / MXTZ have no split at the date (new releases: their splits come with the September template), DJ TUUV 4 and DJ IKERAUS 1 `seller_not_in_split`, qaraqshy ambiguous (two live pseudonyms). The 23 prices of the Main workbook are `buyout` operations through the operation import (`external_id` = `main:<row>`, `channel` Main; DJ Zynx left out as the owner said; qaraqshy's price waits for the pseudonym): `buyouts` of the books are filled (К1 of D-173 closed). The report is `data/mvp-load/stage2/buyouts_report.csv`.
  - **The write-offs** of `writeoffs.json` (11 rows, Σ 9 646,93: publishing deducted by Main while B17 accrued nothing; the owner's decision of 2026-10-18 «списать как историю») are corrections of the start: `adjustment` rows of the opening balances (`scripts/stage2/load-writeoffs.ts`), never our `writeoff` (whose sign is the opposite).
  - **The start is one number in the master area (D-174 refined).** The cabinet position mixes royalties, publishing and payouts; the November payroll of the master area carries it whole, so a start row holds the cabinet amount in `master_cents` and 0 in `publishing_cents` (the publishing part of the `terms` is kept in the comment). The split by the `terms` of the first load made the master payable of every owner with publishing income negative (−1,43M over 544 owners); the live rows were corrected in place (`UPDATE … master_cents = master_cents + publishing_cents`, 347 rows, documented here as a migration fix), `load-cabinet.ts` and `load-writeoffs.ts` write master only. The publishing of the quarters after the cut (B17 2026-Q3 and later, ASCAP) is the publishing payroll.
  - **The threshold:** `PAYOUT_THRESHOLD_CENTS` defaults to 5 000 (`main.ts`; the e2e stand keeps 0).
  - **The September report** (`2217440_1052428_2026-09-01_2026-09-01.csv.zip`, 3 720 185 lines, net 792 134,860929 as the owner's run) loaded under 0to8 (`scripts/load-believe.ts --label 0to8`), `derive-track-labels.ts --apply` (514 ISRCs changed).
  - **The live store after the stage** (`scripts/stage2/payroll-summary.ts`): the reconciliation of 2026-09: A 792 134,86 = B 340 530,37 + C 65 744,89 + ours 385 859,60, discrepancy 0; C by reason: owner_unconfirmed 42 480,98, split_unconfirmed 10 509,25, assumption 5 890,97, no_split 3 764,61, artist_without_owner 2 867,86, no_isrc 122,25, unknown_pair 65,97, label_unmapped 43,00. The payroll of 0to8 at `asOf = 2026-09`, master: 1 192 owners, start Σ +48 702,35 (the cabinet −9 031,23 without the 16 ambiguous nicks, + 17 300 adjustments, + 9 646,93 write-offs), earned Σ 440 357,15; 164 owners allowed, Σ 882 270,60; 797 `owner_not_confirmed` (the 177 owners created for the start have no contract yet), 193 `nothing_to_pay`, 37 below the threshold, 1 `start_missing`; 698 positive (Σ 1 072 647,41), 492 negative (Σ −826 547,90). `check-start.ts`: 1 144 equal, 5 missing, 0 differ. The owner's own run of 2026-10-23 gave participants 385 188,19 / label 404 722,52 / unallocated 1 008,74: the row-by-row reconciliation is A16 of the analysis (stage 3 adds the gates of module 17).
- Checked: `tests/layers/split.test.ts`, `categorise.test.ts`, G21, `facade.test.ts` (the no-split row: C 10, the queue group), `split-import.test.ts` (the label row: classes, a split without rows, the money moves from C to ours, a repeat), `scope.test.ts` (the worlds with a split without rows), the frontend guard tests; the full backend suite and the e2e (10, 12, 20, 21 adjusted) after the stage.
- Open:

  - О2: the 9 label strings of September not in the aliases (≈ $59, C `label_unmapped` 43,00 in the reconciliation) wait for the owner's mapping (A4).
  - О3: the albums without ISRC (`album_rule.json`, 61 lines, $122,25: C `no_isrc`) stay D-035 ⚠.
  - О4: `bookBMatches` of the payroll is false by construction once a start exists (bookB = the book after the cut, reconciliationB = the raw rows of the whole period); the control sheet keeps discrepancy 0 per row.
- References: `packages/backend/src/facade/layers/{split,types}.ts`, `calculation.ts`, `queue.ts`, `split-import.ts`; `packages/shared/src/{api-responses,reasons,split-import}.ts`; `packages/backend/scripts/stage2/{load-label-splits,load-buyouts,load-writeoffs,payroll-summary}.ts`, `scripts/start/load-cabinet.ts`; `packages/frontend/src/{i18n/ru/{reasons,work,splitImport}.ts,pages/work/actions/action-table.ts}`; `e2e/golden/{10,12,20,21}-*.spec.ts`; `docs/agents/12_golden_cases.md` G21, `19_golden_screens.md` S08/4; `docs/разбор_пакета_2026-11-06.md`.
