## D-176: The admission to the payout (stage 3 of the pack of 2026-11-06): the payee and the channel, the holds and the disputed history, the answers «pay», the threshold of the contract, the gate in the order of the owner's module 17
- Date: 2026-11-06
- Question: the owner's module 17 («Ведомость и допуск») decides for every row of the payroll «готово» or a reason, in this order: вне ведомости (ниже порога) → холд → спорная история → технический блокер (нет договора, получателя, канала) → нет заключения → изменилось после проверки → нужен ответ → готово; the threshold comes from the contract, $50 by default. Folder 6 of the pack carries the decisions: `payee_decisions.json` (8: the channel and the payee of a participant), `holds.json` (8), `history_exceptions.json` (17, one with `impact: block_row`), `payout_answers.json` (8, an answer holds for the same amount and the same question only). Until now the gate knew the contract, a blocked payout, nothing to pay and one threshold of the organization.
- Decision:
  - **Three entities of the settlements module** (migration `0021_admission.sql`, Drizzle `payees`, `holds`, `payoutAnswers`; `org_id` on each, no foreign keys): `settlements.payees` (one active per owner: `channel` tipalti | easystaff | other, `payee_ref` = the id in the cash register or null = by the payment history, `status` confirmed | proposed, `basis`, `valid_from`, `source`; `setPayee` replaces the previous active one with the reason «replaced»), `settlements.holds` (`kind` decision | artist_request | question | dispute, `blocks` (a dispute informs unless it blocks), `reason`, `release_when`, `amount_cents`, `period`, `review_on`, `since`, `label_id` = the book or null = every book, `status` active | released with the reason), `settlements.payout_answers` (`due_cents`: the answer holds for exactly that payable, `decision` pay | pay_task, `why`, `about`, `answered_by`, `label_id`). Rows are never edited: a replacement, a release or a deactivation with a reason. `owners.contracts.payout_threshold_cents` (null = the default of the organization; `createContract` takes it, `setContractThreshold` sets it, `approvedContracts` returns it).
  - **The gate** (`payout-gate.ts`, `Admission` beside the balance): owner_not_confirmed → no_contract_for_label → start_missing → nothing_to_pay → below_threshold (the contract's threshold under the label by the payroll's contract choice, else `PAYOUT_THRESHOLD_CENTS`) → **hold** (an active hold that blocks, in the book or in every book) → payout_blocked → **no_payee** → **needs_answer** (the owner had no payout in the book and no active answer «pay» for exactly `amountCents`) → allowed. The owner's «нет заключения» and «изменилось после проверки» have no data yet (`payout_checks.json` was not sent): not implemented. The contract checks stay first (a row without a contract is not in the payroll at all); «вне ведомости» (nothing to pay, the threshold) comes before the blockers as the owner's order says (the old test of `payout_blocked` before `nothing_to_pay` changed).
  - **The checks «no payee» and «needs an answer» act once the organization has entered its first payee row** (`Admission.active` = a payee row exists, active or not). Without one a world pays as before: the tests, the e2e seed and an organization that does not keep payees are untouched; the real organization turned them on with the 8 decisions of the owner. The February-style «by the payment history» payees (the owner's empty `payee` = the channel of the last payment) need the cash registers of stage 4: on the live data 149 owners are `no_payee` until then.
  - **The facade** (`admission.ts`): `listAdmission({ ownerId, labelId, active }, viewer)` (the rows with the owner's name, the holds and answers of the book; a manager names one of its labels), `setPayee`, `deactivatePayee`, `createHold` (the amount in dollars → cents), `releaseHold`, `createAnswer`, `deactivateAnswer`, `setContractThreshold`; `admissionOf(rows, …)` builds the gate's input, `admissionIndex` reads every owner's rows once for the payables and the payroll, `contractThresholds` picks the contract as the payroll does. `balanceWithFiles` reads the owner's rows in the same snapshot as the operations and the starts and answers `balance.admission { active, thresholdCents, hadPayout, payee, holds, answers }`; `createPayout` throws `hold`, `no_payee`, `needs_answer`.
  - **The API**: `GET /settlements/admission?ownerId=&label=&active=` (read_money settlements label), `POST /settlements/payees`, `POST /settlements/payees/:id/deactivate`, `POST /settlements/holds` (pay settlements label: the body names the book), `POST /settlements/holds/:id/release`, `POST /settlements/answers` (pay settlements label), `POST /settlements/answers/:id/deactivate`, `PATCH /contracts/:id { payoutThresholdCents }` (pay settlements org); the manager table + 8 lines (the reads L, the writes x); `createContractBody.payoutThresholdCents`.
  - **The loader** `scripts/stage3/load-admission.ts <folder 6> [--apply]`: a row per decision of the owner keyed by the nick (the owner of the pseudonym; `source` = `<file>:<id or party>` makes it idempotent), the holds and answers under 0to8, a disputed item blocks only with `impact: block_row`, a closed item is not loaded. On the live store: 8 payees (2 Tipalti with a payee id, 6 EasyStaff by history), 8 holds (6 active, 2 «нужно решение» prefixed), 14 open disputes (1 blocking: a payout outside the start, 2 450,49), 8 answers; 3 closed items skipped. The September payroll of 0to8 after it (`payroll-summary.ts`): 8 owners allowed (Σ 62 975,06), 149 `no_payee` (Σ 647 077,71), 7 `hold` (Σ 172 217,83), 37 below the threshold, 797 `owner_not_confirmed`, 193 `nothing_to_pay`, 1 `start_missing`.
  - **Frontend**: P10 the tab «Допуск» (`pages/owners/AdmissionTab.tsx`: the gate of the balance with its reason (`admission-gate` `data-reason`), the payee (`payee-<id>` `data-channel`, `action-set-payee` → `PayeeDialog`, `action-deactivate-payee`), the holds (`hold-<id>` `data-kind` / `data-status` / `data-blocks`, `action-create-hold` → `HoldDialog`, `action-release-hold-<id>`), the answers (`answer-<id>` `data-due` / `data-active`, `action-create-answer` → `AnswerDialog` pre-filled with the gate's amount, `action-deactivate-answer-<id>`), the threshold (`admission-threshold-value` `data-cents`, `action-set-threshold` → `ThresholdDialog`), the reason dialog (`field-admission-reason`, `action-admission-reason-submit`); `admission-inactive` while the organization has no payee); the gate chips `hold`, `no_payee`, `needs_answer` on P9 / P10 / the payroll; e2e `46-admission.spec.ts` (S56).
- Checked: `tests/admission.test.ts` (the pure gate order with the admission, the activation by the first payee, the first payout and the answer for the exact amount, the payables and the payroll answering the same gate, a replaced and a deactivated payee, a hold of another book, a dispute that informs or blocks, the release, the contract threshold over the default, a contract created with a threshold, the viewer), `aggregates.test.ts` (the order), `api-contract.test.ts` (the eight operations in the scenario; the payout of the scenario goes through the admission), `org-scope.test.ts` (32 tables, the walk with B's contract, payee, hold and answer), `abilities.test.ts` (105 operations); the full backend suite and the e2e after the stage.
- Open:
  - О1: the channel «by the payment history» (the owner's empty `payee`) and the checks «нет заключения» / «изменилось после проверки» need the cash registers and `payout_checks.json` (stage 4).
  - О2: an answer stays active after the payout it allowed; the gate needs none for the second payout (the owner had one), so it changes nothing; a deactivation with a reason is the way to withdraw it.
  - О3: the threshold of a contract applies through the payroll's contract choice (the label's contract first, then the newest); several contracts with different thresholds follow that choice.
- References: `packages/backend/migrations/postgres/0021_admission.sql`; `packages/backend/src/modules/settlements/{schema,repository,service}.ts`, `modules/owners/{schema,repository,service}.ts`; `packages/backend/src/facade/{admission,payout-gate,context,payouts,payroll,aggregates,index}.ts`; `packages/shared/src/{api-schemas,api-responses}.ts`; `packages/backend/src/api/{operations.ts,routes/{settlements,owners}.ts}`; `packages/backend/scripts/stage3/load-admission.ts`; `packages/backend/tests/{admission,aggregates,api-contract,org-scope,abilities}.test.ts`, `tests/helpers/manager-answers.ts`; `packages/frontend/src/pages/owners/{AdmissionTab,OwnerPage}.tsx`, `src/api/{client,coverage}.ts`, `e2e/golden/46-admission.spec.ts`; `docs/разбор_пакета_2026-11-06.md` (A8–A11).
- Status: accepted (the owner's module 17 and the decisions of folder 6 of 2026-11-06)

## D-177: Read access for Claude over MCP: the read token, the paging of the long lists, readable operation ids
- Date: 2026-11-06
- Question: the owner wants Claude to read the ERP through MCP. The API had no credential for a program (only the cookie and the system key, which sees no money), the long lists answered up to 11 MB, and the generated tool names were unreadable.
- Decision:
  - **The MCP server** is the package `@ivotoby/openapi-mcp-server` (pinned 1.17.0) over `/api/v1/openapi.json`, started by `packages/backend/scripts/mcp-erp.ts` from `.mcp.json`: only GET operations, without the tags `auth`, `orgs`, `users`. Nothing of it runs inside the API.
  - **The read token:** `MCP_READ_TOKEN` in `.env` (≥ 32 characters; unset = off), `Authorization: Bearer`. It is checked before the cookie in both `AUTH_MODE`s; a wrong token or another scheme is 401, never the rights of `off`. The principal is the new kind `reader`: `read` and `read_money` of every label of `ERP_DEFAULT_ORG`, every other action `403 forbidden`; `X-Org` of another organization is 403. The read-only rule is therefore on the server, not in the tool list.
  - **The paging** (`api/paging.ts`, `S.pageQuery`, at the routes: the facade and the money are untouched): `limit` (1–500) and `offset` on `/owners`, `/owners/payables`, `/owners/payroll` (`rows`), `/artists`, `/artists/balances`, `/tracks`, `/tracks/summary`, `/tracks/catalog`, `/releases`, `/documents`, `/settlements/operations`, `/settlements/start` (`rows`). A session without both gets the whole list as before, so the screens do not change; with one of them, and always for the read token (default 50), the answer carries `total`, `limit`, `offset`: bare arrays become `{ total, limit, offset, items }` (`R.listOrPage`), objects keep their fields. The read token also gets 50 by default on `/splits`, `/participations`, `/ads`. The `limit` of `/documents` is now 1–500 (nobody passed it) and gives a page.
  - **`operationId`** of every operation in the OpenAPI document: `get_owners_by_id_balance` (`operationIdOf`), the names of the MCP tools.
- Checked: `tests/read-token.test.ts` (both modes on the real chain: every operation that is not a read answers 403; 401 on a wrong token; the pages; the whole list of a session; the unique ids); the tools called over stdio on the live API.
- Open:
  - О1: a page does not make a calculation cheaper: `/owners/payables` still calculates everything (≈ 8 s) and cuts afterwards.
  - О2: `/signals` keeps its own `limit`; the sheets `waiting`, `buyouts` of the payroll are not paged.
  - О3: the data the tools read (names, amounts) enter the context of Claude: the owner accepted this for the local check on 2026-11-06; a wider use is the owner's decision.
  - О4: the token is one per installation, without a list of revocations: it is changed in `.env`. Remote use from claude.ai needs OAuth and a public address.
- References: `packages/backend/src/api/{paging,openapi}.ts`, `src/auth/{abilities,middleware}.ts`, `src/orgs/middleware.ts`, `scripts/mcp-erp.ts`, `.mcp.json`, `packages/shared/src/api-{schemas,responses}.ts`; recipe 19
- Status: accepted (the owner's request of 2026-11-06)

## D-178: The meaning of the fields in the zod schemas, and how it reaches Claude
- Date: 2026-11-06
- Question: Claude sees the API only through the texts of the OpenAPI document; about three quarters of the response fields had no description, the money fields of the balance among them, and the package of D-177 shows an operation's `description` instead of its `summary`, so operations with errors lost their summary.
- Decision:
  - **The descriptions:** one text per field name in `const D` of `api-responses.ts` (the same field says the same thing in every answer); a field whose meaning depends on the place (`period.*`, `total.earned`, `positions[].amount`) has its own text beside it. Written from `balance.ts`, `reports.ts`, the layers, not from the names. The query parameters of the reads too. No schema shape changed: only `.describe()`.
  - **The description of an operation** (`descriptionOf` in `openapi.ts`) is the summary, the errors and, on an operation with `explain: true` in `operations.ts` (ten money answers: the balances, payables, payroll, overview, reconciliation, track money and summary, the queue), a section «Response fields» built from the zod schema two levels deep (`responseFieldsOf`). The package of MCP puts only the request schema into a tool, so this is the one way the meaning of an answer reaches Claude.
  - **The server instructions** (`SERVER_INSTRUCTIONS` of `scripts/mcp-erp.ts`): the money model A = B + C + ours, owner and pseudonym, the book, what `total.payable` and `payout` mean, how to page.
  - **The guard:** `tests/api-descriptions.test.ts`: every scalar of every read answer and every parameter of a read has a meaning, except the names listed in `PLAIN`; the bare text of `money` does not count.
- Checked: `tests/api-descriptions.test.ts`; the tool list over stdio on the live API.
- Open:
  - О1: the schemas of writes and imports are described only where the common names reach them; the guard covers the reads.
  - О2: the operations without `explain` show no response fields; the list grows by need, each costs context in every session.
  - О3: the frontend bundle carries the texts (`api-responses.ts` is shared).
- References: `packages/shared/src/api-{responses,schemas}.ts`, `packages/backend/src/api/{openapi,operations}.ts`, `packages/backend/scripts/mcp-erp.ts`
- Status: accepted (the owner's request of 2026-11-06)

## D-179: P31 «Подключить Claude»: the instruction in the interface, one description of the connection
- Date: 2026-11-06
- Question: the owner wants to hand the MCP connection to another person through the site, which is reachable at the owner's KeenDNS address.
- Decision:
  - **The page** `/claude` (`pages/claude/ClaudePage.tsx`, sidebar «Организация», `nav-claude`, `op: GET /users`: the token reads every label, so only who may manage the organization sees it; no API call): the command for Claude Code, the block for the application, what to ask first, what to know. The address is `window.location.origin`, so the page is right on any host; the token is never shown, a placeholder `ТОКЕН` stands for it; without https (and not on localhost) `claude-insecure` warns. Without a clipboard (http) the text is selected by hand.
  - **One description** `@erp/shared/mcp-connection`: the arguments, the environment, `MCP_INSTRUCTIONS`, `claudeCodeCommand`, `claudeDesktopConfig`, used by the page and by `scripts/mcp-erp.ts`, so somebody without the repository gets the same reads and the same instructions.
- Checked: `tests/claude-connection.test.ts`, `e2e/golden/47-claude.spec.ts`; the generated command run through `claude mcp add` (project scope, a scratch folder); the page in the browser.
- Open:
  - О1: on 2026-11-06 the public address leads to the port 3001, an old process without the token and the paging, in `AUTH_MODE=off`: anybody with the address reads and writes without a login. The owner decides on `AUTH_MODE=on` and the restart; this decision changes neither.
  - О2: the application block is checked as JSON only, not in Claude Desktop.
- References: `packages/shared/src/mcp-connection.ts`, `packages/frontend/src/pages/claude/ClaudePage.tsx`, `packages/backend/scripts/mcp-erp.ts`
- Status: accepted (the owner's request of 2026-11-06)

## D-180: The remote MCP endpoint: `/api/mcp`, P31 gives the remote connection
- Date: 2026-11-06
- Question: the owner wants Claude to connect remotely by the public address, not through a local `npx`.
- Decision:
  - **The endpoint** `POST /api/mcp` (`src/api/mcp.ts`, `@modelcontextprotocol/sdk` 1.32.2, Streamable HTTP, stateless, JSON answers), mounted after the authentication and outside `/api/v1`, so it is not an operation of the contract. Only the read token gets in, in both `AUTH_MODE`s (a cookie or nothing: 401); GET and DELETE 405.
  - **The tools** are the reads of `operations.ts` the token may call, without the tags `auth`, `orgs`, `users`; the name is the `operationId` with hyphens, the description `descriptionOf`, the input the path parameters and the query (`z.toJSONSchema`, required by `safeParse(undefined)`). A call goes through this server's own HTTP API on the loopback with the same token: rights, paging and errors are the API's.
  - **The connection** needs no OAuth: Claude Code takes `--transport http --header`, a custom connector of claude.ai and the application takes «No sign in» with a request header. A connector connects from the cloud of Anthropic, so the address has to be public.
  - **P31** shows the remote address, the command, the steps of the connector and the header; the address is `VITE_PUBLIC_ORIGIN` (the repo's `.env`, read at the build through `envDir`), else the address of the page; without https it warns.
- Checked: `tests/read-token.test.ts` (both modes, the list, a call, the errors), `claude mcp list` on the live API.
- Open:
  - О1: the connector of claude.ai is not tried yet: it needs the public process restarted on this code.
  - О2: the local script `scripts/mcp-erp.ts` and the package of D-177 stay for this repository; they can go once the endpoint has proved itself.
  - О3: `AUTH_MODE=off` on the public address stays by the owner's decision of 2026-11-06.
- References: `packages/backend/src/api/{mcp,server-app}.ts`, `packages/shared/src/mcp-connection.ts`, `packages/frontend/src/pages/claude/ClaudePage.tsx`, `packages/frontend/vite.config.ts`
- Status: accepted (the owner's request of 2026-11-06)

## D-181: The position per pseudonym («ники не сальдируются»); the buyouts by the registry of 2026-11-03; the loaders of the update of 2026-11-06
- Date: 2026-11-07
- Question: the owner's answers of 2026-11-06 (evening, `context_data/для_разработчика_2026-11-06_update/`) reverse two things of D-174 … D-176: the start is on the nick and «ники одного человека не сальдируются: минус одного ника не гасит плюс другого; к выплате получателю = Σ положительных позиций его ников» (формулы v1 §1, §5.4, §9); a buyout acts from the day of signing: everything not paid to the seller on that day on the bought ISRCs stays with the label (R5), the first month of the label per contract is the column of `transition_buyouts.csv`. The rest of the answers (NoGhost half of the label share, `no_split`, the threshold, the write-offs as a plus) match what was built.
- Decision:
  - **The position** (`facade/balance.ts`): every pseudonym has a position per book and area, `start + earned − recouped − adsCharged − paid + returns + credits + adjustments − writtenOff` of its own items (the start rows, its master income, the recoup of the positions it holds, its advertising, the operations with its `artistId`), plus `shared`; the owner's own position (`OWNER_POSITION = ''`) holds what has no pseudonym: the publishing of the owner (the writers are mapped to the owner, D-087), the operations without a pseudonym. `payable` stays the formula = the net of every position (Σ positions = the formula, book by book: `Case.verify()`); **`payableShown` = Σ of the positive positions** of the pseudonyms and of the owner's own, per book and per area (`byArea.payableShown`), `payableNegative` = some position is negative, `ownerPosition` on the book, the areas and the total; the artist row carries `position`, `positionNegative`, `byLabel[].position / positionByArea`. The gate (`context.ts gateOf`) and the button read `payableShown` (the area: `min(area shown, book shown)`); the payroll's pseudonym rows carry their position in `payable` (`payableShown` = its positive part), the owner row the owner's own, the total row Σ positive. An owner with one pseudonym and nothing of its own is byte for byte the old one (`payableShown` = max(payable, 0)).
  - **The allocation of a payout** (`settlements.operations.allocation`, migration 0022): the button splits the amount over the positive positions of the area pro rata (floored to cents, the residue to the largest) and stores `{ "<artistId>": cents, "": cents }`; the balance reduces exactly those positions, a return without a pseudonym follows the allocation of its payout (`splitCents`), the snapshot keeps `allocation` and `payoutByArtist` (`net` = the position, `shareOfPayout` = the cents). A payout with a pseudonym (every migrated one: all 2 400 of the live store carry one) reduces that pseudonym; a payout without either (the shape before this decision) leaves the owner's own position negative, and that deficit is shared to the positive positions of the same book and area, the largest first (a payment to the payee «разносится по его участникам»); what no positive position can take stays on the owner's own.
  - **The buyouts** (`scripts/stage3/apply-buyout-workbook.ts`): the registry is «Выкупленные записи — сплиты после выкупа.xlsx» (1 032 ISRC, 20 contracts; the PandaDoc csv of D-175 covered 512), the first month of the label per seller is «первый месяц лейбла» of `transition_buyouts.csv` (= the first reporting month not paid to the seller on the signing day: R5 with the monthly splits, M52); a seller row ≥ the bought percent → the whole row to the label (`facade.buyout`), a row above it → the bought part only (`changeSplit`, the owner: «обнулять нужно эту часть доли»). Applied on the live store: 979 whole rows (14 sellers), 53 partial shares (DJ ZENITE 16.6 / 25 / 12, Habazane 20), 11 already zero, 8 sellers not in the split, 34 without a split, 4 ambiguous (qaraqshy); the only post-start change is **LDRR from 2026-09** (132 ISRC): its September earned 2 657,43 → 0. The Prblmsss price `main:15225` stays (a buyout operation is outside payable; the owner says the start adjustment covers it).
  - **The loaders of the update**: `scripts/stage3/load-payout-checks.ts` (`payout_checks.json` of 2026-10-20, the payout route of the old ERP: the channel and the payee of 163 owners as `proposed` payees, basis «маршрут по истории платежей», a decision beats it: 8 kept; its 23 open questions as holds of kind `question`; applied: 154 payees, 23 holds → the September `no_payee` fell from 149 to 59, `hold` 7 → 21, the allowed 8 → 83), `scripts/stage3/load-ascap-supplement.ts` (the owner's list `ASCAP_доплата_артистам_2026-11-06.csv`: group «А — не вошёл в старт» with «начислить» = 128 `credit` operations of 2026-11-06, Σ 154 965,56, through the operation import with the basis document «ASCAP вне старта — список владельца 2026-11-06» and `external_id` `ascap-2026-11-06:<line>`; the 3 rows «кроме выкупленных работ после даты подписания» (2 792,75), the 88 of group «Б» (30 955,46, the owner checks them against the start first) and LDRR (9 915,00, excluded) are listed in `ascap_left.csv`, not loaded); the nine label strings of September as aliases (0to8: «Bleached Records / 0to8», «0to8 / Bleached Records», «0to8 / no job records», «0to8 x Ahh Music», «0to8 & BIG Records», «0to8 x fate»; NoGhost: «0to8 x NoGhost Records», «0to8 x NoGhost», «0to8 x NoGhost Recorsd»; ≈ $60 of `label_unmapped`).
  - **Checked against the owner's rule on the live store**: at `asOf` 2026-09 under 0to8 two owners have pseudonyms of both signs (TRXSHBXY −13 524,77 / WXRMANE +5 436,36 → shown 5 436,36 instead of nothing; DJ Chanzin +337,24 / DJ THSIZE −118,72 → 337,24 instead of 218,52). The SXLLX example of the Echo8 note: under 0to8 at 2026-08 the start 170,55 = payable 170,55 (the owner's target); the Echo8-account files stay a book of their own. The DJ TUUV case №49 (the owner's «сначала реклама и минусы, потом аванс»): the end position is the same under both orders (every term is a subtraction; what differs is only how much of the advance stays unrecouped at a given month), so no layer changes; it matters for a forgiveness at the signing date (stage В) and is noted there.
- Checked: `tests/position.test.ts` (the two signs, the gate and the payroll rows, the button's allocation and its snapshot, the owner's own position with publishing and a payout of the area `all`, a return by the allocation, a payout without an allocation shared the largest first, a migrated payout with a pseudonym), `Case.verify()` (Σ positions = the formula per book, `payableShown` = Σ positive, Σ areas of `ownerPosition`), `start.test.ts` (the button pays 93,5: S2's −5 stays its own), the golden cases unchanged, the full suite, the e2e.
- Open:
  - О1: a payout made to the owner without a pseudonym before this decision has no allocation and is shared by the rule above; none exists in the live store (every history payout names its pseudonym).
  - О2: the owner's question «к какому нику относить выплату без ника» is answered by the allocation (the button never pays without one); the pro-rata rule is ours, the owner may prefer the largest first.
  - О3: group «Б» of the ASCAP list needs the owner's method of «вошёл ли в старт» (the cabinet's `terms` do not tell ASCAP from B17); the three rows «кроме выкупленных работ» need the net amount.
  - О4: 59 owners stay `no_payee` after the route file; the 23 questions of the route block them as holds until the owner answers.
  - О5: the positions of the owner's module 17 are per participant with publishing per writer; ours keep the owner's publishing on the owner's own position until the publishing stage (Г) maps the writers to pseudonyms.
- References: `packages/backend/src/facade/{balance,context,payouts,payroll}.ts`, `packages/backend/src/modules/settlements/{schema,service}.ts`, `migrations/postgres/0022_payout_allocation.sql`, `packages/shared/src/api-responses.ts`, `packages/backend/scripts/stage3/{apply-buyout-workbook,load-payout-checks,load-ascap-supplement}.ts`, `packages/frontend/src/pages/owners/BalanceTab.tsx`, `docs/разбор_пакета_2026-11-06.md` §8
- Status: accepted (the owner's answers of 2026-11-06)

## D-182: Publishing v2: the report summaries in a module of their own, beside the old one
- Date: 2026-11-07
- Question: the owner wants the publishing data sorted out by hand, with songwriters and songs as their own records, before any link to owners or ISRC is decided. The old module mixes the statement lines with the writer → owner map and feeds the money.
- Decision:
  - **A new module `pubv2`** (schema `pubv2`, migration 0023): `files`, `songwriters`, `songwriter_aliases`, `songs`, `song_aliases`, `accruals`. It holds only what the reports say: no owner, no ISRC, no share. `label_id` sits on the file alone (the book is the file, D-169).
  - **The source is the summary of a report**, read by the shape of a sheet (`summary-format.ts`): `periods` (Q4 2024 + Q1 2025, the writer is the one rights-holder of the lines), `blocks` (`SONGWRITER: name` then its songs: the Cover of Q2 and Q3 2025, the Songwriter Breakdown of Q1 and Q2 2026, the `Updated` run), `flat` (Q4 2025).
  - **A file is a partition**: reads take `status = 'loaded'`; a quarter held by a loaded file of the label is `409 period_loaded` unless `replace=1` (the old file becomes `superseded`); `POST /publishing-v2/files/:id/remove` deletes the partition and restores what it replaced. Nothing cites these rows, so the physical removal is safe here, unlike D-173.
  - **A spelling with the same `normalizeName` is the same record**; another spelling makes a new record, nothing merges them yet.
  - **The money does not move**: `calculate()`, the layers, the balance and the old module never read `pubv2` (`tests/pubv2.test.ts`). The `Updated` run of Q2 2026 is loaded here and not in the old module; D-173 and §4.3 of the pack analysis stay open for the money.
  - **The pages** P32 `/publishing-v2` (songwriters, files, upload) and P33 `/publishing-v2/:id` (spellings, quarters, songs). Reads are `read_money` under `scope: label`, the writes are `approve`.
- Checked: `tests/pubv2.test.ts` (the three shapes, the partition, the boundary), the contract, `org-scope.test.ts`, `abilities.test.ts`. On the live store all seven reports load: 34 songwriters, 1 169 songs, 4 321 accruals; the sum of each file equals the total it names, up to 0.07 in the two reports whose Cover gives cents; the quarters equal `pub_lines` (Q2 2025 +0.06, Q3 2025 +0.06, the rest 0).
- Open:
  - О1: Q2 2025: the Cover and the lines differ between two songwriters by about $41 (the sum of the quarter agrees): a question to B17 or the owner.
  - О2: 19 song titles carry broken characters from the file (`√`); they stay separate songs until an alias joins them.
  - О3: one rights-holder of the old module exists only as a broken spelling ($0.17 in Q2 2026): `pub_lines` lost a character at load; `pubv2` reads the name whole. The cause in the old reader is not found.
  - О4: the links to owners and ISRC, and whether the calculation moves here (D-087 or the owner's rules v3): nothing decided. The old rules need the operation type and the authors, which `accruals` does not hold.
- References: `packages/backend/src/modules/pubv2/`, `packages/backend/migrations/postgres/0023_pubv2.sql`, `packages/backend/src/api/routes/publishing-v2.ts`, `packages/backend/scripts/pubv2/load.ts`, `packages/frontend/src/pages/reference/publishing-v2/`
- Status: accepted (the owner's request of 2026-11-07)

## D-183: The links of publishing v2 to owners: a module of decisions, a tab with recommendations
- Date: 2026-11-07
- Question: the owner wants a list on `/publishing-v2` that recommends an owner for a songwriter, lets the operator take it or choose another, and records the decision outside the module, at the level of the facade.
- Decision:
  - **A new module `links`** (schema `links`, migration 0024), table `songwriter_owner`: `songwriter_id`, `owner_id`, `source` (`recommended` / `manual`), `decided_by`, `decided_at`. The facade has no tables (D-012), so the decision lives in a module of its own and `pubv2` and `owners` still know nothing of each other. One row per songwriter; a new decision replaces it, the `audit` module keeps the history.
  - **The recommendation** (`facade/pubv2-links.ts`) decides nothing: the old map `pub_writers`, then one person-owner with this name or alias, then one with the same words in another order. Nothing is linked without the operator.
  - **A publishing owner is a person**: `409 owner_is_label`, as in the old `mapWriter`. A merged owner is read through `resolveOwnerId`.
  - **`GET` / `POST /publishing-v2/links`**, the tab «Связи» of P32: those without a link first, «Подтвердить» takes the recommendation, «Другой» / «Выбрать» / «Изменить» open the picker.
- Checked: `tests/pubv2.test.ts` (the three kinds of recommendation, confirm and change, a label refused, the boundary), the contract, `org-scope.test.ts`, the e2e. On the live store 31 of 34 songwriters have a recommendation and no link was set.
- Open:
  - О1: 30 of the 31 recommendations lead to an owner the old loader created from the B17 name itself (`origin = publishing`, no pseudonym; 13 of them with a contract): confirming one keeps the legal name, it does not find the artist behind it. The operator chooses the real owner where it is another record.
  - О2: the links are not read by the money; the move of the calculation stays with D-182 О4.
- References: `packages/backend/src/modules/links/`, `packages/backend/src/facade/pubv2-links.ts`, `packages/backend/migrations/postgres/0024_links.sql`, `packages/frontend/src/pages/reference/publishing-v2/LinksTab.tsx`
- Status: accepted (the owner's request of 2026-11-07)

## D-184: The songs of publishing v2 to ISRCs: candidates through the owner, the set by hand
- Date: 2026-11-07
- Question: the owner wants the recordings of every song set by hand, with candidates found through the linked owner, on a screen of its own.
- Decision:
  - **`links.song_isrc`** (migration 0025): `song_id`, `isrc`, `source` (`recommended` = it was among the candidates, `manual` = entered), `decided_by`, `decided_at`. A song has many ISRCs; a decision replaces the whole set, the `audit` module keeps the history.
  - **The candidates** (`facade/pubv2-links.ts`): the tracks of `owners.tracks` with the same title without its version (`baseTitle`: no `(…)`, `[…]`, ` - …`), those whose open split has a pseudonym of the linked owner first (`owner`), the rest after (`title`). The title is needed because 30 of the 31 linked owners have no pseudonym: through the owner alone the list would be empty.
  - **`GET /publishing-v2/songs`, `GET …/songs/:id/candidates`, `POST …/songs/:id/isrcs`**, the tab «Треки» of P32: the songs without an ISRC first; the dialog ticks the confirmed ones, or the owner's until something is confirmed; nothing is written until «Сохранить». An ISRC outside the catalog is `404`.
  - **The money does not read it** (`tests/pubv2.test.ts`).
- Checked: `tests/pubv2.test.ts`, the contract, `org-scope.test.ts`, the e2e. On the live store: 1 169 songs, 1 080 with candidates, 89 without, 17 with a candidate of the linked owner, at most 45 candidates; no ISRC was set.
- Open:
  - О1: until the songwriters are linked to the owners with pseudonyms, nearly every candidate is only a title match: common titles lead to tracks of other artists.
  - О2: the 56 titles of the `Updated` run absent from every detail tab and the 19 broken titles may have no candidate: the field «Другой ISRC» takes them by hand.
- References: `packages/backend/src/facade/pubv2-links.ts`, `packages/backend/migrations/postgres/0025_song_isrc.sql`, `packages/frontend/src/pages/reference/publishing-v2/TracksTab.tsx`
- Status: accepted (the owner's request of 2026-11-07)

## D-185: The terms of publishing in `links`; the source of the calculation behind a flag; the shadow run
- Date: 2026-11-07
- Question: the owner keeps the terms approach (buyouts change the percent, one owner has 90 %), money goes to the owner and is not divided by song; the terms should sit with the links, and the calculation should be able to move to publishing v2.
- Decision:
  - **`links.publishing_terms`** (migration 0026): the columns of `publishing.pub_terms` plus `decided_by`, the 100 rows copied one to one, the old table stays. A term is never edited: `setPublishingTerm` closes the open term at the date and opens a new one (`409 terms_overlap` otherwise). `POST /publishing-v2/terms`, the button «Сплит» and the column «Процент» of the tab «Связи».
  - **The source** (`facade/pubv2-source.ts`): `snapshotForCalculation()` answered from `pubv2` and `links` in the shape of the old module, wrapped in `openFacade` as `scope.ts` wraps a service, so `calculation.ts` and the layers are untouched. `PUBLISHING_SOURCE=v2` or `publishingSource: 'v2'` turns it on; the default is `v1` and nothing changes.
  - **Under v2** a line has no `songwriters`, so the equal parts of D-087 do not apply and the whole line goes to the owner of its songwriter; no `operationType`, so the one typed term does not apply; a songwriter without a link waits as `writer_unmapped`.
  - **The shadow run** `scripts/pubv2/shadow.ts`: `calculate()` over both sources, per quarter, reason and owner.
- Checked: `tests/pubv2.test.ts` (a term closes the open one, v2 sends the line to the owner with the percent of `links`, a songwriter without a link waits), the contract, `org-scope.test.ts`, the e2e. The shadow run on the live store: the quarters to Q4 2025 equal v1 up to the rounding of the Cover; Q1 2026 the author amount falls from 866 425.41 to 725 378.09, Q2 2026 the payable rises by the `Updated` run; B falls from 1 430 712.23 to 1 277 692.24.
- Open:
  - О1: the flag stays `v1`. Before `v2`: D-087 must be cancelled by the owner (one co-author loses about $541k of author amount), and the songwriters linked to owners that hold the terms.
  - О2: the old `POST /publishing/terms` writes only `pub_terms`, the new one only `links`: until the switch the money reads the old table, so a new percent has no effect on it.
  - О3: the reads around the calculation still name the old statements: `reports.ts`, `payouts.ts`; `reassignOwner` does not know `links`.
- References: `packages/backend/src/facade/pubv2-source.ts`, `packages/backend/scripts/pubv2/shadow.ts`, `packages/backend/migrations/postgres/0026_publishing_terms.sql`, `packages/frontend/src/pages/reference/publishing-v2/TermDialog.tsx`
- Status: accepted (the owner's request of 2026-11-07); the switch itself waits for О1

## D-186: A B17 line belongs to its rights-holder; the equal parts of D-087 are cancelled for v2
- Date: 2026-11-07
- Question: D-087 divides a line among our writers of `songwriters`; the owner says the label looks only at the rights-holder.
- Decision:
  - A line of B17 goes whole to the owner of its rights-holder, then the percent of that owner's term (D-185). `songwriters` is not read for money.
  - This is how the source v2 already works (`facade/pubv2-source.ts`); the source v1 keeps D-087 until the flag moves (D-185 О1), so the old answers do not change before the switch.
  - The merge of owners is not extended to `links` for now: a merge goes into the owner that holds the terms, so the merged record has none (asked by the owner on 2026-11-07).
- Checked: the shadow run; for the largest rights-holder (term 50 % until 2026-10-14) the lines are 626 110.81 under v1 and 991 740.33 under v2, the author amount 313 055.41 and 495 870.16; Q2 2025, whose lines carry no `songwriters`, is equal in both.
- Open: О1: a merge the other way (the owner with terms merged into another) would leave its terms in `links` on the merged id; then `links` must learn `reassignOwner`.
- References: `packages/backend/src/facade/pubv2-source.ts`, D-087, D-185
- Status: accepted (the owner's answer of 2026-11-07); supersedes D-087 for the source v2

## D-187: The owner of a songwriter suggested through its songs
- Date: 2026-11-07
- Question: the B17 names are legal names, the terms and contracts sit on the owners of the pseudonyms; the owner asked to base the suggestions on the songs.
- Decision:
  - For every song of a songwriter the facade takes the tracks of the catalog with its title (`baseTitle`), the pseudonyms of their open splits and the person-owners behind them, and counts the songs per owner. The leader (at least half of the songs and twice the next) becomes the recommendation with the reason `songs`, before the old map and the name.
  - `GET /publishing-v2/links` adds `songs`, `songCandidates` (at most three owners with the number of songs and `hasTerms`) and `recommendation.hasTerms`; the dialog «Изменить» lists the candidates. Nothing is linked without the operator.
  - Also answered: v2 holds what the report holds (the Cover of Q2 2025 differs from its detail tab by about $41 between two rights-holders; the summary is taken as it is); the typed term of one owner (50 % for Synchronization beside 90 % for any type) does not apply under v2, since the summaries carry no operation type.
- Checked: `tests/pubv2.test.ts` (the owner behind the songs wins over the name); on the live store 24 of 34 songwriters get a recommendation by songs, 21 of them to an owner with a term, 20 differ from the current link.
- Open: О1: the typed term: under v2 the 90 % covers the Synchronization lines too (about $36k of lines since 2026, the author amount about $14k higher); the owner decides whether that is right.
- References: `packages/backend/src/facade/pubv2-links.ts`, `packages/frontend/src/pages/reference/publishing-v2/LinksTab.tsx`
- Status: accepted (the owner's request of 2026-11-07)

## D-188: The terms of v2 without an operation type; a mistaken term is cancelled, not edited
- Date: 2026-11-07
- Question: the owner divides publishing only by the owner's percent; the type of the contract and the operation type of the report decide nothing. One owner's terms were set by the stage 6 loader, not by a person: 90 % for any type and 50 % for Synchronization, from a hard-coded rule for the contracts named «administration» (`scripts/mvp/steps-main.ts`), starting at the signing date because the parser did not read the effective date of the text.
- Decision:
  - **No operation type in `links.publishing_terms`** (migration 0027): the typed rows are cancelled with the reason, the column and the unique index by type go; the unique start is per owner among the rows not cancelled. The source v2 hands every term to the layers with `operationType = null`.
  - **Cancellation** `POST /publishing-v2/terms/:id/cancel` (body `reason`), the button «Отменить» of the dialog «Сплит»: only the latest term of an owner (`409 term_not_last`), the term it had closed is open again, the row stays with `cancelled_at`, `cancelled_by`, `cancel_reason` and never counts.
  - **The data fix**, on the owner's instruction: the owner's 90 % from 2026-01-01 cancelled (the contract takes effect on 2025-12-23), a new 90 % from 2025-12-01 with the same document. The 90 % itself stays as the loader set it; the owner has not confirmed or changed the percent.
  - The old `publishing.pub_terms` and the source v1 keep their rows.
- Checked: `tests/pubv2.test.ts` (cancel only the latest, the previous one open again, a new start after a cancel), the contract, `org-scope.test.ts`, `abilities.test.ts`, the shadow run.
- Open: О1: the percent of that owner (90 %) comes from the loader's rule, not from the contract text; the quarters before 2025-12 have no term and wait.
- References: `packages/backend/src/modules/links/`, `packages/backend/migrations/postgres/0027_terms_cancel.sql`, `packages/frontend/src/pages/reference/publishing-v2/TermDialog.tsx`
- Status: accepted (the owner's answer of 2026-11-07)

## D-189: The switch to publishing v2
- Date: 2026-11-07
- Question: the owner decided to move the money of publishing to v2 (D-182…D-188) after the shadow runs.
- Decision:
  - **The reads around the calculation follow the source**: `facade/pubv2-source.ts` answers, besides `snapshotForCalculation`, `listStatements` / `activeStatements` (the files of v2 in the shape of a statement: the payout snapshot, the sources page, the overview, the onboarding, the signal of statements without a label), `termsOf` (the terms of `links` on the owner card) and `listWriters` (the songwriters of v2 with their links; nothing ambiguous). `facade/pubv2-links.ts` keeps reading the old module itself for its hint `old_map`.
  - **The old module takes no writes under v2**: `ingestB17`, `replaceB17`, `resolveWriters`, `mapWriter`, `setPublishingTerms` refuse with `409 publishing_v2_active`; its rows stay for the source `v1` and the rollback.
  - **The flag** `PUBLISHING_SOURCE=v2` in `.env`; the processes read it at start. The rollback is `v1` and a restart.
  - `scripts/check-owner-scope.ts` builds its calculation on the same source.
- Checked: `tests/pubv2.test.ts` (under v2 the statements, the owner card and the writers come from v2, the old writes are refused; under v1 nothing changes), `check-start.ts` under v2 (1 144 owners equal to the cabinet, 5 missing, 0 differ), `check-owner-scope.ts` under v2, the e2e. The shadow run of D-188: B 2 282 242.82 under v2 against 1 430 712.23 under v1.
- Open: О1: the owners with a start row are unchanged up to the cut (2026-08): the publishing of Q3 2026 and later moves their payable. О2: the quarters without a term (one owner before 2025-12, four owners without a percent) and seven songwriters without a link wait in C. О3: the old module goes after one publishing payout under v2.
- References: `packages/backend/src/facade/pubv2-source.ts`, `packages/backend/src/facade/sources.ts`, `packages/backend/scripts/check-owner-scope.ts`
- Status: accepted (the owner's decision of 2026-11-07)


## D-190: One ISRC under one song per songwriter
- Date: 2026-11-07
- Question: may one ISRC be linked to several songs of publishing v2 (D-184)? The owner: the label buys the tracks of the contract's schedule (by ISRC), not the author; co-authors who registered their share appear in the reports as their own songwriter with the same title.
- Decision:
  - **Two songs of one songwriter with one ISRC are a mistake** (a doubled or broken title): `POST /publishing-v2/songs/:id/isrcs` refuses with `409 isrc_on_songwriter_song`, checked in the facade inside the write transaction.
  - **Two songwriters with one ISRC are co-authorship** and stay allowed: each gets its own line and its own term.
  - No database constraint: the link table keeps the key (song, ISRC).
- Checked: `tests/pubv2.test.ts` (the refusal leaves the set unchanged; a second songwriter takes the same ISRC).
- Open: the buyout as a publishing term per ISRC (the registry of buyouts as the basis; a song whose ISRCs disagree goes to the operator) waits for the links of songs to ISRCs, none of which are set yet.
- References: `packages/backend/src/facade/pubv2-links.ts`
- Status: accepted (the owner's decision of 2026-11-07)

## D-191: The songwriter in the link song → ISRC; the hint of a co-author
- Date: 2026-11-07
- Question: the owner asked that the database itself keep the rule of D-190, and that linking the same ISRC for a co-author be easy.
- Decision:
  - **Migration 0028**: `links.song_isrc.songwriter_id` (NOT NULL, the songwriter of the song, written by the facade) and the unique index `song_isrc_one_per_songwriter (org_id, songwriter_id, isrc)`. A song never changes its songwriter in `pubv2`, so the copy does not go stale. The check of D-190 in the facade stays for its clear error; the index stops a write past the facade.
  - **The hint**: `GET /publishing-v2/songs/:id/candidates` gives each candidate `coAuthors` (the other songwriters whose songs already have this ISRC); the order is the owner's tracks, then those of a co-author, then the title only. The tab «Треки» marks them «уже у соавтора: …» and pre-selects them with the owner's tracks. A suggestion only: nothing is linked by itself.
- Checked: `tests/pubv2.test.ts` (the hint of the co-author; the index refuses a second song of one songwriter written past the facade).
- Open: a merge of two songwriters (an alias of an already loaded spelling) must move this column in the same transaction; none exists yet.
- References: `packages/backend/migrations/postgres/0028_song_isrc_songwriter.sql`, `packages/backend/src/facade/pubv2-links.ts`, `packages/frontend/src/pages/reference/publishing-v2/TracksTab.tsx`
- Status: accepted (the owner's decision of 2026-11-07)

## D-192: The terms of an ISRC in publishing v2
- Date: 2026-11-07
- Question: a buyout of the label is fixed by the tracks of the contract's schedule (the owner: «we buy the ISRCs of the appendix, not the author»), and some tracks have special terms. B17 pays a song (rights-holder + title), not an ISRC. How does a percent of one track reach the money without changing `calculate()` and the layers?
- Decision:
  - **A term may name an ISRC**: `links.publishing_terms.isrc` (null = the owner's own percent, as before) and `basis` (the contract, the share sold; free text). Migration 0029. The same rules within one owner and one ISRC: a new term closes the open one, a mistake is cancelled (D-188); a term of an ISRC never closes the owner's own. `POST /publishing-v2/terms` takes `isrc` and `basis`; an ISRC outside the catalog is `404`.
  - **The percent is stored, not the share sold**: special terms are a percent anyway; the share sold of the registry goes into `basis`.
  - **The song takes it through its ISRCs** (`facade/pubv2-isrc-terms.ts`, pure, used by the calculation and the screen): only the terms of the song's owner, in force at the quarter date. No ISRC with a term → the owner's own percent. Every ISRC with a term of one percent → that term. Otherwise (a version without a term, or two percents) → a conflict: the line waits as `publishing_terms_unconfirmed` at the highest percent in question (the owner's or one of the ISRCs'), since B17 gives one sum per song and nothing is guessed. A song without ISRCs keeps the owner's percent: the songs of the buyouts must be linked first.
  - **No layer change**: `pubv2-source.ts` uses the typed-term slot of the layer (D-128): the line gets `operationType = isrc:<termId>` and the term is copied under that key; a conflict gets its own term without a document. `pubv2.linesForCalculation` names the song (grouped by the song, not the title: the same rows). A term without an approved document waits like the owner's.
  - **The screen**: the tab «Треки» has the column «Условие» (`song-terms-<songId>`, `data-state` owner | isrc | conflict) and the button «Условие» → `IsrcTermsDialog` (the state, the history by ISRC with the cancel of the latest, a new term for the chosen ISRCs of the song, all by default). `GET /publishing-v2/songs` gives `terms`, `…/candidates` the owner, `terms` and `isrcTerms`.
- Checked: `tests/pubv2.test.ts` (a buyout at 0 from its quarter, a conflict waits at the highest percent, one percent on every version, the owner's own term untouched, a cancel within its ISRC, an unknown ISRC, a term of another owner does not count, a term without a document waits).
- Open: О1: the buyout registry (by ISRC, the share sold, the date) is not loaded as terms yet: it waits for the links of the songs to ISRCs (none in the live store). О2: the conflict uses the reason `publishing_terms_unconfirmed`; its own reason would change the shared enum and the categorise layer.
- References: `packages/backend/migrations/postgres/0029_isrc_terms.sql`, `packages/backend/src/facade/pubv2-isrc-terms.ts`, `packages/backend/src/facade/pubv2-source.ts`, `packages/frontend/src/pages/reference/publishing-v2/IsrcTermsDialog.tsx`
- Status: accepted (the owner's decision of 2026-11-07)

## D-193: The ISRCs of a song: any track of the catalog, found by ISRC or title
- Date: 2026-11-08
- Question: the dialog «ISRC песни» on the tab «Треки» listed only the hints (tracks of the same title) and had one free field «Другой ISRC» below the list, added silently on save; the operator could not find a track whose title differs (another spelling, a remix name) and did not see what the field would add.
- Decision: the free field is replaced by the search of the catalog (`EntityPicker` kind `tracks`, `GET /search`: an exact ISRC first, else a substring of the title). A picked track joins the list of the dialog checked (it can be unchecked like a hint), the field empties for the next one. The backend is unchanged: an ISRC outside `owners.tracks` is still 404, a link not among the hints is stored as `manual` (D-184), the rule of D-190 still applies.
- References: `packages/frontend/src/pages/reference/publishing-v2/TracksTab.tsx`
- Status: accepted

## D-194: Under publishing v2 the screens write to v2; a merge of owners moves the links
- Date: 2026-11-08
- Question: with `PUBLISHING_SOURCE=v2` (D-189) the owner page, the balance and the queue already read v2, but the button «Условия» of the owner's tab «Паблишинг», the queue actions of `writer_unmapped` / `publishing_terms_unconfirmed` (and the signal `ambiguousWriters`) opened the dialogs of the old module, which refuses writes (`publishing_v2_active`); the old page «Паблишинг» stayed in the sidebar; a merge of owners moved only the old module's writers.
- Decision:
  - `GET /auth/me` (and the login answer) carries `publishingSource: 'v1' | 'v2'` (`src/shared/publishing-source.ts`, the one reading of `PUBLISHING_SOURCE`, also used by `openFacade`; the schema defaults to `v1`). The UI reads it through `usePublishingV2()`.
  - `PublishingTermsDialog` under v2 is the `TermDialog` of v2 (the owner's percent of `links`, the history from the owner card, cancel of the latest); `MapWriterDialog` under v2 finds the songwriter of v2 by its name (unique per organization) in `GET /publishing-v2/links` and opens `ChooseOwnerDialog` (`songwriter-v2-missing` when it is not there). Every caller (the owner's tab, the queue, the signals) follows; the owner's button names `POST /publishing-v2/terms`.
  - The sidebar hides «Паблишинг» (v1) under v2; the page stays by its address with the note `publishing-v1-closed` and a link to v2.
  - A merge of owners under v2 also moves `links.songwriter_owner` rows of the merged owner to the survivor (`links.reassignOwner`, in the merge transaction through the adapter's `reassignOwner`). Terms stay with their record, as in the old module; the facade's choice (`ownerTermAt`, `isrcTermOf`) takes the survivor's own term first, then a merged one.
- Checked: `tests/pubv2.test.ts` (the merge moves the link, the line is paid at the survivor's 70 over the merged 30; the pure choice), `tests/auth.test.ts` (`publishingSource` in `me`), the frontend `nav.test.ts`.
- References: `packages/backend/src/shared/publishing-source.ts`, `packages/frontend/src/pages/work/actions/dialogs.tsx`, `packages/backend/src/facade/pubv2-source.ts`
- Status: accepted (the owner's request of 2026-11-08)

## D-195: The release registry without the file: preview the diff, apply the accepted changes
- Date: 2026-11-08
- Question: the registry of D-172 (`owners.releases`) stored the hash and the source of the file it came from and imported in one step (all or nothing). The owner wants the Believe release export (UPC, release date and time, title, version, artist, label string, track count, catalogue number, territories, stores) loaded periodically: a row per (organization, label, UPC), a new export only updates it, and the operator first sees what would change. The release date is how a track (its UPCs) gets a date, for the cuts of the catalogue that come next.
- Decision:
  - **The table** (migration 0030, recreated: it was empty in every store): `org_id, label_id, upc` (the key; the index `(org, upc)` stays), `release_title`, `version`, `artist_string`, `label_string`, `released_at timestamp without time zone` (the export's date and time as given, no zone in the value; a date alone is 00:00:00), `track_count`, `catalog_number`, `territories`, `stores`, `created_at` (the UPC entered the registry), `updated_at`. No `source`, no `file_sha256`: the audit keeps who changed what.
  - **`POST /releases/preview?label=&source=believe`** (upload; `source` picks the reader, `RELEASE_READERS`, a new format is a new reader): nothing written, no audit row. Every line: `create`, `update` with the fields that differ (`changes [{ field, before, after }]`, the registry row as `before`), `error` (its codes; a UPC whose lines disagree is an error on every line), `duplicate` (a repeated equal line, ignored); `unchanged` only counted; `missing` = the registry UPCs the file does not list (they stay). A bad line no longer refuses the file.
  - **`POST /releases/preview/apply?label=`** (JSON, up to 20 MB on this path only, the rest of the API keeps 1 MB): the accepted changes `{ upc, action, release, before }`, one transaction, all or nothing; a create of a UPC that exists or an update whose row differs from its `before` = `409 release_changed { upcs }` (preview again). Audit: the counts and the UPCs. `POST /releases/import` is removed.
  - The API row: `releaseTitle` (was `title`), `releasedAt`, `releaseDate` (its day), `createdAt`, `updatedAt` (was `importedAt`); under «Все» the row of a UPC is the last updated one.
  - P7 «Импорт релизов» is two steps: the file → the totals and the rows (checkboxes, all new and changed checked; the diff per field) → «Применить отмеченные».
- Checked: `tests/releases.test.ts` (the reader keeps the time and reports bad lines; the preview classes, the diff, the partial apply, `release_changed`, the large body, the audit), `tests/owners.test.ts` (created_at kept on a rewrite), the contract and the rights tables, e2e S48.
- Volume (found on the real export, 8 235 releases): the upsert goes in chunks of 2 000 rows (14 parameters a row, PostgreSQL takes 65 535 in one statement) inside the one transaction; `sources.isrcsOfUpcs` asks ClickHouse in chunks of 1 000 UPCs (a form field of its HTTP interface has a size limit). `tests/releases.test.ts` applies and reads 6 000 releases.
- Open: О1: the cuts of the catalogue (the generation of an ISRC by the earliest release date of its UPCs) and the signals «ISRC без даты релиза», «у релиза нет даты» are the next step.
- References: `packages/backend/migrations/postgres/0030_release_registry_v2.sql`, `packages/backend/src/facade/release-registry.ts`, `packages/backend/src/modules/owners/believe-export.ts`, `packages/frontend/src/pages/catalog/ReleaseImportDialog.tsx`
- Status: accepted (the owner's decision of 2026-11-08)

## D-196: The release signals tell what is wrong, never how much
- Date: 2026-11-08
- Question: with the real registry loaded (8 235 releases of 0to8) `releasesIncomplete` listed 4 364 releases, 4 354 of them absent from the reports altogether (new or not selling), and `releasesUnknown` summed money. The owner: a signal only says that something is wrong; no money in it.
- Decision:
  - The three signals read the release registry and the UPCs of the loaded files of each label with a registry (`sources.isrcsOfUpcs`, now with the last reporting month of the UPC), not the calculation. A label without a registry has none.
  - `releasesIncomplete` («Состав релиза не совпадает с отчётами»): a registry release whose UPC is in the label's reports with a number of distinct ISRCs other than its `# of track`, fewer or more; a release absent from the reports is not a signal.
  - `releasesUnknown` («Релиз из отчётов не найден в реестре»): a UPC of the label's reports not in its registry, with the title and artist of the report, its ISRCs and its last reporting month (the latest first); no money.
  - `releasesWithoutDate` («У релиза нет даты»): a registry row without `released_at`.
  - The onboarding checklist counts `releasesIncomplete` the same way.
  - «ISRC без даты релиза» waits for the cuts of the catalogue: today it is the consequence of `releasesUnknown`.
- Checked: `tests/releases.test.ts` (the pure rules, the stack: the money pin, the counts under a label and under «Все»), `tests/sources-service.test.ts`, e2e 21 (25 lists).
- References: `packages/backend/src/facade/release-registry.ts` (`releaseSignals`), `packages/backend/src/facade/reports.ts`, `packages/frontend/src/pages/work/signals/label-signals.tsx`
- Status: accepted (the owner's decision of 2026-11-08)


## D-197: The API and the client in dev containers, restarted on change
- Date: 2026-11-08
- Question: the owner asked whether the app can run in Docker during development and still pick up every edit.
- Decision:
  - `docker-compose.app.dev.yml` (development only) adds three services on `node:24-alpine` over a bind mount of the repo: `deps` (one-shot `npm ci` into named volumes for the three `node_modules`, skipped while the sha256 of `package-lock.json` equals the one it stored), `api` (`npm run dev` = `tsx watch`, :3000) and `web` (`npm run dev:client` = Vite with HMR, :5173). Ports on 127.0.0.1 only.
  - The containers read the same `.env` through the mount; compose sets only the in-network addresses (`DB_HOST=postgres`, `DB_PORT=5432`, `CLICKHOUSE_HOST=clickhouse`, `CLICKHOUSE_HTTP_PORT=8123`), which `process.loadEnvFile` does not override.
  - The Vite proxy target is `API_PROXY` (default `http://localhost:3000`, unchanged on the host); `web` sets `http://api:3000`.
  - The file names no database service and depends on none: it is always used on top of both stack files (`npm run dev:docker`, `dev:docker:logs`, `dev:docker:down`), so it never recreates postgres with the bind mount of `docker-compose.yml`.
  - Tests and e2e stay on the host; the host `npm run dev` / `dev:client` still work (stop the containers first: the same ports).
- Checked: `up --dry-run` touches only `deps`, `api`, `web`; `/api/v1/health` 200 directly and through the Vite proxy; an edit of `src/main.ts` restarts the API and an edit of `App.tsx` is an HMR update without polling (Docker Desktop on macOS).
- References: `docker-compose.app.dev.yml`, `packages/frontend/vite.config.ts`, `package.json`
- Status: accepted (the owner's request of 2026-11-08)
